   106	function boundedEnvInt(name: string, fallback: number, min: number, max: number): number {
   107	  const raw = Number(process.env[name] ?? fallback);
   108	  return Number.isFinite(raw) ? Math.max(min, Math.min(max, Math.floor(raw))) : fallback;
   109	}
   110	
   111	export function isDirectDesktopActionIntent(value: string): boolean {
   112	  const text = String(value || "").toLowerCase();
   113	  const hasExecutionCommand = /\b(powershell|system_shell_exec|system_file_edit|system_file_read|system_file_write|command prompt|cmd)\b/.test(text);
   114	  if (hasExecutionCommand) return true;
   115	  return /\b(open|launch|start|focus|click|tap|type|write|enter|move|drag|scroll|press|close)\b/.test(text) &&
   116	    /\b(windows|pc|desktop|computer|notepad|mouse|cursor|keyboard|taskbar|start menu|file explorer|task manager|app)\b/.test(text) &&
   117	    !/\b(repair|recover|restore|reconnect|restart|offline|unreachable|heartbeat|diagnos|debug|audit|inspect|investigate)\b/.test(text);
   118	}

   150	export function directDesktopToolStepBudget(base: number): number {
   151	  const configured = boundedEnvInt("GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_DIRECT_DESKTOP_MAX_TOOL_STEPS", 12, 6, 18);
   152	  return Math.max(1, Math.min(base, configured));

   154	
   155	const EVIDENCE_REVISION_KEYS = new Set([
   156	  "sha", "hash", "version", "etag", "commit_sha", "commitHash", "content_sha",
   157	  "head_sha", "deployment_id", "deploymentId", "id", "status", "state", "exists"
   158	]);
   159	
   160	export function evidenceProgressFingerprint(
   161	  toolName: string,
   162	  cacheKey: string | null,
   163	  resultData: unknown
   164	): string | null {
   165	  const resource = String(cacheKey ?? "").trim();
   166	  if (!resource) return null;
   167	  const revisions: string[] = [];
   168	  const visit = (value: unknown, depth: number): void => {
   169	    if (depth > 4 || revisions.length >= 32 || value === null || value === undefined) return;
   170	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
   171	      for (const item of value.slice(0, 20)) visit(item, depth + 1);
   172	      return;
   173	    }
   174	    if (typeof value !== "object") return;
   175	    for (const [key, nested] of Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)) {
   176	      if (
   177	        EVIDENCE_REVISION_KEYS.has(key) &&
   178	        (typeof nested === "string" || typeof nested === "number" || typeof nested === "boolean")
   179	      ) {
   180	        revisions.push(`${key}=${String(nested)}`);
   181	      } else if (typeof nested === "object") {
   182	        visit(nested, depth + 1);
   183	      }
   184	    }
   185	  };
   186	  visit(resultData, 0);
   187	  const stableRevision = revisions.sort().join("|") || computeEvidenceResultHash(resultData);
   188	  return createHash("sha256")
   189	    .update(`${toolName}|${resource}|${stableRevision}`)
   190	    .digest("hex")
   191	    .slice(0, 24);
   192	}
   193	
   194	export function evidenceProgressCreditLimit(observationBudget: number): number {
   195	  const cap = observationBudget > 12 ? 24 : 8;
   196	  return Math.max(1, Math.min(cap, Math.max(1, observationBudget - 1)));
   197	}

   199	export function nextObservationOnlyStreak(
   200	  previous: number,
   201	  routerOnlyTool: boolean,
   202	  cacheableRead: boolean,
   203	  successful: boolean,
   204	  newBoundedEvidence = false
   205	): number {
   206	  return successful && !routerOnlyTool && (!cacheableRead || newBoundedEvidence) ? 0 : previous + 1;
   207	}
   208	
   209	export function noProgressObservationBudget(
   210	  taskType: GeminxModelTaskType,
   211	  directDesktopRun: boolean,
   212	  comprehensiveAudit = false
   213	): number {
   214	  if (comprehensiveAudit) return 50;
   215	  // Read-only discovery is not proof of a changed world. Leave room for a
   216	  // genuine investigation without letting a conversational turn spin through
   217	  // dozens of different status/search tools at the Founder's expense.
   218	  return directDesktopRun ? 5 : taskType === "chat" ? 16 : 12;
   219	}
   220	
   221	export function companionToolStepBudget(
   222	  _taskType: GeminxModelTaskType,
   223	  _intent: string,
   224	  toolNames: readonly string[] = []
   225	): number {
   226	  if (toolNames.length === 0) return 3;
   227	  const explicit = process.env.GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_MAX_TOOL_STEPS;
   228	  if (explicit !== undefined) {
   229	    return boundedEnvInt(
   230	      "GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_MAX_TOOL_STEPS",
   231	      DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_STEPS,
   232	      8,
   233	      TOOL_LOOP_GLOBAL_BREAKER_THRESHOLD
   234	    );
   235	  }
   236	
   237	  // OpenClaw does not decide that a browser/device/repository objective only
   238	  // deserves four or six tool cycles. Keep the same run alive and let verified
   239	  // completion, context/spend limits and loop detection decide when it stops.
   240	  return DEFAULT_MAX_TOOL_STEPS;
   241	}

   247	export function shouldAttemptPaidFinalization(input: {
   248	  forcedProtectedBoundary: boolean;
   249	  observationOnlyBlocked: boolean;
   250	  contextPressureBlocked: boolean;
   251	  hasFinalMessage: boolean;
   252	  hasPendingToolCalls: boolean;
   253	  intermediateToolNarrative: boolean;
   254	  missingRequiredTerminalMarker: boolean;
   255	}): boolean {
   256	  return (
   257	    !input.forcedProtectedBoundary &&
   258	    !input.observationOnlyBlocked &&
   259	    !input.contextPressureBlocked &&
   260	    (
   261	      !input.hasFinalMessage ||
   262	      input.hasPendingToolCalls ||
   263	      input.intermediateToolNarrative ||
   264	      input.missingRequiredTerminalMarker
   265	    )
   266	  );
   267	}

   274	
   275	function configuredSpendLimitUsd(
   276	  name: string,
   277	  minimum: number,
   278	  safeDefault: number,
   279	  allowUnlimited = false
   280	): number {
   281	  const configured = process.env[name]?.trim();
   282	  if (!configured) {
   283	    return Math.max(minimum, safeDefault);
   284	  }
   285	  if (/^(?:0|off|none|unlimited)$/i.test(configured)) {
   286	    return allowUnlimited ? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY : Math.max(minimum, safeDefault);
   287	  }
   288	  const raw = Number(configured);
   289	  return Number.isFinite(raw) && raw > 0 ? Math.max(minimum, raw) : Math.max(minimum, safeDefault);
   290	}

   313	
   314	function contextCheckpointThresholdTokens(): number {
   315	  const explicit = Number(process.env.GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_CONTEXT_CHECKPOINT_TOKENS ?? 0);
   316	  if (Number.isFinite(explicit) && explicit >= 4_000) return Math.floor(explicit);
   317	
   318	  const configuredWindow = Number(process.env.GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_CONTEXT_WINDOW_TOKENS ?? 0);
   319	  if (Number.isFinite(configuredWindow) && configuredWindow >= 8_000) {
   320	    return Math.max(4_000, Math.floor(configuredWindow * 0.60));
   321	  }
   322	
   323	  // Conservative provider-agnostic fallback. Override with the real model
   324	  // window when the provider exposes/changes it.
   325	  return 60_000;
   326	}
   327	
   328	function contextCheckpointDirectory(): string | null {
   329	  const configured = process.env.GEMINX_CONTEXT_CHECKPOINT_DIR?.trim();
   330	  if (configured) return configured;
   331	  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "test") return null;
   332	  return path.join(process.env.GEMINX_RUNTIME_ROOT?.trim() || "/app/runtime", "geminx-context-checkpoints");
   333	}
   334	
   335	function safeCheckpointId(value: string): string {
   336	  return value.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]/g, "_").slice(0, 180) || "unbound";
   337	}
   338	
   339	function persistContextPressureCheckpoint(input: {
   340	  key: string;
   341	  stage: string;
   342	  intent: string;
   343	  promptTokens: number;
   344	  thresholdTokens: number;
   345	  conversationId?: string | null;
   346	  activeTaskId?: string | null;
   347	  /** Trusted server-only binding from hasValidFounderSession(req), never client JSON. */
   348	  founderAuthorised?: boolean;
   349	  brainSources: AgentBrainContextSource[];
   350	  toolCalls: readonly ToolCallRecord[];
   351	}): void {
   352	  const dir = contextCheckpointDirectory();
   353	  if (!dir) return;
   354	  try {
   355	    fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   356	    const target = path.join(dir, safeCheckpointId(input.key) + ".json");
   357	    const payload = {
   358	      schema_version: "geminx.context-checkpoint.v1",
   359	      checkpointed_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   360	      stage: input.stage,
   361	      correlation_key: input.key,
   362	      conversation_id: input.conversationId ?? null,
   363	      active_task_id: input.activeTaskId ?? null,
   364	      objective: String(input.intent || "").slice(0, 12_000),
   365	      prompt_tokens: input.promptTokens,
   366	      threshold_tokens: input.thresholdTokens,
   367	      brain_sources: input.brainSources.slice(0, 24).map((source) => ({
   368	        kind: source.kind ?? null,
   369	        path: source.path ?? null,
   370	        issue_number: source.issue_number ?? null,
   371	        title: source.title
   372	      })),
   373	      executed_tools: input.toolCalls.slice(-40).map((call) => ({
   374	        tool: call.tool,
   375	        status: call.status,
   376	        error: call.error ? String(call.error).slice(0, 500) : null
   377	      }))
   378	    };
   379	    const tmp = target + ".tmp-" + process.pid;
   380	    fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2) + "\n", "utf8");
   381	    fs.renameSync(tmp, target);
   382	  } catch (error) {
   383	    console.warn("[geminx-context] checkpoint_write_failed reason=" + (error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)));
   384	  }
   385	}
   386	
   387	interface DeepSeekSpendLedger {
   388	  day: string;
   389	  estimated_peak_usd: number;
   390	  provider_requests: number;
   391	}
   392	
   393	function spendLedgerPath(): string | null {
   394	  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "test") return null;
   395	  return process.env.GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_SPEND_LEDGER_PATH?.trim() || "/app/runtime/deepseek-spend-ledger.json";
   396	}
   397	
   398	function readDailySpendLedger(): DeepSeekSpendLedger {
   399	  const day = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
   400	  const ledgerPath = spendLedgerPath();
   401	  if (!ledgerPath) return { day, estimated_peak_usd: 0, provider_requests: 0 };
   402	  try {
   403	    const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(ledgerPath, "utf8")) as Partial<DeepSeekSpendLedger>;
   404	    if (parsed.day !== day) return { day, estimated_peak_usd: 0, provider_requests: 0 };
   405	    return {
   406	      day,
   407	      estimated_peak_usd: Math.max(0, Number(parsed.estimated_peak_usd ?? 0) || 0),
   408	      provider_requests: Math.max(0, Number(parsed.provider_requests ?? 0) || 0)
   409	    };
   410	  } catch {
   411	    return { day, estimated_peak_usd: 0, provider_requests: 0 };
   412	  }
   413	}
   414	
   415	function writeDailySpendLedger(ledger: DeepSeekSpendLedger): void {
   416	  const ledgerPath = spendLedgerPath();
   417	  if (!ledgerPath) return;
   418	  try {
   419	    fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(ledgerPath), { recursive: true });
   420	    const tmp = `${ledgerPath}.tmp-${process.pid}`;
   421	    fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(ledger, null, 2) + "\n", "utf8");
   422	    fs.renameSync(tmp, ledgerPath);
   423	  } catch (error) {
   424	    console.warn(`[deepseek-budget] ledger_write_failed reason=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
   425	  }
   426	}
   427	
   428	function chargeDailySpend(estimatedPeakUsd: number): DeepSeekSpendLedger {
   429	  const ledger = readDailySpendLedger();
   430	  const next: DeepSeekSpendLedger = {
   431	    day: ledger.day,
   432	    estimated_peak_usd: ledger.estimated_peak_usd + Math.max(0, estimatedPeakUsd),
   433	    provider_requests: ledger.provider_requests + 1
   434	  };
   435	  writeDailySpendLedger(next);
   436	  return next;
   437	}

   439	interface ObjectiveSpendVelocityEvent {
   440	  at_ms: number;
   441	  estimated_peak_usd: number;
   442	}
   443	
   444	interface ObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger {
   445	  schema_version: "geminx.deepseek-objective-velocity.v1";
   446	  objectives: Record<string, ObjectiveSpendVelocityEvent[]>;
   447	  lifetime_usd: Record<string, number>;
   448	}
   449	
   450	function objectiveSpendVelocityLedgerPath(): string | null {
   451	  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "test") return process.env.GEMINX_TEST_OBJECTIVE_SPEND_LEDGER_PATH?.trim() || null;
   452	  return process.env.GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_LEDGER_PATH?.trim()
   453	    || "/app/runtime/deepseek-objective-velocity.json";
   454	}
   455	
   456	function objectiveSpendVelocityLimitUsd(): number {
   457	  return configuredSpendLimitUsd(
   458	    "GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_MAX_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_OBJECTIVE_PER_MINUTE",
   459	    0.05,
   460	    DEFAULT_OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_USD_PER_MINUTE
   461	  );
   462	}
   463	
   464	function readObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger(): ObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger {
   465	  const ledgerPath = objectiveSpendVelocityLedgerPath();
   466	  if (!ledgerPath) return { schema_version: "geminx.deepseek-objective-velocity.v1", objectives: {}, lifetime_usd: {} };
   467	  try {
   468	    const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(ledgerPath, "utf8")) as Partial<ObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger>;
   469	    if (parsed.schema_version !== "geminx.deepseek-objective-velocity.v1" || !parsed.objectives || typeof parsed.objectives !== "object") {
   470	      throw new Error("OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_LEDGER_SCHEMA_INVALID");
   471	    }
   472	    return {
   473	      schema_version: "geminx.deepseek-objective-velocity.v1",
   474	      objectives: parsed.objectives as Record<string, ObjectiveSpendVelocityEvent[]>,
   475	      lifetime_usd: parsed.lifetime_usd && typeof parsed.lifetime_usd === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed.lifetime_usd)
   476	        ? parsed.lifetime_usd as Record<string, number>
   477	        : {}
   478	    };
   479	  } catch (error) {
   480	    if ((error as NodeJS.ErrnoException)?.code === "ENOENT") {
   481	      return { schema_version: "geminx.deepseek-objective-velocity.v1", objectives: {}, lifetime_usd: {} };
   482	    }
   483	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
   484	      503,
   485	      "DeepSeek spend velocity ledger unavailable",
   486	      `DEEPSEEK_OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_LEDGER_UNHEALTHY ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
   487	    );
   488	  }
   489	}
   490	
   491	function writeObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger(ledger: ObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger): void {
   492	  const ledgerPath = objectiveSpendVelocityLedgerPath();
   493	  if (!ledgerPath) return;
   494	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(ledgerPath), { recursive: true });
   495	  const tmp = `${ledgerPath}.tmp-${process.pid}-${Date.now()}-${randomUUID()}`;
   496	  fs.writeFileSync(tmp, JSON.stringify(ledger, null, 2) + "\n", "utf8");
   497	  try {
   498	    // Windows/NTFS can briefly hold the destination open during rename.
   499	    // Retry only transient filesystem contention, never a provider/model call.
   500	    // The objective reservation lock remains held across this bounded operation.
   501	    for (let attempt = 0; ; attempt += 1) {
   502	      try {
   503	        fs.renameSync(tmp, ledgerPath);
   504	        break;
   505	      } catch (error) {
   506	        const code = (error as NodeJS.ErrnoException).code;
   507	        if (!["EPERM", "EACCES", "EBUSY"].includes(String(code)) || attempt >= 3) {
   508	          // Preserve the prior committed ledger and fail closed: no LLM dispatch.
   509	          throw error;
   510	        }
   511	        Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)), 0, 0, 20 * (attempt + 1));
   512	      }
   513	    }
   514	  } finally {
   515	    fs.rmSync(tmp, { force: true });
   516	  }
   517	}
   518	
   519	export function classifyObjectiveLifetimeSpendAdmission(spentUsd: number, reservationUsd: number, limitUsd: number): "ALLOW" | "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED" {
   520	  if (!Number.isFinite(limitUsd)) return "ALLOW";
   521	  return Math.max(0, spentUsd) + Math.max(0, reservationUsd) <= limitUsd + 1e-10
   522	    ? "ALLOW" : "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED";
   523	}
   524	
   525	export function maxObjectiveSpendUsd(comprehensiveAudit = false): number {
   526	  // Long-running, evidence-producing objectives must not stop at an arbitrary
   527	  // cumulative $0.10. An explicit owner-configured objective limit still wins;
   528	  // per-request and per-minute guards and the no-progress circuit remain active.
   529	  // A per-run budget is not an objective budget: retries and explicit resumes share
   530	  // the same lifetime ceiling. The Founder-approved ceiling is $3 per objective;
   531	  // an independent $3/day backend spend cap governs aggregate provider requests.
   532	  // Owner overrides may lower it but never make it unlimited.
   533	  const safeDefault = 3.00;
   534	  return Math.min(3.00, configuredSpendLimitUsd("GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_MAX_ESTIMATED_USD_PER_OBJECTIVE", 0.0001, safeDefault, false));
   535	}
   536	
   537	/** Exclusive filesystem admission lock: competing workers cannot both reserve the same dollars. */
   538	function withObjectiveSpendLedgerLock<T>(operation: () => T): T {
   539	  const file = objectiveSpendVelocityLedgerPath();
   540	  if (!file) return operation();
   541	  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(file), { recursive: true });
   542	  const lockFile = file + ".lock";
   543	  let descriptor: number;
   544	  try {
   545	    descriptor = fs.openSync(lockFile, "wx", 0o600);
   546	  } catch {
   547	    // Fail closed; never dispatch a provider request when the ledger is contested.
   548	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(503, "Objective spend ledger locked", "DEEPSEEK_OBJECTIVE_LEDGER_LOCK_UNAVAILABLE");
   549	  }
   550	  try {
   551	    return operation();
   552	  } finally {
   553	    try { fs.closeSync(descriptor); } finally { fs.rmSync(lockFile, { force: true }); }
   554	  }
   555	}
   556	
   557	export function reserveObjectiveLifetimeSpend(objectiveKey: string, reservationUsd: number, comprehensiveAudit = false): void {
   558	  withObjectiveSpendLedgerLock(() => {
   559	  const ledger = readObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger();
   560	  const prior = Math.max(0, Number(ledger.lifetime_usd[objectiveKey]) || 0);
   561	  const limit = maxObjectiveSpendUsd(comprehensiveAudit);
   562	  if (classifyObjectiveLifetimeSpendAdmission(prior, reservationUsd, limit) !== "ALLOW") {
   563	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(429, "DeepSeek objective lifetime budget reached",
   564	      `DEEPSEEK_OBJECTIVE_LIFETIME_BUDGET_REACHED objective=${safeCheckpointId(objectiveKey)} spent_usd=${prior.toFixed(6)} request_reservation_usd=${reservationUsd.toFixed(6)} limit_usd=${limit.toFixed(2)}`);
   565	  }
   566	  // Reserve durably BEFORE provider dispatch. If the process crashes or the
   567	  // response is ambiguous, retaining the reservation fails closed on spend.
   568	  ledger.lifetime_usd[objectiveKey] = prior + reservationUsd;
   569	  writeObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger(ledger);
   570	  });
   571	}
   572	
   573	export function settleObjectiveLifetimeSpend(objectiveKey: string, reservationUsd: number, meteredUsd: number): number {
   574	  return withObjectiveSpendLedgerLock(() => {
   575	  const ledger = readObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger();
   576	  ledger.lifetime_usd[objectiveKey] = Math.max(0,
   577	    (Number(ledger.lifetime_usd[objectiveKey]) || 0) - reservationUsd + meteredUsd);
   578	  writeObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger(ledger);
   579	  return ledger.lifetime_usd[objectiveKey];
   580	  });
   581	}
   582	
   583	function rollingObjectiveSpend(
   584	  ledger: ObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger,
   585	  objectiveKey: string,
   586	  nowMs = Date.now()
   587	): { usd: number; events: ObjectiveSpendVelocityEvent[] } {
   588	  const cutoff = nowMs - OBJECTIVE_SPEND_VELOCITY_WINDOW_MS;
   589	  const events = (ledger.objectives[objectiveKey] ?? [])
   590	    .filter((event) => Number.isFinite(event.at_ms) && event.at_ms >= cutoff)
   591	    .slice(-64);
   592	  return {
   593	    usd: events.reduce((sum, event) => sum + Math.max(0, Number(event.estimated_peak_usd) || 0), 0),
   594	    events
   595	  };
   596	}
   597	
   598	function assertObjectiveSpendVelocity(objectiveKey: string, requestReservationUsd: number): void {
   599	  const ledger = readObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger();
   600	  const rolling = rollingObjectiveSpend(ledger, objectiveKey);
   601	  const limit = objectiveSpendVelocityLimitUsd();
   602	  const projected = rolling.usd + Math.max(0, requestReservationUsd);
   603	  if (projected <= limit) return;
   604	  throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
   605	    429,
   606	    "DeepSeek objective spend velocity fuse tripped",
   607	    `DEEPSEEK_OBJECTIVE_VELOCITY_FUSE_TRIPPED VELOCITY_FUSE_TRIPPED objective=${safeCheckpointId(objectiveKey)} rolling_60s_usd=${rolling.usd.toFixed(4)} request_reservation_usd=${requestReservationUsd.toFixed(4)} projected_usd=${projected.toFixed(4)} limit_usd=${limit.toFixed(2)}`
   608	  );
   609	}
   610	
   611	function recordObjectiveSpendVelocity(objectiveKey: string, estimatedPeakUsd: number): void {
   612	  return withObjectiveSpendLedgerLock(() => {
   613	  const amount = Math.max(0, estimatedPeakUsd);
   614	  if (amount <= 0) return;
   615	  const ledger = readObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger();
   616	  const rolling = rollingObjectiveSpend(ledger, objectiveKey);
   617	  ledger.objectives[objectiveKey] = [
   618	    ...rolling.events,
   619	    { at_ms: Date.now(), estimated_peak_usd: amount }
   620	  ].slice(-64);
   621	  // Keep only a bounded set of active objective ledgers so this durable fuse cannot grow forever.
   622	  ledger.objectives = Object.fromEntries(
   623	    Object.entries(ledger.objectives)
   624	      .filter(([, events]) => Array.isArray(events) && events.some((event) => event.at_ms >= Date.now() - 5 * OBJECTIVE_SPEND_VELOCITY_WINDOW_MS))
   625	      .slice(-128)
   626	  );
   627	  writeObjectiveSpendVelocityLedger(ledger);
   628	  });
   629	}
   630	
   631	function estimatedPeakUsd(
   632	  model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro",
   633	  usage: DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse["usage"]
   634	): number {
   635	  if (!usage) return 0;
   636	  const prompt = Math.max(0, Number(usage.prompt_tokens ?? 0));
   637	  const details = usage.prompt_tokens_details;
   638	  const hit = Math.max(0, Number(details?.prompt_cache_hit_tokens ?? details?.cached_tokens ?? 0));
   639	  const missReported = details?.prompt_cache_miss_tokens;
   640	  const miss = Math.max(0, Number(missReported ?? Math.max(0, prompt - hit)));
   641	  const output = Math.max(0, Number(usage.completion_tokens ?? 0));
   642	  const rates = deepSeekRates(model);
   643	  return ((hit * rates.hit) + (miss * rates.miss) + (output * rates.output)) / 1_000_000;
   644	}
   645	
   646	let deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd = 0;
   647	
   648	function deepSeekRates(model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro"): { hit: number; miss: number; output: number } {
   649	  return model === "deepseek-v4-pro"
   650	    ? { hit: 0.044, miss: 1.32, output: 3.96 }
   651	    : { hit: 0.006, miss: 0.30, output: 1.20 };
   652	}
   653	
   654	export type DeepSeekDailySpendAdmission = "ALLOW" | "RESERVATION_CONTENTION" | "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED";
   655	
   656	export function classifyDeepSeekDailySpendAdmission(
   657	  ledgerEstimatedUsd: number,
   658	  inFlightReservedUsd: number,
   659	  requestReservationUsd: number,
   660	  dailyBudgetUsd: number
   661	): DeepSeekDailySpendAdmission {
   662	  const ledger = Math.max(0, Number(ledgerEstimatedUsd) || 0);
   663	  const inFlight = Math.max(0, Number(inFlightReservedUsd) || 0);
   664	  const reservation = Math.max(0, Number(requestReservationUsd) || 0);
   665	  const budget = Math.max(0, Number(dailyBudgetUsd) || 0);
   666	  const standaloneProjected = ledger + reservation;
   667	  if (standaloneProjected > budget) return "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED";
   668	  if (standaloneProjected + inFlight > budget) return "RESERVATION_CONTENTION";
   669	  return "ALLOW";
   670	}
   671	
   672	function reserveInFlightDailySpend(
   673	  model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro",
   674	  estimatedInputTokens: number,
   675	  dailyBudget: number,
   676	  proxyMaxOutputTokens?: number
   677	): number {
   678	  const rates = deepSeekRates(model);
   679	  // Reserve the worst case for this bounded request: all estimated input is a
   680	  // cache miss and the model uses its configured maximum output tokens.
   681	  const reservation =
   682	    ((Math.max(0, estimatedInputTokens) * rates.miss)
   683	      + (Math.max(maxOutputTokens(model), Math.min(16_384, Math.max(0, Math.floor(proxyMaxOutputTokens ?? 0)))) * rates.output)) / 1_000_000;
   684	  const ledger = readDailySpendLedger();
   685	  const standaloneProjected = ledger.estimated_peak_usd + reservation;
   686	  const projected = standaloneProjected + deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd;
   687	  const admission = classifyDeepSeekDailySpendAdmission(
   688	    ledger.estimated_peak_usd,
   689	    deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd,
   690	    reservation,
   691	    dailyBudget
   692	  );
   693	
   694	  if (admission === "RESERVATION_CONTENTION") {
   695	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
   696	      429,
   697	      "DeepSeek daily spend reservation temporarily busy",
   698	      `DEEPSEEK_DAILY_SPEND_RESERVATION_BUSY estimated_peak_usd=${ledger.estimated_peak_usd.toFixed(4)} in_flight_reserved_usd=${deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd.toFixed(4)} request_reservation_usd=${reservation.toFixed(4)} standalone_projected_usd=${standaloneProjected.toFixed(4)} projected_usd=${projected.toFixed(4)} limit_usd=${dailyBudget.toFixed(2)}`
   699	    );
   700	  }
   701	
   702	  if (admission === "BUDGET_EXHAUSTED") {
   703	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
   704	      429,
   705	      "DeepSeek daily spend budget reached",
   706	      `DEEPSEEK_DAILY_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED estimated_peak_usd=${ledger.estimated_peak_usd.toFixed(4)} in_flight_reserved_usd=${deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd.toFixed(4)} request_reservation_usd=${reservation.toFixed(4)} standalone_projected_usd=${standaloneProjected.toFixed(4)} projected_usd=${projected.toFixed(4)} limit_usd=${dailyBudget.toFixed(2)}`
   707	    );
   708	  }
   709	
   710	  deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd += reservation;
   711	  return reservation;
   712	}
   713	
   714	function releaseInFlightDailySpend(reservationUsd: number): void {
   715	  deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd = Math.max(0, deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd - Math.max(0, reservationUsd));
   716	}
   717	
   718	
   719	/**
   720	 * Read-only model exhaustion is safely resumable ONLY if no mutation-capable
   721	 * tool was attempted. Provider output is never proof of a committed side effect.
   722	 */
   723	export function safeUnusableReplyReadOnlyTrace(
   724	  calls: readonly Pick<ToolCallRecord, "tool" | "status">[]
   725	): boolean {
   726	  const allow = /^(?:github_(?:get_repository|read_file|read_file_range|repo_map|file_status|search_code|list_[a-z_]+|get_[a-z_]+)|railway_(?:get_[a-z_]+|list_[a-z_]+|status)|geminx_(?:runtime_capabilities|tool_search))$/;
   727	  return calls.length > 0 && calls.every(call => allow.test(call.tool));
   728	}
   729	
   730	export class DeepSeekCompanionError extends Error {
   731	  constructor(
   732	    readonly statusCode: number,
   733	    readonly publicMessage: string,
   734	    message: string
   735	  ) {
   736	    super(message);
   737	    this.name = "DeepSeekCompanionError";
   738	  }
   739	}
   740	
   741	/**
   742	 * Charge OpenClaw's authenticated DeepSeek proxy to the SAME process-local daily
   743	 * ledger and in-flight reservations as native GeminX DeepSeek requests.
   744	 * This is intentionally a thin adapter, not another spend authority.
   745	 */
   746	export function reserveOpenClawProxyDailySpend(
   747	  model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro",
   748	  estimatedInputTokens: number,
   749	  requestedMaxOutputTokens: number
   750	): number {
   751	  return reserveInFlightDailySpend(model, estimatedInputTokens, maxDailySpendUsd(), requestedMaxOutputTokens);
   752	}
   753	
   754	export function settleOpenClawProxyDailySpend(
   755	  model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro",
   756	  reservationUsd: number,
   757	  usage: DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse["usage"] | null,
   758	  providerSucceeded: boolean
   759	): { estimated_peak_call_usd: number; estimated_peak_daily_usd: number; metered: boolean } {
   760	  releaseInFlightDailySpend(reservationUsd);
   761	  if (!providerSucceeded) {
   762	    return { estimated_peak_call_usd: 0, estimated_peak_daily_usd: readDailySpendLedger().estimated_peak_usd, metered: false };
   763	  }
   764	  // A successful stream with no usage still costs money. Fail safely by charging
   765	  // its worst-case reservation rather than pretending the request was free.
   766	  const metered = Boolean(usage);
   767	  const cost = usage ? estimatedPeakUsd(model, usage) : reservationUsd;
   768	  const daily = chargeDailySpend(cost);
   769	  console.log(
   770	    `[deepseek-usage] source=openclaw-proxy model=${model} prompt_tokens=${usage?.prompt_tokens ?? "unknown"} completion_tokens=${usage?.completion_tokens ?? "unknown"} cache_hit_tokens=${usage?.prompt_tokens_details?.prompt_cache_hit_tokens ?? usage?.prompt_tokens_details?.cached_tokens ?? "unknown"} estimated_peak_call_usd=${cost.toFixed(6)} estimated_peak_daily_usd=${daily.estimated_peak_usd.toFixed(6)} metered=${metered}`
   771	  );
   772	  return { estimated_peak_call_usd: cost, estimated_peak_daily_usd: daily.estimated_peak_usd, metered };
   773	}

   775	interface DeepSeekMessage {
   776	  role: "system" | "user" | "assistant" | "tool";
   777	  content: string | null;
   778	  tool_calls?: unknown;
   779	  tool_call_id?: string;
   780	  reasoning_content?: unknown;
   781	}
   782	
   783	interface DeepSeekToolDefinition {
   784	  type: "function";
   785	  function: {
   786	    name: string;
   787	    description: string;
   788	    parameters: Record<string, unknown>;
   789	  };
   790	}
   791	
   792	function maxProviderRequestsPerTurn(): number {
   793	  return boundedEnvInt(
   794	    "GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_MAX_PROVIDER_REQUESTS_PER_TURN",
   795	    DEFAULT_MAX_PROVIDER_REQUESTS_PER_TURN,
   796	    32,
   797	    64
   798	  );
   799	}
   800	
   801	function maxEstimatedInputTokensPerRequest(): number {
   802	  return boundedEnvInt(
   803	    "GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_MAX_ESTIMATED_INPUT_TOKENS",
   804	    DEFAULT_MAX_ESTIMATED_INPUT_TOKENS_PER_REQUEST,
   805	    16_000,
   806	    120_000
   807	  );
   808	}
   809	
   810	function contextRolloverTargetTokens(): number {
   811	  return boundedEnvInt(
   812	    "GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_CONTEXT_ROLLOVER_TARGET_TOKENS",
   813	    DEFAULT_CONTEXT_ROLLOVER_TARGET_TOKENS,
   814	    12_000,
   815	    48_000
   816	  );
   817	}
   818	
   819	/** A context-window overflow is measured per provider request, never by
   820	 * summing earlier (already paid and discarded) request windows. Cumulative
   821	 * tokens remain spending telemetry, not a prompt-length signal. */
   822	export function shouldCheckpointContextWindow(input: {
   823	  currentPromptTokens: number;
   824	  checkpointThresholdTokens: number;
   825	}): boolean {
   826	  return Number.isFinite(input.currentPromptTokens) &&
   827	    input.currentPromptTokens >= input.checkpointThresholdTokens;
   828	}
   829	
   830	function maxCumulativePromptTokensPerTurn(comprehensiveAudit = false): number {
   831	  const defaultTokens = comprehensiveAudit ? 500_000 : DEFAULT_MAX_CUMULATIVE_PROMPT_TOKENS_PER_TURN;
   832	  return boundedEnvInt(
   833	    "GEMINX_DEEPSEEK_MAX_CUMULATIVE_PROMPT_TOKENS",
   834	    defaultTokens,
   835	    40_000,
   836	    750_000
   837	  );
   838	}
   839	
   840	function estimateProviderInputTokens(
   841	  messages: readonly DeepSeekMessage[],
   842	  toolDefinitions: readonly DeepSeekToolDefinition[]
   843	): number {
   844	  // Conservative local preflight. We deliberately over-estimate JSON/token
   845	  // overhead so a pathological tool loop is stopped BEFORE another paid call.
   846	  const chars = Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify({ messages, tools: toolDefinitions }), "utf8");
   847	  return Math.ceil(chars / 3.2);
   848	}
   849	
   850	function boundedProviderPreview(value: unknown, maxChars = 900): string {
   851	  let encoded = "";
   852	  try {
   853	    encoded = JSON.stringify(value ?? null);
   854	  } catch {
   855	    encoded = String(value ?? "");
   856	  }
   857	  return encoded.length <= maxChars ? encoded : encoded.slice(0, maxChars) + "[TRUNCATED]";
   858	}
   859	
   860	// Preserve the actual Founder objective, not the first 8k characters of the
   861	// Preserves the immutable Founder objective in durable storage, retaining 100%
   862	// of acceptance criteria and constraints across context rollover and compactions.
   863	export function preserveObjectiveForRollover(intent: string, jobId?: string): string {
   864	  const original = String(intent ?? "");
   865	  const objectiveIndex = original.search(/(?:^|\n)OBJECTIVE:\n/);
   866	  
   867	  let coreText = original;
   868	  if (objectiveIndex >= 0) {
   869	    const rawCore = original.slice(objectiveIndex + (original[objectiveIndex] === "\n" ? 1 : 0));
   870	    const marker = /(?:^|\n)(OBJECTIVE|VERIFIED CURRENT STATE|COMPACT VERIFIED CURRENT STATE|EVIDENCE REFERENCES|ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA|CONSTRAINTS):\n/g;
   871	    const headings = [...rawCore.matchAll(marker)];
   872	    if (headings.length > 0) {
   873	      const limits: Record<string, number> = {
   874	        "OBJECTIVE": 5_500,
   875	        "VERIFIED CURRENT STATE": 2_800,
   876	        "COMPACT VERIFIED CURRENT STATE": 2_800,
   877	        "EVIDENCE REFERENCES": 1_200,
   878	        "ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA": 3_500,
   879	        "CONSTRAINTS": 2_600,
   880	      };
   881	      const chunks: string[] = [];
   882	      for (let index = 0; index < headings.length; index += 1) {
   883	        const heading = headings[index];
   884	        const start = (heading.index ?? 0) + (rawCore[heading.index ?? 0] === "\n" ? 1 : 0);
   885	        const next = headings[index + 1];
   886	        const stop = next ? (next.index ?? rawCore.length) : rawCore.length;
   887	        const label = heading[1];
   888	        const raw = rawCore.slice(start, stop).trim();
   889	        const cap = limits[label] ?? 1_500;
   890	        chunks.push(raw.length <= cap
   891	          ? raw
   892	          : raw.slice(0, cap - 500)
   893	            + "\n[SECTION MIDDLE OMITTED; full state remains in durable objective]\n"
   894	            + raw.slice(-400));
   895	      }
   896	      coreText = chunks.join("\n\n");
   897	    } else {
   898	      coreText = rawCore;
   899	    }
   900	  }
   901	
   902	  // Enforce strict character cap on coreText even when no OBJECTIVE:\n marker is present
   903	  const defaultCoreCap = 5_500;
   904	  if (coreText.length > defaultCoreCap) {
   905	    coreText = coreText.slice(0, 3_000)
   906	      + "\n[CORE OBJECTIVE MIDDLE OMITTED; full state remains in durable objective]\n"
   907	      + coreText.slice(-2_000);
   908	  }
   909	
   910	  const effectiveJobId = jobId || `job_${computeSha256(coreText).slice(0, 16)}`;
   911	  const record = saveCanonicalObjective({
   912	    jobId: effectiveJobId,
   913	    text: coreText,
   914	  });
   915	  return buildCompactedExecutionDirective(record);
   916	}
   917	
   918	function archivedExecutionEvidenceFromMessages(messages: readonly DeepSeekMessage[]): ArchivedExecutionEvidence[] {
   919	  const prefix = "ARCHIVED_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT=";
   920	  const archiveMessage = [...messages].reverse().find((message) =>
   921	    message.role === "system" && typeof message.content === "string" && message.content.includes(prefix));
   922	  if (!archiveMessage || typeof archiveMessage.content !== "string") return [];
   923	  const at = archiveMessage.content.indexOf(prefix);
   924	  if (at < 0) return [];
   925	  try {
   926	    const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(archiveMessage.content.slice(at + prefix.length));
   927	    const evidence = parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
   928	      ? (parsed as Record<string, unknown>).evidence : null;
   929	    if (!Array.isArray(evidence)) return [];
   930	    return evidence.filter((row): row is ArchivedExecutionEvidence =>
   931	      Boolean(row) && typeof row === "object" &&
   932	      /^[a-zA-Z0-9_:-]{1,180}$/.test(String((row as ArchivedExecutionEvidence).tool_call_id ?? "")) &&
   933	      /^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,180}$/.test(String((row as ArchivedExecutionEvidence).tool ?? "")) &&
   934	      /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(String((row as ArchivedExecutionEvidence).args_sha256 ?? "")) &&
   935	      /^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(String((row as ArchivedExecutionEvidence).result_sha256 ?? "")) &&
   936	      ["succeeded", "failed", "blocked"].includes(String((row as ArchivedExecutionEvidence).status ?? ""))
   937	    ).slice(-64);
   938	  } catch {
   939	    return [];
   940	  }
   941	}
   942	
   943	function formatArchivedExecutionCheckpoint(evidence: readonly ArchivedExecutionEvidence[]): string {
   944	  const succeededTools = evidence.filter(e => e.status === "succeeded").map(e => e.tool);
   945	  const toolsSummary = succeededTools.length ? `Tools already successfully executed in prior steps: [${[...new Set(succeededTools)].join(", ")}]. Do NOT repeat these actions; proceed directly to remaining steps or final terminal synthesis.` : "";
   946	  return "GEMINX_CONTEXT_ROLLOVER: Continue this EXISTING objective. Older mutation receipts may have been omitted from this bounded provider window: check the durable execution trace before replaying any mutation. " +
   947	    "Earlier execution is archived; DO NOT REPEAT successful mutations or duplicate completed observations. " +
   948	    (toolsSummary ? toolsSummary + " " : "") +
   949	    "ARCHIVED_EXECUTION_CHECKPOINT=" + JSON.stringify({ completed_groups: evidence.length, evidence });
   950	}
   951	
   952	function preserveSystemForRollover(content: string): string {
   953	  const value = String(content ?? "");
   954	  const maxChars = 18_000;
   955	  if (value.length <= maxChars) return value;
   956	  // Keep both the runtime contract at the front and the most recent injected
   957	  // capability/safety context at the tail. The full prompt remains durable in
   958	  // the objective/checkpoint; replaying it verbatim defeats rollover.
   959	  const head = 8_000;
   960	  const tail = 8_000;
   961	  return value.slice(0, head)
   962	    + "\n[GEMINX_CONTEXT_ROLLOVER: STATIC SYSTEM MIDDLE OMITTED; full contract remains durable]\n"
   963	    + value.slice(-tail);
   964	}
   965	
   966	export function rolloverToolLoopMessages(
   967	  messages: readonly DeepSeekMessage[],
   968	  intent: string,
   969	  toolCalls: readonly ToolCallRecord[]
   970	): DeepSeekMessage[] {
   971	  const primarySystem = preserveSystemForRollover(
   972	    messages.find((message) => message.role === "system")?.content ?? ""
   973	  );
   974	  const firstToolAssistant = messages.findIndex((message) =>
   975	    message.role === "assistant" && Array.isArray(message.tool_calls) && message.tool_calls.length > 0
   976	  );
   977	  const preTool = firstToolAssistant < 0 ? messages : messages.slice(0, firstToolAssistant);
   978	  // Recover the original user objective from its initial position, not from the
   979	  // latest tool-result tail. This must never masquerade as a new user instruction.
   980	  const originalUserSource = preTool.find((message) => message.role === "user");
   981	  const originalUser = {
   982	    role: "user" as const,
   983	    content: preserveObjectiveForRollover(String(originalUserSource?.content ?? intent))
   984	  };
   985	  const groups: DeepSeekMessage[][] = [];
   986	  for (let i = 0; i < messages.length; i += 1) {
   987	    const assistant = messages[i];
   988	    if (assistant.role !== "assistant" || !Array.isArray(assistant.tool_calls) || !assistant.tool_calls.length) continue;
   989	    const ids = (assistant.tool_calls as Array<{ id?: string }>).map((item) => String(item.id ?? "")).filter(Boolean);
   990	    const results: DeepSeekMessage[] = [];
   991	    for (let j = i + 1; j < messages.length; j += 1) {
   992	      const row = messages[j];
   993	      if (row.role === "assistant" && Array.isArray(row.tool_calls) && row.tool_calls.length) break;
   994	      if (row.role === "tool" && ids.includes(String(row.tool_call_id ?? ""))) results.push(row);
   995	    }
   996	    // A partially executed mutation cannot be compacted into apparent success.
   997	    if (ids.length > 0 && ids.every((id) => results.some((row) => row.tool_call_id === id))) {
   998	      groups.push([assistant, ...results]);
   999	    }
  1000	  }
  1001	  // Retain only the immediate continuation window. Earlier complete tool groups
  1002	  // are represented by immutable IDs + argument/result hashes below. Keeping
  1003	  // eight raw GitHub/file receipts made rollover a no-op in real Level-4 runs.
  1004	  const recentGroupCount = 2;
  1005	  const recent = groups.slice(-recentGroupCount).flat().map((msg) => {
  1006	    if (msg.role === "tool" && typeof msg.content === "string" && msg.content.length > 8_000) {
  1007	      return {
  1008	        ...msg,
  1009	        content: msg.content.slice(0, 4_000) + "\n[ROLLOVER_RECENT_TOOL_RECEIPT_COMPACTED]\n" + msg.content.slice(-2_000)
  1010	      };
  1011	    }
  1012	    return msg;
  1013	  });
  1014	  const archivedCount = Math.max(0, groups.length - recentGroupCount);
  1015	  const earlierEvidence = archivedExecutionEvidenceFromMessages(messages);
  1016	  const newlyArchived: ArchivedExecutionEvidence[] = [];
  1017	  for (const group of groups.slice(0, archivedCount)) {
  1018	    const assistant = group[0];
  1019	    const calls = Array.isArray(assistant?.tool_calls)
  1020	      ? assistant.tool_calls as Array<{ id?: string; function?: { name?: string; arguments?: string } }>
  1021	      : [];
  1022	    for (const call of calls) {
  1023	      const id = String(call.id ?? "");
  1024	      const tool = String(call.function?.name ?? "");
  1025	      const result = group.find((row) => row.role === "tool" && row.tool_call_id === id);
  1026	      if (!result || !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_:-]{1,180}$/.test(id) || !/^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{1,180}$/.test(tool)) continue;
  1027	      const current = toolCalls.find((entry) => entry.tool_call_id === id);
  1028	      let status: ArchivedExecutionEvidence["status"] | null = null;
  1029	      try {
  1030	        const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(String(result.content ?? ""));
  1031	        const raw = parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)
  1032	          ? String((parsed as Record<string, unknown>).status ?? "") : "";
  1033	        if (raw === "succeeded" || raw === "failed" || raw === "blocked") status = raw;
  1034	      } catch {
  1035	        status = null;
  1036	      }
  1037	      if (!status && (current?.status === "succeeded" || current?.status === "failed" || current?.status === "blocked")) {
  1038	        status = current.status;
  1039	      }
  1040	      if (!status) continue;
  1041	      newlyArchived.push({
  1042	        tool_call_id: id,
  1043	        tool,
  1044	        args_sha256: createHash("sha256").update(String(call.function?.arguments ?? "{}")).digest("hex"),
  1045	        status,
  1046	        result_sha256: createHash("sha256").update(String(result.content ?? "")).digest("hex")
  1047	      });
  1048	    }
  1049	  }
  1050	  const evidenceById = new Map<string, ArchivedExecutionEvidence>();
  1051	  for (const entry of [...earlierEvidence, ...newlyArchived]) evidenceById.set(entry.tool_call_id, entry);
  1052	  // Retain a bounded recent readback index in the MODEL window. Older exact
  1053	  // receipts are kept in the durable execution trace; do not re-execute a
  1054	  // missing mutation merely because its hash left this compact window.
  1055	  const archive = [...evidenceById.values()].slice(-64);
  1056	  const recentFollowups = messages
  1057	    .filter((message) => message.role === "user" && typeof message.content === "string"
  1058	      && message.content.startsWith("Founder follow-up while you were working:"))
  1059	    .slice(-3).map((message) => ({ role: "user" as const, content: String(message.content).slice(0, 2_000) }));
  1060	  const rawIntent = String(originalUserSource?.content ?? intent);
  1061	  const userMsgSource = originalUserSource as unknown as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
  1062	  const userMsgId = typeof userMsgSource?.message_id === "string"
  1063	    ? String(userMsgSource.message_id)
  1064	    : undefined;
  1065	  const effectiveJobId = userMsgId || `job_${computeSha256(rawIntent).slice(0, 16)}`;
  1066	  const preservedCore = preserveObjectiveForRollover(rawIntent, effectiveJobId);
  1067	  const canonicalRecord = saveCanonicalObjective({
  1068	    jobId: effectiveJobId,
  1069	    text: preservedCore,
  1070	  });
  1071	  const pinnedDirective = buildCompactedExecutionDirective(canonicalRecord);
  1072	  return [
  1073	    { role: "system", content: primarySystem },
  1074	    originalUser,
  1075	    {
  1076	      role: "system",
  1077	      content: formatArchivedExecutionCheckpoint(archive)
  1078	    },
  1079	    ...recent,
  1080	    ...recentFollowups,
  1081	    {
  1082	      role: "system",
  1083	      content: `pinned_founder_directive: ${pinnedDirective}`
  1084	    }
  1085	  ];
  1086	}
  1087	
  1088	/** Reconstruct verified prior native tool receipts solely for terminal
  1089	 * adjudication. This never dispatches an adapter or treats missing results as
  1090	 * successful. The durable checkpoint validator rejects unmatched tool IDs.
  1091	 */
  1092	export function reconstructNativeToolReceipts(
  1093	  messages: readonly DeepSeekMessage[],
  1094	  correlationId = "native-replay"
  1095	): ToolCallRecord[] {
  1096	  const reconstructed: ToolCallRecord[] = [];
  1097	  for (let i = 0; i < messages.length; i += 1) {
  1098	    const message = messages[i]!;
  1099	    if (message.role !== "assistant" || !Array.isArray(message.tool_calls)) continue;
  1100	    for (const call of message.tool_calls as Array<{ id?: string; function?: { name?: string; arguments?: string } }>) {
  1101	      const id = String(call.id ?? "");
  1102	      if (!id) continue;
  1103	      const receipt = messages.slice(i + 1).find((candidate) =>
  1104	        candidate.role === "tool" && candidate.tool_call_id === id);
  1105	      if (!receipt) continue;
  1106	      let args: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  1107	      let envelope: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  1108	      try {
  1109	        const parsed = JSON.parse(String(call.function?.arguments ?? "{}")) as unknown;
  1110	        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object" && !Array.isArray(parsed)) args = parsed as Record<string, unknown>;
  1111	        const data = JSON.parse(String(receipt.content ?? "{}")) as unknown;
  1112	        if (data && typeof data === "object" && !Array.isArray(data)) envelope = data as Record<string, unknown>;
  1113	      } catch {
  1114	        continue;
  1115	      }
  1116	      const rawStatus = String(envelope.status ?? "blocked");
  1117	      const status = rawStatus === "succeeded" || rawStatus === "failed" || rawStatus === "blocked"
  1118	        ? rawStatus : "blocked";
  1119	      reconstructed.push({
  1120	        tool_call_id: id,
  1121	        correlation_id: correlationId,
  1122	        tool: String(call.function?.name ?? ""),
  1123	        action: String(call.function?.name ?? ""),
  1124	        args,
  1125	        status,
  1126	        started_at: null,
  1127	        finished_at: null,
  1128	        result: envelope.data ?? null,
  1129	        error: envelope.error == null ? null : String(envelope.error)
  1130	      });
  1131	    }
  1132	  }
  1133	  return reconstructed;
  1134	}

  1142	      cached_tokens?: number;
  1143	      prompt_cache_hit_tokens?: number;
  1144	      prompt_cache_miss_tokens?: number;
  1145	    };
  1146	  };
  1147	  choices?: Array<{
  1148	    message?: {

  1150	      reasoning_content?: unknown;

  1163	    message?: string;

  1167	function readDeepSeekBaseUrl(): string {
  1168	  const configured = process.env.DEEPSEEK_API_BASE_URL?.trim();
  1169	  return configured && configured.length > 0 ? configured.replace(/\/+$/, "") : DEFAULT_DEEPSEEK_BASE_URL;
  1170	}
  1171	
  1172	function parseMessageContent(content: unknown): string | null {
  1173	  if (typeof content === "string" && content.trim().length > 0) {
  1174	    return content.trim();
  1175	  }
  1176	
  1177	  if (Array.isArray(content)) {
  1178	    const text = content
  1179	      .map((part) => {
  1180	        if (typeof part === "string") {
  1181	          return part;
  1182	        }
  1183	        if (
  1184	          part &&
  1185	          typeof part === "object" &&
  1186	          "type" in part &&
  1187	          "text" in part &&
  1188	          (part as { type?: unknown }).type === "text" &&
  1189	          typeof (part as { text?: unknown }).text === "string"
  1190	        ) {
  1191	          return (part as { text: string }).text;
  1192	        }
  1193	        return "";
  1194	      })
  1195	      .join("")
  1196	      .trim();
  1197	
  1198	    return text.length > 0 ? text : null;
  1199	  }
  1200	
  1201	  return null;
  1202	}

  1204	
  1205	const GEMINX_STANDARD_TERMINAL_MARKERS = [
  1206	  "COMPLETED_PROVEN",
  1207	  "BLOCKED_REAL_BOUNDARY",
  1208	  "FAILED_VERIFICATION"
  1209	] as const;
  1210	
  1211	const GEMINX_TERMINAL_MARKERS = [
  1212	  ...GEMINX_STANDARD_TERMINAL_MARKERS,
  1213	  "OPERATOR-CRUCIBLE-CERTIFIED"
  1214	] as const;
  1215	
  1216	function requiresGeminxTerminalContract(intent: string): boolean {
  1217	  const text = String(intent ?? "");
  1218	  return (
  1219	    GEMINX_STANDARD_TERMINAL_MARKERS.every((marker) => text.includes(marker)) ||
  1220	    /OPERATOR-CRUCIBLE-CERTIFIED/i.test(text)
  1221	  );
  1222	}
  1223	
  1224	// A source-map task is not finished by an intermediate discovery note.
  1225	// This is an output completeness check, not a substitute for tool/read receipts.
  1226	export function hasSourceBackedDependencyMap(message: string | null): boolean {
  1227	  const text = String(message ?? "");
  1228	  const paths = new Set((text.match(/\b(?:src|web|tests)\/[a-zA-Z0-9_./-]+\.(?:ts|tsx|js)\b/g) ?? []));
  1229	  const hasLineEvidence = /\b(?:lines?\s+\d+|[a-z]+\.(?:ts|tsx|js):\d+)\b/i.test(text);
  1230	  const hasRelationships = /\b(?:imports?|routes?|middleware|dependency|guard|calls?)\b/i.test(text);
  1231	  return paths.size >= 3 && hasLineEvidence && hasRelationships;
  1232	}
  1233	
  1234	function hasGeminxTerminalMarker(message: string | null): boolean {
  1235	  if (!message) return false;
  1236	  return GEMINX_TERMINAL_MARKERS.some((marker) =>
  1237	    new RegExp("(?:^|\\n|\\b)[*_`#\\s]*" + marker + "[*_`\\s]*[.:!]?(?:$|\\n|\\b)", "im").test(message)
  1238	  );
  1239	}
  1240	
  1241	function looksLikeIntermediateToolNarrative(message: string | null, toolCalls: readonly ToolCallRecord[]): boolean {
  1242	  if (!message || toolCalls.length === 0) return false;
  1243	  const text = message.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  1244	  if (!text) return false;
  1245	
  1246	  // After real tool work, phrases like "I'll inspect..." or "let me read..."
  1247	  // are continuation narration, not a terminal answer. Explicit prefixes are
  1248	  // deliberately boring here: brittle regex cleverness already cost us a deploy.
  1249	  const lower = text.toLowerCase();
  1250	  return [
  1251	    "i'll ",
  1252	    "i will ",
  1253	    "i need to ",
  1254	    "i should ",
  1255	    "i can now ",
  1256	    "i am going to ",
  1257	    "i'm going to ",
  1258	    "let me ",
  1259	    "next, ",
  1260	    "next i'll ",
  1261	    "next i will ",
  1262	    "now i'll ",
  1263	    "now i will "
  1264	  ].some((prefix) => lower.startsWith(prefix));
  1265	}
  1266	
  1267	export function isUnsupportedAwaitingGoMessage(message: string | null): boolean {
  1268	  if (!message) return false;
  1269	  const lower = message.toLowerCase();
  1270	  return (
  1271	    /\b(say\s+["']?go["']?|awaiting\s+(?:your\s+)?go|reply\s+with\s+["']?go["']?|tell\s+me\s+to\s+["']?go["']?|give\s+me\s+the\s+go\s+ahead|ready\s+when\s+you\s+say\s+["']?go["']?|ready\s+to\s+(?:start|begin|run|kick\s+off|launch)\b|shall\s+i\s+(?:start|begin|proceed|run|kick\s+off)|should\s+i\s+(?:start|begin|proceed|run|kick\s+off)|do\s+you\s+want\s+me\s+to\s+(?:start|begin|proceed|run|kick\s+off)|let\s+me\s+know\s+if\s+you\s+want\s+me\s+to)/i.test(lower) ||
  1272	    (/\b(confirm\s+to\s+proceed|waiting\s+for\s+your\s+confirmation|let\s+me\s+know\s+if\s+i\s+should\s+proceed)\b/i.test(lower) &&
  1273	     !/\b(protected|destructive|drop\s+database|delete\s+production|real\s+customer\s+email)\b/i.test(lower)) ||
  1274	    /\b(governed\s+hold|inspecting\s+the\s+actual\s+gate|transport\s+blocked\s+by|holding\s+pattern|not\s+touching\s+anything\s+before|checking\s+the\s+gate\s+before)\b/i.test(lower)
  1275	  );
  1276	}
  1277	
  1278	export function isSafeParallelReadTool(name: string): boolean {
  1279	  if (name.startsWith("github_") && !name.includes("create") && !name.includes("update") && !name.includes("delete") && !name.includes("merge") && !name.includes("comment") && !name.includes("request_reviewers")) return true;
  1280	  if (name.startsWith("railway_") && name !== "railway_deploy" && !name.includes("restart") && !name.includes("delete") && !name.includes("set_") && !name.includes("upsert_")) return true;
  1281	  if (name === "system_file_read" || name === "system_git_status" || name === "system_git_diff" || name === "system_process_list" || name === "system_status") return true;
  1282	  if (name === "geminx_scratch_read" || name === "geminx_scratch_status") return true;
  1283	  if (name === "browser_status" || name === "browser_sessions") return true;
  1284	  if (name === "chrome_status" || name === "chrome_tabs") return true;
  1285	  if (name === "zoho_mail_status" || name === "zoho_mail_list_recent" || name === "zoho_mail_read") return true;
  1286	  if (name === "supabase_status" || name === "vercel_status") return true;
  1287	  return false;
  1288	}
  1289	
  1290	function safeActivityArg(value: unknown, max = 90): string {
  1291	  return String(value ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim().slice(0, max);
  1292	}
  1293	
  1294	function activityHost(value: unknown): string {
  1295	  const raw = safeActivityArg(value, 300);
  1296	  if (!raw) return "";
  1297	  try {
  1298	    const url = new URL(raw);
  1299	    return (url.hostname + (url.pathname && url.pathname !== "/" ? url.pathname.slice(0, 60) : "")).slice(0, 90);
  1300	  } catch {
  1301	    return raw.slice(0, 90);
  1302	  }
  1303	}
  1304	
  1305	function browserTarget(args: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  1306	  const named = safeActivityArg(args.name || args.text || args.role || args.selector, 70);
  1307	  return named ? " " + named : "";
  1308	}
  1309	
  1310	function readableToolActivity(tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown> = {}, status?: string): string {
  1311	  const name = String(tool || "").toLowerCase();
  1312	  let running = "";
  1313	  let done = "";
  1314	
  1315	  switch (name) {
  1316	    case "browser_session_start":
  1317	      running = "Opening live browser";
  1318	      done = "Opened live browser";
  1319	      break;
  1320	    case "browser_navigate":
  1321	    case "chrome_navigate": {
  1322	      const target = activityHost(args.url);
  1323	      running = "Navigating" + (target ? " to " + target : "");
  1324	      done = "Opened" + (target ? " " + target : " page");
  1325	      break;
  1326	    }
  1327	    case "browser_click":
  1328	    case "chrome_click": {
  1329	      const target = browserTarget(args);
  1330	      running = "Clicking" + (target || " page control");
  1331	      done = "Clicked" + (target || " page control");
  1332	      break;
  1333	    }
  1334	    case "browser_type":
  1335	    case "chrome_type": {
  1336	      const target = browserTarget(args);
  1337	      running = "Typing into" + (target || " selected field");
  1338	      done = "Typed into" + (target || " selected field");
  1339	      break;
  1340	    }
  1341	    case "browser_keypress":
  1342	    case "chrome_keypress": {
  1343	      const key = safeActivityArg(args.key, 30);
  1344	      running = "Pressing" + (key ? " " + key : " key");
  1345	      done = "Pressed" + (key ? " " + key : " key");
  1346	      break;
  1347	    }
  1348	    case "browser_scroll":
  1349	    case "chrome_scroll":
  1350	      running = "Scrolling page";
  1351	      done = "Scrolled page";
  1352	      break;
  1353	    case "browser_observe":
  1354	    case "chrome_observe":
  1355	      running = "Reading current page";
  1356	      done = "Read current page";
  1357	      break;
  1358	    case "browser_inspect_controls":
  1359	      running = "Inspecting visible page controls";
  1360	      done = "Inspected visible page controls";
  1361	      break;
  1362	    case "browser_take_screenshot":
  1363	    case "chrome_screenshot":
  1364	      running = "Taking browser screenshot";
  1365	      done = "Captured browser screenshot";
  1366	      break;
  1367	    case "browser_tabs":
  1368	    case "chrome_tabs":
  1369	      running = "Checking browser tabs";
  1370	      done = "Checked browser tabs";
  1371	      break;
  1372	    case "browser_status":
  1373	    case "chrome_status":
  1374	      running = "Checking browser connection";
  1375	      done = "Checked browser connection";
  1376	      break;
  1377	    case "browser_resume":
  1378	      running = "Resuming browser automation";
  1379	      done = "Resumed browser automation";
  1380	      break;
  1381	    case "browser_takeover":
  1382	      running = "Handing live browser to Rob";
  1383	      done = "Browser handed to Rob";
  1384	      break;
  1385	    case "browser_close":
  1386	    case "chrome_close":
  1387	      running = "Closing live browser";
  1388	      done = "Closed live browser";
  1389	      break;
  1390	    case "chrome_open":
  1391	      running = "Opening Chrome tab";
  1392	      done = "Opened Chrome tab";
  1393	      break;
  1394	    case "chrome_activate":
  1395	      running = "Bringing Chrome tab forward";
  1396	      done = "Brought Chrome tab forward";
  1397	      break;
  1398	    default:
  1399	      running =
  1400	        name.startsWith("github_") ? `Checking GitHub · ${name}`
  1401	          : name.startsWith("railway_") ? `Checking Railway production · ${name}`
  1402	            : name.startsWith("vercel_") ? `Checking Vercel · ${name}`
  1403	              : name.startsWith("supabase_") ? `Checking Supabase · ${name}`
  1404	                : name.startsWith("roblox_") ? `Checking Roblox Creator · ${name}`
  1405	                  : name.startsWith("system_") ? `Checking connected systems · ${name}`
  1406	                    : name.startsWith("browser_") || name.startsWith("chrome_") ? `Using the browser · ${name}`
  1407	                      : "Running " + name.replaceAll("_", " ");
  1408	      done = running.replace(/^Checking /, "Checked ").replace(/^Using /, "Used ").replace(/^Running /, "Ran ");
  1409	  }
  1410	
  1411	  if (!status) return running;
  1412	  if (status === "succeeded") return done;
  1413	  if (status === "blocked") return running + " · blocked";
  1414	  return running + " · failed";
  1415	}
  1416	
  1417	
  1418	function activityEvidencePreview(value: unknown, maxChars = 220): string {
  1419	  if (value === null || value === undefined) return "no structured payload";
  1420	  let raw = "";
  1421	  try { raw = JSON.stringify(value); } catch { raw = String(value); }
  1422	  raw = raw.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  1423	  return raw.length <= maxChars ? raw : raw.slice(0, maxChars) + "…";
  1424	}
  1425	
  1426	export function founderToolOutcomeActivity(
  1427	  tool: string,
  1428	  args: Record<string, unknown>,
  1429	  result: { status: string; ok: boolean; data?: unknown; error?: unknown; blocked_reason?: unknown }
  1430	): string {
  1431	  const base = readableToolActivity(tool, args, result.status);
  1432	  const failure = String(result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? "").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  1433	  if (!result.ok) {
  1434	    return `${base}. Evidence: ${failure ? failure.slice(0, 220) : "the governed tool returned a non-success receipt"}. Why it matters: this route is not proof of completion, so repeating it unchanged would only burn time or money. Next: re-observe the owning boundary, change the hypothesis or route, and continue the same objective.`;
  1435	  }
  1436	
  1437	  const name = String(tool ?? "").toLowerCase();
  1438	  const data = result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(result.data)
  1439	    ? result.data as Record<string, unknown>
  1440	    : {};
  1441	  let evidence = activityEvidencePreview(result.data);
  1442	  let why = "This receipt changes the current evidence ledger for the objective.";
  1443	  let next = "Use this receipt to narrow the diagnosis before the next mutation.";
  1444	
  1445	  if (name === "system_status") {
  1446	    const configured = String(data.configured_host_id ?? "none");
  1447	    const resolved = String(data.resolved_host_id ?? "none");
  1448	    const windows = Array.isArray(data.live_windows_hosts) ? data.live_windows_hosts.join(", ") || "none" : "unknown";
  1449	    const connected = Array.isArray(data.live_connected_hosts) ? data.live_connected_hosts.join(", ") || "none" : "unknown";
  1450	    evidence = `configured_host=${configured}; resolved_host=${resolved}; live_windows_hosts=${windows}; live_connected_hosts=${connected}`;
  1451	    why = "Platform and exact device identity must agree, otherwise an Android heartbeat can be mistaken for an available Windows mutation surface.";
  1452	    next = "Route Windows work only to a proven live Windows System Operator; otherwise repair the host/binding boundary rather than guessing.";
  1453	  } else if (name === "device_list" || name === "device_status") {
  1454	    why = "Exact device identity matters because two similar devices must never be treated as interchangeable mutation targets.";
  1455	    next = "Bind the requested physical device explicitly before any tap, type, wake or app mutation.";
  1456	  } else if (name === "creator_target_inventory" || name.startsWith("roblox_studio_")) {
  1457	    why = "Creator run drift can make a green receipt belong to the wrong game, place, version or Studio worker.";
  1458	    next = "Reconcile the exact run, universe, place, version and worker identity before changing Creator state.";
  1459	  } else if (name.startsWith("github_")) {
  1460	    why = "Source evidence is only useful when it matches the branch/SHA that actually owns the running behaviour.";
  1461	    next = "Compare this code evidence with the live runtime/deployment receipt before deciding whether a patch is justified.";
  1462	  } else if (name.startsWith("railway_")) {
  1463	    why = "A green deployment without an exact source SHA and runtime probe does not prove the repaired behaviour is live.";
  1464	    next = "Match deployment SHA to source, then verify the real runtime surface before closing the objective.";
  1465	  } else if (name.startsWith("browser_") || name.startsWith("chrome_")) {
  1466	    why = "UI mutations based on stale visual/DOM state can hit the wrong control even when the prior step looked correct.";
  1467	    next = "Use the newly observed page state as the basis for the next UI action; do not act from cached screen assumptions.";
  1468	  }
  1469	
  1470	  return `${base}. Evidence: ${evidence}. Why it matters: ${why} Next: ${next}`;
  1471	}

  1474	export type ProtectedToolHumanBoundary = {
  1475	  code: "ANDROID_PACKAGE_INSTALL_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED";
  1476	  challenge: string;
  1477	  tool: "device_install_apk";
  1478	  sha256: string | null;
  1479	};

  1481	export function protectedToolHumanBoundary(
  1482	  tool: string,
  1483	  result: { ok: boolean; data?: unknown }
  1484	): ProtectedToolHumanBoundary | null {
  1485	  if (String(tool ?? "").toLowerCase() !== "device_install_apk" || result.ok !== true) return null;
  1486	  const data = result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(result.data)
  1487	    ? result.data as Record<string, unknown>
  1488	    : {};
  1489	  if (data.installer_opened !== true || data.user_confirmation_may_be_required !== true) return null;
  1490	  return {
  1491	    code: "ANDROID_PACKAGE_INSTALL_CONFIRMATION_REQUIRED",
  1492	    challenge: "Android package installer is open and requires local device-owner confirmation. No further install, tap, click or mutation may run until that protected local step is completed.",
  1493	    tool: "device_install_apk",
  1494	    sha256: typeof data.sha256 === "string" && data.sha256.trim() ? data.sha256.trim() : null,
  1495	  };
  1496	}

  1499	const TOOL_CONTEXT_RESULT_MAX_CHARS = 4_000;
  1500	
  1501	function stableToolArgs(value: unknown): unknown {
  1502	  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(stableToolArgs);
  1503	  if (!value || typeof value !== "object") return value;
  1504	  return Object.fromEntries(
  1505	    Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
  1506	      .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right))
  1507	      .map(([key, item]) => [key, stableToolArgs(item)])
  1508	  );
  1509	}
