  1511	export function toolReadCacheKey(tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {
  1512	  const name = String(tool ?? "").toLowerCase();
  1513	  const cacheable =
  1514	    /^(?:geminx_runtime_capabilities|geminx_tool_search|github_(?:(?:get|list|read|search|compare)_[a-z0-9_]+|repo_map|trace_status)|creator_target_inventory|roblox_studio_(?:status|console))$/.test(name);
  1515	  return cacheable ? `${name}:${JSON.stringify(stableToolArgs(args))}` : null;
  1516	}
  1517	
  1518	export function toolContinuationAssistantMessage(message: {
  1519	  content?: unknown;
  1520	  tool_calls?: unknown;
  1521	  reasoning_content?: unknown;
  1522	} | undefined): DeepSeekMessage {
  1523	  const rawReasoning = typeof message?.reasoning_content === "string"
  1524	    ? message.reasoning_content
  1525	    : "";
  1526	  // DeepSeek thinking mode requires reasoning_content to be passed back on subsequent tool turns.
  1527	  // If provider reasoning is an unbounded/raw dump (>8k chars), reset to empty string to prevent sliding-window blowup.
  1528	  const reasoning = rawReasoning.length <= 8_000 ? rawReasoning : "";
  1529	  return {
  1530	    role: "assistant",
  1531	    content: message?.content ? String(message.content) : null,
  1532	    ...(message?.tool_calls ? { tool_calls: message.tool_calls } : {}),
  1533	    reasoning_content: reasoning
  1534	  };
  1535	}
  1536	
  1537	/**
  1538	 * DeepSeek requires EVERY assistant tool_calls item to be followed by exactly
  1539	 * one matching tool response before another role appears. The runtime appends
  1540	 * recovery instructions as system messages inside the multi-tool loop, which
  1541	 * can interleave these messages and trigger provider HTTP 400.
  1542	 *
  1543	 * Rebuild provider-bound history at the trust boundary without executing any
  1544	 * additional tools or turning an unverified operation into success. Missing
  1545	 * responses become explicit BLOCKED receipts, not invented success records.
  1546	 */
  1547	export function reconcileDeepSeekToolTranscript(input: readonly DeepSeekMessage[]): DeepSeekMessage[] {
  1548	  const output: DeepSeekMessage[] = [];
  1549	  for (let cursor = 0; cursor < input.length;) {
  1550	    const message = input[cursor]!;
  1551	    const toolCalls = message.role === "assistant" && Array.isArray(message.tool_calls)
  1552	      ? message.tool_calls as Array<{ id?: unknown; function?: unknown; type?: unknown }>
  1553	      : [];
  1554	    if (toolCalls.length > 0) {
  1555	      const seen = new Set<string>();
  1556	      const accepted = toolCalls.filter((call) => {
  1557	        const id = call && typeof call.id === "string" ? call.id.trim() : "";
  1558	        if (!id || seen.has(id)) return false;
  1559	        seen.add(id);
  1560	        return true;
  1561	      });
  1562	      const acceptedIds = new Set(accepted.map((call) => String(call.id)));
  1563	      const receipts = new Map<string, DeepSeekMessage>();
  1564	      const deferred: DeepSeekMessage[] = [];
  1565	      let next = cursor + 1;
  1566	      for (; next < input.length; next += 1) {
  1567	        const subsequent = input[next]!;
  1568	        if (subsequent.role === "user" || subsequent.role === "assistant") break;
  1569	        if (subsequent.role === "tool" && subsequent.tool_call_id && acceptedIds.has(subsequent.tool_call_id)) {
  1570	          if (!receipts.has(subsequent.tool_call_id)) receipts.set(subsequent.tool_call_id, subsequent);
  1571	          else deferred.push({
  1572	            role: "system",
  1573	            content: "DUPLICATE_TOOL_RESULT_IGNORED: only one result per tool_call_id may be replayed."
  1574	          });
  1575	        } else if (subsequent.role === "tool") {
  1576	          deferred.push({
  1577	            role: "system",
  1578	            content: "ORPHAN_TOOL_RESULT_IGNORED: tool result did not match the preceding assistant tool call."
  1579	          });
  1580	        } else {
  1581	          deferred.push(subsequent);
  1582	        }
  1583	      }
  1584	      const assistantReasoning = typeof message.reasoning_content === "string"
  1585	        ? message.reasoning_content
  1586	        : (accepted.length > 0 ? "" : undefined);
  1587	      output.push({
  1588	        role: "assistant",
  1589	        content: message.content,
  1590	        ...(assistantReasoning !== undefined ? { reasoning_content: assistantReasoning } : {}),
  1591	        ...(accepted.length ? { tool_calls: accepted } : {})
  1592	      });
  1593	      for (const call of accepted) {
  1594	        const id = String(call.id);
  1595	        output.push(receipts.get(id) ?? {
  1596	          role: "tool",
  1597	          tool_call_id: id,
  1598	          content: JSON.stringify({
  1599	            ok: false,
  1600	            status: "blocked",
  1601	            blocked_reason: "TOOL_RESULT_MISSING_UNVERIFIED",
  1602	            error: "No matching runtime receipt exists for this call; no success or external mutation is claimed."
  1603	          })
  1604	        });
  1605	      }
  1606	      if (accepted.length !== toolCalls.length) {
  1607	        output.push({
  1608	          role: "system",
  1609	          content: "MALFORMED_TOOL_CALL_NOT_REPLAYED: duplicate or missing call IDs were excluded; do not claim execution."
  1610	        });
  1611	      }
  1612	      output.push(...deferred);
  1613	      cursor = next;
  1614	      continue;
  1615	    }
  1616	    if (message.role === "tool") {
  1617	      output.push({
  1618	        role: "system",
  1619	        content: "ORPHAN_TOOL_RESULT_IGNORED: no preceding assistant tool_calls block owns this response."
  1620	      });
  1621	    } else {
  1622	      output.push(message);
  1623	    }
  1624	    cursor += 1;
  1625	  }
  1626	  return output;
  1627	}
  1628	
  1629	// Never replay provider-private reasoning_content into a follow-up request.
  1630	// Tool continuation needs the assistant's tool_calls and optional visible content
  1631	// only. Replaying raw reasoning can balloon the next prompt by hundreds of
  1632	// thousands of tokens without adding executable evidence.
  1633	export function compactToolReceiptForModel(result: {
  1634	  ok: boolean;
  1635	  status: string;
  1636	  data?: unknown;
  1637	  error?: unknown;
  1638	  blocked_reason?: unknown;
  1639	  status_code?: number;
  1640	  stderr?: unknown;
  1641	  stdout?: unknown;
  1642	  tool_call_id?: string;
  1643	  correlation_id?: string;
  1644	  duration_ms?: number;
  1645	  schema_validated?: boolean;
  1646	}): string {
  1647	  const envelope = {
  1648	    ok: result.ok,
  1649	    status: result.status,
  1650	    schema_validated: result.schema_validated === true,
  1651	    data: result.data ?? null,
  1652	    error: result.error ?? null,
  1653	    blocked_reason: result.blocked_reason ?? null,
  1654	    status_code: result.status_code ?? null,
  1655	    stderr: result.stderr ?? null,
  1656	    stdout: result.stdout ?? null,
  1657	    tool_call_id: result.tool_call_id ?? null,
  1658	    correlation_id: result.correlation_id ?? null,
  1659	    duration_ms: result.duration_ms ?? null
  1660	  };
  1661	  const full = JSON.stringify(envelope);
  1662	  if (full.length <= TOOL_CONTEXT_RESULT_MAX_CHARS) return full;
  1663	
  1664	  const dataJson = JSON.stringify(result.data ?? null);
  1665	  const previewBudget = Math.max(1_000, TOOL_CONTEXT_RESULT_MAX_CHARS - 1_500);
  1666	  const headBudget = Math.ceil(previewBudget / 2);
  1667	  const tailBudget = Math.floor(previewBudget / 2);
  1668	  return JSON.stringify({
  1669	    ok: result.ok,
  1670	    status: result.status,
  1671	    schema_validated: result.schema_validated === true,
  1672	    data_compacted_for_model_context: true,
  1673	    original_receipt_chars: full.length,
  1674	    data_preview_head: dataJson.slice(0, headBudget),
  1675	    data_preview_tail: dataJson.slice(-tailBudget),
  1676	    error: result.error ?? null,
  1677	    blocked_reason: result.blocked_reason ?? null,
  1678	    status_code: result.status_code ?? null,
  1679	    stderr: result.stderr ?? null,
  1680	    stdout: typeof result.stdout === "string" ? result.stdout.slice(0, 4096) : result.stdout ?? null,
  1681	    tool_call_id: result.tool_call_id ?? null,
  1682	    correlation_id: result.correlation_id ?? null,
  1683	    duration_ms: result.duration_ms ?? null,
  1684	    note: "Full receipt remains in the authenticated GeminX tool trace; model context is bounded."
  1685	  });
  1686	}
  1687	
  1688	const COMPACT_CONVERSATION_MAX_TURNS = 4;
  1689	const COMPACT_CONVERSATION_MAX_CHARS = 4_000;
  1690	const FULL_CONVERSATION_MAX_TURNS = 8;
  1691	const FULL_CONVERSATION_MAX_CHARS = 12_000;
  1692	
  1693	const SYNTHETIC_RUNTIME_FAILURE_TURNS = new Set([
  1694	  "Provider/runtime failure: no assistant answer was produced for the previous request.",
  1695	  "Provider unavailable: DeepSeek had no usable credit, so no answer was produced for the previous request."
  1696	]);
  1697	
  1698	function isLegacyRuntimeTroubleshootingTurn(
  1699	  turn: { role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }
  1700	): boolean {
  1701	  if (turn.role !== "assistant") return false;
  1702	  const text = String(turn.content ?? "").trim();
  1703	  if (!text) return false;
  1704	  if (SYNTHETIC_RUNTIME_FAILURE_TURNS.has(text)) return true;
  1705	
  1706	  // Before native NEO owned Founder chat, failed OpenClaw sessions sometimes
  1707	  // produced troubleshooting prose that told Rob to /reset or /new. That is
  1708	  // transport debris, not durable conversational truth, and must not poison
  1709	  // later native NEO turns.
  1710	  return /\/(?:reset|new)\b/i.test(text)
  1711	    && /\b(?:context|overflow|session|model|error|failure|busted)\b/i.test(text);
  1712	}
  1713	
  1714	function isLightweightPresencePing(intent: string): boolean {
  1715	  const normalized = String(intent ?? "")
  1716	    .trim()
  1717	    .toLowerCase()
  1718	    .replace(/[!?.,]+$/g, "")
  1719	    .replace(/\s+/g, " ");
  1720	
  1721	  return /^(?:yo+|hey+|hi+|hello+|hiya|sup|what'?s up|you there|are you there|morning|afternoon|evening)$/.test(normalized);
  1722	}
  1723	
  1724	function sanitizedConversationTurns(
  1725	  turns: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }>,
  1726	  intent: string
  1727	): Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }> {
  1728	  // A casual ping is a presence check, not an invitation to relitigate stale
  1729	  // provider failures. Keep it conversational and let the current runtime speak.
  1730	  if (isLightweightPresencePing(intent)) return [];
  1731	
  1732	  const cleaned = turns.filter((turn) => !isLegacyRuntimeTroubleshootingTurn(turn));
  1733	  const deduped: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }> = [];
  1734	
  1735	  for (const turn of cleaned) {
  1736	    const previous = deduped[deduped.length - 1];
  1737	    const currentText = String(turn.content ?? "").trim();
  1738	    const previousText = String(previous?.content ?? "").trim();
  1739	
  1740	    // Failed retries can leave duplicate short user objectives after the synthetic
  1741	    // failure marker is removed. Collapse only adjacent identical short user turns.
  1742	    if (
  1743	      turn.role === "user"
  1744	      && previous?.role === "user"
  1745	      && currentText.length <= 240
  1746	      && currentText.toLowerCase() === previousText.toLowerCase()
  1747	    ) {
  1748	      deduped[deduped.length - 1] = { role: "user", content: currentText };
  1749	      continue;
  1750	    }
  1751	
  1752	    deduped.push({ role: turn.role, content: currentText });
  1753	  }
  1754	
  1755	  return deduped;
  1756	}
  1757	
  1758	const MATERIAL_CONTEXT_PATTERN = /\b(build|create|fix|repair|change|update|deploy|publish|test|check|inspect|audit|search|research|find|look up|open|click|navigate|schedule|remind|send|email|apply|continue|resume|status|github|repo|repository|railway|vercel|browser|chrome|roblox|creator|geminx|openclaw|brain|runtime|provider|worker|tool|code|website|app|job|calendar|file|document|spreadsheet|current|latest|today|news|remember|earlier|last time|what happened|same task|control|mouse|keyboard|windows|pc|device|phone|tablet|screenshot|notepad|type|tap|swipe|scroll|unlock|wake|microphone|mic)\b/i;
  1759	const CONTINUITY_STATUS_PATTERN = /\b(?:last night|overnight|yesterday|earlier today|this morning|morning update|overnight update|what did (?:we|you) do|what work did (?:we|you) do|how did (?:the )?work go|what got done|what was done|where did we leave off|while i was asleep|while i was sleeping)\b/i;
  1760	const DEEP_BRAIN_CONTEXT_PATTERN = /\b(?:agent brain|github brain|your brain|memory|remember|history|continuity|handoff|doctrine|governance|architecture|ownership|soul|identity|boot chain|boot files?|current truth|task graph|prior decision|previous decision|why did we decide|rehydrat(?:e|ion)|roblox|game creator|creator roadmap)\b/i;
  1761	const AG_SENTINEL_DEEP_BOOT_PATTERN = /\b(?:ag sentinel|antigravity|bootleg[- ]?agi|127 conversations?|recovered sentinel|recovery|boot files?|boot chain|ag brain|agent brain|your brain|your memory|our conversations?|our history|who are you|your identity|prove (?:you are|you're) (?:ag|antigravity)|read (?:all|your).*(?:files?|brain|boot)|rehydrat(?:e|ion))\b/i;
  1762	const OPERATIONAL_FOLLOWUP_PATTERN = /^(?:why(?:\s+.*)?|what happened(?:\s+.*)?|what(?:'s| is) going on(?:\s+.*)?|continue(?:\s+.*)?|carry on(?:\s+.*)?|try again(?:\s+.*)?|do it(?:\s+.*)?|fix it(?:\s+.*)?|finish it(?:\s+.*)?|keep going(?:\s+.*)?|prove it(?:\s+.*)?|test it(?:\s+.*)?|is it fixed(?:\s+.*)?|did it work(?:\s+.*)?|where were you(?:\s+.*)?|why do you keep losing your way(?:\s+.*)?)$/i;
  1763	
  1764	function recentOperationalContext(turns: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }>): boolean {
  1765	  return turns.slice(-6).some((turn) => MATERIAL_CONTEXT_PATTERN.test(String(turn.content ?? "")));
  1766	}
  1767	
  1768	export function needsFullCompanionContext(
  1769	  intent: string,
  1770	  _taskType: GeminxModelTaskType,
  1771	  recentTurns: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }> = []
  1772	): boolean {
  1773	  const text = String(intent ?? "").trim();
  1774	  if (!text) return false;
  1775	  if (isLightweightPresencePing(text)) return false;
  1776	  if (CONTINUITY_STATUS_PATTERN.test(text)) return true;
  1777	  if (DEEP_BRAIN_CONTEXT_PATTERN.test(text)) return true;
  1778	
  1779	  // Local OpenClaw did not reload its whole doctrine library merely because a
  1780	  // request involved a file, tool, app, test, worker, provider, PC or phone.
  1781	  // Those are live-capability concerns and should be solved by tools. A short
  1782	  // same-session follow-up already has the recent transcript, so keep it on the
  1783	  // compact identity lane. Only fall back to durable retrieval when there is no
  1784	  // useful recent operational context to continue from.
  1785	  return OPERATIONAL_FOLLOWUP_PATTERN.test(text) && !recentOperationalContext(recentTurns);
  1786	}
  1787	
  1788	function boundedPriorTurns(
  1789	  turns: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }>,
  1790	  maxTurns: number,
  1791	  maxChars: number
  1792	): Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }> {
  1793	  const selected: Array<{ role: "user" | "assistant"; content: string }> = [];
  1794	  let remaining = Math.max(0, maxChars);
  1795	
  1796	  for (let index = turns.length - 1; index >= 0 && selected.length < maxTurns && remaining > 0; index -= 1) {
  1797	    const turn = turns[index];
  1798	    if (!turn) continue;
  1799	    const raw = String(turn.content ?? "");
  1800	    if (!raw) continue;
  1801	
  1802	    if (raw.length <= remaining) {
  1803	      selected.push({ role: turn.role, content: raw });
  1804	      remaining -= raw.length;
  1805	      continue;
  1806	    }
  1807	
  1808	    const truncationMarker = "\n[truncated]";
  1809	    if (remaining <= truncationMarker.length) break;
  1810	    const content = raw.slice(0, remaining - truncationMarker.length) + truncationMarker;
  1811	    selected.push({ role: turn.role, content });
  1812	    remaining -= content.length;
  1813	  }
  1814	
  1815	  return selected.reverse();
  1816	}
  1817

  1934	}
  1935	
  1936	function providerBoundedBrainContext(context: string, maxChars: number): string {
  1937	  if (context.length <= maxChars) return context;
  1938	  return context.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxChars - 14)) + "\n[truncated]";
  1939	}
  1940	
  1941	function brainContextBlock(
  1942	  brain: { status: AgentBrainContextStatus; sources: AgentBrainContextSource[]; context: string },
  1943	  intent: string
  1944	): string {
  1945	  const brainContext = brain.status === "attached"
  1946	    ? `<agent_brain_context status="attached" source_count="${brain.sources.length}">\n${providerBoundedBrainContext(brain.context, 12_000)}\n</agent_brain_context>`
  1947	    : `<agent_brain_context status="${brain.status}" source_count="0">No Agent Brain context was attached for this request.</agent_brain_context>`;
  1948	  return [
  1949	    "FOUNDER REQUEST:",
  1950	    intent,
  1951	    "",
  1952	    "GEMINX AGENT BRAIN REFERENCE DATA:",
  1953	    brainContext
  1954	  ].join("\n");
  1955	}
  1956	
  1957	function compactIdentityContextBlock(
  1958	  brain: { status: AgentBrainContextStatus; sources: AgentBrainContextSource[]; context: string }
  1959	): string | null {
  1960	  if (brain.status !== "attached" || !brain.context) return null;
  1961	  return [
  1962	    `source_count=${brain.sources.length}`,
  1963	    providerBoundedBrainContext(brain.context, 40_000)
  1964	  ].join("\n");
  1965	}
  1966	
  1967	function toolRoutingIntentText(intent: string): string {
  1968	  const raw = String(intent || "");
  1969	  const objectiveMarker = "\nOBJECTIVE:\n";
  1970	  const objectiveIndex = raw.lastIndexOf(objectiveMarker);
  1971	  if (objectiveIndex < 0) return raw;
  1972	
  1973	  const start = objectiveIndex + objectiveMarker.length;
  1974	  const terminalMarkers = [
  1975	    "\nVERIFIED CURRENT STATE:",
  1976	    "\nCOMPACT VERIFIED CURRENT STATE:",
  1977	    "\nEVIDENCE REFERENCES:",
  1978	    "\nACCEPTANCE CRITERIA:",
  1979	    "\nCONSTRAINTS:",
  1980	    "\nTerminal contract:"
  1981	  ];
  1982	  let end = raw.length;
  1983	  for (const marker of terminalMarkers) {
  1984	    const index = raw.indexOf(marker, start);
  1985	    if (index >= 0 && index < end) end = index;
  1986	  }
  1987	  return raw.slice(start, end).trim() || raw;
  1988	}
  1989	
  1990	export function isExplicitTextOnlyReplyIntent(intent: string): boolean {
  1991	  const text = toolRoutingIntentText(intent).trim().toLowerCase();
  1992	  if (!text) return false;
  1993	  if (/\b(?:no tool calls?|no tools|don['’]?t call tools|without tools?|just talk|plain text|text only|conversation only|just answer)\b/i.test(text)) {
  1994	    return true;
  1995	  }
  1996	  if (/\b(?:interview|brainstorm|architectural question|diagnostic question)\b/i.test(text)) {
  1997	    return true;
  1998	  }
  1999	  // The Founder asked for an answer to COPY, not for a file or machine action.
  2000	  // A concrete external target/action always keeps the operator path available.
  2001	  const realExecution = /\b(?:execute|run\s+(?:it|this|the\s+test|a\s+test)|deploy|commit|push|publish|install|delete|remove\s+the|click|navigate|open\s+the\s+browser|use\s+(?:the|your)\s+tools|tool\s+receipts?|read\s+back|save\s+(?:it|to)|write\s+to|create\s+(?:it\s+)?in|github|repository|repo|railway|scratch|on\s+(?:my|the)\s+(?:pc|computer|phone|windows)|system_shell_exec)\b/i.test(text);
  2002	  if (realExecution) return false;
  2003	  return /^(?:please\s+)?(?:give\s+me|write|draft|make|create|generate|produce|compose|prepare)\s+(?:(?:me|us)\s+)?(?:(?:a|an|the|another|some)\s+)?(?:test(?:\s+prompt|\s+plan)?|operator\s+test|prompt|plan|checklist|email|message|text|explanation|summary|script|instructions?|questions?)\b/i.test(text)
  2004	    || /^(?:(?:please\s+)?(?:read\s+and\s+reply|respond|reply\s+only|just\s+answer)|(?:what\s+(?:is|was)\s+the\s+problem|why\s+(?:is|did|does)\s+this|how do you feel|how do u feel|what do you think|how are you|how are u))\b/i.test(text);
  2005	}
  2006	
  2007	export function isRecognisedBusinessCommand(text: string): boolean {
  2008	  return /\b(digital\s*ambush|ambush\s*(?:run|batch|canary|prospect)|social\s*blitz|sophie\s*social|system\s*health|health\s*check)\b/i.test(text);
  2009	}

  2011	// Provider protocol maximum. The live progressive tool window remains smaller
  2012	// to preserve context budgets and tool-loop continuity; all authorised tools
  2013	// remain reachable through geminx_tool_search within the same objective.
  2014	export const NATIVE_PROVIDER_TOOL_DECLARATION_LIMIT = 128;
  2015	
  2016	export function buildToolDefinitions(
  2017	  toolRegistry: ToolRegistry | null,
  2018	  intent: string,
  2019	  taskType: GeminxModelTaskType,
  2020	  agentMode: "neo" | "ag" = "neo",
  2021	  officeAgent?: OfficeAgentId | null,
  2022	  companyScope?: string | null
  2023	): DeepSeekToolDefinition[] {
  2024	  if (!toolRegistry) return [];
  2025	
  2026	  const channelTruth = nativeChannelSnapshot();
  2027	  // Specialist workers must never see engineering, shell or cross-company tool
  2028	  // declarations. Enforcement at execution time still applies independently,
  2029	  // but withholding forbidden schemas prevents prompt steering and wasted turns.
  2030	  // Preserve AG supervisor/NEO inventory; this gate applies to office specialists.
  2031	  const specialist = officeAgent && ["brian", "mark", "kerry", "sophie", "ads"].includes(officeAgent)
  2032	    ? officeAgent : null;
  2033	  const all = toolRegistry.listTools().filter((definition) =>
  2034	    definition.name !== "web_search" &&
  2035	    toolCanBeAdvertised(definition.name, channelTruth) &&
  2036	    (!specialist || checkOfficeAgentToolAccess({

  2441	        "system_file_write"
  2442	      ];
  2443	      for (const name of physicalCore) {
  2444	        const definition = all.find((candidate) => candidate.name === name);
  2445	        if (definition) addDefinition(definition);
  2446	      }
  2447	    }
  2448	
  2449	    // Reserve the real browser/hosting verification capabilities before AG's
  2450	    // other always-on tool families consume the bounded first-turn schema.
  2451	    if (agMultiSurfaceLiveQaIntent) {
  2452	      for (const name of [
  2453	        "geminx_runtime_capabilities", "geminx_tool_search", "browser_status",
  2454	        "browser_session_start", "browser_navigate", "browser_observe",
  2455	        "browser_set_viewport", "browser_take_screenshot", "browser_close",
  2456	        "railway_list_deployments", "railway_get_deployment", "railway_get_logs",
  2457	        "railway_get_service_instance", "railway_list_variable_names"
  2458	      ]) {
  2459	        const definition = all.find(candidate => candidate.name === name);
  2460	        if (definition && effective.some(item => item.name === name)) addDefinition(definition);
  2461	      }
  2462	    }
  2463	    // AGX must not be forced to overwrite entire files because the two
  2464	    // surgical editing tools were pushed out of the first-turn schema cap.
  2465	    // Priority is presentation only: no tool is disabled or permission-gated.
  2466	    if (agentMode === "ag" && (
  2467	      ["code", "build", "repair"].includes(taskType) ||
  2468	      /\b(?:edit|patch|append|fix|repair|modify|change|refactor|stylesheet|css|source|codebase|git|commit)\b/.test(text)
  2469	    )) {
  2470	      const isIsolatedWorkspaceRepair = /\b(?:disposable[- ]math|harness[- ]disposable[- ]test|isolated\s+workspace|workspace_)\b/i.test(text);
  2471	      for (const name of [
  2472	        "geminx_tool_search","github_read_file","github_patch_file",
  2473	        "github_append_file","github_create_or_update_file","github_file_status",
  2474	        ...(isIsolatedWorkspaceRepair ? [
  2475	          "workspace_create_isolated","workspace_read_file","workspace_write_file",
  2476	          "workspace_run_verification"
  2477	        ] : [])
  2478	      ]) {
  2479	        const definition=effective.find(item => item.name === name);
  2480	        if (definition) addDefinition(definition);
  2481	      }
  2482	    }
  2483	    for (const definition of effective) {
  2484	      if (always.has(definition.name)) addDefinition(definition);
  2485	    }
  2486	
  2487	    // Seed the exact cross-family tools implied by the objective before filling
  2488	    // any one family. This preserves the schema cost bound without letting
  2489	    // GitHub consume every slot in a GitHub + Railway repair, or generic system
  2490	    // tools crowd out the screenshot/input controls for desktop work.
  2491	    const priorityToolNames: string[] = [];
  2492	    const addPriority = (...names: string[]) => {
  2493	      for (const name of names) if (!priorityToolNames.includes(name)) priorityToolNames.push(name);
  2494	    };
  2495	
  2496	    if (explicitIssueOrPrInspection) {
  2497	      addPriority(
  2498	        "github_read_file",
  2499	        "github_get_issue",
  2500	        "github_get_pull_request",
  2501	        "github_get_repository",
  2502	        "github_file_status"
  2503	      );
  2504	      if (prefixes.has("railway_")) {
  2505	        addPriority("railway_list_deployments", "railway_get_logs", "railway_get_deployment");
  2506	      }
  2507	    }
  2508	
  2509	    if (!isCloudReadInspectionPriority && (explicitPhysicalOperatorIntent || prefixes.has("system_"))) {
  2510	      addPriority(
  2511	        "system_status",
  2512	        ...(windowsRecoveryIntent ? ["openclaw_admin_rpc", "openclaw_status"] : []),
  2513	        "system_shell_exec",
  2514	        "system_file_read",
  2515	        "system_file_edit",
  2516	        "system_file_write",
  2517	        "system_screenshot_capture",
  2518	        "system_desktop_snapshot",
  2519	        "system_desktop_uia_find",
  2520	        "system_desktop_operator_step",
  2521	        "system_device_tap",
  2522	        "system_device_type_text"
  2523	      );
  2524	    }
  2525	
  2526	    if (platformMaintenanceIntent) {
  2527	      addPriority(
  2528	        "github_get_repository",
  2529	        "github_file_status",
  2530	        "github_delete_file",
  2531	        "github_create_or_update_file",
  2532	        "github_create_branch",
  2533	        "shell_run",
  2534	        "newton_connector_delegate"
  2535	      );
  2536	    }
  2537	    if (prefixes.has("railway_")) {
  2538	      addPriority("railway_list_deployments", "railway_get_logs", "railway_get_deployment");
  2539	    }
  2540	
  2541	    if (isCloudReadInspectionPriority && (explicitPhysicalOperatorIntent || prefixes.has("system_"))) {
  2542	      addPriority(
  2543	        "system_status",
  2544	        ...(windowsRecoveryIntent ? ["openclaw_admin_rpc", "openclaw_status"] : []),
  2545	        "system_shell_exec",
  2546	        "system_file_read",
  2547	        "system_file_edit",
  2548	        "system_file_write",
  2549	        "system_screenshot_capture",
  2550	        "system_desktop_snapshot",
  2551	        "system_desktop_uia_find",
  2552	        "system_desktop_operator_step",
  2553	        "system_device_tap",
  2554	        "system_device_type_text"
  2555	      );
  2556	    }
  2557	
  2558	    if (prefixes.has("device_")) {
  2559	      addPriority("device_status", "device_open_app", "device_ui_tree", "device_screenshot");
  2560	    }
  2561	    if (/\b(scratch|artifact|scratchpad)\b/i.test(text)) {
  2562	      addPriority(
  2563	        "geminx_scratch_write",
  2564	        "geminx_scratch_read",
  2565	        "geminx_scratch_status",
  2566	        "geminx_scratch_delete"
  2567	      );
  2568	    }
  2569	    if (/\b(zoho|email|outreach)\b/i.test(text)) {
  2570	      addPriority(
  2571	        "zoho_mail_list_recent",
  2572	        "zoho_mail_read",
  2573	        "zoho_mail_send",
  2574	        "zoho_mail_status"
  2575	      );
  2576	    }
  2577	    if (/\b(runway|image|images|picture|pictures|photo|photos|draw|drawing|illustration|artwork|mockup|render|generate video|video generation)\b/i.test(text)) {
  2578	      addPriority("google_image_generate", "runway_generate_image");
  2579	    }
  2580	    if (prefixes.has("google_search_console_")) {
  2581	      addPriority("google_search_console_status", "google_search_console_list_sites");
  2582	    }
  2583	    if (prefixes.has("chrome_")) {
  2584	      addPriority("chrome_status", "chrome_tabs", "chrome_open");
  2585	    }
  2586	    for (const name of priorityToolNames) {
  2587	      const definition = all.find((candidate) => candidate.name === name);
  2588	      if (definition) addDefinition(definition);
  2589	    }
  2590	
  2591	    // Do not let one large family (notably github_*) starve later capability
  2592	    // families out of the bounded initial schema set. Seed a small operational
  2593	    // core from every detected family first, then use the remaining budget for
  2594	    // the normal priority order. This keeps the model's advertised capability
  2595	    // truth aligned with the actions it can actually invoke on the first pass.
  2596	    const familyCoreTools: Record<string, readonly string[]> = {
  2597	      "geminx_scratch_": [
  2598	        "geminx_scratch_write",
  2599	        "geminx_scratch_read",
  2600	        "geminx_scratch_status",
  2601	        "geminx_scratch_delete"
  2602	      ],
  2603	      "zoho_": [
  2604	        "zoho_mail_status",
  2605	        "zoho_mail_list_recent",
  2606	        "zoho_mail_read",
  2607	        "zoho_mail_send"
  2608	      ],
  2609	      "github_": [
  2610	        "github_get_repository",
  2611	        "github_file_status",
  2612	        "github_read_file",
  2613	        "github_delete_file",
  2614	        "github_create_or_update_file",
  2615	        "github_get_issue",
  2616	        "github_get_pull_request",
  2617	        "github_repo_map",
  2618	        "github_search_code",
  2619	        "github_read_file_range",
  2620	        "github_get_commit",
  2621	        "github_compare_commits",
  2622	        "github_create_branch",
  2623	        "github_create_pull_request"
  2624	      ],
  2625	      "railway_": [
  2626	        "railway_list_deployments",
  2627	        "railway_get_logs",
  2628	        "railway_get_build_logs",
  2629	        "railway_get_deployment",
  2630	        "railway_get_service"
  2631	      ],
  2632	      "shell_": ["shell_run"],
  2633	      "vercel_": ["vercel_status"],
  2634	      "supabase_": ["supabase_status"],
  2635	      "system_": [
  2636	        "system_status",
  2637	        "system_shell_exec",
  2638	        "system_screenshot_capture",
  2639	        "system_desktop_snapshot",
  2640	        "system_desktop_uia_find",
  2641	        "system_desktop_operator_step",
  2642	        "system_device_tap",
  2643	        "system_device_type_text",
  2644	        "system_process_list",
  2645	        "system_file_read",
  2646	        "system_file_write",
  2647	        "system_git_status",
  2648	        "system_git_diff",
  2649	        "ag_live_desktop_status",
  2650	        "ag_live_desktop_task"
  2651	      ],
  2652	      "device_": [
  2653	        "device_list",
  2654	        "device_status",
  2655	        "device_open_app",
  2656	        "device_ui_tree",
  2657	        "device_screenshot"
  2658	      ],
  2659	      "browser_": [
  2660	        "browser_status",
  2661	        "browser_sessions",
  2662	        "browser_session_start",
  2663	        "browser_observe",
  2664	        "browser_close"
  2665	      ],
  2666	      "chrome_": [
  2667	        "chrome_status",
  2668	        "chrome_tabs",
  2669	        "chrome_open",
  2670	        "chrome_observe",
  2671	        "chrome_close"
  2672	      ],
  2673	      "openclaw_": ["openclaw_admin_rpc", "openclaw_status", "openclaw_delegate"],
  2674	      "creator_": ["creator_target_inventory"],
  2675	      "roblox_": ["roblox_studio_status"],
  2676	      "google_search_console_": [
  2677	        "google_search_console_status",
  2678	        "google_search_console_list_sites",
  2679	        "google_search_console_search_analytics",
  2680	        "google_search_console_url_inspect",
  2681	        "google_search_console_list_sitemaps"
  2682	      ]
  2683	    };
  2684	
  2685	    for (const prefix of preferredPrefixes) {
  2686	      const core = familyCoreTools[prefix] ?? [];
  2687	      for (const name of core) {
  2688	        const definition = effective.find((candidate) => candidate.name === name);
  2689	        if (definition) addDefinition(definition);
  2690	      }
  2691	    }
  2692	    for (const prefix of preferredPrefixes) {
  2693	      for (const definition of effective) {
  2694	        if (definition.name.startsWith(prefix)) addDefinition(definition);
  2695	      }
  2696	    }
  2697	    for (const definition of effective) {
  2698	      addDefinition(definition);
  2699	    }
  2700	    bounded = picked;
  2701	  }
  2702	
  2703	  console.log(
  2704	    `[deepseek-tools] task_type=${taskType} exposed=${bounded.length} registry_total=${all.length} prefixes=${[...prefixes].join(",") || "minimal"} progressive_search=${always.has("geminx_tool_search")}`
  2705	  );
  2706	
  2707	  return bounded.map((definition) => ({
  2708	    type: "function" as const,
  2709	    function: {
  2710	      name: definition.name,
  2711	      description: definition.description,
  2712	      parameters: definition.input_schema as Record<string, unknown>
  2713	    }
  2714	  }));
  2715	}
  2716	
  2717	export function progressivelyDiscloseToolSchemas(
  2718	  current: DeepSeekToolDefinition[],
  2719	  registry: ToolRegistry,
  2720	  data: unknown,
  2721	  maxTotalDefinitions = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY,
  2722	  protectedToolNames: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(["geminx_tool_search"])
  2723	): DeepSeekToolDefinition[] {
  2724	  if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || Array.isArray(data)) return current;
  2725	  const matches = (data as Record<string, unknown>).matches;
  2726	  if (!Array.isArray(matches)) return current;
  2727	
  2728	  const requested = new Set(
  2729	    matches
  2730	      .map((row) => row && typeof row === "object" && !Array.isArray(row) ? String((row as Record<string, unknown>).name ?? "") : "")
  2731	      .filter(Boolean)
  2732	      .slice(0, 6)
  2733	  );
  2734	  if (requested.size === 0) return current;
  2735	
  2736	  const byName = new Map(current.map((tool) => [tool.function.name, tool]));
  2737	  for (const definition of registry.listTools()) {
  2738	    if (!toolCanBeAdvertised(definition.name, nativeChannelSnapshot())) continue;
  2739	    if ((!requested.has(definition.name) && !protectedToolNames.has(definition.name)) || definition.name === "web_search") continue;
  2740	    byName.set(definition.name, {
  2741	      type: "function" as const,
  2742	      function: {
  2743	        name: definition.name,
  2744	        description: definition.description,
  2745	        parameters: definition.input_schema as Record<string, unknown>
  2746	      }
  2747	    });
  2748	  }
  2749	
  2750	  const requestedTools = [...requested]
  2751	    .map((name) => byName.get(name))
  2752	    .filter((tool): tool is DeepSeekToolDefinition => Boolean(tool));
  2753	  if (requestedTools.length === 0) return current;
  2754	
  2755	  if (!Number.isFinite(maxTotalDefinitions)) {
  2756	    const existingNames = new Set(current.map((tool) => tool.function.name));
  2757	    const additions = requestedTools.filter((tool) => !existingNames.has(tool.function.name));
  2758	    if (additions.length > 0) {
  2759	      console.log(`[deepseek-tools] progressive_disclosure added=${additions.length} tools=${additions.map((item) => item.function.name).join(",")}`);
  2760	    }
  2761	    return additions.length > 0 ? [...current, ...additions] : current;
  2762	  }
  2763	
  2764	  // Operator parity without schema explosion: capability discovery stays available
  2765	  // for the entire objective, but the provider only sees a bounded working set.
  2766	  // The newest explicitly discovered tools win; the search router and the last
  2767	  // few actually-used tools are retained; stale unexecuted schemas are evicted.
  2768	  const cap = Math.max(4, Math.floor(maxTotalDefinitions));
  2769	  // Pinned workflow tools outrank unrelated newly discovered schemas.
  2770	  // For runtime caps (cap >= 16), automatically compact soft-pinned workflow schemas
  2771	  // via priority-ordered LRU when protectedFitting would overflow or starve newly
  2772	  // requested schemas, preserving hard-pinned router tools and the 6 most recent tools.
  2773	  let protectedFitting = [...protectedToolNames]
  2774	    .map((name) => byName.get(name))
  2775	    .filter((tool): tool is DeepSeekToolDefinition => Boolean(tool));
  2776	  const preliminaryPinnedNames = new Set(protectedFitting.map((tool) => tool.function.name));
  2777	  const preliminaryExtrasCount = requestedTools.filter((tool) => !preliminaryPinnedNames.has(tool.function.name)).length;
  2778	  const reservedForExtras = preliminaryExtrasCount > 0 ? Math.min(preliminaryExtrasCount, Math.max(1, Math.min(6, Math.floor(cap * 0.15)))) : 0;
  2779	  const maxProtectedBudget = cap >= 16 ? Math.max(4, cap - reservedForExtras) : cap;
  2780	  if (cap >= 16 && protectedFitting.length > maxProtectedBudget) {
  2781	    const hardPinned = new Set(["geminx_tool_search", "geminx_runtime_capabilities"]);
  2782	    const tailProtected = new Set(protectedFitting.slice(-6).map((t) => t.function.name));
  2783	    const compacted: DeepSeekToolDefinition[] = [];
  2784	    const evictable: DeepSeekToolDefinition[] = [];
  2785	    for (const tool of protectedFitting) {
  2786	      if (hardPinned.has(tool.function.name) || tailProtected.has(tool.function.name)) {
  2787	        compacted.push(tool);
  2788	      } else {
  2789	        evictable.push(tool);
  2790	      }
  2791	    }
  2792	    const remainingProtectedSlots = Math.max(0, maxProtectedBudget - compacted.length);
  2793	    const retainedSoft = remainingProtectedSlots > 0 ? evictable.slice(-remainingProtectedSlots) : [];
  2794	    protectedFitting = [...compacted, ...retainedSoft].slice(0, maxProtectedBudget);
  2795	  }
  2796	  if (protectedFitting.length > cap) {
  2797	    throw new Error(`GEMINX_WORKFLOW_SCHEMA_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED pinned=${protectedFitting.length} cap=${cap}`);
  2798	  }
  2799	  const pinnedNames = new Set(protectedFitting.map((tool) => tool.function.name));
  2800	  const requestedExtras = requestedTools.filter((tool) => !pinnedNames.has(tool.function.name));
  2801	  const remainingSlots = cap - protectedFitting.length;
  2802	  // Preserve active workflow tools first. A broad unrelated search must not
  2803	  // abort an otherwise valid operator workflow when some disclosure slots remain.
  2804	  // When no slot exists, fail closed rather than silently hide every requested tool.
  2805	  if (requestedExtras.length > 0 && remainingSlots === 0) {
  2806	    throw new Error(`GEMINX_WORKFLOW_SCHEMA_CAPACITY_EXCEEDED pinned=${protectedFitting.length} requested=${requestedExtras.length} cap=${cap}`);
  2807	  }
  2808	  const admittedExtras = requestedExtras.slice(0, remainingSlots);
  2809	  const requestedNames = new Set(admittedExtras.map((tool) => tool.function.name));
  2810	  const ordinaryCurrent = current.filter(
  2811	    (tool) => !pinnedNames.has(tool.function.name) && !requestedNames.has(tool.function.name)
  2812	  );
  2813	  const ordinarySlots = Math.max(0, cap - protectedFitting.length - admittedExtras.length);
  2814	  const retainedOrdinary = ordinarySlots > 0 ? ordinaryCurrent.slice(-ordinarySlots) : [];
  2815	  const next = [...protectedFitting, ...retainedOrdinary, ...admittedExtras];
  2816	
  2817	  const previousNames = new Set(current.map((tool) => tool.function.name));
  2818	  const addedNames = next
  2819	    .map((tool) => tool.function.name)
  2820	    .filter((name) => !previousNames.has(name));
  2821	  const evictedNames = current
  2822	    .map((tool) => tool.function.name)
  2823	    .filter((name) => !next.some((tool) => tool.function.name === name));
  2824	  if (addedNames.length > 0 || evictedNames.length > 0) {
  2825	    console.log(
  2826	      `[deepseek-tools] progressive_window size=${next.length}/${cap} added=${addedNames.join(",") || "none"} evicted=${evictedNames.join(",") || "none"}`
  2827	    );
  2828	  }
  2829	  return next;
  2830	}
  2831	
  2832	export function toolWorkflowFamilyPrefix(toolName: string): string {
  2833	  const clean = String(toolName ?? "").trim();
  2834	  if (!clean) return "";
  2835	  const parts = clean.split("_").filter(Boolean);
  2836	  if (parts.length >= 3) return parts.slice(0, 2).join("_") + "_";
  2837	  if (parts.length >= 2) return parts[0] + "_";
  2838	  return clean + "_";
  2839	}
  2840	
  2841	export function activeWorkflowSiblingNames(
  2842	  registry: ToolRegistry,
  2843	  toolName: string,
  2844	  maxNames = 8
  2845	): string[] {
  2846	  const prefix = toolWorkflowFamilyPrefix(toolName);
  2847	  if (!prefix) return [];
  2848	  const cap = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, Math.floor(maxNames)));
  2849	  const all = registry.listTools().map((definition) => String(definition.name));
  2850	  const exact = all.includes(toolName) ? [toolName] : [];
  2851	  const siblings = all
  2852	    .filter((name) => name !== toolName && name.startsWith(prefix))
  2853	    .sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
  2854	  return [...exact, ...siblings].slice(0, cap);
  2855	}
  2856	
  2857	export function shouldAdoptActiveWorkflowFamily(
  2858	  activeToolNames: ReadonlySet<string>,
  2859	  candidateToolName: string,
  2860	  candidateIsCacheableRead: boolean
  2861	): boolean {
  2862	  if (activeToolNames.size === 0) return true;
  2863	  const currentPrefix = toolWorkflowFamilyPrefix([...activeToolNames][0] ?? "");
  2864	  const candidatePrefix = toolWorkflowFamilyPrefix(candidateToolName);
  2865	  if (currentPrefix === candidatePrefix) return true;
  2866	  return !candidateIsCacheableRead;
  2867	}
  2868	
  2869	function flashToolExecutionDecision(): { model: "deepseek-flash"; thinking: "disabled"; reasoning_effort: null } {
  2870	  return { model: "deepseek-flash", thinking: "disabled", reasoning_effort: null };
  2871	}
  2872	
  2873	export function operatorGradeToolExecutionDecision(
  2874	  resolved: { model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro"; thinking: "enabled" | "disabled"; reasoning_effort: string | null },
  2875	  _taskType: GeminxModelTaskType,
  2876	  _intent: string
  2877	): { model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro"; thinking: "enabled" | "disabled"; reasoning_effort: string | null } {
  2878	  // Pro is the THINK-ONLY architect. It must never own the operator/tool loop.
  2879	  // Explicit Pro selection is handled as a separate tool-free plan first.
  2880	  // All tools and terminal verification are run by the original Flash executor.
  2881	  if (resolved.model === "deepseek-v4-pro") return flashToolExecutionDecision();
  2882	  return { ...resolved };
  2883	}
  2884	
  2885	export function buildRequestBody(
  2886	  decision: { model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro"; thinking: "enabled" | "disabled"; reasoning_effort: string | null },
  2887	  messages: DeepSeekMessage[],
  2888	  toolDefinitions: DeepSeekToolDefinition[],
  2889	  forcedToolName?: string | null
  2890	): Record<string, unknown> {
  2891	  const toolExecution = toolDefinitions.length > 0;
  2892	  // Provider-level non-bypassable guard, including explicit Pro UI choice and
  2893	  // emergency failover: expensive models never receive tool schemas.
  2894	  if (decision.model === "deepseek-v4-pro" && toolExecution) {
  2895	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(400, "Pro is think-only: tool execution forbidden", "Pro is think-only: tool execution forbidden (PRO_THINK_ONLY_TOOLS_FORBIDDEN)");
  2896	  }
  2897	  // DeepSeek forbids named/required tool_choice with thinking enabled.
  2898	  // Deterministic forced-tool invocations stay non-thinking, but normal
  2899	  // multi-step tool use follows the router's reasoning decision.
  2900	  const effectiveThinking = forcedToolName && toolExecution ? "disabled" : decision.thinking;
  2901	  return {
  2902	    model: decision.model,
  2903	    messages: effectiveThinking === "enabled"
  2904	      ? messages.map((m) => {
  2905	          if (m.role === "assistant" && (m as any).tool_calls && !(m as any).reasoning_content) {
  2906	            return { ...m, reasoning_content: "" };
  2907	          }
  2908	          return m;
  2909	        })
  2910	      : messages.map(({ reasoning_content: _privateReasoning, ...rest }) => rest),
  2911	    stream: false,
  2912	    thinking: { type: effectiveThinking },
  2913	    max_tokens: decision.model === "deepseek-v4-pro"
  2914	      ? Math.min(2048, maxOutputTokens(decision.model))
  2915	      : maxOutputTokens(decision.model),
  2916	    ...(effectiveThinking === "enabled" && decision.reasoning_effort ? { reasoning_effort: decision.reasoning_effort } : {}),
  2917	    ...(toolExecution
  2918	      ? {
  2919	          tools: toolDefinitions,
  2920	          tool_choice: forcedToolName
  2921	            ? { type: "function", function: { name: forcedToolName } }
  2922	            : "auto"
  2923	        }
  2924	      : {})
  2925	  };
  2926	}
  2927	
  2928	async function deepseekChat(
  2929	  apiKey: string,
  2930	  decision: { model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro"; thinking: "enabled" | "disabled"; reasoning_effort: string | null },
  2931	  messages: DeepSeekMessage[],
  2932	  toolDefinitions: DeepSeekToolDefinition[],
  2933	  timeoutOverrideMs?: number,
  2934	  forcedToolName?: string | null
  2935	): Promise<DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse> {
  2936	  const timeoutMs = Math.max(
  2937	    15000,
  2938	    Math.min(
  2939	      180000,
  2940	      Number(timeoutOverrideMs ?? process.env.DEEPSEEK_API_TIMEOUT_MS ?? (decision.model === "deepseek-v4-pro" ? 120000 : DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS))
  2941	    )
  2942	  );
  2943	  const request = () => fetch(`${readDeepSeekBaseUrl()}/chat/completions`, {
  2944	    method: "POST",
  2945	    headers: {
  2946	      "content-type": "application/json",
  2947	      authorization: `Bearer ${apiKey}`
  2948	    },
  2949	    body: JSON.stringify(buildRequestBody(decision, messages, toolDefinitions, forcedToolName)),
  2950	    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs)
  2951	  });
  2952	
  2953	  let response: Response;
  2954	  try {
  2955	    response = await request();
  2956	  } catch (error) {
  2957	    // A transport timeout/disconnect is ambiguous: the provider may already have
  2958	    // accepted or billed the request. Never resend the same large prompt blindly.
  2959	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  2960	      502,
  2961	      "DeepSeek temporarily timed out",
  2962	      `DEEPSEEK_AMBIGUOUS_NETWORK_FAILURE_NO_AUTORETRY: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`
  2963	    );
  2964	  }
  2965	
  2966	  // 429 is an explicit rate-limit response and is safe for one bounded retry.
  2967	  // 5xx responses are not replayed here because the provider-side execution/billing
  2968	  // state may be ambiguous and the durable objective layer can reconcile instead.
  2969	  if (response.status === 429) {
  2970	    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 700));
  2971	    try {
  2972	      response = await request();
  2973	    } catch {
  2974	      // Keep the original 429 response below.
  2975	    }
  2976	  }
  2977	
  2978	  if (!response.ok) {
  2979	    const raw = await response.text();
  2980	    const detail = raw.trim().slice(0, 300);
  2981	    if (response.status === 402) {
  2982	      throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  2983	        402,
  2984	        "DeepSeek has no usable credit for this request",
  2985	        `DeepSeek returned HTTP 402${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`
  2986	      );
  2987	    }
  2988	    if (
  2989	      response.status === 413 ||
  2990	      /(?:bounded context budget|context budget.*(?:exceeded|overflow)|input exceeded.*context|prompt too large)/i.test(detail)
  2991	    ) {
  2992	      throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  2993	        429,
  2994	        "GeminX context pressure requires compact continuation",
  2995	        `GEMINX_CONTEXT_PRESSURE_PROVIDER_413_NO_REPLAY status=${response.status}${detail ? ` detail=${detail}` : ""}`
  2996	      );
  2997	    }
  2998	    if (
  2999	      response.status === 400 &&
  3000	      /reasoning_content.*thinking mode/i.test(detail) &&
  3001	      decision.thinking === "enabled"
  3002	    ) {
  3003	      console.warn("[deepseek-fallback] reasoning_content error encountered; retrying turn with thinking disabled");
  3004	      const fallbackDecision = { ...decision, thinking: "disabled" as const, reasoning_effort: null };
  3005	      return deepseekChat(apiKey, fallbackDecision, messages, toolDefinitions, timeoutOverrideMs, forcedToolName);
  3006	    }
  3007	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  3008	      502,
  3009	      "DeepSeek temporarily could not complete that",
  3010	      `DeepSeek returned HTTP ${response.status}${detail ? `: ${detail}` : ""}`
  3011	    );
  3012	  }
  3013	
  3014	  // A truncated provider JSON body is transport failure, not model speech and
  3015	  // not evidence of an executed tool. Keep this typed so the durable worker can
  3016	  // checkpoint the original objective instead of crashing on bare SyntaxError.
  3017	  let parsed: unknown;
  3018	  try {
  3019	    parsed = await response.json();
  3020	  } catch {
  3021	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  3022	      502,
  3023	      "DeepSeek returned malformed JSON; original objective is not completed",
  3024	      "DEEPSEEK_MALFORMED_PROVIDER_JSON_NO_TOOL_EXECUTED"
  3025	    );
  3026	  }
  3027	  if (!parsed || typeof parsed !== "object" || Array.isArray(parsed) ||
  3028	      !Array.isArray((parsed as Record<string, unknown>).choices)) {
  3029	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  3030	      502,
  3031	      "DeepSeek returned an invalid response envelope; original objective is not completed",
  3032	      "DEEPSEEK_INVALID_PROVIDER_ENVELOPE_NO_TOOL_EXECUTED"
  3033	    );
  3034	  }
  3035	  return parsed as DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse;
  3036	}
  3037	function parseToolCalls(payload: DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse): Array<{
  3038	  id: string;
  3039	  name: string;
  3040	  args: Record<string, unknown>;
  3041	  malformed?: boolean;
  3042	  parse_error?: string;
  3043	}> {
  3044	  const message = payload.choices?.[0]?.message;
  3045	  const calls = message?.tool_calls ?? [];
  3046	  if (calls.length > 0) {
  3047	    return calls
  3048	      .filter((call) => call.function?.name)
  3049	      .map((call) => {
  3050	        const parsedArgs = parseToolArgumentsStrict(call.function?.arguments ?? "{}");
  3051	        return {
  3052	          id: call.id ?? `call_${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 10)}`,
  3053	          name: call.function!.name!,
  3054	          args: parsedArgs.args,
  3055	          malformed: !parsedArgs.ok,
  3056	          parse_error: parsedArgs.error ?? (parsedArgs.ok ? undefined : "MALFORMED_JSON_TOOL_ARGUMENTS")
  3057	        };
  3058	      });
  3059	  }
  3060	
  3061	  // Fallback: If DeepSeek output tool calls embedded as DSML / XML tags in message.content
  3062	  const rawContent = typeof message?.content === "string" ? message.content : "";
  3063	  if (rawContent && (/<(?:[|｜]+\s*DSML\s*[|｜]+\s*)?(?:calls|invoke|function_calls)\b|<\s*invoke\s+name=|<\s*tool_call\s*>/i.test(rawContent))) {
  3064	    const embedded = extractEmbeddedToolCalls(rawContent);
  3065	    if (embedded.length > 0) {
  3066	      console.log(`[deepseek-tools] extracted_${embedded.length}_embedded_tool_calls tools=${embedded.map((e) => e.name).join(",")}`);
  3067	      if (message) {
  3068	        message.tool_calls = embedded.map((call) => ({
  3069	          id: call.id,
  3070	          type: "function",
  3071	          function: {
  3072	            name: call.name,
  3073	            arguments: JSON.stringify(call.args)
  3074	          }
  3075	        }));
  3076	        message.content = rawContent
  3077	          .replace(/<\s*(?:[|｜]+\s*DSML\s*[|｜]+\s*)?calls[\s\S]*?<\/\s*(?:[|｜]+\s*DSML\s*[|｜]+\s*)?calls\s*>/gi, "")
  3078	          .replace(/<\s*(?:[|｜]+\s*DSML\s*[|｜]+\s*)?invoke[\s\S]*?<\/\s*(?:[|｜]+\s*DSML\s*[|｜]+\s*)?invoke\s*>/gi, "")
  3079	          .replace(/<\s*tool_call[\s\S]*?<\/\s*tool_call\s*>/gi, "")
  3080	          .trim() || null;
  3081	      }
  3082	      return embedded;
  3083	    }
  3084	  }
  3085	
  3086	  return [];
  3087	}
  3088	
  3089	export interface DeepSeekCompanionReply {
  3090	  message: string;
  3091	  model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro";
  3092	  provider: "deepseek";
  3093	  boot_profile_id: string;
  3094	  correlation_id?: string;
  3095	  conversation_id?: string;
  3096	  active_run_id?: string | null;
  3097	  brain_context_status: AgentBrainContextStatus;
  3098	  brain_sources: AgentBrainContextSource[];
  3099	  tool_calls?: ToolCallRecord[];
  3100	  multi_step?: boolean;
  3101	  /** Internal continuation signal. Never render directly to the Founder. */
  3102	  context_pressure_resume_required?: boolean;
  3103	  /** Runtime-generated paid no-progress fuse. Never derived from model prose. */
  3104	  no_progress_observation_fuse?: boolean;
  3105	  background_handoff?: AgContinuationHandoff;
  3106	}
  3107	
  3108	/**
  3109	 * Optional durable conversation continuity binding. When provided, prior turns
  3110	 * are loaded (bounded) before handling the follow-up and the active run/task/domain
  3111	 * binding informs Agent Brain retrieval. Fresh or reloaded conversations with no
  3112	 * store use the legacy stateless path and remain fully backward compatible.
  3113	 */
  3114	export interface CompanionConversationOptions {
  3115	  store?: ConversationStore | null;
  3116	  conversationId?: string | null;
  3117	  /** The real Founder HQ conversation receiving scheduled follow-ups. Never use an internal obj_transcript_ id. */
  3118	  founderConversationId?: string | null;
  3119	  activeRunId?: string | null;
  3120	  /** Optional client-supplied durable message id to preserve identity across optimistic transcripts. */
  3121	  messageId?: string | null;
  3122	  clientMessageId?: string | null;
  3123	  /** Optional caller-supplied active task id to bind. */
  3124	  activeTaskId?: string | null;
  3125	  /** Authenticated server-only Founder-session proof; never accept client hints. */
  3126	  founderAuthorised?: boolean;
  3127	  /** Immutable founder objective text. Wrapper state can change between resumed turns. */
  3128	  executionObjective?: string;
  3129	  /** Optional bounded live runtime snapshot supplied by the calling product surface. */
  3130	  runtimeContext?: string | null;
  3131	  /** Clean founder-visible text to persist when the model receives enriched hidden media context. */
  3132	  displayIntent?: string | null;
  3133	  /** Attachment metadata shown again when HQ restores the conversation. */
  3134	  attachments?: ConversationAttachment[];
  3135	  /** Explicit DeepSeek model choice. "deepseek" keeps cheap-first automatic routing. */
  3136	  modelPreference?: string | null;
  3137	  /** Runtime-enforced compact continuation after context pressure; skips full Brain replay for the same objective. */
  3138	  forceCompactContext?: boolean;
  3139	  /** Canonical agent identity for this HQ conversation. */
  3140	  agentMode?: "neo" | "ag";
  3141	  /** Active AI Office agent identity (geminx, ag, neo, brian, mark, kerry, sophie, ads). */
  3142	  officeAgent?: OfficeAgentId;
  3143	  /** Authoritative company/venture scope for this turn (entreprenuity, wild-botanics, holding). */
  3144	  companyScope?: string | null;
  3145	  /** Required server channels explicitly declared by a trusted job envelope. */
  3146	  requiredConnectors?: NativeChannel[];
  3147	  /** Observable activity only; never hidden reasoning. */
  3148	  onActivity?: (event: { phase: string; status: "running" | "done" | "blocked" | "failed"; label: string; tool?: string; session_id?: string; url?: string; challenge?: string; requires_human?: boolean }) => void;
  3149	  /** Consume founder follow-ups at safe checkpoints during a durable task. */
  3150	  drainFollowups?: () => string[];
  3151	}
  3152	
  3153	/** The execution transcript and the Founder conversation are different durable records.
  3154	 *  A delayed reply must go back to the Founder conversation, never obj_transcript_*.
  3155	 *  Reject unbound background tasks instead of pretending an orphaned schedule will show up.
  3156	 */
  3157	export function resolveScheduledFounderConversationId(
  3158	  conversation: Pick<CompanionConversationOptions, "conversationId" | "founderConversationId" | "activeTaskId"> | null | undefined,
  3159	  transcriptId?: string | null
  3160	): string {
  3161	  const founderId = String(conversation?.founderConversationId ?? "").trim();
  3162	  if (founderId && !founderId.startsWith("obj_transcript_")) return founderId;
  3163	  const directId = String(transcriptId ?? conversation?.conversationId ?? "").trim();
  3164	  if (directId && !directId.startsWith("obj_transcript_")) return directId;
  3165	  return "";
  3166	}
  3167	
  3168	export async function generateCompanionReply(intent: string): Promise<DeepSeekCompanionReply> {
  3169	  return generateCompanionReplyWithTools(intent, null, null);
  3170	}
  3171	
  3172	export async function generateCompanionReplyWithTools(
  3173	  intent: string,
  3174	  correlationId: string | null,
  3175	  toolRegistry: ToolRegistry | null,
  3176	  brainRetriever?: AgentBrainContextRetriever,
  3177	  conversation?: CompanionConversationOptions,
  3178	  taskType: GeminxModelTaskType = "chat"
  3179	): Promise<DeepSeekCompanionReply> {
  3180	  const channels = requireNativeChannels(conversation?.requiredConnectors ?? [], nativeChannelSnapshot());
  3181	  if (!channels.ok) {
  3182	    // Zero LLM calls; eligible repair tasks should be dispatched to the
  3183	    // existing AG authority by the parent worker, not hallucinated by Flash.
  3184	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(503, "BLOCKED_TURN_0_UNBOUND_CONNECTOR: " + channels.missing.join(","), channels.code);
  3185	  }
  3186	  const apiKey = process.env.DEEPSEEK_API_KEY?.trim() ?? "";
  3187	  // Non-durable chat turns without a stable run ID must never accidentally
  3188	  // share one lifetime budget across the entire conversation or every guest.
  3189	  const standaloneObjectiveKey = "geminx-adhoc-" + randomUUID();
  3190	  const explicitModel = String(conversation?.modelPreference ?? "deepseek").trim().toLowerCase();
  3191	  // A hard tool I/O or quota fault is a capability issue, not a request for
  3192	  // additional paid reasoning. Pro analysis must not be triggered by one.
  3193	  const capabilityFailureWithoutReasoning =
  3194	    /\b(?:BLOCKED_TOOL_IO|TOOL_IO_DEGRADED|PROVIDER_QUOTA_EXHAUSTED|RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED|credit_balance_exhausted|BUDGET_EXHAUSTED)\b/i.test(intent);
  3195	  const proThinkOnlyRequested =
  3196	    !capabilityFailureWithoutReasoning && (
  3197	      explicitModel === "deepseek-v4-pro" ||
  3198	      ["architecture", "red-team", "reasoning"].includes(taskType) ||
  3199	      /\b(?:production migration|rotate (?:a |the )?(?:credential|secret)|irreversible|destructive schema|multi.system root cause|security critical)\b/i.test(intent)
  3200	    );
  3201	  const routedTaskType: GeminxModelTaskType = taskType;
  3202	  const router = new DeepSeekV4Router({
  3203	    apiKeyPresent: apiKey.length > 0,
  3204	    providerAvailable: true,
  3205	    forceModel:
  3206	      explicitModel === "deepseek-v4-pro" ? "deepseek-v4-pro"
  3207	        : explicitModel === "deepseek-flash" ? "deepseek-flash"
  3208	          : null
  3209	  });
  3210	  const decision = router.route(routedTaskType);
  3211	
  3212	  if (decision.provider !== "deepseek" || !decision.model) {
  3213	    throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(503, "companion llm unavailable", decision.reason);
  3214	  }
  3215	
  3216	  const resolved: { model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro"; thinking: "enabled" | "disabled"; reasoning_effort: string | null } =
  3217	    decision.model === "deepseek-v4-pro"
  3218	      ? {
  3219	          model: "deepseek-v4-pro",
  3220	          thinking: decision.thinking,
  3221	          reasoning_effort: decision.reasoning_effort
  3222	        }
  3223	      : {
  3224	          model: "deepseek-flash",
  3225	          thinking: decision.thinking,
  3226	          reasoning_effort: decision.reasoning_effort
  3227	        };
  3228	
  3229	  // Durable conversation continuity: load/derive the active binding and prior turns.
  3230	  const store = conversation?.store ?? null;
  3231	  let conversationRecord: ConversationRecord | null = null;
  3232	  let conversationId: string | null | undefined = conversation?.conversationId ?? null;
  3233	  const activeRunId = conversation?.activeRunId ?? null;
  3234	  const activeTaskId = conversation?.activeTaskId ?? null;
  3235	
  3236	  if (store) {
  3237	    if (conversationId) {
  3238	      conversationRecord = store.get(conversationId);
  3239	    }
  3240	    if (!conversationRecord) {
  3241	      const created = store.create(conversationId || undefined);
  3242	      conversationRecord = created;
  3243	      conversationId = created.conversation_id;
  3244	    }
  3245	  }
  3246	
  3247	  const rawPriorTurns = conversationRecord
  3248	    ? conversationRecord.turns.map((turn) => ({ role: turn.role as "user" | "assistant", content: turn.content }))
  3249	    : [];
  3250	  const agentMode = conversation?.agentMode === "ag" ? "ag" : "neo";
  3251	  const activeOfficeAgent: OfficeAgentId = conversation?.officeAgent
  3252	    ? normalizeOfficeAgentId(conversation.officeAgent, agentMode)
  3253	    : agentMode;
  3254	  const fullContext =
  3255	    conversation?.forceCompactContext === true
  3256	      ? false
  3257	      : needsFullCompanionContext(intent, routedTaskType, rawPriorTurns) ||
  3258	        (agentMode === "ag" && AG_SENTINEL_DEEP_BOOT_PATTERN.test(String(intent || "")));
  3259	
  3260	  // Tool capability is independent from Brain-context size. A compact follow-up
  3261	  // such as "go do it" must not lose device/browser/GitHub tools merely because
  3262	  // we do not need a full Agent Brain retrieval on that turn.
  3263	  let effectiveToolRegistry = toolRegistry;
  3264	  let withTools = effectiveToolRegistry !== null;
  3265	  // Context retrieval is independent of tool authority. A prior debugging or
  3266	  // operator turn must NEVER inject its GitHub/Railway/shell schemas into a
  3267	  // fresh conversational request. Explicit durable continuations are routed
  3268	  // through the task ledger in runs.ts, not by matching old chat keywords.
  3269	  let toolDefinitions = buildToolDefinitions(
  3270	    effectiveToolRegistry,
  3271	    intent,
  3272	    routedTaskType,
  3273	    agentMode,
  3274	    activeOfficeAgent,
  3275	    conversation?.companyScope ?? null
  3276	  );
  3277	  // Preserve the entire scratch lifecycle family for its objective. Initial
  3278	  // permissions still come from ToolRegistry; this adds NO privileges.
  3279	  const objectivePinnedToolNames = new Set<string>();
  3280	  if (/(?:^|\b)(?:temporary|temp|scratch)(?:\s+(?:file|artifact|text))?\b|\bruntime\s+(?:file|artifact|scratch)\b/i.test(toolRoutingIntentText(intent))) {
  3281	    const authorisedNames = new Set<string>(effectiveToolRegistry?.listTools().map((tool) => String(tool.name)) ?? []);
  3282	    for (const name of ["geminx_scratch_write", "geminx_scratch_read", "geminx_scratch_status", "geminx_scratch_delete"]) {
  3283	      if (authorisedNames.has(name)) objectivePinnedToolNames.add(name);
  3284	    }
  3285	    // Advertise objective-critical authorised tools on the FIRST model request,
  3286	    // not merely after the model decides to discover them.
  3287	    if (objectivePinnedToolNames.size > 0 && effectiveToolRegistry) {
  3288	      toolDefinitions = progressivelyDiscloseToolSchemas(
  3289	        toolDefinitions, effectiveToolRegistry,
  3290	        { matches: [...objectivePinnedToolNames].map((name) => ({ name })) },
  3291	        24,
  3292	        new Set(["geminx_tool_search", ...objectivePinnedToolNames])
  3293	      );
  3294	    }
  3295	  }
  3296	  const sanitizedPriorTurns = sanitizedConversationTurns(rawPriorTurns, intent);
  3297	  const priorTurns = boundedPriorTurns(
  3298	    sanitizedPriorTurns,
  3299	    fullContext ? FULL_CONVERSATION_MAX_TURNS : COMPACT_CONVERSATION_MAX_TURNS,
  3300	    fullContext ? FULL_CONVERSATION_MAX_CHARS : COMPACT_CONVERSATION_MAX_CHARS
  3301	  );
  3302	
  3303	  // Write the founder objective through BEFORE any Brain/provider/tool work. If the
  3304	  // process dies mid-turn, the next process can recover the exact objective instead
  3305	  // of seeing an empty session and suppressing recovery.
  3306	  if (store && conversationRecord && conversationId) {
  3307	    const visibleIntent = conversation?.displayIntent ?? intent;
  3308	    const derivedDomain = deriveDomain(conversationRecord.domain, intent);
  3309	    const effectiveMessageId = conversation?.messageId ?? conversation?.clientMessageId;
  3310	    conversationRecord = store.appendTurn(conversationRecord, {
  3311	      role: "user",
  3312	      ...(effectiveMessageId ? { message_id: effectiveMessageId, client_message_id: effectiveMessageId } : {}),
  3313	      content: visibleIntent,
  3314	      at: new Date().toISOString(),
  3315	      ...(conversation?.attachments?.length ? { attachments: conversation.attachments } : {})
  3316	    }, {
  3317	      active_run_id: activeRunId,
  3318	      active_task_id: activeTaskId,
  3319	      domain: derivedDomain,
  3320	      summary: visibleIntent
  3321	    });
  3322	  }
  3323	
  3324	  // Active domain/goal from the durable binding must inform Agent Brain retrieval so
  3325	  // a bare follow-up is not contaminated by unrelated giant bodies.
  3326	  const retrievalContext: AgentBrainRetrievalContext | undefined = conversationRecord
  3327	    ? {
  3328	        activeDomain: conversationRecord.domain,
  3329	        activeGoal: conversationRecord.summary || undefined
  3330	      }
  3331	    : undefined;
  3332	  const continuityStatusQuery = CONTINUITY_STATUS_PATTERN.test(intent);
  3333	  const isFollowUp =
  3334	    !continuityStatusQuery &&
  3335	    priorTurns.length > 0 &&
  3336	    recentOperationalContext(rawPriorTurns) &&
  3337	    /^(why|what happened|continue|try again)$/i.test(intent.trim());
  3338	  conversation?.onActivity?.({ phase: "context", status: "running", label: "Checking relevant context" });
  3339	  // Never pass GeminX CEO's generic bootstrap as Mark/Sophie/Kerry/Brian/Adam's
  3340	  // specialist training. These agents require their own canonical Brain research.
  3341	  const specialistBootId = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
  3342	    OFFICE_SPECIALIST_BRAIN_PATHS, activeOfficeAgent
  3343	  ) ? activeOfficeAgent as OfficeSpecialistBrainId : null;
  3344	  const activeBrainRetriever = brainRetriever ??
  3345	    (specialistBootId
  3346	      ? configuredOfficeSpecialistBrainContextRetriever(specialistBootId)
  3347	      : configuredAgentBrainContextRetriever());
  3348	  const identityRetriever = activeBrainRetriever as AgentBrainContextRetriever & {
  3349	    retrieveIdentityCapsule?: () => Promise<{ status: AgentBrainContextStatus; sources: AgentBrainContextSource[]; context: string }>;
  3350	  };
  3351	  const brain = fullContext && !isFollowUp
  3352	    ? await activeBrainRetriever.retrieve(intent, retrievalContext)
  3353	    : typeof identityRetriever.retrieveIdentityCapsule === "function"
  3354	      ? await identityRetriever.retrieveIdentityCapsule()
  3355	      : { status: "empty" as const, sources: [], context: "" };
  3356	  conversation?.onActivity?.({ phase: "context", status: "done", label: "Context ready" });
  3357	
  3358	  // Do not execute authorised tasks on an unverified boot. Preserve the chat reply
  3359	  // even if recording this blocked state encounters a separate storage failure.
  3360	  // Specialist office agents (Sophie, Brian, Mark, Kerry, Ads) take their identity
