  3664	    // Flash owns both tool-bearing and tool-free execution. A Pro request is a
  3665	    // brief before Flash, never a model switch for the job or its completion.
  3666	    const primaryDecision = operatorToolDecision;
  3667	    conversation?.onActivity?.({
  3668	      phase: "model",
  3669	      status: "running",
  3670	      label: primaryDecision.model === "deepseek-v4-pro"
  3671	        ? "Using explicitly selected DeepSeek Pro for this run"
  3672	        : "Using DeepSeek V4.1 Flash as the configured primary model for this run"
  3673	    });
  3674	    const interactiveFounderTurn = Boolean(conversationId) && !activeTaskId && routedTaskType === "chat";
  3675	    let providerRequestOrdinal = 0;
  3676	    let estimatedPeakSpend = 0;
  3677	    let cumulativePromptTokens = 0;
  3678	    // Soft checkpoint signal: only a single oversize live provider window
  3679	    // triggers compaction. Cumulative usage is spend telemetry, not window size.
  3680	    let contextPressureReached = false;
  3681	    // Hard continuation signal: set only when unfinished tool work outlives the
  3682	    // bounded in-turn rollover budget. Do not confuse "we checkpointed" with
  3683	    // "this turn cannot finish".
  3684	    let contextResumeRequired = false;
  3685	    let contextRolloverCount = 0;
  3686	    const callDeepSeek = async (
  3687	      stage: string,
  3688	      requestMessages: DeepSeekMessage[],
  3689	      requestTools: DeepSeekToolDefinition[],
  3690	      requestDecision = resolved,
  3691	      forcedToolName: string | null = null
  3692	    ): Promise<DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse> => {
  3693	      const taskBudget = maxTaskSpendUsd();
  3694	      const dailyBudget = maxDailySpendUsd();
  3695	      if (providerRequestOrdinal > 0 && estimatedPeakSpend >= taskBudget) {
  3696	        throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  3697	          429,
  3698	          "DeepSeek task spend budget reached",
  3699	          `DEEPSEEK_TASK_SPEND_BUDGET_REACHED estimated_peak_usd=${estimatedPeakSpend.toFixed(4)} limit_usd=${taskBudget.toFixed(2)}`
  3700	        );
  3701	      }
  3702	      if (providerRequestOrdinal >= maxProviderRequestsPerTurn()) {
  3703	        throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  3704	          429,
  3705	          "DeepSeek provider request budget reached",
  3706	          `DEEPSEEK_PROVIDER_REQUEST_BUDGET_REACHED requests=${providerRequestOrdinal} limit=${maxProviderRequestsPerTurn()}`
  3707	        );
  3708	      }
  3709	
  3710	      // Reconcile before token budgeting AND before any paid provider request.
  3711	      // Multi-tool responses must remain adjacent even if repair hints were
  3712	      // inserted between tool executions.
  3713	      let outboundMessages = reconcileDeepSeekToolTranscript(requestMessages);
  3714	      let estimatedInputTokens = estimateProviderInputTokens(outboundMessages, requestTools);
  3715	      const preflightLimit = maxEstimatedInputTokensPerRequest();
  3716	      const rolloverTarget = Math.min(preflightLimit, contextRolloverTargetTokens());
  3717	      if (estimatedInputTokens > rolloverTarget) {
  3718	        // The 48k preflight value is a hard stop, not a target. Compact routine
  3719	        // operator traffic proactively around 24k estimated tokens so a modest
  3720	        // qualification does not replay 30k-40k provider prompts merely because
  3721	        // they technically fit. Durable evidence stays in the trace and can be
  3722	        // re-read precisely if the model genuinely needs it.
  3723	        const rolled = rolloverToolLoopMessages(requestMessages, intent, toolCalls);
  3724	        const rolledEstimate = estimateProviderInputTokens(rolled, requestTools);
  3725	        if (rolledEstimate < estimatedInputTokens) {
  3726	          console.warn(
  3727	            `[geminx-context] preflight_rollover correlation_id=${correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? conversationId ?? "unbound"} stage=${stage} estimated_before=${estimatedInputTokens} estimated_after=${rolledEstimate} target=${rolloverTarget} hard_limit=${preflightLimit}`
  3728	          );
  3729	          outboundMessages = rolled;
  3730	          estimatedInputTokens = rolledEstimate;
  3731	        }
  3732	      }
  3733	      if (estimatedInputTokens > preflightLimit) {
  3734	        throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  3735	          429,
  3736	          "GeminX context pressure stopped an oversized provider request before spend",
  3737	          `GEMINX_CONTEXT_PRESSURE_PREFLIGHT estimated_input_tokens=${estimatedInputTokens} limit=${preflightLimit} stage=${stage}`
  3738	        );
  3739	      }
  3740	
  3741	      const reservationUsd = reserveInFlightDailySpend(requestDecision.model, estimatedInputTokens, dailyBudget);
  3742	      const objectiveVelocityKey = correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? standaloneObjectiveKey;
  3743	      try {
  3744	        assertObjectiveSpendVelocity(objectiveVelocityKey, reservationUsd);
  3745	        reserveObjectiveLifetimeSpend(objectiveVelocityKey, reservationUsd, comprehensiveAudit);
  3746	      } catch (error) {
  3747	        releaseInFlightDailySpend(reservationUsd);
  3748	        throw error;
  3749	      }
  3750	      providerRequestOrdinal += 1;
  3751	      let response: DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse;
  3752	      try {
  3753	        const initialFounderTimeoutMs =
  3754	          interactiveFounderTurn && stage === "initial"
  3755	            ? 20_000
  3756	            : undefined;
  3757	        response = await deepseekChat(
  3758	          apiKey,
  3759	          requestDecision,
  3760	          outboundMessages,
  3761	          requestTools,
  3762	          initialFounderTimeoutMs,
  3763	          forcedToolName
  3764	        );
  3765	      } catch (error) {
  3766	        releaseInFlightDailySpend(reservationUsd);
  3767	        throw error;
  3768	      }
  3769	      const usage = response.usage;
  3770	      const callPeakSpend = usage ? estimatedPeakUsd(requestDecision.model, usage) : reservationUsd;
  3771	      estimatedPeakSpend += callPeakSpend;
  3772	      releaseInFlightDailySpend(reservationUsd);
  3773	      const objectiveLifetimeUsd = settleObjectiveLifetimeSpend(objectiveVelocityKey, reservationUsd, callPeakSpend);
  3774	      const dailyAfter = chargeDailySpend(callPeakSpend);
  3775	      recordObjectiveSpendVelocity(objectiveVelocityKey, callPeakSpend);
  3776	      console.log(
  3777	        `[deepseek-usage] correlation_id=${correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? conversationId ?? "unbound"} request=${providerRequestOrdinal} stage=${stage} model=${requestDecision.model} prompt_tokens=${usage?.prompt_tokens ?? "unknown"} completion_tokens=${usage?.completion_tokens ?? "unknown"} total_tokens=${usage?.total_tokens ?? "unknown"} cache_hit_tokens=${usage?.prompt_tokens_details?.prompt_cache_hit_tokens ?? usage?.prompt_tokens_details?.cached_tokens ?? "unknown"} cache_miss_tokens=${usage?.prompt_tokens_details?.prompt_cache_miss_tokens ?? "unknown"} estimated_peak_call_usd=${callPeakSpend.toFixed(6)} estimated_peak_task_usd=${estimatedPeakSpend.toFixed(6)} estimated_peak_objective_lifetime_usd=${objectiveLifetimeUsd.toFixed(6)} objective_lifetime_budget_usd=${spendBudgetLabel(maxObjectiveSpendUsd(comprehensiveAudit))} task_budget_usd=${spendBudgetLabel(taskBudget)} request_reservation_usd=${reservationUsd.toFixed(6)} in_flight_reserved_usd=${deepSeekInFlightReservedUsd.toFixed(6)} estimated_peak_daily_usd=${dailyAfter.estimated_peak_usd.toFixed(6)} daily_budget_usd=${spendBudgetLabel(dailyBudget)}`
  3778	      );
  3779	      const promptTokens = Math.max(0, Number(usage?.prompt_tokens ?? 0));
  3780	      cumulativePromptTokens += promptTokens;
  3781	      const checkpointThreshold = contextCheckpointThresholdTokens();
  3782	      if (shouldCheckpointContextWindow({
  3783	        currentPromptTokens: promptTokens,
  3784	        checkpointThresholdTokens: checkpointThreshold
  3785	      })) {
  3786	        contextPressureReached = true;
  3787	        persistContextPressureCheckpoint({
  3788	          key: correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? conversationId ?? "unbound",
  3789	          stage,
  3790	          intent,
  3791	          promptTokens,
  3792	          thresholdTokens: checkpointThreshold,
  3793	          conversationId,
  3794	          activeTaskId,
  3795	          brainSources: brain.sources,
  3796	          toolCalls
  3797	        });
  3798	        conversation?.onActivity?.({
  3799	          phase: "context",
  3800	          status: "done",
  3801	          label: "Checkpointed durable task state before context pressure became unsafe"
  3802	        });
  3803	      }
  3804	      return response;
  3805	    };
  3806	
  3807	    const initialToolDefinitionCount = toolDefinitions.length;
  3808	    // Keep a bounded provider schema working set while allowing the operator to
  3809	    // discover new capability families for the full lifetime of the objective.
  3810	    const maxToolDefinitionsForObjective = Math.max(64, initialToolDefinitionCount);
  3811	
  3812	    // Founder policy: at most one think-only Pro consultation before a high-
  3813	    // complexity objective's Flash execution. Resumes reuse the existing plan
  3814	    // in the same durable transcript and never buy another identical plan.
  3815	    const proPlanMarker = "[THINK_ONLY_PRO_PLAN_V1]";
  3816	    const priorProPlan = Boolean(savedCheckpoint?.messages.some(
  3817	      (message) => String(message.content ?? "").startsWith(proPlanMarker)
  3818	    ));
  3819	    if (proThinkOnlyRequested && !priorProPlan) {
  3820	      const planQuestion = String(conversation?.executionObjective ?? intent).slice(0, 5000);
  3821	      const planMessages: DeepSeekMessage[] = [
  3822	        {
  3823	          role: "system",
  3824	          content: "You are a THINK-ONLY engineering architect, not an operator. " +
  3825	            "No tools, commands, external actions or claims of completed work. " +
  3826	            "Return a SHORT explicit plan using DIAGNOSIS, DECISION, EXECUTION_STEPS, " +
  3827	            "RISKS_AND_ROLLBACK, VERIFY_TESTS, STOP_CONDITIONS. " +
  3828	            "Address any high-risk decision BEFORE Flash mutates state. " +
  3829	            "If the evidence is insufficient, say so, do not invent outputs."
  3830	        },
  3831	        {
  3832	          role: "user",
  3833	          content: "Root objective (bounded; secrets must not be reproduced):\n" +
  3834	            planQuestion + "\n\nTask class: " + routedTaskType +
  3835	            "\nOriginal operator retains all tool permissions and execution."
  3836	        }
  3837	      ];
  3838	      const thinkingDecision = {
  3839	        model: "deepseek-v4-pro" as const,
  3840	        thinking: "enabled" as const,
  3841	        reasoning_effort: "high"
  3842	      };
  3843	      try {
  3844	        const planResult = await callDeepSeek("think_only_plan", planMessages, [], thinkingDecision);
  3845	        const planContent = parseMessageContent(planResult.choices?.[0]?.message?.content)?.trim() ?? "";
  3846	        if (planContent) {
  3847	          // This is a planner packet, NOT a real tool receipt or Founder command.
  3848	          // Persist within the same task transcript so a restart won't repeat Pro.
  3849	          messages.push({
  3850	            role: "assistant",
  3851	            content: proPlanMarker + "\n" + planContent.slice(0, 8000) +
  3852	              "\nThis is advice only. Flash must independently check authorisation, execute real tools and verify actual receipts."
  3853	          });
  3854	          persistExecutionCheckpoint("CLEAN");
  3855	          console.log("[deepseek-escalation] phase=think_only_plan executor=deepseek-flash strategist=deepseek-v4-pro tools=0 status=handed_back");
  3856	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  3857	            phase: "model", status: "done",
  3858	            label: "DeepSeek Pro analysed the difficult decision; Flash is executing and verifying"
  3859	          });
  3860	        } else {
  3861	          console.warn("[deepseek-escalation] phase=think_only_plan Pro plan empty, bypassing bubble-wrap to allow Flash direct execution");
  3862	        }
  3863	      } catch (planErr: any) {
  3864	        console.warn("[deepseek-escalation] phase=think_only_plan Pro call failed or returned empty, proceeding directly to Flash execution:", planErr?.message || planErr);
  3865	      }
  3866	    }
  3867	    // Tool execution is the cheap deterministic lane. A hard objective may still
  3868	    // use Pro/Astra for a separate bounded reasoning packet, but do not pay Pro
  3869	    // rates merely to choose/call tools and then replay that context repeatedly.
  3870	    const initialDecision = primaryDecision;
  3871	    let providerFailoverDecision: {
  3872	      model: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-v4-pro";
  3873	      thinking: "disabled";
  3874	      reasoning_effort: null;
  3875	    } | null = null;
  3876	    let payload: DeepSeekChatCompletionResponse;
  3877	    try {
  3878	      payload = await callDeepSeek("initial", messages, toolDefinitions, initialDecision);
  3879	    } catch (error) {
  3880	      const flashInitialProviderFailure =
  3881	        interactiveFounderTurn
  3882	        && initialDecision.model === "deepseek-flash"
  3883	        && error instanceof DeepSeekCompanionError
  3884	        && error.statusCode === 502
  3885	        && process.env.GEMINX_ALLOW_PAID_PRO_FAILOVER === "true";
  3886	      if (!flashInitialProviderFailure) throw error;
  3887	
  3888	      // Do not automatically escalate to expensive Pro without explicit Founder model selection
  3889	      const proAllowed = false; // hard founder policy: never fail over tool execution to Pro
  3890	      if (!proAllowed) {
  3891	        throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  3892	          502,
  3893	          "DeepSeek Flash is temporarily unavailable (HTTP 502 Bad Gateway). Paid Pro escalation is held for Founder approval.",
  3894	          "DEEPSEEK_FLASH_502_PRO_HELD"
  3895	        );
  3896	      }
  3897	
  3898	      // Direct Founder chat when Pro is explicitly permitted
  3899	      providerFailoverDecision = {
  3900	        model: "deepseek-v4-pro",
  3901	        thinking: "disabled",
  3902	        reasoning_effort: null
  3903	      };
  3904	      console.warn(
  3905	        `[deepseek-failover] correlation_id=${correlationId ?? conversationId ?? "unbound"} reason=FLASH_INITIAL_PROVIDER_FAILURE route=FOUNDER_INTERACTIVE_PRO_ONCE`
  3906	      );
  3907	      conversation?.onActivity?.({
  3908	        phase: "model",
  3909	        status: "running",
  3910	        label: "Flash provider error · recovering this Founder chat on DeepSeek Pro"
  3911	      });
  3912	      payload = await callDeepSeek(
  3913	        "initial_flash_5xx_failover",
  3914	        messages,
  3915	        toolDefinitions,
  3916	        providerFailoverDecision
  3917	      );
  3918	    }
  3919	    const readToolCache = new Map<string, Awaited<ReturnType<ToolRegistry["execute"]>>>();
  3920	    let forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage: string | null = null;
  3921	    let forcedNextToolName: string | null = null;
  3922	
  3923	    // OpenClaw-style loop control: observe repeated tool + args + RESULT patterns
  3924	    // inside the current run. A changing result is progress and therefore never
  3925	    // increments the same pattern. Warning/critical/global thresholds mirror the
  3926	    // proven OpenClaw defaults (10/20/30).
  3927	    const toolOutcomePatternCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  3928	    const loopRetiredToolNames = new Set<string>();
  3929	    // Stop a permission-denied inspection tool after two real denials rather
  3930	    // than waiting for 20 identical tool+args+result patterns. Different
  3931	    // arguments must not reset the budget or create another paid search loop.
  3932	    const deniedInspectionToolCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  3933	    let repeatedToolOutcomeEvents = 0;
  3934	    const routerOnlyTools = new Set(["geminx_runtime_capabilities", "geminx_tool_search"]);
  3935	    let successfulRuntimeCapabilityReads = 0;
  3936	    let successfulToolSearches = 0;
  3937	    let discoverySearchesSinceConcreteAction = 0;
  3938	    let discoverySearchTemporarilyYielded = false;
  3939	    const maxDiscoverySearchesWithoutConcreteAction = 3;
  3940	    const pendingDiscoveredToolNames = new Set<string>();
  3941	    const activeWorkflowToolNames = new Set<string>();
  3942	    let incompleteTerminalContinuations = 0;
  3943	    const terminalContractRequired = requiresGeminxTerminalContract(intent);
  3944	    const sourceDependencyMapRequested = /\b(?:dependency\s+map|trace\s+(?:the\s+)?runtime\s+authentication\s+middleware|source-backed\s+dependency)\b/i.test(intent);
  3945	    // Extra bounded rounds ONLY for explicit source-map audits, not generic chats.
  3946	    const maxIncompleteTerminalContinuations = sourceDependencyMapRequested ? 3 : 1;
  3947	
  3948	    const directDesktopRun = agentMode === "neo" && isDirectDesktopActionIntent(toolRoutingIntentText(intent));
  3949	    const loopWarningThreshold = directDesktopRun ? 2 : TOOL_LOOP_WARNING_THRESHOLD;
  3950	    const loopCriticalThreshold = directDesktopRun ? 3 : TOOL_LOOP_CRITICAL_THRESHOLD;
  3951	    const loopGlobalBreakerThreshold = directDesktopRun ? 8 : TOOL_LOOP_GLOBAL_BREAKER_THRESHOLD;
  3952	    let observationOnlyStreak = 0;
  3953	    const maxObservationOnlySteps = noProgressObservationBudget(routedTaskType, directDesktopRun, comprehensiveAudit);
  3954	    let observationOnlyFuseTriggered = false;
  3955	    const observedEvidenceFingerprints = new Set<string>();
  3956	    const evidenceTracker = new EvidenceFingerprintTracker();
  3957	    let evidenceProgressCreditsUsed = 0;
  3958	    const maxEvidenceProgressCredits = evidenceProgressCreditLimit(maxObservationOnlySteps);
  3959	    let desktopMutationSucceeded = false;
  3960	    let desktopObservationsBeforeMutation = 0;
  3961	    let desktopObservationsSinceMutation = 0;
  3962	    let desktopPreActionFuseApplied = false;
  3963	    let desktopPostActionFuseApplied = false;
  3964	    const desktopBlockedCounts = new Map<string, number>();
  3965	
  3966	    let toolStepLimit = Math.max(
  3967	      1,
  3968	      Math.min(
  3969	        companionToolStepBudget(routedTaskType, intent, toolDefinitions.map((tool) => tool.function.name)),
  3970	        maxProviderRequestsPerTurn() - 2 // reserve initial request + one terminal finalization
  3971	      )
  3972	    );
  3973	    // Founder interactions get a predictable clean execution budget.
  3974	    // No capability-discovery expansion may consume the reserved final answer.
  3975	    if (interactiveFounderTurn) toolStepLimit = Math.min(toolStepLimit, 16);
  3976	    if (directDesktopRun) {
  3977	      toolStepLimit = directDesktopToolStepBudget(toolStepLimit);
  3978	      console.log(`[deepseek-tools] direct_desktop_budget=${toolStepLimit} reason=bounded_motor_orchestration`);
  3979	    }
  3980	    let mutationVerificationNudges = 0;
  3981	    let permissionCorrectionAttempts = 0;
  3982	    for (let step = 0; step < toolStepLimit; step += 1) {
  3983	      const parsedCalls = parseToolCalls(payload);
  3984	      const currentStepHasConcreteTool = parsedCalls.some((call) => !routerOnlyTools.has(call.name));
  3985	
  3986	      if (parsedCalls.length === 0 || !effectiveToolRegistry) {
  3987	        const candidateMessage = parseMessageContent(payload.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);
  3988	
  3989	        // Silent Internal Verification Nudge (Mutations Only):
  3990	        // Only trigger when a state-mutating tool executed during the current turn without a subsequent read verification.
  3991	        const hasMutations = toolCalls.some(call => call.status === "succeeded" && isLikelyMutationTool(call.tool));
  3992	        const hasVerificationAfterMutation = hasFreshIndependentCompletionEvidence(toolCalls as unknown as AutonomousTerminalToolCall[]);
  3993	        const isMediaOrOfficeRun = Boolean(activeOfficeAgent) || toolCalls.some(call => call.tool.startsWith("runway_") || call.tool.startsWith("google_image_"));
  3994	        const needsMutationVerificationNudge = !isMediaOrOfficeRun && hasMutations && !hasVerificationAfterMutation && mutationVerificationNudges < 1;
  3995	
  3996	        if (needsMutationVerificationNudge && candidateMessage && step < toolStepLimit - 1) {
  3997	          mutationVerificationNudges += 1;
  3998	          messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: candidateMessage });
  3999	          messages.push({
  4000	            role: "system",
  4001	            content:
  4002	              "POST-ACTION VERIFICATION NUDGE: A state mutation occurred during this run. Before completing your turn, perform a read/verification check on the target to confirm the new state, then provide your natural response."
  4003	          });
  4004	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4005	            phase: "verification-nudge",
  4006	            status: "running",
  4007	            label: "Verifying post-mutation state"
  4008	          });
  4009	          const nudgeDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? operatorToolDecision;
  4010	          payload = await callDeepSeek(
  4011	            `mutation_verification_nudge_${mutationVerificationNudges}`,
  4012	            messages,
  4013	            toolDefinitions,
  4014	            nudgeDecision
  4015	          );
  4016	          continue;
  4017	        }
  4018	
  4019	        // Anti-Stall In-Loop Dispatch:
  4020	        // If DeepSeek hesitates with "say go" / "awaiting your go" or confirmation questions
  4021	        // without invoking a tool on an authorised task, correct it immediately and let the loop continue.
  4022	        if (
  4023	          permissionCorrectionAttempts < 1 &&
  4024	          candidateMessage &&
  4025	          isUnsupportedAwaitingGoMessage(candidateMessage) &&
  4026	          step < toolStepLimit - 1
  4027	        ) {
  4028	          permissionCorrectionAttempts += 1;
  4029	          console.log(`[deepseek-tools] in_loop_rejected_unsupported_awaiting_go correlation_id=${correlationId ?? "unbound"}`);
  4030	          messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: candidateMessage });
  4031	          messages.push({
  4032	            role: "system",
  4033	            content:
  4034	              "FOUNDER_DIRECTIVE: This objective has already been authorised by the Founder. " +
  4035	              "Do not ask for confirmation, output holding phrases, or claim governed holds. " +
  4036	              "You must select and invoke the required concrete tool now to execute or inspect the task, or report verified findings in plain words."
  4037	          });
  4038	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4039	            phase: "anti-stall",
  4040	            status: "running",
  4041	            label: "Authorised objective active · proceeding with required tool dispatch instead of awaiting confirmation"
  4042	          });
  4043	          const correctiveDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? operatorToolDecision;
  4044	          payload = await callDeepSeek("corrective_action_dispatch", messages, toolDefinitions, correctiveDecision);
  4045	          continue;
  4046	        }
  4047	
  4048	        const missingSourceDependencyMap = sourceDependencyMapRequested && !hasSourceBackedDependencyMap(candidateMessage);
  4049	        const canContinueIncompleteTerminal =
  4050	          parsedCalls.length === 0 &&
  4051	          Boolean(candidateMessage) &&
  4052	          Boolean(effectiveToolRegistry) &&
  4053	          ((terminalContractRequired && !hasGeminxTerminalMarker(candidateMessage)) || missingSourceDependencyMap) &&
  4054	          incompleteTerminalContinuations < maxIncompleteTerminalContinuations &&
  4055	          providerRequestOrdinal < maxProviderRequestsPerTurn() - 1;
  4056	
  4057	        if (canContinueIncompleteTerminal) {
  4058	          if (candidateMessage) {
  4059	            messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: candidateMessage });
  4060	          }
  4061	          incompleteTerminalContinuations += 1;
  4062	          messages.push({
  4063	            role: "user",
  4064	            content: missingSourceDependencyMap
  4065	              ? "The requested source-backed dependency map is still incomplete. Preserve the SAME objective and actual tool receipts. " +
  4066	                "Inspect the missing source definitions, imports and route consumers using authorised tools. " +
  4067	                "Report at least three distinct real source paths with line numbers and how the middleware relationships connect. " +
  4068	                "Do not invent locations or call preliminary discoveries a finished map. If a real access boundary prevents completion, report it explicitly."
  4069	              : "Continue from the CURRENT transcript and tool receipts. Do not restart the objective, do not repeat the original request, " +
  4070	                "and do not rerun completed tools unless fresh evidence is genuinely required. If more governed action is required, use the available tools now. " +
  4071	                "Otherwise produce the final concise result and end with exactly one terminal marker on its own line: " +
  4072	                "COMPLETED_PROVEN, BLOCKED_REAL_BOUNDARY, or FAILED_VERIFICATION."
  4073	          });
  4074	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4075	            phase: "continuation",
  4076	            status: "running",
  4077	            label: `Incomplete terminal turn · continuing inside the same run (${incompleteTerminalContinuations}/${maxIncompleteTerminalContinuations})`
  4078	          });
  4079	          const continuationDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? operatorToolDecision;
  4080	          payload = await callDeepSeek(
  4081	            `incomplete_terminal_continue_${incompleteTerminalContinuations}`,
  4082	            messages,
  4083	            toolDefinitions,
  4084	            continuationDecision
  4085	          );
  4086	          continue;
  4087	        }
  4088	        break;
  4089	      }
  4090	
  4091	      // Append the assistant message (including its tool_calls) so the chain stays coherent.
  4092	      const assistantMessage = payload.choices?.[0]?.message;
  4093	      messages.push(toolContinuationAssistantMessage(assistantMessage));
  4094	      persistExecutionCheckpoint("PENDING_TOOL", parsedCalls[0]?.id ?? null);
  4095	
  4096	      // Execute each requested tool through the canonical registry. Identical
  4097	      // read-only probes are reused inside one turn until a mutation invalidates
  4098	      // the cache. This stops Flash from burning rounds re-reading the same GitHub
  4099	      // or Creator state while preserving fresh verification after real actions.
  4100	      const allCallsSafeParallel =
  4101	        parsedCalls.length > 1 &&
  4102	        parsedCalls.every((call) => !call.malformed && isSafeParallelReadTool(call.name) && !loopRetiredToolNames.has(call.name));
  4103	
  4104	      const parallelFetchMap = new Map<string, ToolInvocationResult>();
  4105	      if (allCallsSafeParallel && effectiveToolRegistry) {
  4106	        console.log(
  4107	          `[deepseek-tools] parallel_read_wave count=${parsedCalls.length} tools=${parsedCalls.map((c) => c.name).join(",")}`
  4108	        );
  4109	        const settled = await Promise.allSettled(
  4110	          parsedCalls.map(async (call) => {
  4111	            const effectiveArgs = call.name === "geminx_schedule_message"
  4112	              ? { ...call.args, conversation_id: resolveScheduledFounderConversationId(conversation, conversationId) }
  4113	              : call.args;
  4114	            const cacheKey = toolReadCacheKey(call.name, effectiveArgs);
  4115	            const cached = cacheKey ? readToolCache.get(cacheKey) : undefined;
  4116	            if (cached) {
  4117	              return {
  4118	                id: call.id,
  4119	                result: { ...cached, tool_call_id: call.id, correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id }
  4120	              };
  4121	            }
  4122	            const res = await effectiveToolRegistry.execute({
  4123	              tool_call_id: call.id,
  4124	              correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4125	              tool: call.name,
  4126	              args: effectiveArgs
  4127	            });
  4128	            return { id: call.id, result: res };
  4129	          })
  4130	        );
  4131	        for (const item of settled) {
  4132	          if (item.status === "fulfilled") {
  4133	            parallelFetchMap.set(item.value.id, item.value.result);
  4134	          }
  4135	        }
  4136	      }
  4137	
  4138	      for (const call of parsedCalls) {
  4139	        if (call.malformed) {
  4140	          const now = new Date().toISOString();
  4141	          const malformedResult = {
  4142	            ok: false,
  4143	            status: "blocked" as const,
  4144	            tool_call_id: call.id,
  4145	            correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4146	            tool: call.name,
  4147	            status_code: 400,
  4148	            blocked_reason: "TOOL_ARGUMENTS_MALFORMED_JSON",
  4149	            error: `TOOL_ARGUMENTS_MALFORMED_JSON: ${call.parse_error ?? "Invalid JSON arguments"}`,
  4150	            started_at: now,
  4151	            finished_at: now,
  4152	            duration_ms: 0,
  4153	            schema_validated: false
  4154	          };
  4155	          toolCalls.push({
  4156	            tool_call_id: call.id,
  4157	            correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4158	            tool: call.name,
  4159	            action: call.name,
  4160	            args: call.args,
  4161	            status: "blocked",
  4162	            started_at: now,
  4163	            finished_at: now,
  4164	            result: null,
  4165	            error: malformedResult.error
  4166	          });
  4167	          messages.push({
  4168	            role: "tool",
  4169	            tool_call_id: call.id,
  4170	            content: compactToolReceiptForModel(malformedResult)
  4171	          });
  4172	          messages.push({
  4173	            role: "system",
  4174	            content: `TOOL_ARGUMENTS_MALFORMED_JSON: The arguments for ${call.name} were not valid JSON (${call.parse_error ?? "syntax error"}). Provide well-formed JSON arguments or continue from current receipts.`
  4175	          });
  4176	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4177	            phase: "tool-loop",
  4178	            status: "blocked",
  4179	            label: `Blocked malformed tool call · ${call.name}`,
  4180	            tool: call.name
  4181	          });
  4182	          continue;
  4183	        }
  4184	
  4185	        const effectiveArgs = call.name === "geminx_schedule_message"
  4186	          ? { ...call.args, conversation_id: resolveScheduledFounderConversationId(conversation, conversationId) }
  4187	          : call.args;
  4188	
  4189	        // A retired tool is a hard runtime boundary, including for AG mode.
  4190	        // Models can repeat a previously seen tool name from transcript history,
  4191	        // so refuse it here before any adapter call.
  4192	        if (loopRetiredToolNames.has(call.name)) {
  4193	          const now = new Date().toISOString();
  4194	          const retiredResult = {
  4195	            ok: false,
  4196	            status: "blocked" as const,
  4197	            tool_call_id: call.id,
  4198	            correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4199	            tool: call.name,
  4200	            status_code: 409,
  4201	            blocked_reason: "TOOL_RETIRED_NO_PROGRESS",
  4202	            error: `TOOL_RETIRED_NO_PROGRESS: ${call.name} was retired for this run after repeated or redundant desktop probing.`,
  4203	            started_at: now,
  4204	            finished_at: now,
  4205	            duration_ms: 0,
  4206	            schema_validated: true
  4207	          };
  4208	          toolCalls.push({
  4209	            tool_call_id: call.id,
  4210	            correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4211	            tool: call.name,
  4212	            action: call.name,
  4213	            args: effectiveArgs,
  4214	            status: "blocked",
  4215	            started_at: now,
  4216	            finished_at: now,
  4217	            result: null,
  4218	            error: retiredResult.error
  4219	          });
  4220	          messages.push({
  4221	            role: "tool",
  4222	            tool_call_id: call.id,
  4223	            content: compactToolReceiptForModel(retiredResult)
  4224	          });
  4225	          messages.push({
  4226	            role: "system",
  4227	            content: `TOOL_RETIRED_NO_PROGRESS: ${call.name} is closed for this run. Do not ask for it again. Advance with an available concrete action, one targeted verification probe, or finish from current receipts.`
  4228	          });
  4229	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4230	            phase: "tool-loop",
  4231	            status: "blocked",
  4232	            label: `Skipped retired no-progress tool · ${call.name}`,
  4233	            tool: call.name
  4234	          });
  4235	          observationOnlyStreak = nextObservationOnlyStreak(
  4236	            observationOnlyStreak,
  4237	            false,
  4238	            false,
  4239	            false,
  4240	            false
  4241	          );
  4242	          observationOnlyFuseTriggered = observationOnlyStreak >= maxObservationOnlySteps;
  4243	          continue;
  4244	        }
  4245	
  4246	        const cacheKey = toolReadCacheKey(call.name, effectiveArgs);
  4247	        const cached = cacheKey ? readToolCache.get(cacheKey) : undefined;
  4248	
  4249	        conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4250	          phase: "tool",
  4251	          status: "running",
  4252	          label: cached ? `Reusing current read · ${call.name}` : readableToolActivity(call.name, effectiveArgs),
  4253	          tool: call.name
  4254	        });
  4255	
  4256	        // Persist intent-to-dispatch BEFORE handing control to any adapter. A
  4257	        // process death before a corresponding receipt is an unknown outcome,
  4258	        // never permission to replay the mutation.
  4259	        if (!cached) persistExecutionCheckpoint("PENDING_TOOL", call.id);
  4260	        const rawResult = parallelFetchMap.get(call.id)
  4261	          ? parallelFetchMap.get(call.id)!
  4262	          : cached
  4263	          ? {
  4264	              ...cached,
  4265	              tool_call_id: call.id,
  4266	              correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4267	              started_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  4268	              finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  4269	              duration_ms: 0
  4270	            }
  4271	          : await effectiveToolRegistry.execute({
  4272	              tool_call_id: call.id,
  4273	              correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4274	              tool: call.name,
  4275	              args: effectiveArgs
  4276	            });
  4277	        const validated_tool_receipt = validateToolResultSchema({
  4278	          tool_call_id: call.id,
  4279	          correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4280	          tool: call.name,
  4281	          args: effectiveArgs
  4282	        }, rawResult);
  4283	        const result = validated_tool_receipt;
  4284	
  4285	        // A different successful read or fresh capability schema is not
  4286	        // necessarily forward progress. Without this semantic budget, Flash
  4287	        // cycled through 20 paid requests while rotating read-only GitHub
  4288	        // searches and never reaching the user's requested action.
  4289	        const evidenceRecord =
  4290	          result.ok && cacheKey !== null
  4291	            ? evidenceTracker.recordRead(call.name, effectiveArgs, result.data)
  4292	            : null;
  4293	        const evidenceFingerprint =
  4294	          result.ok && cacheKey !== null
  4295	            ? evidenceProgressFingerprint(call.name, cacheKey, result.data)
  4296	            : null;
  4297	        const newBoundedEvidence =
  4298	          Boolean(evidenceFingerprint) &&
  4299	          (!observedEvidenceFingerprints.has(evidenceFingerprint as string) || Boolean(evidenceRecord?.isNewEvidence)) &&
  4300	          evidenceProgressCreditsUsed < maxEvidenceProgressCredits;
  4301	        if (evidenceFingerprint) observedEvidenceFingerprints.add(evidenceFingerprint);
  4302	        if (newBoundedEvidence) evidenceProgressCreditsUsed += 1;
  4303	        observationOnlyStreak = nextObservationOnlyStreak(
  4304	          observationOnlyStreak,
  4305	          routerOnlyTools.has(call.name),
  4306	          cacheKey !== null,
  4307	          result.ok,
  4308	          newBoundedEvidence
  4309	        );
  4310	        // Polling an asynchronous video render job is awaiting a GPU completion, not a stagnant read loop.
  4311	        if (call.name === "runway_task_status") {
  4312	          observationOnlyStreak = 0;
  4313	        }
  4314	        // A subsequent verified action in the SAME multi-tool response clears
  4315	        // the observation streak; never block a real mutation merely because
  4316	        // earlier reads in that batch reached the temporary threshold.
  4317	        observationOnlyFuseTriggered = observationOnlyStreak >= maxObservationOnlySteps;
  4318	
  4319	        const toolLoopSystemMessages: string[] = [];
  4320	        if (directDesktopRun) {
  4321	          const observation = DIRECT_DESKTOP_OBSERVATION_TOOLS.has(call.name);
  4322	          const mutation = DIRECT_DESKTOP_MUTATION_TOOLS.has(call.name);
  4323	
  4324	          if (mutation && result.ok) {
  4325	            desktopMutationSucceeded = true;
  4326	            desktopObservationsSinceMutation = 0;
  4327	            desktopPostActionFuseApplied = false;
  4328	          }
  4329	
  4330	          if (observation) {
  4331	            if (result.ok) {
  4332	              if (desktopMutationSucceeded) desktopObservationsSinceMutation += 1;
  4333	              else desktopObservationsBeforeMutation += 1;
  4334	
  4335	              // Connection/runtime status is a one-shot preflight in a motor task.
  4336	              // Re-reading it does not prove visible desktop progress.
  4337	              if (call.name === "system_status") {
  4338	                loopRetiredToolNames.add(call.name);
  4339	                toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter((tool) => tool.function.name !== call.name);
  4340	                toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4341	                  "DIRECT_DESKTOP_STATUS_ONE_SHOT: system_status succeeded and is now retired for this run. Do not recheck connectivity unless a later tool receipt explicitly proves the host disconnected."
  4342	                );
  4343	              }
  4344	            } else if (result.status === "blocked" || result.status === "failed") {
  4345	              const blockedCount = (desktopBlockedCounts.get(call.name) ?? 0) + 1;
  4346	              desktopBlockedCounts.set(call.name, blockedCount);
  4347	              const isDeterministicAuthDenial =
  4348	                /PATH_OUTSIDE_AUTHORISED_WORKSPACE|EXECUTABLE_NOT_AUTHORISED|SCHEDULED_TASK_NOT_AUTHORISED/i.test(
  4349	                  String(result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? "")
  4350	                );
  4351	              if (isDeterministicAuthDenial) {
  4352	                const denialReason = String(result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? "AUTHORISATION_DENIED");
  4353	                for (const name of ["system_file_write", "system_file_edit", "system_file_read", "system_shell_exec"]) {
  4354	                  loopRetiredToolNames.add(name);
  4355	                }
  4356	                toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4357	                  (t) => !["system_file_write", "system_file_edit", "system_file_read", "system_shell_exec"].includes(t.function.name)
  4358	                );
  4359	                toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4360	                  `DETERMINISTIC_AUTHORISATION_DENIED: ${denialReason}. This action is outside the authorized policy boundary. Do NOT attempt to circumvent this restriction using shell execution, alternate file tools, or repetitive retries. Declare the boundary and report the required permission to Rob immediately.`
  4361	                );
  4362	              } else if (blockedCount >= 2) {
  4363	                loopRetiredToolNames.add(call.name);
  4364	                toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter((tool) => tool.function.name !== call.name);
  4365	                toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4366	                  `DIRECT_DESKTOP_BLOCKED_TOOL_RETIRED: ${call.name} has failed or blocked twice in this desktop run. Do not retry it. Use another already-exposed desktop path or finish from current evidence.`
  4367	                );
  4368	              }
  4369	            }
  4370	
  4371	            if (
  4372	              !desktopMutationSucceeded &&
  4373	              !desktopPreActionFuseApplied &&
  4374	              desktopObservationsBeforeMutation >= 3
  4375	            ) {
  4376	              desktopPreActionFuseApplied = true;
  4377	              for (const name of DIRECT_DESKTOP_BROAD_OBSERVATION_TOOLS) loopRetiredToolNames.add(name);
  4378	              toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4379	                (tool) => !DIRECT_DESKTOP_BROAD_OBSERVATION_TOOLS.has(tool.function.name)
  4380	              );
  4381	              toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4382	                "DIRECT_DESKTOP_PREACTION_FUSE: three successful desktop observations are enough to choose an action. Broad status/windows/snapshot/UIA-scan tools are retired until this run ends. Perform the required motor action, use one targeted UIA find or screenshot if genuinely needed, or finish."
  4383	              );
  4384	              conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4385	                phase: "tool-loop",
  4386	                status: "running",
  4387	                label: "Enough desktop context gathered · action required",
  4388	                tool: call.name
  4389	              });
  4390	            }
  4391	
  4392	            if (
  4393	              desktopMutationSucceeded &&
  4394	              !desktopPostActionFuseApplied &&
  4395	              desktopObservationsSinceMutation >= 2
  4396	            ) {
  4397	              desktopPostActionFuseApplied = true;
  4398	              for (const name of DIRECT_DESKTOP_BROAD_OBSERVATION_TOOLS) loopRetiredToolNames.add(name);
  4399	              toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4400	                (tool) => !DIRECT_DESKTOP_BROAD_OBSERVATION_TOOLS.has(tool.function.name)
  4401	              );
  4402	              toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4403	                "DIRECT_DESKTOP_PROGRESS_FUSE: a real desktop action succeeded and two fresh observations followed it. Do not restart diagnosis or re-list broad desktop state. Perform the next required motor action, use one targeted UIA find or screenshot, or finish from the receipts already present."
  4404	              );
  4405	              conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4406	                phase: "tool-loop",
  4407	                status: "running",
  4408	                label: "Desktop action verified · advancing instead of rechecking",
  4409	                tool: call.name
  4410	              });
  4411	            }
  4412	          }
  4413	
  4414	          if (call.name === "system_shell_exec" && result.ok) {
  4415	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4416	              "DIRECT_DESKTOP_SHELL_TRUTH: shell success is supporting evidence only. It does not count as visible desktop progress. If the objective is graphical, verify or continue with the specific desktop motor/UI tools rather than restarting broad diagnostics."
  4417	            );
  4418	          }
  4419	        }
  4420	
  4421	        const toolFailureDetail = String(
  4422	          result.error ??
  4423	          result.blocked_reason ??
  4424	          (result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && "error" in (result.data as Record<string, unknown>)
  4425	            ? (result.data as Record<string, unknown>).error
  4426	            : "") ??
  4427	          ""
  4428	        );
  4429	        const isToolFailureOrBlocked = !result.ok || result.status === "failed" || result.status === "blocked";
  4430	
  4431	        if (isToolFailureOrBlocked) {
  4432	          // GitHub / Railway scope failures are deterministic until credentials
  4433	          // change. Do not spend dozens of provider calls repeatedly finding
  4434	          // a new spelling of a repository or deployment lookup. Retire only
  4435	          // this inspection operation, never another operation or a writer.
  4436	          const deniedInspectionTool =
  4437	            /^(?:github_(?:get|list|read|search|repo_map|file_status)|railway_(?:get|list|status|inspect))/.test(call.name) &&
  4438	            [401, 403].includes(Number(result.status_code));
  4439	          if (deniedInspectionTool) {
  4440	            const attempts = (deniedInspectionToolCounts.get(call.name) ?? 0) + 1;
  4441	            deniedInspectionToolCounts.set(call.name, attempts);
  4442	            if (attempts >= 2) {
  4443	              loopRetiredToolNames.add(call.name);
  4444	              toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter((tool) => tool.function.name !== call.name);
  4445	              toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4446	                `INSPECTION_AUTH_DENIED: ${call.name} returned authorization denial ${attempts} times. This operation is retired for the current run. Do not repeat it with alternative arguments; use an already-authorized operation or report the actual permission boundary. No code patch or token is authorized by this error.`
  4447	              );
  4448	              console.warn(
  4449	                `[deepseek-tools] inspect_permission_fuse tool=${call.name} count=${attempts} code=${result.status_code} retired=true`
  4450	              );
  4451	            }
  4452	          }
  4453	          const railwayScopedAuthDenied =
  4454	            call.name.startsWith("railway_") &&
  4455	            /RAILWAY_GRAPHQL_ERROR:[^\\n]*(?:Not Authorized|Unauthori[sz]ed)|RAILWAY_HTTP_(?:401|403)\\b|RAILWAY_AUTH_REJECTED/i.test(toolFailureDetail);
  4456	          if (railwayScopedAuthDenied) {
  4457	            // The Railway adapter already tried both configured read-only headers
  4458	            // within this SINGLE invocation. Another tool name/arg pair cannot
  4459	            // change the credential's project scope, and burns model credits.
  4460	            for (const tool of toolDefinitions) {
  4461	              if (/^railway_(?:get|list|status|inspect)/.test(tool.function.name)) {
  4462	                loopRetiredToolNames.add(tool.function.name);
  4463	              }
  4464	            }
  4465	            toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4466	              tool => !/^railway_(?:get|list|status|inspect)/.test(tool.function.name)
  4467	            );
  4468	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4469	              "RAILWAY_SCOPED_AUTH_DENIED: live Railway returned Not Authorized despite the adapter's bounded read-only credential fallback. " +
  4470	              "Do NOT retry Railway with different lookup arguments or claim authenticated deployment evidence. " +
  4471	              "Continue independent GitHub, browser and source checks in this SAME objective. " +
  4472	              "A real Railway permission repair requires checking the scoped credential against the exact existing project; never request secrets in chat."
  4473	            );
  4474	            console.warn(`[deepseek-tools] railway_auth_scope_fuse correlation_id=${correlationId ?? "unbound"} read_tools_retired=true`);
  4475	          }
  4476	          if (call.name === "github_read_file_range" &&
  4477	              /owner, repo and path are required/.test(toolFailureDetail)) {
  4478	            const badArgs = toolCalls.filter(entry =>
  4479	              entry.tool === call.name &&
  4480	              /owner, repo and path are required/.test(String(entry.error ?? ""))).length;
  4481	            if (badArgs >= 2) {
  4482	              loopRetiredToolNames.add(call.name);
  4483	              toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(tool => tool.function.name !== call.name);
  4484	              toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4485	                "GITHUB_FILE_ARGUMENTS_INVALID: this file-range tool was attempted twice without explicit owner, repo and path. " +
  4486	                "Do not repeat an empty call. Continue using github_repo_map or github_get_repository with valid explicit owner/repo, " +
  4487	                "or use a known exact file path through github_read_file. Never invent an unknown file path."
  4488	              );
  4489	              console.warn(`[deepseek-tools] github_file_args_fuse correlation_id=${correlationId ?? "unbound"} retired=true`);
  4490	            }
  4491	          }
  4492	          const isSystemHostDisconnected =
  4493	            (call.name.startsWith("system_") || call.name.startsWith("desktop_")) &&
  4494	            /system (?:operator )?host .* is not connected|founder windows system operator is not connected|system host.*not connected|not connected/i.test(toolFailureDetail);
  4495	
  4496	          if (isSystemHostDisconnected) {
  4497	            for (const tool of toolDefinitions) {
  4498	              if (tool.function.name.startsWith("system_") || tool.function.name.startsWith("desktop_")) {
  4499	                loopRetiredToolNames.add(tool.function.name);
  4500	              }
  4501	            }
  4502	            if (effectiveToolRegistry) {
  4503	              for (const def of effectiveToolRegistry.listTools()) {
  4504	                if (def.name.startsWith("system_") || def.name.startsWith("desktop_")) {
  4505	                  loopRetiredToolNames.add(def.name);
  4506	                }
  4507	              }
  4508	            }
  4509	            for (const name of [...activeWorkflowToolNames]) {
  4510	              if (name.startsWith("system_") || name.startsWith("desktop_")) {
  4511	                activeWorkflowToolNames.delete(name);
  4512	              }
  4513	            }
  4514	            toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4515	              (t) => !t.function.name.startsWith("system_") && !t.function.name.startsWith("desktop_")
  4516	            );
  4517	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4518	              "SYSTEM_HOST_DISCONNECTED_CIRCUIT_BREAKER: The Windows System Operator host is not connected. All system_* and desktop_* tools have been retired for this turn. Do NOT attempt to invoke local system or desktop commands again in this run. " +
  4519	              "AUTONOMOUS PIVOT: If this task involves checking git, auditing files, or code changes, use GitHub tools (github_read_file, github_create_or_update_file, etc.) directly.   4520	            );
  4521	            conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4522	              phase: "tool-loop",
  4523	              status: "blocked",
  4524	              label: "System host disconnected · fenced system tools and pivoted",
  4525	              tool: call.name
  4526	            });
  4527	          }
  4528	
  4529	          const isBrowserHostDisconnected =
  4530	            call.name.startsWith("browser_") &&
  4531	            /browser (?:operator )?host .* is not connected|browser host.*not connected|not connected/i.test(toolFailureDetail);
  4532	
  4533	          if (isBrowserHostDisconnected) {
  4534	            for (const tool of toolDefinitions) {
  4535	              if (tool.function.name.startsWith("browser_")) {
  4536	                loopRetiredToolNames.add(tool.function.name);
  4537	              }
  4538	            }
  4539	            if (effectiveToolRegistry) {
  4540	              for (const def of effectiveToolRegistry.listTools()) {
  4541	                if (def.name.startsWith("browser_")) {
  4542	                  loopRetiredToolNames.add(def.name);
  4543	                }
  4544	              }
  4545	            }
  4546	            for (const name of [...activeWorkflowToolNames]) {
  4547	              if (name.startsWith("browser_")) {
  4548	                activeWorkflowToolNames.delete(name);
  4549	              }
  4550	            }
  4551	            toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter((t) => !t.function.name.startsWith("browser_"));
  4552	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4553	              "BROWSER_HOST_DISCONNECTED_CIRCUIT_BREAKER: The Browser Operator host is not connected. All browser_* tools have been retired for this turn. Do NOT retry browser tools. Conclude or pivot using other available capabilities."
  4554	            );
  4555	            conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4556	              phase: "tool-loop",
  4557	              status: "blocked",
  4558	              label: "Browser host disconnected · fenced browser tools",
  4559	              tool: call.name
  4560	            });
  4561	          }
  4562	
  4563	          const isChromeBridgeDisconnected =
  4564	            call.name.startsWith("chrome_") &&
  4565	            /chrome bridge .* is not connected|chrome bridge.*not connected|not connected/i.test(toolFailureDetail);
  4566	
  4567	          if (isChromeBridgeDisconnected) {
  4568	            for (const tool of toolDefinitions) {
  4569	              if (tool.function.name.startsWith("chrome_")) {
  4570	                loopRetiredToolNames.add(tool.function.name);
  4571	              }
  4572	            }
  4573	            toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter((t) => !t.function.name.startsWith("chrome_"));
  4574	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4575	              "CHROME_BRIDGE_DISCONNECTED_CIRCUIT_BREAKER: The Chrome Bridge extension is not connected. All chrome_* tools have been retired for this turn. Use browser_* tools or report the boundary."
  4576	            );
  4577	          }
  4578	
  4579	          const isUniversalAuthDenial =
  4580	            /PATH_OUTSIDE_AUTHORISED_WORKSPACE|EXECUTABLE_NOT_AUTHORISED|SCHEDULED_TASK_NOT_AUTHORISED/i.test(toolFailureDetail);
  4581	
  4582	          if (isUniversalAuthDenial) {
  4583	            for (const name of ["system_file_write", "system_file_edit", "system_file_read", "system_shell_exec"]) {
  4584	              loopRetiredToolNames.add(name);
  4585	            }
  4586	            toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4587	              (t) => !["system_file_write", "system_file_edit", "system_file_read", "system_shell_exec"].includes(t.function.name)
  4588	            );
  4589	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4590	              `DETERMINISTIC_AUTHORISATION_DENIED: ${toolFailureDetail}. This action is outside the authorized policy boundary. Do NOT attempt to circumvent this restriction using shell execution, alternate file tools, or repetitive retries. Declare the boundary immediately.`
  4591	            );
  4592	          }
  4593	        }
  4594	
  4595	        const outcomePattern = createHash("sha256")
  4596	          .update(JSON.stringify({
  4597	            tool: call.name,
  4598	            args: effectiveArgs,
  4599	            status: result.status,
  4600	            result: boundedProviderPreview(result.data ?? result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? null, 1_200)
  4601	          }))
  4602	          .digest("hex")
  4603	          .slice(0, 24);
  4604	        const outcomePatternCount = (toolOutcomePatternCounts.get(outcomePattern) ?? 0) + 1;
  4605	        toolOutcomePatternCounts.set(outcomePattern, outcomePatternCount);
  4606	        if (outcomePatternCount > 1) repeatedToolOutcomeEvents += 1;
  4607	
  4608	        if (outcomePatternCount === loopWarningThreshold) {
  4609	          toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4610	            `TOOL_LOOP_WARNING: the exact tool+args+result pattern for ${call.name} has repeated ${outcomePatternCount} times without state change. ` +
  4611	            "Do not repeat that same action unchanged. Change the hypothesis, arguments or tool path, or finish from existing evidence."
  4612	          );
  4613	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4614	            phase: "tool-loop",
  4615	            status: "running",
  4616	            label: `Repeated no-progress tool pattern detected (${outcomePatternCount}/${loopCriticalThreshold}) · changing strategy`,
  4617	            tool: call.name
  4618	          });
  4619	        }
  4620	
  4621	        if (outcomePatternCount >= loopCriticalThreshold) {
  4622	          loopRetiredToolNames.add(call.name);
  4623	          toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter((tool) => tool.function.name !== call.name);
  4624	          toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4625	            `TOOL_LOOP_CRITICAL: ${call.name} produced the same result for the same arguments ${outcomePatternCount} times. ` +
  4626	            "That tool is retired for the remainder of this run. Use a materially different governed path or produce the terminal answer from evidence already gathered."
  4627	          );
  4628	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4629	            phase: "tool-loop",
  4630	            status: "blocked",
  4631	            label: `Retired repeated no-progress tool for this run (${outcomePatternCount} identical outcomes)`,
  4632	            tool: call.name
  4633	          });
  4634	        }
  4635	
  4636	        if (repeatedToolOutcomeEvents >= loopGlobalBreakerThreshold) {
  4637	          toolDefinitions = [];
  4638	          toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4639	            `TOOL_LOOP_GLOBAL_BREAKER: ${repeatedToolOutcomeEvents} repeated no-progress outcome events occurred in this run. ` +
  4640	            "All further tool execution is closed for this run. Produce the terminal result using the receipts already gathered; do not request another tool."
  4641	          );
  4642	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4643	            phase: "tool-loop",
  4644	            status: "blocked",
  4645	            label: `Global no-progress tool breaker opened (${repeatedToolOutcomeEvents}/${loopGlobalBreakerThreshold})`
  4646	          });
  4647	        }
  4648	
  4649	        // A successful capability manifest is not a terminal observation. It is an
  4650	        // execution router. Expose the canonical source-inspection and Newton connector
  4651	        // bridge actions immediately so the next model step can ACT on capability gaps
  4652	        // instead of asking the Founder to choose a repo/service or narrating that a
  4653	        // provider is not natively configured. This mirrors OpenClaw's proven pattern:
  4654	        // status -> choose available executor -> continue same objective.
  4655	        if (call.name === "geminx_runtime_capabilities" && result.ok && effectiveToolRegistry) {
  4656	          successfulRuntimeCapabilityReads += 1;
  4657	          toolDefinitions = progressivelyDiscloseToolSchemas(
  4658	            toolDefinitions,
  4659	            effectiveToolRegistry,
  4660	            {
  4661	              matches: [
  4662	                "office_digital_ambush_run",
  4663	                "office_digital_ambush_batch",
  4664	                "office_social_blitz_run",
  4665	                "zoho_send",
  4666	                "system_health",
  4667	                "newton_connector_delegate",
  4668	                "newton_connector_status"
  4669	              ].map((name) => ({ name }))
  4670	            },
  4671	            maxToolDefinitionsForObjective,
  4672	            new Set([
  4673	              "geminx_tool_search",
  4674	              ...(successfulRuntimeCapabilityReads === 0 ? ["geminx_runtime_capabilities"] : []),
  4675	              ...objectivePinnedToolNames,
  4676	              ...pendingDiscoveredToolNames,
  4677	              ...activeWorkflowToolNames,
  4678	              ...toolCalls.slice(-4).map((entry) => entry.tool).filter((name) => !routerOnlyTools.has(name))
  4679	            ])
  4680	          );
  4681	          const expandedToolStepLimit = Math.max(
  4682	            1,
  4683	            Math.min(
  4684	              companionToolStepBudget(routedTaskType, intent, toolDefinitions.map((tool) => tool.function.name)),
  4685	              maxProviderRequestsPerTurn() - 2
  4686	            )
  4687	          );
  4688	          if (expandedToolStepLimit > toolStepLimit && !interactiveFounderTurn) {
  4689	            console.log(
  4690	              `[deepseek-tools] expanded_tool_budget from=${toolStepLimit} to=${expandedToolStepLimit} reason=capability_manifest_action_routes`
  4691	            );
  4692	            toolStepLimit = expandedToolStepLimit;
  4693	          }
  4694	          // The capability manifest is stable for this semantic turn. Retire it
  4695	          // after the first successful read so Flash cannot burn the turn repeatedly
  4696	          // asking the runtime what it already proved. One initial search hop is still
  4697	          // forced when the manifest was the only observation.
  4698	          if (successfulRuntimeCapabilityReads >= 1) {
  4699	            toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4700	              (tool) => tool.function.name !== "geminx_runtime_capabilities"
  4701	            );
  4702	          }
  4703	          // Capability discovery must remain model-directed. A successful
  4704	          // manifest read may disclose concrete routes, but it must never force a
  4705	          // second meta-tool hop. Forcing geminx_tool_search here caused harmless
  4706	          // Founder tasks to stall in discovery/continuation loops and waste
  4707	          // provider requests even when concrete tools were already available.
  4708	          if (
  4709	            !currentStepHasConcreteTool &&
  4710	            successfulToolSearches === 0 &&
  4711	            toolDefinitions.some((tool) => tool.function.name === "geminx_tool_search")
  4712	          ) {
  4713	            forcedNextToolName = null;
  4714	            console.log(
  4715	              `[deepseek-tools] next_tool_choice=auto reason=capability_manifest_disclosed_routes`
  4716	            );
  4717	          }
  4718	        }
  4719	
  4720	        if (call.name === "geminx_tool_search" && result.ok && effectiveToolRegistry) {
  4721	          successfulToolSearches += 1;
  4722	          discoverySearchesSinceConcreteAction += 1;
  4723	
  4724	          const discoveredToolNames = new Set(
  4725	            (
  4726	              result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(result.data) &&
  4727	              Array.isArray((result.data as Record<string, unknown>).matches)
  4728	                ? (result.data as Record<string, unknown>).matches as unknown[]
  4729	                : []
  4730	            )
  4731	              .map((row) =>
  4732	                row && typeof row === "object" && !Array.isArray(row)
  4733	                  ? String((row as Record<string, unknown>).name ?? "")
  4734	                  : ""
  4735	              )
  4736	              .filter(Boolean)
  4737	              .slice(0, 6)
  4738	          );
  4739	          const registeredToolNames = new Set<string>(
  4740	            effectiveToolRegistry.listTools().map((definition) => String(definition.name))
  4741	          );
  4742	          for (const name of discoveredToolNames) {
  4743	            if (!routerOnlyTools.has(name) && registeredToolNames.has(name)) {
  4744	              pendingDiscoveredToolNames.add(name);
  4745	            }
  4746	          }
  4747	
  4748	          toolDefinitions = progressivelyDiscloseToolSchemas(
  4749	            toolDefinitions,
  4750	            effectiveToolRegistry,
  4751	            result.data,
  4752	            maxToolDefinitionsForObjective,
  4753	            new Set([
  4754	              "geminx_tool_search",
  4755	              ...(successfulRuntimeCapabilityReads === 0 ? ["geminx_runtime_capabilities"] : []),
  4756	              ...objectivePinnedToolNames,
  4757	              ...pendingDiscoveredToolNames,
  4758	              ...activeWorkflowToolNames,
  4759	              ...toolCalls.slice(-4).map((entry) => entry.tool).filter((name) => !routerOnlyTools.has(name))
  4760	            ])
  4761	          );
  4762	          const expandedToolStepLimit = Math.max(
  4763	            1,
  4764	            Math.min(
  4765	              companionToolStepBudget(routedTaskType, intent, toolDefinitions.map((tool) => tool.function.name)),
  4766	              maxProviderRequestsPerTurn() - 2
  4767	            )
  4768	          );
  4769	          if (expandedToolStepLimit > toolStepLimit && !interactiveFounderTurn) {
  4770	            console.log(
  4771	              `[deepseek-tools] expanded_tool_budget from=${toolStepLimit} to=${expandedToolStepLimit} reason=progressive_capability_discovery`
  4772	            );
  4773	            toolStepLimit = expandedToolStepLimit;
  4774	          }
  4775	          const searchData =
  4776	            result.data && typeof result.data === "object" && !Array.isArray(result.data)
  4777	              ? result.data as Record<string, unknown>
  4778	              : {};
  4779	          const searchMatches = Array.isArray(searchData.matches) ? searchData.matches : [];
  4780	          const concreteMatches = searchMatches
  4781	            .map((row) =>
  4782	              row && typeof row === "object" && !Array.isArray(row)
  4783	                ? String((row as Record<string, unknown>).name ?? "")
  4784	                : ""
  4785	            )
  4786	            .filter((name) =>
  4787	              Boolean(name) &&
  4788	              !routerOnlyTools.has(name) &&
  4789	              toolDefinitions.some((tool) => tool.function.name === name)
  4790	            );
  4791	
  4792	          // Discovery expands the governed capability surface; it must not seize
  4793	          // execution control. The previous implementation forced the first
  4794	          // concrete match returned by a broad search. That made arbitrary ranking
  4795	          // order drive real actions (for example Runway/Roblox status calls during
  4796	          // a GeminX runtime audit) and then durable recovery retried the poisoned
  4797	          // objective. Preserve the original objective + receipts and let the model
  4798	          // select from the disclosed concrete tools with tool_choice=auto.
  4799	          if (concreteMatches.length > 0) {
  4800	            console.log(
  4801	              `[deepseek-tools] disclosed_concrete_tools=${concreteMatches.join(",")} next_tool_choice=auto reason=objective_selects_after_discovery`
  4802	            );
  4803	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4804	              "DISCOVERY_RESULT_ACTION_REQUIRED: capability search returned concrete governed tools that can advance the current objective. " +
  4805	              "Use the best matching concrete tool now instead of continuing to browse the registry merely because more capabilities exist."
  4806	            );
  4807	          }
  4808	
  4809	          if (
  4810	            concreteMatches.length > 0 &&
  4811	            discoverySearchesSinceConcreteAction >= maxDiscoverySearchesWithoutConcreteAction
  4812	          ) {
  4813	            toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter(
  4814	              (tool) => tool.function.name !== "geminx_tool_search"
  4815	            );
  4816	            discoverySearchTemporarilyYielded = true;
  4817	            toolLoopSystemMessages.push(
  4818	              "DISCOVERY_ACTION_YIELD: three consecutive successful capability searches occurred without a concrete tool attempt. " +
  4819	              "Capability search is temporarily hidden for the next action step. Use one currently disclosed concrete tool or finish from existing evidence. " +
  4820	              "Search will reopen automatically after any concrete tool attempt."
  4821	            );
  4822	            console.log(
  4823	              `[deepseek-tools] discovery_action_yield searches=${discoverySearchesSinceConcreteAction} search_hidden=true reason=concrete_action_required`
  4824	            );
  4825	          }
  4826	
  4827	          // Capability discovery remains available for the lifetime of the objective.
  4828	          // The SEARCH -> ACT yield is temporary and reopens after a concrete tool attempt.
  4829	
  4830	        }
  4831	
  4832	        if (cacheKey && !cached && result.ok) {
  4833	          readToolCache.set(cacheKey, result);
  4834	        } else if (!cacheKey) {
  4835	          // Any mutation or non-cacheable observation may change the world. Force
  4836	          // subsequent verification probes to observe it again instead of trusting
  4837	          // a pre-action cache entry.
  4838	          readToolCache.clear();
  4839	        }
  4840	
  4841	        const browserMeta = browserActivityMetadata(call.name, result.data);
  4842	        conversation?.onActivity?.({
  4843	          phase: "tool",
  4844	          status: result.status === "succeeded" ? "done" : result.status === "blocked" ? "blocked" : "failed",
  4845	          label: founderToolOutcomeActivity(call.name, call.args, result),
  4846	          tool: call.name,
  4847	          ...browserMeta
  4848	        });
  4849	        if (result.ok && call.name.startsWith("browser_") && conversation?.drainFollowups) {
  4850	          await waitForBrowserHumanResume(effectiveToolRegistry, correlationId ?? call.id, result.data, conversation?.onActivity);
  4851	        }
  4852	        toolCalls.push({
  4853	          tool_call_id: call.id,
  4854	          correlation_id: correlationId ?? call.id,
  4855	          tool: call.name,
  4856	          action: call.name,
  4857	          args: call.args,
  4858	          status: result.status,
  4859	          started_at: result.started_at,
  4860	          finished_at: result.finished_at,
  4861	          result: result.data ?? null,
  4862	          error: result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? null
  4863	        });
  4864	
  4865	        // Two identical permission-denied repo/deployment reads are sufficient
  4866	        // evidence for this turn. Retire the offending tool schema; don't spend
  4867	        // another 20 model steps repeating an unchangeable 403 boundary.
  4868	        if (result.status === "blocked" &&
  4869	            (call.name === "github_get_repository" || call.name === "railway_list_deployments") &&
  4870	            toolCalls.filter((entry) => entry.tool === call.name &&
  4871	              entry.status === "blocked" &&
  4872	              JSON.stringify(entry.args ?? {}) === JSON.stringify(call.args ?? {})).length >= 2) {
  4873	          loopRetiredToolNames.add(call.name);
  4874	          toolDefinitions = toolDefinitions.filter((definition) => definition.function.name !== call.name);
  4875	          console.warn(`[deepseek-tools] blocked_read_retired tool=${call.name} correlation_id=${correlationId ?? "unbound"} duplicate_count=2`);
  4876	        }
  4877	
  4878	        messages.push({
  4879	          role: "tool",
  4880	          tool_call_id: call.id,
  4881	          content: compactToolReceiptForModel(result)
  4882	        });
  4883	        const pendingIds = unresolvedNativeToolCallIds(messages as ExecutionTranscriptMessage[]);
  4884	        persistExecutionCheckpoint(pendingIds.length > 0 ? "PENDING_TOOL" : "CLEAN", pendingIds[0] ?? null);
  4885	
  4886	        if (!routerOnlyTools.has(call.name)) {
  4887	          discoverySearchesSinceConcreteAction = 0;
  4888	          pendingDiscoveredToolNames.clear();
  4889	
  4890	          // Keep the current workflow's bounded sibling tools resident. Operators
  4891	          // frequently need write -> read -> verify -> cleanup or mutate -> observe
  4892	          // sequences; capability discovery must not evict the hands required to
  4893	          // finish the workflow that is already in progress.
  4894	          if (effectiveToolRegistry) {
  4895	            // Read/status observations from another capability family must not steal
  4896	            // ownership from an unfinished workflow. A new family may take ownership
  4897	            // only when no workflow exists yet, it is the same family, or the tool is
  4898	            // non-cacheable (a concrete mutation/action rather than a reusable read).
  4899	            const shouldAdoptWorkflow =
  4900	              !loopRetiredToolNames.has(call.name) &&
  4901	              shouldAdoptActiveWorkflowFamily(
  4902	                activeWorkflowToolNames,
  4903	                call.name,
  4904	                cacheKey !== null
  4905	              );
  4906	
  4907	            if (shouldAdoptWorkflow) {
  4908	              // Do not evict a previous phase's verification/cleanup tools

  4910	              // bounded provider inventory now fails explicitly if it cannot
  4911	              // retain the required protected set.
  4912	              const siblings = activeWorkflowSiblingNames(effectiveToolRegistry, call.name, 8);
  4913	              for (const name of siblings) {
  4914	                if (loopRetiredToolNames.has(name)) continue;
  4915	                if (activeWorkflowToolNames.size >= 28 && !activeWorkflowToolNames.has(name)) {
  4916	                  const oldest = activeWorkflowToolNames.values().next().value;
  4917	                  if (oldest) activeWorkflowToolNames.delete(oldest);
  4918	                }
  4919	                activeWorkflowToolNames.add(name);
  4920	              }

  4922	
  4923	            if (activeWorkflowToolNames.size > 0) {

  4930	                  "geminx_tool_search",
  4931	                  ...(successfulRuntimeCapabilityReads === 0 ? ["geminx_runtime_capabilities"] : []),

  4938	                (tool) => !loopRetiredToolNames.has(tool.function.name)

  4943	            }
  4944	          }
  4945	
  4946	          if (discoverySearchTemporarilyYielded && effectiveToolRegistry) {

  4953	                "geminx_tool_search",

  4956	                ...activeWorkflowToolNames,

  4958	              ])

  4961	            messages.push({

  4965	                "Continue the SAME objective without repeating completed discovery."

  4971	        }
  4972	
  4973	        if (
  4974	          !result.ok &&

  4983	              "A failed route is not evidence that the capability is globally unavailable. Do not ask the Founder to repair an internal fault while a governed repair path remains discoverable."
  4984	          });
  4985	        }

  4989	
  4990	        // A provider quota rejection is a real external boundary, not a new
  4991	        // objective for a paid self-repair loop. The native tool receipt above

  4993	        // speculate that an earlier Zoho email succeeded/failed.
  4994	        const imageRateLimited = (
  4995	          call.name === "office_digital_ambush_run" ||

  5013	            tool: call.name

  5016	        }
  5017	
  5018	        const protectedBoundary = protectedToolHumanBoundary(call.name, result);
  5019	        if (protectedBoundary) {
  5020	          forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage =
