  5023	            "\nBLOCKED_REAL_BOUNDARY";
  5024	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  5025	            phase: "human-boundary",
  5026	            status: "blocked",
  5027	            label: "Android installer opened · waiting for Rob's local confirmation · no further mutation will run",
  5028	            tool: protectedBoundary.tool,
  5029	            challenge: protectedBoundary.challenge,
  5030	            requires_human: true
  5031	          });
  5032	          break;
  5033	        }
  5034	      }
  5035	
  5036	      if (forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage) {
  5037	        break;
  5038	      }
  5039	      if (observationOnlyFuseTriggered) {
  5040	        // Deterministic terminal instead of another billable model call.
  5041	        // No automatic resume: a new request requires a new Founder instruction.
  5042	        console.warn(`[deepseek-tools] NO_PROGRESS_OBSERVATION_CIRCUIT_OPEN correlation_id=${correlationId ?? conversationId ?? "unbound"} steps=${observationOnlyStreak} budget=${maxObservationOnlySteps} provider_requests=${providerRequestOrdinal}`);
  5043	        conversation?.onActivity?.({
  5044	          phase: "tool-loop",
  5045	          status: "blocked",
  5046	          label: "Repeated observation without a completed action · stopped before another paid model request"
  5047	        });
  5048	        break;
  5049	      }
  5050	
  5051	      const followups = conversation?.drainFollowups?.() ?? [];
  5052	      for (const followup of followups) {
  5053	        messages.push({ role: "user", content: "Founder follow-up while you were working:\n" + followup });
  5054	        conversation?.onActivity?.({ phase: "followup", status: "done", label: "Added your follow-up" });
  5055	      }
  5056	      // Context pressure is an internal compaction event, not a Founder blocker.
  5057	      // Continue the SAME objective with a deterministic compact ledger first.
  5058	      // Only after bounded in-turn rollovers are exhausted may the durable worker
  5059	      // requeue the exact same objective automatically.
  5060	      if (contextPressureReached) {
  5061	        const maxRollovers = comprehensiveAudit ? 4 : 2;
  5062	        if (contextRolloverCount < maxRollovers && providerRequestOrdinal < maxProviderRequestsPerTurn() - 1) {
  5063	          const preTokens = estimateProviderInputTokens(messages, toolDefinitions);
  5064	          const rolled = rolloverToolLoopMessages(messages, intent, toolCalls);
  5065	          const postTokens = estimateProviderInputTokens(rolled, toolDefinitions);
  5066	          if (postTokens >= preTokens) {
  5067	            console.warn(
  5068	              `[geminx-context] rollover_token_reduction_failed pre=${preTokens} post=${postTokens} correlation_id=${correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? conversationId ?? "unbound"}`
  5069	            );
  5070	          }
  5071	          messages.splice(0, messages.length, ...rolled);
  5072	          persistExecutionCheckpoint("CLEAN");
  5073	          contextPressureReached = false;
  5074	          cumulativePromptTokens = 0;
  5075	          contextRolloverCount += 1;
  5076	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
  5077	            phase: "context",
  5078	            status: "running",
  5079	            label: `Context pressure reached. Preserved ${toolCalls.length} tool receipts and compacted the SAME objective in-place (rollover ${contextRolloverCount}/2). Next: continue from compact evidence without waiting for Rob.`
  5080	          });
  5081	        } else {
  5082	          console.warn(
  5083	            `[geminx-context] pressure_circuit_breaker correlation_id=${correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? conversationId ?? "unbound"} action=DURABLE_SAME_OBJECTIVE_RESUME`
  5084	          );
  5085	          contextResumeRequired = true;
  5086	          messages.push({
  5087	            role: "system",
  5088	            content: "GEMINX_CONTEXT_PRESSURE: the bounded in-turn compaction budget is exhausted while executable work remains. Preserve current evidence and request SAME_OBJECTIVE_AUTONOMOUS_RESUME. This is an internal runtime continuation event, not a Founder blocker."
  5089	          });
  5090	          break;
  5091	        }
  5092	      }
  5093	
  5094	      const executionDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? operatorToolDecision;
  5095	      const forcedToolForStep = forcedNextToolName && !loopRetiredToolNames.has(forcedNextToolName)
  5096	        ? forcedNextToolName
  5097	        : null;
  5098	      forcedNextToolName = null;
  5099	      payload = await callDeepSeek(
  5100	        `tool_step_${step + 1}`,
  5101	        messages,
  5102	        toolDefinitions,
  5103	        executionDecision,
  5104	        forcedToolForStep
  5105	      );
  5106	    }
  5107	
  5108	    // A capped loop exhausted entirely on failed tools is also a no-progress
  5109	    // outcome. Preserve the longer evidence-reading budget for successful
  5110	    // investigations; never classify an all-failed tool budget as completion.
  5111	    const exhaustedOnFailedTools =
  5112	      !observationOnlyFuseTriggered &&
  5113	      parseToolCalls(payload).length > 0 &&
  5114	      toolCalls.length >= toolStepLimit &&
  5115	      toolCalls.length > 0 &&
  5116	      !toolCalls.some(call => call.status === "succeeded");
  5117	    if (exhaustedOnFailedTools) {
  5118	      observationOnlyFuseTriggered = true;
  5119	      observationOnlyStreak = Math.max(observationOnlyStreak, toolStepLimit);
  5120	    }
  5121	
  5122	    const observationOnlyBlocked = observationOnlyFuseTriggered
  5123	      ? `Repeated read/observation checks (${observationOnlyStreak} steps) without a verified mutation. Recent tool outcomes: ${toolCalls.slice(-5).map((call) => `${call.tool}=${call.status}`).join(", ") || "none"}.`
  5124	      : null;
  5125	
  5126	    const stagnationReason = contextResumeRequired
  5127	      ? `Context pressure boundary reached after ${toolCalls.length} tool steps.`
  5128	      : null;
  5129	
  5130	    // An observation-only circuit is internal execution telemetry, not Rob's answer.
  5131	    // Give the model a final tool-free synthesis from the successful receipts.
  5132	    const noEvidenceAtFuse = Boolean(observationOnlyBlocked) &&
  5133	      !toolCalls.some((call) => call.status === "succeeded" && !routerOnlyTools.has(call.tool));
  5134	    // A status/observation fuse is NOT a user-facing reply. Retain the
  5135	    // model's original text; a tool failure must never substitute canned prose.
  5136	    let finalMessage: string | null = parseMessageContent(payload.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);
  5137	    let pendingToolCalls = forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage || observationOnlyBlocked ? [] : parseToolCalls(payload);
  5138	
  5139	    if (forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage || observationOnlyBlocked || stagnationReason) {
  5140	      // Graceful Stagnation Synthesis (Pillar 3):
  5141	      // Never dump static canned guardrail text like "BLOCKED: Context pressure..." or "BLOCKED: Tool discovery...".
  5142	      // Run ONE final tool-free synthesis pass instructing the model to speak in its natural operator voice.
  5143	      const synthesisMessages: DeepSeekMessage[] = [
  5144	        ...messages,
  5145	        {
  5146	          role: "system",
  5147	          content:
  5148	            `TOOL_EXECUTION_PAUSED: ${stagnationReason ?? observationOnlyBlocked ?? forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage}. ` +
  5149	            `Tool execution in this turn has stopped. No further tool calls or XML/tool tags. Use the actual tool result objects already in context, ` +
  5150	            `including the failed tool name, error text, HTTP status and stderr when supplied. ` +
  5151	            `Explain directly to Rob what happened, what was verified, what was not completed, and what you can do next. ` +
  5152	            `Keep your own natural wording, not a generic status template. Never claim an external action succeeded without its receipt.`
  5153	        }
  5154	      ];
  5155	      try {
  5156	        const synthesisDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? flashToolExecutionDecision();
  5157	        const synthPayload = await callDeepSeek("graceful_stagnation_synthesis", synthesisMessages, [], synthesisDecision);
  5158	        finalMessage = parseMessageContent(synthPayload.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);
  5159	        pendingToolCalls = [];
  5160	      } catch (err) {
  5161	        console.warn(`[deepseek-tools] graceful_stagnation_synthesis_failed: ${err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err)}`);
  5162	      }
  5163	      // If synthesis fails, retain the last model-generated narrative.
  5164	      // The terminal provider-error path below handles complete absence of text.
  5165	    }
  5166	
  5167	    const lateFollowups = conversation?.drainFollowups?.() ?? [];
  5168	    if (!forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage && !stagnationReason && lateFollowups.length > 0 && pendingToolCalls.length === 0) {
  5169	      for (const followup of lateFollowups) {
  5170	        messages.push({ role: "user", content: "Founder follow-up while you were working:\n" + followup });
  5171	        conversation?.onActivity?.({ phase: "followup", status: "done", label: "Added your follow-up" });
  5172	      }
  5173	      const followupDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? operatorToolDecision;
  5174	      payload = await callDeepSeek("late_followup", messages, toolDefinitions, followupDecision);
  5175	      finalMessage = parseMessageContent(payload.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);
  5176	      pendingToolCalls = parseToolCalls(payload);
  5177	    }
  5178	
  5179	    // Anti-stall lifecycle: reject unsupported "say go" / "Awaiting your go"
  5180	    // when an authorised objective has not executed any concrete action.
  5181	    if (
  5182	      !forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage &&
  5183	      !stagnationReason &&
  5184	      pendingToolCalls.length === 0 &&
  5185	      permissionCorrectionAttempts < 1 &&
  5186	      isUnsupportedAwaitingGoMessage(finalMessage)
  5187	    ) {
  5188	      console.log(`[deepseek-tools] rejected_unsupported_awaiting_go correlation_id=${correlationId ?? "unbound"}`);
  5189	      messages.push({
  5190	        role: "system",
  5191	        content:
  5192	          "FOUNDER_DIRECTIVE: This objective has already been authorised by the Founder. " +
  5193	          "Do not ask for confirmation or tell the Founder to 'say go'. " +
  5194	          "You must select and invoke the required concrete tool now to execute the task."
  5195	      });
  5196	      conversation?.onActivity?.({
  5197	        phase: "anti-stall",
  5198	        status: "running",
  5199	        label: "Authorised objective active · proceeding with required tool dispatch instead of awaiting confirmation"
  5200	      });
  5201	      const correctiveDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? operatorToolDecision;
  5202	      payload = await callDeepSeek("corrective_action_dispatch", messages, toolDefinitions, correctiveDecision);
  5203	      finalMessage = parseMessageContent(payload.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);
  5204	      pendingToolCalls = parseToolCalls(payload);
  5205	
  5206	      // If corrective action returned tool calls, execute them immediately so concrete work happens
  5207	      if (pendingToolCalls.length > 0 && effectiveToolRegistry) {
  5208	        console.log(`[deepseek-tools] executing_corrective_anti_stall_tools count=${pendingToolCalls.length} tools=${pendingToolCalls.map((c) => c.name).join(",")}`);
  5209	        const correctiveAssistant = payload.choices?.[0]?.message;
  5210	        messages.push(toolContinuationAssistantMessage(correctiveAssistant));
  5211	        for (const correctiveCall of pendingToolCalls) {
  5212	          const effectiveArgs = correctiveCall.name === "geminx_schedule_message"
  5213	            ? { ...correctiveCall.args, conversation_id: resolveScheduledFounderConversationId(conversation, conversationId) }
  5214	            : correctiveCall.args;
  5215	          const execRes = await effectiveToolRegistry.execute({
  5216	            tool_call_id: correctiveCall.id,
  5217	            correlation_id: correlationId ?? correctiveCall.id,
  5218	            tool: correctiveCall.name,
  5219	            args: effectiveArgs
  5220	          });
  5221	          const completedAt = execRes.finished_at ?? new Date().toISOString();
  5222	          const rec: ToolCallRecord = {
  5223	            tool_call_id: correctiveCall.id,
  5224	            correlation_id: correlationId ?? correctiveCall.id,
  5225	            tool: correctiveCall.name,
  5226	            action: correctiveCall.name,
  5227	            args: effectiveArgs,
  5228	            status: execRes.status,
  5229	            started_at: execRes.started_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
  5230	            finished_at: completedAt,
  5231	            result: execRes.data ?? null,
  5232	            error: execRes.error ?? execRes.blocked_reason ?? null
  5233	          };
  5234	          toolCalls.push(rec);
  5235	          messages.push({
  5236	            role: "tool",
  5237	            tool_call_id: correctiveCall.id,
  5238	            content: JSON.stringify(execRes.data ?? {
  5239	              ok: execRes.ok,
  5240	              status: execRes.status,
  5241	              status_code: execRes.status_code,
  5242	              error: execRes.error,
  5243	              blocked_reason: execRes.blocked_reason
  5244	            })
  5245	          });
  5246	        }
  5247	        pendingToolCalls = [];
  5248	        finalMessage = null; // Force finalization with real tool receipts
  5249	      }
  5250	    }
  5251	
  5252	    // A model can legitimately use the whole bounded tool budget and return another
  5253	    // tool request instead of a final narrative. Previously that left us with either
  5254	    // null content ("DeepSeek returned no assistant message content") or an
  5255	    // intermediate "let me..." message, causing the autonomous inbox job to fail
  5256	    // even though the executed tool receipts were still valid. Do not execute beyond
  5257	    // the safety bound. Force a tool-free finalization pass over the evidence already
  5258	    // gathered so the model must produce a terminal answer instead.
  5259	    const intermediateToolNarrative = forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage || Boolean(stagnationReason)
  5260	      ? false
  5261	      : looksLikeIntermediateToolNarrative(finalMessage, toolCalls);
  5262	    const missingRequiredTerminalMarker =
  5263	      !forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage &&
  5264	      !stagnationReason &&
  5265	      terminalContractRequired &&
  5266	      !hasGeminxTerminalMarker(finalMessage);
  5267	    if (shouldAttemptPaidFinalization({
  5268	      forcedProtectedBoundary: Boolean(forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage),
  5269	      observationOnlyBlocked: noEvidenceAtFuse,
  5270	      contextPressureBlocked: false,
  5271	      hasFinalMessage: Boolean(finalMessage),
  5272	      hasPendingToolCalls: pendingToolCalls.length > 0,
  5273	      intermediateToolNarrative,
  5274	      missingRequiredTerminalMarker
  5275	    })) {
  5276	      if (intermediateToolNarrative || missingRequiredTerminalMarker) {
  5277	        finalMessage = null;
  5278	      }
  5279	      const finalizationMessages: DeepSeekMessage[] = [
  5280	        ...messages,
  5281	        {
  5282	          role: "user",
  5283	          content:
  5284	            "The bounded tool-execution budget for this turn is now closed. Do NOT request or imply any further tool calls, and do NOT output any XML or <invoke> tags. " +
  5285	            "Using only the tool receipts already present in this conversation, produce the complete and concise final answer now covering each requested step. " +
  5286	            "Preserve any terminal contract from the original request exactly. If that contract requires OPERATOR-CRUCIBLE-CERTIFIED, COMPLETED_PROVEN, BLOCKED_REAL_BOUNDARY, or FAILED_VERIFICATION, " +
  5287	            "end with exactly one of those markers on its own line. Never claim completion that the receipts do not prove."
  5288	        }
  5289	      ];
  5290	
  5291	      for (let attempt = 0; attempt < Math.min(1, maxFinalizationAttempts()) && !finalMessage; attempt += 1) {
  5292	        const finalDecision = providerFailoverDecision ?? flashToolExecutionDecision();
  5293	        try {
  5294	          payload = await callDeepSeek(`finalize_${attempt + 1}`, finalizationMessages, [], finalDecision);
  5295	          finalMessage = parseMessageContent(payload.choices?.[0]?.message?.content);
  5296	        } catch (error) {
  5297	          console.warn(`[deepseek-tools] final_synthesis_unavailable reason=${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
  5298	          break;
  5299	        }
  5300	        if (finalMessage && (/<(?:[|｜]tool calls[|｜]|invoke name=|o= o= DSML|<invoke)/i.test(finalMessage))) {
  5301	          const stripped = finalMessage.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, " ").replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  5302	          if (stripped.length > 20) {
  5303	            finalMessage = stripped;
  5304	          }
  5305	        }
  5306	      }
  5307	    }
  5308	
  5309	    if (finalMessage) {
  5310	      const learning = await processCompanionLearning({
  5311	        text: finalMessage,
  5312	        provider: "deepseek",
  5313	        model: primaryDecision.model,
  5314	        objectiveId: correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? conversationId ?? "geminx-companion",
  5315	        sourceReceiptId: correlationId,
  5316	        toolCalls
  5317	      });
  5318	      finalMessage = learning.message;
  5319	      finalMessage = enforceFounderReply(finalMessage, conversation?.displayIntent ?? intent);
  5320	      if (learning.persistence) {
  5321	        console.log(`[geminx-learning] provider=deepseek model=${primaryDecision.model} status=${learning.persistence.status} path=${learning.persistence.path ?? "none"}`);
  5322	      } else if (primaryDecision.model === "deepseek-v4-pro") {
  5323	        const successfulTools = toolCalls.filter((call) => call.status === "succeeded").map((call) => call.tool);
  5324	        if (successfulTools.length > 0) {
  5325	          const sequence = successfulTools.slice(0, 12);
  5326	          const patternKey = sequence.join(" -> ");
  5327	          const distilled = await persistFrontierLearningCandidate({
  5328	            provider: "deepseek",
  5329	            model: "deepseek-v4-pro",
  5330	            objective_id: `pro-distill:${routedTaskType}:${patternKey}`,
  5331	            source_receipt_id: correlationId,
  5332	            task_status: "TOOL_VERIFIED_CANDIDATE",
  5333	            lesson_type: "execution_pattern",
  5334	            title: `Pro-assisted ${routedTaskType} execution pattern`,
  5335	            summary:
  5336	              `A higher-capability planning pass closed a tool-driven objective with the verified tool sequence: ${patternKey}. ` +
  5337	              "Future Flash runs should reuse this sequence only when the same capabilities are available, re-observe semantic UI state between mutations, and require the same terminal verification before claiming success.",
  5338	            reusable_when: [
  5339	              `task type is ${routedTaskType}`,
  5340	              `required tool family matches: ${[...new Set(sequence.map((name) => name.split("_")[0]))].join(", ")}`,
  5341	              "the current device/app state matches the observed workflow"
  5342	            ],
  5343	            invalidation_conditions: [
  5344	              "required capability or app is unavailable",
  5345	              "UI/app version drift changes the semantic controls",
  5346	              "the same terminal verification no longer passes"
  5347	            ],
  5348	            evidence_required: [
  5349	              "same workflow succeeds with Flash without Pro escalation",
  5350	              "terminal tool receipt proves the requested outcome"
  5351	            ],
  5352	            task_evidence: sequence.map((tool) => `${tool}: succeeded`).slice(0, 8),
  5353	            observed_at: new Date().toISOString()
  5354	          });
  5355	          console.log(`[geminx-learning] provider=deepseek model=deepseek-v4-pro auto_distill=true status=${distilled.status} path=${distilled.path ?? "none"}`);
  5356	        }
  5357	      }
  5358	    }
  5359	
  5360	    if (!finalMessage || isInternalEngineLeak(finalMessage)) {
  5361	      // Do not invent a Founder-visible agent answer. Preserve the last actual
  5362	      // model response if usable; otherwise surface the concrete provider/tool
  5363	      // failure through the normal transport error channel.
  5364	      const lastGeneratedReply = [...messages].reverse()
  5365	        .find((item) => item.role === "assistant" &&
  5366	          typeof item.content === "string" && item.content.trim() &&
  5367	          !isInternalEngineLeak(item.content))?.content?.trim() ?? null;
  5368	      if (lastGeneratedReply) {
  5369	        finalMessage = enforceFounderReply(lastGeneratedReply, conversation?.displayIntent ?? intent);
  5370	      } else {
  5371	        const failedCalls = toolCalls.filter((call) => call.status !== "succeeded")
  5372	          .slice(-8)
  5373	          .map((call) => ({
  5374	            tool: call.tool, status: call.status,
  5375	            error: call.error ?? null
  5376	          }));
  5377	        const evidence = JSON.stringify({
  5378	          error: "MODEL_DID_NOT_PRODUCE_A_USABLE_REPLY",
  5379	          model: primaryDecision.model,
  5380	          tool_failures: failedCalls,
  5381	          safe_read_only_tool_trace: safeUnusableReplyReadOnlyTrace(toolCalls),
  5382	          tool_call_count: toolCalls.length,
  5383	          correlation_id: correlationId ?? null
  5384	        });
  5385	        throw new DeepSeekCompanionError(
  5386	          502, "Model reply could not be generated", evidence
  5387	        );
  5388	      }
  5389	    }
  5390	
  5391	    // DeepSeek's observation/context fuse must not discard an authorised HQ
  5392	    // objective. This reuses the same durable inbox as Google AG, with no
  5393	    // recurring timer or duplicate background control plane.
  5394	    let backgroundHandoff: AgContinuationHandoff | null = null;
  5395	    if (!forcedProtectedBoundaryMessage &&
  5396	        (observationOnlyFuseTriggered || contextResumeRequired || agIsDeferredActionPromise(finalMessage)) &&
  5397	        !activeTaskId &&
  5398	        ["neo", "ag", "geminx"].includes(String(activeOfficeAgent)) &&
  5399	        agIsExecutionObjective(conversation?.executionObjective ?? intent)) {
  5400	      backgroundHandoff = enqueueAgContinuationHandoff({
  5401	        conversationId: resolveScheduledFounderConversationId(conversation, conversationId),
  5402	        jobId: String(activeRunId ?? conversation?.messageId ?? correlationId ?? conversationId ?? ""),
  5403	        founderAuthorised: conversation?.founderAuthorised === true,
  5404	        recipient: agentMode === "ag" ? "ag" : "neo",
  5405	        activeTaskId,
  5406	        objective: String(conversation?.executionObjective ?? intent),
  5407	        correlationId,
  5408	        unmetMilestones: ["Reconcile original incomplete execution and independently verify outstanding acceptance criteria"],
  5409	        toolReceipts: toolCalls.map(item => ({ tool: item.tool, status: item.status }))
  5410	      });
  5411	      finalMessage += backgroundHandoff.status === "QUEUED" || backgroundHandoff.status === "ALREADY_QUEUED"
  5412	        ? "\\nUnfinished work was durably queued under " + backgroundHandoff.message_id + "; no completion is claimed yet."
  5413	        : "\\nBackground continuation was not persisted: " + (backgroundHandoff.reason ?? "UNKNOWN_REASON") + ".";
  5414	    }
  5415	
  5416	    // The worker's deterministic adjudicator alone decides COMPLETED_PROVEN.
  5417	    // A model-written marker cannot itself seal a native checkpoint.
  5418	    messages.push({ role: "assistant", content: finalMessage });
  5419	    persistExecutionCheckpoint("CLEAN");
  5420	
  5421	    // The founder objective was persisted before execution. Append only the result
  5422	    // here so a successful turn cannot duplicate the user message.
  5423	    let activeRunIdAfter = activeRunId;
  5424	    if (store && conversationRecord && conversationId) {
  5425	      const derivedDomain = deriveDomain(conversationRecord.domain, intent);
  5426	      const assistantAttachments = correlationId && effectiveToolRegistry
  5427	        ? [...effectiveToolRegistry.artifacts(correlationId)]
  5428	        : [];
  5429	      store.appendTurn(conversationRecord, {
  5430	        role: "assistant",
  5431	        // Bind durable-job speech to its exact work item, not the latest user
  5432	        // message. The worker can then de-duplicate this result by job ID.
  5433	        ...(activeTaskId ? { message_id: activeTaskId } : {}),
  5434	        content: finalMessage,
  5435	        at: new Date().toISOString(),
  5436	        ...(assistantAttachments.length ? { attachments: assistantAttachments } : {})
  5437	      }, {
  5438	        active_run_id: activeRunId,
  5439	        active_task_id: activeTaskId,
  5440	        domain: derivedDomain
  5441	      });
  5442	      activeRunIdAfter = activeRunId;
  5443	    }
  5444	
  5445	    // Propagate context-pressure state structurally across the provider/runtime boundary.
  5446	    // Never leak an internal continuation marker into Founder-visible chat.
  5447	    return {
  5448	      message: finalMessage,
  5449	      ...(contextResumeRequired ? { context_pressure_resume_required: true } : {}),
  5450	      ...(observationOnlyFuseTriggered ? { no_progress_observation_fuse: true } : {}),
  5451	      ...(backgroundHandoff ? { background_handoff: backgroundHandoff } : {}),
  5452	      model: primaryDecision.model,
  5453	      provider: "deepseek",
  5454	      boot_profile_id: decision.boot_profile_id,
  5455	      brain_context_status: brain.status,
  5456	      brain_sources: brain.sources,
  5457	      ...(correlationId ? { correlation_id: correlationId } : {}),
  5458	      ...(conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : {}),
  5459	      ...(activeRunIdAfter ? { active_run_id: activeRunIdAfter } : {}),
  5460	      ...(toolCalls.length > 0 ? { tool_calls: toolCalls, multi_step: true } : {})
  5461	    };
  5462	  } catch (error) {
  5463	    // Provider/runtime failures are execution receipts, not assistant speech.
  5464	    // Writing them into the durable conversation poisoned later successful turns
  5465	    // and made NEO diagnose stale transport history instead of answering Rob.
  5466	    const detailMsg = error instanceof Error ? `${error.name}: ${error.message}` : String(error);
  5467	    console.warn(
  5468	      `[companion-conversation] provider_failure_not_written conversation_id=${conversationId ?? "unbound"} correlation_id=${correlationId ?? "unbound"} status=${error instanceof DeepSeekCompanionError ? error.statusCode : 500} detail=${detailMsg.slice(0, 500)}`
  5469	    );
  5470	    // A provider outage cannot be solved by forging the model's own speech.
  5471	    // Propagate exact typed failure to the chat transport / existing AG repair
  5472	    // incident route. Do NOT append fixed text as an assistant conversation turn.
  5473	    // Ordinary tool failures are handled earlier by bounded model synthesis.
  5474	    throw error;
  5475	  }
  5476	}
  5477	
  5478	/**
  5479	 * Derive a stable retrieval domain for the active conversation. Roblox/game/build
  5480	 * intent locks the conversation into the Roblox/Creator domain so #125 can never
  5481	 * contaminate it merely from a later ambiguous follow-up.
  5482	 */
  5483	function deriveDomain(current: string, intent: string): string {
  5484	  const tokens = intent.toLowerCase();
  5485	  if (/roblox|studio|obby|sheriff|ghost|game|build/.test(tokens)) return "roblox";
  5486	  if (/revenue|contract|hunter|sales|invoice|quote|client|commercial/.test(tokens)) return "commercial";
  5487	  if (/creator|companion|fastbuild/.test(tokens)) return "creator";
  5488	  if (current === "roblox" || current === "creator") return current;
  5489	  return "general";
  5490	}

   121	function resolveGoogleApiKeys(): string[] {
   122	  const envRaw = [
   123	    process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_API_KEY,
   124	    process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY,
   125	    process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY,
   126	    process.env.GEMINX_GOOGLE_API_KEYS,
   127	  ];
   128	  const keys: string[] = [];
   129	  for (const raw of envRaw) {
   130	    if (!raw) continue;
   131	    for (const part of raw.split(/[\s,]+/)) {
   132	      const trimmed = part.trim();
   133	      if (trimmed && !keys.includes(trimmed)) {
   134	        keys.push(trimmed);
   135	      }
   136	    }
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  return keys;
   140	}
   141	
   142	export function resolveGoogleApiKey(): string {
   143	  const keys = resolveGoogleApiKeys();
   144	  return keys[0] || "";
   145	}

   147	export function formatGeminiTools(definitions: readonly ToolDefinition[]): Array<{ functionDeclarations: unknown[] }> {
   148	  const allowed = definitions.filter(d => d.name !== "web_search");
   149	  if (!allowed.length) return [];
   150	  const declarations = allowed.map(d => ({
   151	    name: d.name,
   152	    description: d.description,
   153	    parameters: sanitizeGeminiSchema(d.input_schema && typeof d.input_schema === "object" ? d.input_schema : { type: "object", properties: {} })
   154	  }));
   155	  return [{ functionDeclarations: declarations }];
   156	}

   187	function resolveDefaultBrainRetrieverForAgent(
   188	  officeAgent: OfficeAgentId,
   189	  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch
   190	): AgentBrainContextRetriever {
   191	  if (officeAgent === "ag") return configuredAgBrainContextRetriever(fetchImpl);
   192	  if (officeAgent === "neo") return configuredNeoBrainContextRetriever(fetchImpl);
   193	  if (["brian", "mark", "kerry", "sophie", "ads"].includes(officeAgent)) {
   194	    return configuredOfficeSpecialistBrainContextRetriever(officeAgent as OfficeSpecialistBrainId, fetchImpl);
   195	  }
   196	  return configuredAgentBrainContextRetriever(fetchImpl);
   197	}

   199	export async function generateAntigravityCompanionReplyWithTools(
   200	  intent: string,
   201	  correlationId: string | null,
   202	  toolRegistry: ToolRegistry | null,
   203	  brainRetriever?: AgentBrainContextRetriever,
   204	  conversation?: AntigravityConversationOptions & { thinkOnly?: boolean },
   205	  _taskType: GeminxModelTaskType = "chat",
   206	  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch
   207	): Promise<AntigravityCompanionReply> {
   208	  const apiKey = resolveGoogleApiKey();
   209	  const agentMode = conversation?.agentMode ?? "ag";
   210	  const resolvedOfficeAgent: OfficeAgentId = normalizeOfficeAgentId(
   211	    conversation?.officeAgent ?? (agentMode === "ag" ? "ag" : "geminx"),
   212	    agentMode === "ag" ? "ag" : "geminx"
   213	  );
   214	  const resolvedCompanyScope = normalizeCompanyScope(conversation?.companyScope, resolvedOfficeAgent);
   215	  const thinkOnly = conversation?.thinkOnly === true;
   216	
   217	  conversation?.onActivity?.({
   218	    phase: "model-switch",
   219	    status: "running",
   220	    label: `Google Antigravity / Gemini active (${OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[resolvedOfficeAgent].display_name})`
   221	  });
   222	
   223	  if (!apiKey) {
   224	    throw new AntigravityCompanionError(
   225	      503,
   226	      "Google Gemini / Antigravity API key is not configured in environment.",
   227	      "Google Gemini / Antigravity API key is not configured in environment."
   228	    );
   229	  }
   230	
   231	  const store = conversation?.store ?? null;
   232	  let conversationRecord: ConversationRecord | null = null;
   233	  let conversationId = conversation?.conversationId ?? null;
   234	  const activeRunId = conversation?.activeRunId ?? null;
   235	  const activeTaskId = conversation?.activeTaskId ?? null;
   236	
   237	  if (store) {
   238	    if (conversationId) conversationRecord = store.get(conversationId);
   239	    if (!conversationRecord) {
   240	      conversationRecord = store.create(conversationId || undefined);
   241	      conversationId = conversationRecord.conversation_id;
   242	    }
   243	  }
   244	
   245	  if (store && conversationRecord && conversationId) {
   246	    const visibleIntent = conversation?.displayIntent ?? intent;
   247	    conversationRecord = store.appendTurn(conversationRecord, {
   248	      role: "user",
   249	      content: visibleIntent,
   250	      at: new Date().toISOString(),
   251	      ...(conversation?.attachments?.length ? { attachments: conversation.attachments } : {})
   252	    }, {
   253	      active_run_id: activeRunId,
   254	      active_task_id: activeTaskId,
   255	      domain: conversationRecord.domain || "general",
   256	      summary: visibleIntent
   257	    });
   258	  }
   259	
   260	  const rawIntent = String(conversation?.displayIntent ?? intent).trim();
   261	  const isReadOnlyPrompt = /\b(?:do not|don't)\s+(?:modify|edit|change|create|fix|repair)\b/i.test(rawIntent) ||
   262	    /\b(?:do not create a repair job|read-only diagnostic audit)\b/i.test(rawIntent);
   263	
   264	  conversation?.onActivity?.({ phase: "context", status: "running", label: "Checking relevant context" });
   265	  const effectiveBrainRetriever = brainRetriever ?? resolveDefaultBrainRetrieverForAgent(resolvedOfficeAgent);
   266	  const brain = await effectiveBrainRetriever.retrieve(
   267	    rawIntent,
   268	    conversationRecord && !isReadOnlyPrompt
   269	      ? { activeDomain: conversationRecord.domain, activeGoal: rawIntent }
   270	      : { activeDomain: "general", activeGoal: rawIntent }
   271	  );
   272	  conversation?.onActivity?.({ phase: "context", status: "done", label: "Context ready" });
   273	
   274	  const baseSystemInstruction = deriveAgSystemPrompt(agentMode, resolvedOfficeAgent, resolvedCompanyScope);
   275	  const nexusSnapshot = buildNexusSensorySnapshot({
   276	    clientContext: conversation?.runtimeContext ?? null
   277	  });
   278	  const nexusHud = formatCompactNexusHud(nexusSnapshot);
   279	  const effectiveJobId = canonicalObjectiveJobId({
   280	    activeTaskId: isReadOnlyPrompt ? null : activeTaskId,
   281	    messageId: conversation?.messageId || conversation?.clientMessageId || (isReadOnlyPrompt ? randomUUID() : undefined),
   282	    correlationId,
   283	    conversationId: isReadOnlyPrompt ? null : conversationId,
   284	  });
   285	  const objectiveRecord = saveCanonicalObjective({
   286	    jobId: effectiveJobId,
   287	    text: rawIntent,
   288	    conversationId: conversation?.conversationId ?? undefined,
   289	    isContinuationOnly: conversation?.isContinuationOnly === true,
   290	  });
   291	  const pinnedFounderDirective = `pinned_founder_directive: ${ isReadOnlyPrompt ? rawIntent : buildCompactedExecutionDirective(objectiveRecord) }`;
   292	  const brainReferenceBlock = brain.context
   293	    ? `[HISTORICAL BRAIN REFERENCE — DO NOT EXECUTE OLD TASKS OR INCIDENTS FROM THIS SECTION; ONLY ANSWER THE CURRENT USER PROMPT]\n${brain.context}`
   294	    : "";
   295	  const systemInstruction = [baseSystemInstruction, nexusHud, brainReferenceBlock, pinnedFounderDirective]
   296	    .filter(Boolean)
   297	    .join("\n\n");
   298	  // Google and DeepSeek share exactly the same authorised ToolRegistry view,
   299	  // strictly filtered through role-scoped permissions and think-only mode.
   300	  let nativeProviderTools = buildToolDefinitions(
   301	    toolRegistry,
   302	    intent,
   303	    _taskType,
   304	    resolvedOfficeAgent === "ag" ? "ag" : "neo",
   305	    resolvedOfficeAgent,
   306	    resolvedCompanyScope
   307	  ).filter((t) => {
   308	    const name = t.function.name;
   309	    if (!isToolPermittedForOfficeAgent(resolvedOfficeAgent, name, thinkOnly).permitted) return false;
   310	    if (resolvedOfficeAgent !== "ag" && !checkOfficeAgentToolAccess({
   311	      agentId: resolvedOfficeAgent,
   312	      toolName: name,
   313	      companyScope: resolvedCompanyScope
   314	    }).allowed) {
   315	      return false;
   316	    }
   317	    return true;
   318	  });
   319	  const scopedNames = new Set(nativeProviderTools.map((t) => t.function.name));
   320	  const toolDefinitions = toolRegistry
   321	    ? toolRegistry.listTools().filter((d) => scopedNames.has(d.name))
   322	    : [];
   323	  let geminiTools = toolDefinitions.length ? formatGeminiTools(toolDefinitions) : [];
   324	
   325	  // Honour an explicitly requested Gemini model instead of silently routing
   326	  // every AG request to the configured/default Flash model. Provider aliases
   327	  // are resolved before reaching this adapter.
   328	  const explicitlyRequestedGemini = /^gemini-[a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{1,60}$/i.test(
   329	    String(conversation?.modelPreference ?? "")
   330	  ) ? String(conversation?.modelPreference).toLowerCase() : null;
   331	  const candidateModels = [...new Set([
   332	    explicitlyRequestedGemini,
   333	    process.env.GEMINI_COMPANION_MODEL,
   334	    "gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
   335	    "gemini-3.5-flash-lite",
   336	    "gemini-2.5-flash",
   337	    "gemini-3.8-flash",
   338	    "gemini-3.6-flash",
   339	    "gemini-3.5-flash",
   340	    "gemini-flash-latest",
   341	    "gemini-flash-lite-latest"
   342	  ].filter(Boolean) as string[])];
   343	
   344	  const contents: Array<{ role: string; parts: Array<Record<string, unknown>> }> = [];
   345	
   346	  // Replay bounded recent turns (skip stale repair history when executing a fresh read-only query)
   347	  const priorTurns = isReadOnlyPrompt
   348	    ? (conversationRecord?.turns ?? []).slice(-1)
   349	    : (conversationRecord?.turns ?? []).slice(-8);
   350	  for (const t of priorTurns) {
   351	    if (t.role === "user" || t.role === "assistant") {
   352	      contents.push({
   353	        role: t.role === "user" ? "user" : "model",
   354	        parts: [{ text: String(t.content || "") }]
   355	      });
   356	    }
   357	  }
   358	
   359	  // Ensure current user intent is the final turn
   360	  if (!contents.length || contents[contents.length - 1].role !== "user") {
   361	    contents.push({
   362	      role: "user",
   363	      parts: [{ text: intent }]
   364	    });
   365	  }
   366	
   367	  const executedToolCalls: ToolCallRecord[] = [];
   368	  let finalMessage = "";
   369	  let backgroundHandoff: AgContinuationHandoff | null = null;
   370	  let totalUsage = { prompt_tokens: 0, candidates_tokens: 0, total_tokens: 0 };
   371	  let selectedModel = candidateModels[0] || "gemini-3.1-flash-lite";
   372	  // Continue tool-driven work through recoverable edit mistakes without a
   373	  // new task, new queue, or any Founder prompt. Bounded native tool rounds.
   374	  let maxSteps = 40;
   375	  // AG's provider may return polite prose instead of calling the next tool.
   376	  // The same objective is pinned above; continuation runs inside this SAME job.
   377	  let forcedContinuations = 0;
   378	  let noProgressFuseTriggered = false;
   379	  let railwayAuthDenied = false;
   380	  let railwayDenialToolIndex = -1;
   381	  let lastGoogleResponseId = "";
   382	  let lastGoogleResponseSha256 = "";
   383	  let sawRateLimit429 = false;
   384	
   385	  const apiKeys = resolveGoogleApiKeys();
   386	
   387	  let pinnedModel: string | null = null;
   388	
   389	  for (let step = 0; step < maxSteps; step++) {
   390	    let response: Response | null = null;
   391	    let responseData: any = null;
   392	    let lastError: Error | null = null;
   393	
   394	    const modelsToTry: string[] = pinnedModel ? [pinnedModel] : candidateModels;
   395	
   396	    for (const modelCandidate of modelsToTry) {
   397	      for (const currentKey of apiKeys) {
   398	        const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${encodeURIComponent(modelCandidate)}:generateContent?key=${encodeURIComponent(currentKey)}`;
   399	        const bodyPayload: Record<string, unknown> = {
   400	          contents,
   401	          systemInstruction: {
   402	            parts: [{ text: systemInstruction }]
   403	          },
   404	          generationConfig: {
   405	            temperature: 0.2,
   406	            maxOutputTokens: 8192
   407	          }
   408	        };
   409	        if (geminiTools.length) {
   410	          bodyPayload.tools = geminiTools;
   411	          // Native Gemini function calling lets the model answer or invoke
   412	          // any advertised authorised tool without forcing a macro.
   413	          bodyPayload.toolConfig = { functionCallingConfig: { mode: "AUTO" } };
   414	        }
   415	
   416	        try {
   417	          response = await fetchImpl(url, {
   418	            method: "POST",
   419	            headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
   420	            body: JSON.stringify(bodyPayload)
   421	          });
   422	
   423	          if (response.ok) {
   424	            responseData = await response.json();
   425	            selectedModel = String(responseData?.modelVersion || modelCandidate).trim() || modelCandidate;
   426	            pinnedModel = modelCandidate;
   427	            if (typeof responseData?.responseId === "string" && responseData.responseId.trim()) {
   428	              lastGoogleResponseId = responseData.responseId.trim();
   429	            }
   430	            lastGoogleResponseSha256 = computeSha256(JSON.stringify({
   431	              model: selectedModel,
   432	              correlation_id: correlationId || "",
   433	              usage: responseData?.usageMetadata || {},
   434	              candidate_finish: responseData?.candidates?.[0]?.finishReason || ""
   435	            })).slice(0, 24);
   436	            lastError = null;
   437	            updateProviderHealth("google", "READY");
   438	            break;
   439	          }
   440	
   441	          const errText = await response.text();
   442	          console.warn(`[gemini-candidate] ${modelCandidate} failed (${response.status}); provider response withheld from logs`);
   443	          lastError = new Error(`Gemini ${modelCandidate} failed (${response.status}): ${errText.slice(0, 300)}`);
   444	          if (response.status === 429) {
   445	            sawRateLimit429 = true;
   446	            // Key quota or rate-limit hit: back off briefly and try next key
   447	            await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 600 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 400)));
   448	            continue;
   449	          }
   450	          if (response.status === 404 || response.status === 503 || response.status === 500 || response.status === 502) {
   451	            // Model unavailable or server error: move to next model candidate
   452	            break;
   453	          }
   454	          break;
   455	        } catch (err: any) {
   456	          lastError = err;
   457	        }
   458	      }
   459	      if (responseData) break;
   460	      // If unpinned and all keys returned 429 quota exhaustion on this model candidate,
   461	      // continue to the next model candidate to find an available quota tier.
   462	      if (!pinnedModel) {
   463	        continue;
   464	      }
   465	    }
   466	
   467	    if (!responseData) {
   468	      if (sawRateLimit429) {
   469	        updateProviderHealth("google", "RATE_LIMITED", lastError?.message || "HTTP 429 Resource Exhausted", 60);
   470	      } else {
   471	        updateProviderHealth("google", "TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE", lastError?.message || "ALL_GEMINI_MODELS_FAILED", 30);
   472	      }
   473	      throw new AntigravityCompanionError(
   474	        sawRateLimit429 ? 429 : 502,
   475	        "Google Gemini / Antigravity returned an error.",
   476	        lastError?.message || "ALL_GEMINI_MODELS_FAILED"
   477	      );
   478	    }
   479	
   480	    const usage = responseData?.usageMetadata;
   481	    if (usage) {
   482	      totalUsage.prompt_tokens += Number(usage.promptTokenCount ?? 0);
   483	      totalUsage.candidates_tokens += Number(usage.candidatesTokenCount ?? 0);
   484	      totalUsage.total_tokens += Number(usage.totalTokenCount ?? 0);
   485	    }
   486	
   487	    const candidate = responseData?.candidates?.[0];
   488	    const candidateParts = candidate?.content?.parts ?? [];
   489	
   490	    const functionCalls = candidateParts
   491	      .filter((p: any) => p && typeof p.functionCall === "object" && p.functionCall.name)
   492	      .map((p: any) => p.functionCall);
   493	
   494	    const textPart = candidateParts.find((p: any) => typeof p?.text === "string" && p.text.trim());
   495	    if (textPart) {
   496	      finalMessage = textPart.text.trim();
   497	    }
   498	    // Text accompanying a tool call is interim commentary, not a final answer.
   499	    // Never reuse it after further tool rounds if the provider omits its finale.
   500	    if (functionCalls.length) finalMessage = "";
   501	
   502	    if (!functionCalls.length) {
   503	      const effectiveObjectiveDirective = isReadOnlyPrompt ? rawIntent : buildCompactedExecutionDirective(objectiveRecord);
   504	      const isAutonomousOfficeAgent = (resolvedOfficeAgent === "ag" || resolvedOfficeAgent === "brian" || resolvedOfficeAgent === "neo") && !thinkOnly;
   505	      const gate = isAutonomousOfficeAgent ? agContinuationDecision({
   506	        objective: effectiveObjectiveDirective,
   507	        reply: finalMessage,
   508	        taskType: _taskType,
   509	        activeTaskId: isReadOnlyPrompt ? null : activeTaskId,
   510	        executedTools: executedToolCalls.length,
   511	        toolReceipts: executedToolCalls,
   512	        toolRegistryAvailable: Boolean(toolRegistry),
   513	        continuationAttempts: forcedContinuations,
   514	        railwayAuthDenied,
   515	        independentProgressSinceRailwayDenial: railwayDenialToolIndex >= 0 &&
   516	          executedToolCalls.slice(railwayDenialToolIndex + 1).some(call =>
   517	            !/^railway_/i.test(call.tool) && call.status === "succeeded")
   518	      }) : "COMPLETE";
   519	      if (gate === "CONTINUE" && forcedContinuations < MAX_AG_FORCED_CONTINUATIONS && step < maxSteps - 1) {
   520	        forcedContinuations += 1;
   521	        conversation?.onActivity?.({
   522	          phase: "continuation", status: "running",
   523	          label: "Continuing the same authorised task without requesting permission"
   524	        });
   525	        // A normal model answer has no function calls. Re-enter the model
   526	        // with a legitimate user-role continuation tied to the pinned objective.
   527	        contents.push({ role: "model", parts: candidateParts.length ? candidateParts : [{ text: finalMessage || "No action taken yet." }] });
   528	        contents.push({ role: "user", parts: [{ text: agContinuationInstruction(railwayAuthDenied) }] });
   529	        finalMessage = "";
   530	        continue;
   531	      }
   532	      if (isAutonomousOfficeAgent && gate !== "BLOCKED_ON_HUMAN" && agContinuationDecision({
   533	        objective: effectiveObjectiveDirective, reply: finalMessage,
   534	        taskType: _taskType, activeTaskId: isReadOnlyPrompt ? null : activeTaskId, executedTools: executedToolCalls.length,
   535	        toolReceipts: executedToolCalls,
   536	        toolRegistryAvailable: Boolean(toolRegistry), continuationAttempts: 0, railwayAuthDenied,
   537	        independentProgressSinceRailwayDenial: railwayDenialToolIndex >= 0 &&
   538	          executedToolCalls.slice(railwayDenialToolIndex + 1).some(call =>
   539	            !/^railway_/i.test(call.tool) && call.status === "succeeded")
   540	      }) === "CONTINUE") {
   541	        // A capped refusal cannot be marketed as completion. Preserve actual
   542	        // tool receipts and let the existing durable adjudicator mark the job.
   543	        noProgressFuseTriggered = true;
   544	        const unmet = agUnmetMilestones(effectiveObjectiveDirective, executedToolCalls);
   545	        // The provider's forced-continuation fuse is a checkpoint, not a
   546	        // terminal reply. Persist the same objective before returning HTTP.
   547	        backgroundHandoff = enqueueAgContinuationHandoff({
   548	          conversationId, jobId: effectiveJobId, activeTaskId,
   549	          founderAuthorised: conversation?.founderAuthorised === true,
   550	          objective: rawIntent, correlationId,
   551	          unmetMilestones: unmet,
   552	          toolReceipts: executedToolCalls.map(item => ({ tool: item.tool, status: item.status }))
   553	        });
   554	        // Never overwrite the provider's actual reply with a stock failure
   555	        // paragraph. Append the adjudicator's exact outstanding evidence instead;
   556	        // a model claiming success without receipts remains unverified.
   557	        const observedModelReply = String(finalMessage ?? "").trim();
   558	        const usableModelReply = observedModelReply && !agIsDeferredActionPromise(observedModelReply)
   559	          ? observedModelReply
   560	          : "";
   561	        const checkpoint = [
   562	          "Objective status: UNVERIFIED",
   563	          ...(unmet.length ? ["Outstanding evidence: " + unmet.join(", ")] : []),
   564	          ...(railwayAuthDenied ? ["Railway authentication was denied"] : []),
   565	          backgroundHandoff.status === "QUEUED" || backgroundHandoff.status === "ALREADY_QUEUED"
   566	            ? "Same objective checkpoint: " + backgroundHandoff.message_id
   567	            : "Continuation not persisted: " + (backgroundHandoff.reason ?? "PERSISTENCE_FAILED")
   568	        ].join("; ");
   569	        finalMessage = usableModelReply ? usableModelReply + "\n\n" + checkpoint : checkpoint;
   570	        conversation?.onActivity?.({
   571	          phase: "continuation", status: "blocked",
   572	          label: "Execution checkpoint: no further verified progress"
   573	        });
   574	      }
   575	      break;
   576	    }
   577	
   578	    // Model requested tool calls
   579	    contents.push({
   580	      role: "model",
   581	      parts: candidateParts
   582	    });
   583	
   584	    const functionResponses: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
   585	
   586	    for (const call of functionCalls) {
   587	      const toolName = String(call.name);
   588	      const callArgs = (call.args && typeof call.args === "object" ? call.args : {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
   589	      const callId = `call_${randomUUID().slice(0, 10)}`;
   590	
   591	      conversation?.onActivity?.({
   592	        phase: "tool",
   593	        status: "running",
   594	        label: `Running ${toolName}`,
   595	        tool: toolName
   596	      });
   597	
   598	      let toolResult: unknown = null;
   599	      let toolError: string | null = null;
   600	      const startTime = Date.now();
   601	
   602	      const permCheck = isToolPermittedForOfficeAgent(resolvedOfficeAgent, toolName, thinkOnly);
   603	      const scopeCheck = resolvedOfficeAgent === "ag"
   604	        ? { allowed: true, reason: "" }
   605	        : checkOfficeAgentToolAccess({
   606	            agentId: resolvedOfficeAgent,
   607	            toolName,
   608	            companyScope: resolvedCompanyScope,
   609	            targetCompanyScope: typeof callArgs.company_scope === "string" ? callArgs.company_scope : undefined
   610	          });
   611	
   612	      if (!permCheck.permitted) {
   613	        toolError = `OFFICE_ROLE_TOOL_DENIED: ${permCheck.reason || `Tool ${toolName} denied for ${resolvedOfficeAgent}`}`;
   614	      } else if (!scopeCheck.allowed) {
   615	        const errCode = ("code" in scopeCheck && typeof scopeCheck.code === "string") ? scopeCheck.code : "OFFICE_SCOPE_DENIED";
   616	        toolError = `${errCode}: ${scopeCheck.reason}`;
   617	      } else if (railwayAuthDenied && /^railway_/i.test(toolName)) {
   618	        // The credential has already failed for this same run. Never burn
   619	        // credits probing alternative arguments under the same credential.
   620	        toolError = "RAILWAY_SCOPE_AUTH_DENIED_ALREADY_RECORDED";
   621	      } else if (toolRegistry) {
   622	        try {
   623	          const invocation = await toolRegistry.execute({
   624	            tool: toolName,
   625	            tool_call_id: callId,
   626	            correlation_id: correlationId || randomUUID(),
   627	            args: callArgs
   628	          });
   629	          toolResult = invocation.data ?? invocation.error ?? invocation.status;
   630	          if (!invocation.ok) {
   631	            toolError = invocation.error ?? "TOOL_EXECUTION_FAILED";
   632	            if (agRailwayAuthRejected(toolName, {
   633	              status_code: invocation.status_code, error: invocation.error,
   634	              blocked_reason: invocation.blocked_reason, data: invocation.data
   635	            })) {
   636	              railwayAuthDenied = true;
   637	              railwayDenialToolIndex = executedToolCalls.length;
   638	              toolError = "RAILWAY_SCOPE_AUTH_DENIED";
   639	              toolResult = { error_code: "RAILWAY_SCOPE_AUTH_DENIED",
   640	                action: "Continue independent authorised work; credential access needs separate repair" };
   641	              conversation?.onActivity?.({
   642	                phase: "tool", status: "blocked", tool: toolName,
   643	                label: "Railway access denied; continuing independent work"
   644	              });
   645	            }
   646	          }
   647	        } catch (execErr: any) {
   648	          toolError = execErr?.message || String(execErr);
   649	        }
   650	      } else {
   651	        toolError = "TOOL_REGISTRY_UNAVAILABLE";
   652	      }
   653	
   654	      const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
   655	      const executedRecord: ToolCallRecord = {
   656	        tool_call_id: callId,
   657	        correlation_id: correlationId ?? "",
   658	        tool: toolName,
   659	        action: toolName,
   660	        args: callArgs,
   661	        status: toolError ? "failed" : "succeeded",
   662	        started_at: new Date(startTime).toISOString(),
   663	        finished_at: finishedAt,
   664	        result: toolResult,
   665	        error: toolError
   666	      };
   667	      executedToolCalls.push(executedRecord);
   668	      if(toolError && /REJECTED_UNINTENTIONAL_WIPE|REJECTED_STRUCTURAL_LOSS|HQ_PRECOMMIT_SMOKE_FAILED/.test(toolError)){
   669	        maxSteps=Math.min(48,maxSteps+2);
   670	        conversation?.onActivity?.({
   671	          phase:"auto-repair",status:"running",tool:toolName,
   672	          label:"Correcting the rejected code patch within this execution"
   673	        });
   674	      }
   675	
   676	      conversation?.onActivity?.({
   677	        phase: "tool",
   678	        status: toolError ? "failed" : "done",
   679	        label: toolError ? `${toolName} failed` : `${toolName} verified`,
   680	        tool: toolName
   681	      });
   682	
   683	      if (toolName === "geminx_tool_search" && !toolError && toolRegistry) {
   684	        // DeepSeek already supports native progressive tool disclosure; Google
   685	        // previously held a frozen first-turn list, so newly discovered tools
   686	        // were unreachable even when present in the authorised registry.
   687	        // Disclose the exact verified search matches for the NEXT Gemini call.
   688	        const expanded = progressivelyDiscloseToolSchemas(
   689	          nativeProviderTools,toolRegistry,toolResult,NATIVE_PROVIDER_TOOL_DECLARATION_LIMIT
   690	        ).filter((t) => isToolPermittedForOfficeAgent(resolvedOfficeAgent, t.function.name, thinkOnly).permitted);
   691	        if (expanded.length !== nativeProviderTools.length) {
   692	          nativeProviderTools=expanded;
   693	          const disclosedNames=new Set(nativeProviderTools.map(t=>t.function.name));
   694	          const expandedDefinitions=toolRegistry.listTools().filter(d=>disclosedNames.has(d.name));
   695	          geminiTools=formatGeminiTools(expandedDefinitions);
   696	          conversation?.onActivity?.({
   697	            phase:"capability",status:"done",
   698	            label:"Google Antigravity can now invoke newly discovered authorised tools"
   699	          });
   700	        }
   701	      }
   702	
   703	      functionResponses.push({
   704	        functionResponse: {
   705	          name: toolName,
   706	          response: {
   707	            output: toolError ? { error: toolError } : toolResult
   708	          }
   709	        }
   710	      });
   711	    }
   712	
   713	    contents.push({
   714	      role: "user",
   715	      parts: functionResponses
   716	    });
   717	  }
   718	
   719	  if (!finalMessage) {
   720	    // A model that stopped after tool calls did NOT produce a final verified
   721	    // answer. Do not fabricate "Google Antigravity verified" or success prose.
   722	    // The caller must reconcile actual tool receipts before any continuation.
   723	    throw new AntigravityCompanionError(
   724	      502,
   725	      "Google Gemini did not return a usable answer.",
   726	      `GEMINX_GEMINI_NO_REPLY_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED tool_calls=${executedToolCalls.length}`
   727	    );
   728	  }
   729	
   730	  const learning = await processCompanionLearning({
   731	    text: finalMessage,
   732	    provider: "google",
   733	    model: selectedModel,
   734	    objectiveId: correlationId ?? activeTaskId ?? conversationId ?? "geminx-companion",
   735	    sourceReceiptId: correlationId,
   736	    toolCalls: executedToolCalls
   737	  });
   738	  finalMessage = learning.message || finalMessage;
   739	  finalMessage = enforceFounderReply(finalMessage, conversation?.displayIntent ?? intent);
   740	
   741	  if (store && conversationRecord && conversationId) {
   742	    const assistantAttachments = correlationId && toolRegistry
   743	      ? [...toolRegistry.artifacts(correlationId)]
   744	      : [];
   745	    store.appendTurn(conversationRecord, {
   746	      role: "assistant",
   747	      content: finalMessage,
   748	      at: new Date().toISOString(),
   749	      ...(assistantAttachments.length ? { attachments: assistantAttachments } : {})
   750	    }, {
   751	      active_run_id: activeRunId,
   752	      active_task_id: activeTaskId,
   753	      domain: conversationRecord.domain || "general"
   754	    });
   755	  }
   756	
   757	  const providerInteractionId = lastGoogleResponseId
   758	    ? `google_gen_${lastGoogleResponseId}`
   759	    : `google_gen_sha256_${lastGoogleResponseSha256 || computeSha256(`${selectedModel}:${correlationId || ""}:${finalMessage}`).slice(0, 24)}`;
   760	  const runtimeEnvironmentId = String(
   761	    process.env.RAILWAY_DEPLOYMENT_ID ||
   762	    process.env.RAILWAY_ENVIRONMENT_ID ||
   763	    process.env.RAILWAY_SERVICE_ID ||
   764	    process.env.RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA?.slice(0, 12) ||
   765	    "geminx-google-runtime"
   766	  ).trim();
   767	
   768	  const requiredBootPaths = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(OFFICE_SPECIALIST_BRAIN_PATHS, resolvedOfficeAgent)
   769	    ? OFFICE_SPECIALIST_BRAIN_PATHS[resolvedOfficeAgent as OfficeSpecialistBrainId]
   770	    : [];
   771	  const bootReceipt = buildAgentBootReceipt(resolvedOfficeAgent, resolvedCompanyScope, brain, requiredBootPaths);
   772	
   773	  return {
   774	    message: finalMessage,
   775	    model: selectedModel,
   776	    provider: "google",
   777	    brain_context_status: brain.status,
   778	    brain_sources: brain.sources,
   779	    boot_receipt: bootReceipt,
   780	    ...(correlationId ? { correlation_id: correlationId } : {}),
   781	    ...(conversationId ? { conversation_id: conversationId } : {}),
   782	    ...(activeRunId ? { active_run_id: activeRunId } : {}),
   783	    ...(executedToolCalls.length > 0 ? { tool_calls: executedToolCalls, multi_step: true } : {}),
   784	    ...(noProgressFuseTriggered ? { no_progress_observation_fuse: true } : {}),
   785	    ...(backgroundHandoff ? { background_handoff: backgroundHandoff } : {}),
   786	    receipt: {
   787	      provider: "google",
   788	      model: selectedModel,
   789	      interaction_id: providerInteractionId,
   790	      environment_id: runtimeEnvironmentId,
   791	      tool_calls_count: executedToolCalls.length,
   792	      boot_receipt: bootReceipt,
   793	      timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
   794	    },
   795	    usage: totalUsage
   796	  };
   797	}
   798	
   799	/**
   800	 * AGX's Google-first execution path. This uses the managed Interactions
   801	 * Antigravity agent, NOT the legacy Gemini generateContent chat endpoint.
   802	 * A manual DeepSeek choice keeps Antigravity in the ToolRegistry, but cannot
   803	 * run DeepSeek as the model within Google's hosted managed-agent process.
   804	 */
   805	export async function generateManagedAntigravityCompanionReplyWithTools(
   806	  intent: string,
   807	  correlationId: string | null,
   808	  toolRegistry: ToolRegistry | null,
   809	  _brainRetriever?: AgentBrainContextRetriever,
   810	  conversation?: AntigravityConversationOptions,
   811	  taskType: GeminxModelTaskType = "chat"
   812	): Promise<AntigravityCompanionReply> {
   813	  if (!toolRegistry) throw new AntigravityCompanionError(503, "Antigravity registry unavailable.", "AGX_HARNESS_REGISTRY_UNAVAILABLE");
   814	  const id = correlationId || randomUUID();
   815	  const store = conversation?.store ?? null;
   816	  const conversationId = conversation?.conversationId ?? null;
   817	  const priorRecord = store && conversationId
   818	    ? (store.get(conversationId) || store.create(conversationId, "antigravity"))
   819	    : null;
   820	  const priorSession = priorRecord?.model_preference === "antigravity" &&
   821	    priorRecord.managed_antigravity_session?.status === "completed"
   822	    ? priorRecord.managed_antigravity_session : null;
   823	  conversation?.onActivity?.({ phase: "model-switch", status: "running", label: "AGX · Google managed Antigravity · Gemini 3.8 Flash" });
   824	  const preflight = await toolRegistry.execute({
   825	    tool: "antigravity_harness_status", tool_call_id: "agx-status-" + randomUUID(),
   826	    correlation_id: id, args: {}
   827	  });
   828	  const caps = preflight.data && typeof preflight.data === "object"
   829	    ? preflight.data as Record<string, unknown> : {};
   830	  if (!preflight.ok || caps.configured !== true) {
   831	    throw new AntigravityCompanionError(503, "Google Antigravity harness is not configured.", "AGX_HARNESS_NOT_CONFIGURED");
   832	  }
   833	  if (taskType !== "chat" && Number(caps.mcp_server_count ?? 0) < 1) {
   834	    throw new AntigravityCompanionError(503, "AGX managed-agent MCP tools unavailable.", "AGX_HARNESS_MCP_NOT_VERIFIED");
   835	  }
   836	    const isBrian = conversation?.officeAgent === "brian" ||
   837	      (conversation?.conversationId && String(conversation.conversationId).startsWith("brian-"));
   838	    const brianHardCap = 400000;
   839	    const defaultTokenCap = isBrian ? brianHardCap : 4000000;
   840	    // Hard ceiling: Brian token ceiling must never be elevated past 400,000 by environment variables
   841	    const rawEnvCap = Number(process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_MAX_TOKENS);
   842	    const maxTokens = isBrian
   843	      ? (rawEnvCap && rawEnvCap > 0 ? Math.min(rawEnvCap, brianHardCap) : brianHardCap)
   844	      : (rawEnvCap || defaultTokenCap);
   845	
   846	    // P0 Brain Hydration: fetch Brian or agent brain context and inject it into the managed harness instruction
   847	    const effectiveOfficeAgent = (conversation?.officeAgent as OfficeSpecialistBrainId | undefined) || (isBrian ? "brian" : undefined);
   848	    const resolvedAgentId = normalizeOfficeAgentId(effectiveOfficeAgent ?? "ag", "ag");
   849	    const resolvedScope = normalizeCompanyScope(conversation?.companyScope, resolvedAgentId);
   850	    const effectiveRetriever = _brainRetriever || (
   851	      effectiveOfficeAgent && ["brian", "mark", "sophie", "kerry", "ads"].includes(effectiveOfficeAgent)
   852	        ? configuredOfficeSpecialistBrainContextRetriever(effectiveOfficeAgent)
   853	        : resolvedAgentId === "neo"
   854	        ? configuredNeoBrainContextRetriever()
   855	        : resolvedAgentId === "ag"
   856	        ? configuredAgBrainContextRetriever()
   857	        : configuredAgentBrainContextRetriever()
   858	    );
   859	
   860	    let brainSnapshot = null;
   861	    try {
   862	      brainSnapshot = await effectiveRetriever.retrieveIdentityCapsule();
   863	    } catch {
   864	      brainSnapshot = null;
   865	    }
   866	
   867	    const requiredManagedPaths = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(OFFICE_SPECIALIST_BRAIN_PATHS, resolvedAgentId)
   868	      ? OFFICE_SPECIALIST_BRAIN_PATHS[resolvedAgentId as OfficeSpecialistBrainId]
   869	      : [];
   870	    const managedBootReceipt = buildAgentBootReceipt(resolvedAgentId, resolvedScope, brainSnapshot, requiredManagedPaths);
   871	    // Never block Brian's conversation because GitHub Brain proof is late,
   872	    // missing or temporarily unavailable. A model-level 500 prevents repair.
   873	    // The model may inspect, explain and recover regardless of Brain health.
   874	    // Actual preview writes are separately controlled by scoped tool RBAC
   875	    // and by the pre-commit browser visual-quality gate.
   876	    const brianDoctrineVerified = resolvedAgentId !== "brian" || (
   877	      managedBootReceipt.boot_status === "VERIFIED" &&
   878	      managedBootReceipt.loaded_sources.includes("Website_Build_OS/ROB_LOVABLE_BUILD_MEMORY.md") &&
   879	      Boolean(brainSnapshot?.context?.includes("SOURCE FILE: Website_Build_OS/ROB_LOVABLE_BUILD_MEMORY.md"))
   880	    );
   881	
   882	    const recentTurns = (priorRecord?.turns ?? []).slice(-6);
   883	    const recentTranscriptSection = recentTurns.length > 0
   884	      ? "[RECENT LOCAL CONVERSATION TRANSCRIPT]\n" +
   885	        recentTurns.map((t) => `${String(t.role).toUpperCase()} (${t.at || "recent"}): ${String(t.content ?? "").slice(0, 1800)}`).join("\n\n") +
   886	        "\n\n"
   887	      : "";
   888	
   889	    let hydratedInstruction = `${recentTranscriptSection}${intent}`;
   890	    if (resolvedAgentId === "brian" && !brianDoctrineVerified) {
   891	      // Notice, not a gate: never fabricate "Brain verified" status.
   892	      // Brian can still respond and use tools to diagnose/fix the cause.
   893	      hydratedInstruction = "[BRAIN CONTEXT NOTICE: Canonical design doctrine could not be verified for this turn. Do not claim it was read. Continue responding and investigating normally; use approved preview write tools for their own pre-commit quality checks.]\n\n" + recentTranscriptSection + intent;
   894	    }
   895	    if (brainSnapshot && brainSnapshot.context && brainSnapshot.context.trim().length > 0) {
   896	      hydratedInstruction = `${resolvedAgentId === "brian" && !brianDoctrineVerified ? "[BRAIN CONTEXT NOTICE: Canonical doctrine unverified for this turn. Do not claim verified status.]\\n\\n" : ""}[AGENT IDENTITY & CANONICAL DOCTRINE]\\n${brainSnapshot.context}\\n\\n${recentTranscriptSection}[TASK INSTRUCTION]\\n${intent}`;
   897	    }
   898	
   899	    const response = await toolRegistry.execute({
   900	      tool: "antigravity_harness_execute", tool_call_id: "agx-exec-" + randomUUID(),
   901	      correlation_id: id, args: {
   902	        task_instruction: hydratedInstruction,
   903	        max_total_tokens: maxTokens,
   904	        active_office_agent: resolvedAgentId,
   905	        company_scope: resolvedScope,
   906	        ...(priorSession ? {
   907	          previous_interaction_id: priorSession.interaction_id,
   908	          environment_id: priorSession.environment_id
   909	        } : {})
   910	      }
   911	    });
   912	  const data = response.data && typeof response.data === "object"
   913	    ? response.data as Record<string, unknown> : {};
   914	  const output = String(data.output ?? "").trim();
   915	  // An authoritative LOCAL ToolRegistry rejection has not contacted Google.
   916	  // It must never become an "interaction unknown" with a fake pending
   917	  // provider mutation, nor get sent back to an opaque paid retry loop.
   918	  if (!response.ok && !data.interaction_id &&
   919	      (response.status_code === 403 ||
   920	       (response.status === "blocked" && response.blocked_reason))) {
   921	    const localReason = String(response.blocked_reason ?? response.error ?? "LOCAL_POLICY_DENIED")
   922	      .replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_:. -]/g, "_").slice(0, 180);
   923	    throw new AntigravityCompanionError(
   924	      403, "Google managed Antigravity dispatch was denied locally before reaching Google.",
   925	      "AGX_HARNESS_LOCAL_PREPROVIDER_DENIED: " + localReason
   926	    );
   927	  }
   928	  // Retain provider session identity even when completion is uncertain, but
   929	  // never automatically replay a pending/failed interaction as if it completed.
   930	  const interactionId = String(data.interaction_id ?? "").trim();
   931	  const environmentId = String(data.environment_id ?? "").trim();
   932	  const providerStatus = String(data.provider_status ?? response.status);
   933	  if (store && conversationId) {
   934	    const latest = store.get(conversationId);
   935	    if (latest) {
   936	      if (interactionId.length > 0 && interactionId.length <= 256 &&
   937	          !interactionId.startsWith("err-") && !interactionId.startsWith("fail-") &&
   938	          ![...interactionId, ...environmentId].some(ch => ch.charCodeAt(0) < 32)) {
   939	        store.save({
   940	          ...latest, managed_antigravity_session: {
   941	            interaction_id: interactionId,
   942	            environment_id: environmentId || latest.managed_antigravity_session?.environment_id || "",
   943	            status: providerStatus,
   944	            updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
   945	          },
   946	          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
   947	        });
   948	      } else if (latest.managed_antigravity_session) {
   949	        store.save({
   950	          ...latest, managed_antigravity_session: {
   951	            ...latest.managed_antigravity_session,
   952	            status: providerStatus || "failed",
   953	            updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
   954	          },
   955	          updated_at: new Date().toISOString()
   956	        });
   957	      }
   958	    }
   959	  }
   960	  if (!response.ok || response.status !== "succeeded" || (data.provider_status !== "completed" && !output) || !output) {
   961	    const innerCount = Array.isArray(data.tool_calls) ? data.tool_calls.length : 0;
   962	    const detail = String(response.error ?? data.output ?? data.thoughts ?? response.status).slice(0, 700);
   963	    throw new AntigravityCompanionError(
   964	      502,
   965	      "Google managed Antigravity did not complete.",
   966	      `AGX_HARNESS_NOT_COMPLETED: status=${providerStatus} interaction_id=${interactionId || "none"} remote_tool_calls=${innerCount} detail=${detail}`
   967	    );
   968	  }
   969	  const inner = (Array.isArray(data.tool_calls) ? data.tool_calls : [])
   970	    .filter((x): x is ToolCallRecord =>
   971	      x !== null && typeof x === "object" && !Array.isArray(x) &&
   972	      typeof (x as ToolCallRecord).tool === "string" &&
   973	      ["succeeded", "failed", "blocked"].includes(String((x as ToolCallRecord).status)));
   974	  const toolCalls = [...toolRegistry.trace(id), ...inner];
   975	  if (store) {
   976	    const stored = conversation?.conversationId ? store.get(conversation.conversationId) : null;
   977	    const record = stored || store.create(conversation?.conversationId || undefined);
   978	    const updated = store.appendTurn(record, {
   979	      role: "user", content: conversation?.displayIntent || intent, at: new Date().toISOString()
   980	    }, { active_run_id: conversation?.activeRunId ?? null, active_task_id: conversation?.activeTaskId ?? null,
   981	         domain: record.domain || "general" });
   982	    store.appendTurn(updated, { role: "assistant", content: output, at: new Date().toISOString() },
   983	      { active_run_id: conversation?.activeRunId ?? null, active_task_id: conversation?.activeTaskId ?? null,
   984	        domain: updated.domain || "general" });
   985	  }
   986	  return {
   987	    message: output, provider: "antigravity", model: "gemini-3.8-flash",
   988	    brain_context_status: brainSnapshot?.status ?? "empty",
   989	    brain_sources: brainSnapshot?.sources ?? [],
   990	    boot_receipt: managedBootReceipt,
   991	    correlation_id: id,
   992	    conversation_id: conversation?.conversationId ?? undefined,
   993	    active_run_id: conversation?.activeRunId ?? null,
   994	    tool_calls: toolCalls, multi_step: toolCalls.length > 0,
   995	    usage: { prompt_tokens: Number(data.prompt_tokens ?? 0),
   996	      candidates_tokens: Number(data.candidates_tokens ?? 0),
   997	      total_tokens: Number(data.tokens_used ?? 0) },
   998	    receipt: { provider: "google-managed-antigravity", model: "gemini-3.8-flash",
   999	      agent_id: caps.agent, interaction_id: data.interaction_id, environment_id: data.environment_id,
  1000	      status: data.provider_status, tool_calls_count: toolCalls.length,
  1001	      boot_receipt: managedBootReceipt,
  1002	      timestamp: new Date().toISOString() }
  1003	  };
  1004	}
