     1	// Antigravity Harness Adapter for GeminX
     2	// Connects GeminX AG agent mode to the official Google Antigravity Managed Agent Harness
     3	// (agent: "antigravity-preview-09-2026") via Google's Interactions API.
     4	// Enforces hard max_total_tokens budget controls, revenue firewall, and typed execution receipts.
     5	
     6	import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     7	import { type ToolCallRecord } from "./types.js";
     8	
     9	export interface AntigravityHarnessConfig {
    10	  apiKey?: string;
    11	  endpoint?: string;
    12	  agentId?: string;
    13	  maxTotalTokens?: number;
    14	  environmentType?: "remote" | "local";
    15	  workspaceSources?: Array<{ target: string; content: string }>;
    16	  mcpServers?: Array<{
    17	    name: string;
    18	    url: string;
    19	    headers?: Record<string, string>;
    20	    allowed_tools?: string[];
    21	  }>;
    22	}
    23	
    24	export interface AntigravityInteractionResult {
    25	  interaction_id: string;
    26	  environment_id?: string;
    27	  status: "completed" | "incomplete" | "failed" | "in_progress";
    28	  output_text: string;
    29	  total_tokens: number;
    30	  prompt_tokens?: number;
    31	  candidates_tokens?: number;
    32	  thoughts?: string;
    33	  tool_calls: ToolCallRecord[];
    34	  receipt: {
    35	    agent_id: string;
    36	    duration_ms: number;
    37	    budget_capped: boolean;
    38	    timestamp: string;
    39	  };
    40	}
    41	
    42	function extractOutputText(data: any): string {
    43	  if (typeof data?.output_text === "string" && data.output_text.trim()) {
    44	    return data.output_text.trim();
    45	  }
    46	  if (Array.isArray(data?.steps)) {
    47	    const texts: string[] = [];
    48	    for (const step of data.steps) {
    49	      if (step?.type === "model_output") {
    50	        if (Array.isArray(step?.content)) {
    51	          for (const c of step.content) {
    52	            if (typeof c === "string" && c.trim()) texts.push(c.trim());
    53	            else if (typeof c?.text === "string" && c.text.trim()) texts.push(c.text.trim());
    54	          }
    55	        } else if (typeof step?.content === "string" && step.content.trim()) {
    56	          texts.push(step.content.trim());
    57	        } else if (typeof step?.text === "string" && step.text.trim()) {
    58	          texts.push(step.text.trim());
    59	        }
    60	      }
    61	    }
    62	    if (texts.length) return texts.join("\n\n").trim();
    63	  }
    64	  if (typeof data?.output === "string" && data.output.trim()) {
    65	    return data.output.trim();
    66	  }
    67	  if (typeof data?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.[0]?.text === "string") {
    68	    return data.candidates[0].content.parts[0].text.trim();
    69	  }
    70	  return "";
    71	}
    72	
    73	export class AntigravityHarnessAdapter {
    74	  private apiKey: string;
    75	  private endpoint: string;
    76	  private agentId: string;
    77	  private maxTotalTokens: number;
    78	  private workspaceSources: Array<{ target: string; content: string }>;
    79	  private mcpServers: Array<{
    80	    name: string;
    81	    url: string;
    82	    headers?: Record<string, string>;
    83	    allowed_tools?: string[];
    84	  }>;
    85	
    86	  constructor(config: AntigravityHarnessConfig = {}) {
    87	    this.apiKey =
    88	      config.apiKey ||
    89	      process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_API_KEY ||
    90	      process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY ||
    91	      process.env.GOOGLE_API_KEY ||
    92	      "";
    93	    this.endpoint = config.endpoint || "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta";
    94	    this.agentId = config.agentId || process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_AGENT_ID || "antigravity-preview-09-2026";
    95	    this.maxTotalTokens = config.maxTotalTokens ||
    96	      Number(process.env.GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_MAX_TOKENS) ||
    97	      4000000;
    98	    this.workspaceSources = (config.workspaceSources || []).map((source) => ({ ...source }));
    99	    this.mcpServers = (config.mcpServers || []).map((server) => ({
   100	      ...server,
   101	      ...(server.headers ? { headers: { ...server.headers } } : {}),
   102	      ...(server.allowed_tools ? { allowed_tools: [...server.allowed_tools] } : {})
   103	    }));
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  public isConfigured(): boolean {
   107	    return this.apiKey.trim().length > 0;
   108	  }
   109	
   110	  public async execute(request: {
   111	    tool: string;
   112	    tool_call_id: string;
   113	    correlation_id?: string;
   114	    args: Record<string, unknown>;
   115	  }): Promise<{
   116	    ok: boolean;
   117	    status: string;
   118	    tool?: string;
   119	    tool_call_id: string;
   120	    correlation_id?: string;
   121	    data?: unknown;
   122	    error?: string;
   123	    started_at: string;
   124	    finished_at: string;
   125	    duration_ms: number;
   126	  }> {
   127	    const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
   128	    const startTime = Date.now();
   129	    const tool = request.tool;
   130	
   131	    if (tool === "antigravity_harness_status") {
   132	      return {
   133	        ok: true,
   134	        status: "succeeded",
   135	        tool: request.tool,
   136	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   137	        correlation_id: request.correlation_id,
   138	        data: {
   139	          configured: this.isConfigured(),
   140	          agent: this.agentId,
   141	          model: "gemini-3.8-flash",
   142	          endpoint: this.endpoint,
   143	          max_total_tokens: this.maxTotalTokens,
   144	          sandbox_environment: "remote",
   145	          workspace_source_count: this.workspaceSources.length,
   146	          mcp_server_count: this.mcpServers.length
   147	        },
   148	        started_at: startedAt,
   149	        finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   150	        duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime
   151	      };
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    if (tool === "antigravity_harness_execute") {
   155	      const instruction = String(request.args?.task_instruction ?? "");
   156	      const activeAgent = String(request.args?.active_office_agent ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   157	      // The managed Google request must never authenticate Brian as AG.
   158	      // AG's broad MCP server is excluded from every Brian execution.
   159	      const agentMcpServers = activeAgent === "brian"
   160	        ? this.mcpServers.filter((server) => server.name === "geminx_brian_preview_hub")
   161	        : this.mcpServers.filter((server) => server.name !== "geminx_brian_preview_hub");
   162	      if (activeAgent === "brian" && agentMcpServers.length !== 1) {
   163	        return {
   164	          ok: false, status: "blocked", tool: request.tool,
   165	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id, correlation_id: request.correlation_id,
   166	          error: "BRIAN_GOOGLE_MCP_PRINCIPAL_UNAVAILABLE",
   167	          started_at: startedAt, finished_at: new Date().toISOString(), duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime
   168	        };
   169	      }
   170	      const roleCeiling = activeAgent === "brian" ? 400_000 : this.maxTotalTokens;
   171	      const serverCeiling = Math.min(this.maxTotalTokens, roleCeiling);
   172	      const requestedMax = Number(request.args?.max_total_tokens);
   173	      const maxTokens = Number.isFinite(requestedMax) && requestedMax > 0
   174	        ? Math.min(requestedMax, serverCeiling)
   175	        : serverCeiling;
   176	      const previousInteractionId = typeof request.args?.previous_interaction_id === "string" ? request.args.previous_interaction_id : undefined;
   177	      const environmentId = typeof request.args?.environment_id === "string" ? request.args.environment_id : undefined;
   178	
   179	      const res = await this.executeTask({
   180	        input: instruction,
   181	        correlationId: request.correlation_id,
   182	        maxTotalTokens: maxTokens,
   183	        previousInteractionId,
   184	        environmentId,
   185	        workspaceSources: this.workspaceSources,
   186	        mcpServers: agentMcpServers
   187	      });
   188	
   189	      return {
   190	        ok: res.status === "completed",
   191	        // Provider lifecycle statuses must be translated to GeminX ToolStatus.
   192	        status: res.status === "completed" ? "succeeded" : res.status === "failed" ? "failed" : "blocked",
   193	        tool: request.tool,
   194	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   195	        correlation_id: request.correlation_id,
   196	        data: {
   197	          output: res.output_text,
   198	          provider_status: res.status,
   199	          interaction_id: res.interaction_id,
   200	          environment_id: res.environment_id,
   201	          tokens_used: res.total_tokens,
   202	          thoughts: res.thoughts,
   203	          tool_calls: res.tool_calls,
   204	          receipt: res.receipt
   205	        },
   206	        error: res.status === "failed" ? res.output_text : undefined,
   207	        started_at: startedAt,
   208	        finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   209	        duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime
   210	      };
   211	    }
   212	
   213	    return {
   214	      ok: false,
   215	      status: "blocked",
   216	      tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   217	      correlation_id: request.correlation_id,
   218	      error: `Unknown tool: ${tool}`,
   219	      started_at: startedAt,
   220	      finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
   221	      duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime
   222	    };
   223	  }
   224	
   225	  public async executeTask(options: {
   226	    input: string;
   227	    correlationId?: string;
   228	    maxTotalTokens?: number;
   229	    previousInteractionId?: string;
   230	    environmentId?: string;
   231	    workspaceSources?: Array<{ target: string; content: string }>;
   232	    mcpServers?: Array<{
   233	      name: string;
   234	      url: string;
   235	      headers?: Record<string, string>;
   236	      allowed_tools?: string[];
   237	    }>;
   238	  }): Promise<AntigravityInteractionResult> {
   239	    const startTime = Date.now();
   240	    const correlationId = options.correlationId || randomUUID();
   241	    const maxTokens = options.maxTotalTokens || this.maxTotalTokens;
   242	
   243	    if (!this.isConfigured()) {
   244	      return {
   245	        interaction_id: `mock-${correlationId}`,
   246	        status: "incomplete",
   247	        output_text: "BLOCKED: GEMINI_API_KEY or GEMINX_ANTIGRAVITY_API_KEY not configured in environment.",
   248	        total_tokens: 0,
   249	        tool_calls: [],
   250	        receipt: {
   251	          agent_id: this.agentId,
   252	          duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   253	          budget_capped: false,
   254	          timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
   255	        }
   256	      };
   257	    }
   258	
   259	    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
   260	      agent: this.agentId,
   261	      input: options.input,
   262	      agent_config: {
   263	        type: "antigravity",
   264	        model: "gemini-3.8-flash",
   265	        max_total_tokens: maxTokens
   266	      }
   267	    };
   268	
   269	    if (options.previousInteractionId) {
   270	      payload.previous_interaction_id = options.previousInteractionId;
   271	    }
   272	
   273	    if (options.environmentId) {
   274	      payload.environment = options.environmentId;
   275	    } else {
   276	      payload.environment = {
   277	        type: "remote",
   278	        sources: (options.workspaceSources || []).map((s) => ({
   279	          type: "inline",
   280	          target: s.target,
   281	          content: s.content
   282	        }))
   283	      };
   284	    }
   285	
   286	    if (options.mcpServers && options.mcpServers.length > 0) {
   287	      payload.tools = options.mcpServers.map((mcp) => {
   288	        const cleanName = mcp.name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g, "_");
   289	        const entry: Record<string, unknown> = {
   290	          type: "mcp_server",
   291	          name: cleanName,
   292	          url: mcp.url
   293	        };
   294	        if (mcp.headers && Object.keys(mcp.headers).length > 0) {
   295	          entry.headers = mcp.headers;
   296	        }
   297	        if (Array.isArray(mcp.allowed_tools) && mcp.allowed_tools.length > 0) {
   298	          entry.allowed_tools = mcp.allowed_tools;
   299	        }
   300	        return entry;
   301	      });
   302	    }
   303	
   304	    const targetUrl = `${this.endpoint}/interactions`;
   305	
   306	    let currentPayload: Record<string, unknown> = payload;
   307	    let data: any = null;
   308	    let totalTokens = 0;
   309	    const parsedToolCalls: ToolCallRecord[] = [];
   310	    const accumulatedOutputs: string[] = [];
   311	    const maxContinuationTurns = 6;
   312	
   313	    try {
   314	      for (let turn = 0; turn < maxContinuationTurns; turn++) {
   315	        const response = await fetch(targetUrl, {
   316	          method: "POST",
   317	          headers: {
   318	            "x-goog-api-key": this.apiKey,
   319	            "content-type": "application/json"
   320	          },
   321	          body: JSON.stringify(currentPayload)
   322	        });
   323	
   324	        if (!response.ok) {
   325	          const errorText = await response.text();
   326	          if ((response.status === 400 || response.status === 404) && currentPayload.previous_interaction_id && turn === 0) {
   327	            console.warn(`[antigravity-harness] previous_interaction_id expired or invalid (${response.status}), retrying as fresh interaction`);
   328	            delete currentPayload.previous_interaction_id;
   329	            delete currentPayload.environment;
   330	            currentPayload.environment = {
   331	              type: "remote",
   332	              sources: (options.workspaceSources || []).map((s) => ({
   333	                type: "inline",
   334	                target: s.target,
   335	                content: s.content
   336	              }))
   337	            };
   338	            continue;
   339	          }
   340	          return {
   341	            interaction_id: `err-${correlationId}`,
   342	            status: "failed",
   343	            output_text: `Antigravity Harness error (${response.status}): ${errorText}`,
   344	            total_tokens: totalTokens,
   345	            tool_calls: parsedToolCalls,
   346	            receipt: {
   347	              agent_id: this.agentId,
   348	              duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   349	              budget_capped: false,
   350	              timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
   351	            }
   352	          };
   353	        }
   354	
   355	        data = (await response.json()) as any;
   356	        const turnTokens = Number(data?.usage?.total_tokens ?? 0);
   357	        totalTokens = Math.max(totalTokens, turnTokens);
   358	        const turnText = extractOutputText(data);
   359	        if (turnText && !accumulatedOutputs.includes(turnText)) {
   360	          accumulatedOutputs.push(turnText);
   361	        }
   362	
   363	        if (Array.isArray(data?.steps)) {
   364	          for (const step of data.steps) {
   365	            if (step?.type === "mcp_server_tool_call" || step?.type === "function_call") {
   366	              const toolName = String(step.name || step.tool || "mcp_tool");
   367	              const toolId = String(step.id || step.tool_call_id || randomUUID());
   368	              const resultStep = data.steps.find((s: any) =>
   369	                (s?.type === "mcp_server_tool_result" || s?.type === "function_result") &&
   370	                (s.id === toolId || s.call_id === toolId || s.tool_call_id === toolId)
   371	              );
   372	              const hasError = Boolean(step.error || resultStep?.error);
   373	              if (!parsedToolCalls.some(tc => tc.tool_call_id === toolId)) {
   374	                parsedToolCalls.push({
   375	                  tool_call_id: toolId,
   376	                  correlation_id: correlationId,
   377	                  tool: toolName,
   378	                  action: toolName,
   379	                  args: (step.arguments || step.args || {}) as Record<string, unknown>,
   380	                  status: hasError ? "failed" : "succeeded",
   381	                  started_at: step.started_at || new Date().toISOString(),
   382	                  finished_at: resultStep?.finished_at || new Date().toISOString(),
   383	                  result: resultStep?.result ?? resultStep?.response ?? null,
   384	                  error: step.error ? String(step.error) : resultStep?.error ? String(resultStep.error) : null
   385	                });
   386	              }
   387	            }
   388	          }
   389	        }
   390	
   391	        if (Array.isArray(data?.tool_calls)) {
   392	          for (const tc of data.tool_calls) {
   393	            const rawStatus = String(tc.status || (tc.error ? "failed" : tc.result !== undefined || tc.response !== undefined ? "succeeded" : "unknown"));
   394	            const validStatus = ["succeeded", "failed", "blocked"].includes(rawStatus)
   395	              ? (rawStatus as "succeeded" | "failed" | "blocked")
   396	              : tc.error
   397	                ? "failed"
   398	                : "failed";
   399	            const tcId = String(tc.id || tc.tool_call_id || randomUUID());
   400	            if (!parsedToolCalls.some(p => p.tool_call_id === tcId)) {
   401	              parsedToolCalls.push({
   402	                tool_call_id: tcId,
   403	                correlation_id: correlationId,
   404	                tool: String(tc.name || tc.tool || "mcp_tool"),
   405	                action: String(tc.name || tc.tool || "mcp_tool"),
   406	                args: (tc.args || tc.parameters || {}) as Record<string, unknown>,
   407	                status: validStatus,
   408	                started_at: tc.started_at || new Date().toISOString(),
   409	                finished_at: tc.finished_at || new Date().toISOString(),
   410	                result: tc.result ?? tc.response ?? null,
   411	                error: tc.error ? String(tc.error) : null
   412	              });
   413	            }
   414	          }
   415	        }
   416	
   417	        const isBudgetHit = totalTokens >= maxTokens;
   418	        if (data?.status === "completed" || data?.status === "failed") {
   419	          break;
   420	        }
   421	
   422	        if (data?.status === "incomplete" && data?.id && (!isBudgetHit || turn < maxContinuationTurns - 1)) {
   423	          const envId = data?.environment_id || (typeof data?.environment === "string" ? data.environment : data?.environment?.id);
   424	          currentPayload = {
   425	            agent: this.agentId,
   426	            input: "continue",
   427	            previous_interaction_id: data.id,
   428	            agent_config: {
   429	              type: "antigravity",
   430	              model: "gemini-3.8-flash",
   431	              max_total_tokens: maxTokens
   432	            },
   433	            ...(envId ? { environment: envId } : {})
   434	          };
   435	          continue;
   436	        }
   437	        break;
   438	      }
   439	
   440	      const isBudgetHit = totalTokens >= maxTokens;
   441	      let textOutput = extractOutputText(data) || accumulatedOutputs[accumulatedOutputs.length - 1] || "";
   442	      // Google can return status=completed with no model_output on the initial
   443	      // response. Re-read that SAME interaction via GET; never POST 'continue'
   444	      // or replay tool mutations simply to recover a missing final message.
   445	      if (data?.status === "completed" && !textOutput &&
   446	          typeof data.id === "string" && /^v1_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,256}$/.test(data.id)) {
   447	        // A managed agent can finish after its last tool call while the
   448	        // terminal text is not yet visible in the first server response.
   449	        // Only GET the ORIGINAL interaction, never POST "continue" here:
   450	        // any new interaction can duplicate external source writes.
   451	        const originalInteractionId = data.id as string;
   452	        for (let readAttempt = 0; readAttempt < 3 && !textOutput; readAttempt++) {
   453	          if (readAttempt > 0) {
   454	            await new Promise<void>(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 250 * readAttempt));
   455	          }
   456	          try {
   457	            const readback = await fetch(`${targetUrl}/${encodeURIComponent(originalInteractionId)}`, {
   458	              method: "GET",
   459	              headers: { "x-goog-api-key": this.apiKey },
   460	              signal: AbortSignal.timeout(8_000)
   461	            });
   462	            if (!readback.ok) {
   463	              // 401/403/404 will not be repaired by a retry; do not mutate.
   464	              if ([401, 403, 404].includes(readback.status)) break;
   465	              continue;
   466	            }
   467	            const snapshot = await readback.json() as any;
   468	            if (snapshot?.id !== originalInteractionId) {
   469	              console.warn("[antigravity-harness] completed interaction readback identity mismatch; no replay");
   470	              break;
   471	            }
   472	            // A GET result with the right ID is authoritative for text, not
   473	            // proof of any specific GitHub/other external write.
   474	            textOutput = extractOutputText(snapshot);
   475	            if (textOutput) {
   476	              console.info("[antigravity-harness] recovered completed model_output via read-only GET");
   477	            }
   478	          } catch {
   479	            console.warn("[antigravity-harness] completed interaction GET unavailable; preserving provider receipts");
   480	          }
   481	        }
   482	      }
   483	      const isComplete = data?.status === "completed" || (Boolean(textOutput) && data?.status !== "failed");
   484	      return {
   485	        interaction_id: data?.id || `int-${correlationId}`,
   486	        environment_id: data?.environment_id || (typeof data?.environment === "string" ? data.environment : data?.environment?.id),
   487	        status: isComplete ? "completed" : (data?.status || "incomplete"),
   488	        output_text: textOutput,
   489	        total_tokens: totalTokens,
   490	        prompt_tokens: data?.usage?.total_input_tokens ?? data?.usage?.prompt_tokens,
   491	        candidates_tokens: data?.usage?.total_output_tokens ?? data?.usage?.candidates_tokens,
   492	        thoughts: data?.thoughts ||
   493	          data?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts?.find((p: any) => p.thought)?.thought ||
   494	          (Array.isArray(data?.steps)
   495	            ? (data.steps.find((s: any) => s.thought)?.thought ||
   496	               data.steps.find((s: any) => s.type === "thought")?.summary?.[0]?.text ||
   497	               data.steps.find((s: any) => s.type === "thought")?.content?.[0]?.text ||
   498	               data.steps.find((s: any) => s.type === "thought")?.text)
   499	            : undefined),
   500	        tool_calls: parsedToolCalls,
   501	        receipt: {
   502	          agent_id: this.agentId,
   503	          duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   504	          budget_capped: isBudgetHit,
   505	          timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
   506	        }
   507	      };
   508	    } catch (err: any) {
   509	      return {
   510	        interaction_id: `fail-${correlationId}`,
   511	        status: "failed",
   512	        output_text: `Network failure connecting to Antigravity Harness: ${err?.message || String(err)}`,
   513	        total_tokens: 0,
   514	        tool_calls: [],
   515	        receipt: {
   516	          agent_id: this.agentId,
   517	          duration_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   518	          budget_capped: false,
   519	          timestamp: new Date().toISOString()
   520	        }
   521	      };
   522	    }
   523	  }
   524	}

    76	export interface ProviderHealthRecord {
    77	  provider: ExecutionProvider;
    78	  status: ProviderHealthStatus;
    79	  last_checked: string;
    80	  cooldown_until?: string;
    81	  last_error?: string;

    84	// In-memory / durable provider health tracker
    85	const providerState: Record<ExecutionProvider, ProviderHealthRecord> = {
    86	  google: { provider: "google", status: "READY", last_checked: new Date().toISOString() },
    87	  deepseek: {
    88	    provider: "deepseek",
    89	    status: process.env.DEEPSEEK_CREDIT_EXHAUSTED === "true" ? "CREDIT_EXHAUSTED" : "READY",
    90	    last_checked: new Date().toISOString(),
    91	  },
    92	  openai: {
    93	    provider: "openai",
    94	    status: process.env.GEMINX_OPENAI_SPEND_ENABLED === "true" ? "READY" : "BUDGET_BLOCKED",
    95	    last_checked: new Date().toISOString(),
    96	  },
    97	};

    99	import { resolveGoogleApiKey } from "../companion/antigravity-chat.js";
   100	
   101	export function getProviderHealth(provider: ExecutionProvider): ProviderHealthRecord {
   102	  const rec = providerState[provider];
   103	  if (rec.cooldown_until && new Date().toISOString() < rec.cooldown_until) {
   104	    return rec;
   105	  }
   106	  return rec;
   107	}
   108	
   109	export function updateProviderHealth(
   110	  provider: ExecutionProvider,
   111	  status: ProviderHealthStatus,
   112	  error?: string,
   113	  cooldownSeconds = 0
   114	): void {
   115	  const now = new Date();
   116	  const cooldownUntil = cooldownSeconds > 0 ? new Date(now.getTime() + cooldownSeconds * 1000).toISOString() : undefined;
   117	  providerState[provider] = {
   118	    provider,
   119	    status,
   120	    last_checked: now.toISOString(),
   121	    last_error: error,
   122	    cooldown_until: cooldownUntil,
   123	  };
   124	}
   125	
   126	export class UniversalModelRouter {
   127	  constructor(
   128	    private readonly toolRegistry?: ToolRegistry,
   129	    private readonly runtimeRoot = geminxPersistentRuntimeRoot()
   130	  ) {}
   131	
   132	  /**
   133	   * Determine the best eligible provider in strict compliance with Operating Law:
   134	   * 1. Google Primary for autonomous engineering and reasoning.
   135	   * 2. DeepSeek Economy for routine / high-frequency tasks when credit is available.
   136	   * 3. OpenAI Code Auditor for independent review, or explicitly authorized execution fallback (never Astra).
   137	   */
   138	  public selectProvider(req: ModelExecutionRequest): { provider: ExecutionProvider | "none"; model: string; reason: string } {
   139	    // Check preferred provider if healthy
   140	    if (req.preferred_provider) {
   141	      const prefHealth = getProviderHealth(req.preferred_provider);
   142	      if (prefHealth.status === "READY") {
   143	        return {
   144	          provider: req.preferred_provider,
   145	          model: this.defaultModelFor(req.preferred_provider, req.task_type),
   146	          reason: `Preferred provider ${req.preferred_provider} is READY.`,
   147	        };
   148	      }
   149	    }
   150	
   151	    if (req.task_type === "code_audit") {
   152	      const openAiHealth = getProviderHealth("openai");
   153	      if (openAiHealth.status === "READY") {
   154	        return {
   155	          provider: "openai",
   156	          model: "gpt-6.1-sol",
   157	          reason: "Code audit strictly routed to independent OpenAI reviewer gpt-6.1-sol.",
   158	        };
   159	      }
   160	      return {
   161	        provider: "none",
   162	        model: "none",
   163	        reason: `OpenAI code auditor unavailable (${openAiHealth.status}); audit must report REVIEW_UNAVAILABLE.`,
   164	      };
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    if (req.task_type === "routine") {
   168	      const deepseekHealth = getProviderHealth("deepseek");
   169	      if (deepseekHealth.status === "READY") {
   170	        return {
   171	          provider: "deepseek",
   172	          model: "deepseek-flash",
   173	          reason: "Routine task routed to economy provider DeepSeek.",
   174	        };
   175	      }
   176	      // Failover to Google Primary
   177	      const googleHealth = getProviderHealth("google");
   178	      if (googleHealth.status === "READY") {
   179	        return {
   180	          provider: "google",
   181	          model: "gemini-2.5-flash",
   182	          reason: `DeepSeek is ${deepseekHealth.status}; falling over to Google Primary for routine task.`,
   183	        };
   184	      }
   185	    }
   186	
   187	    // Default lane: Engineering, Reasoning, Business Action -> Google Primary
   188	    const googleHealth = getProviderHealth("google");
   189	    if (googleHealth.status === "READY") {
   190	      return {
   191	        provider: "google",
   192	        model: req.task_type === "engineering" ? "antigravity-gemini-3.8" : "gemini-2.5-flash",
   193	        reason: "Google Primary selected for engineering/reasoning/business lane.",
   194	      };
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    // Failover to DeepSeek if healthy
   198	    const deepseekHealth = getProviderHealth("deepseek");
   199	    if (deepseekHealth.status === "READY") {
   200	      return {
   201	        provider: "deepseek",
   202	        model: "deepseek-flash",
   203	        reason: `Google is ${googleHealth.status}; failing over to DeepSeek economy execution.`,
   204	      };
   205	    }
   206	
   207	    // Explicitly authorized OpenAI execution fallback ONLY (never Astra)
   208	    if (req.allow_openai_fallback && getProviderHealth("openai").status === "READY") {
   209	      return {
   210	        provider: "openai",
   211	        model: "gpt-6.1-sol",
   212	        reason: "Both Google and DeepSeek unavailable; explicitly authorised OpenAI execution fallback engaged.",
   213	      };
   214	    }
   215	
   216	    return {
   217	      provider: "none",
   218	      model: "none",
   219	      reason: `No authorised healthy provider available (Google: ${googleHealth.status}, DeepSeek: ${deepseekHealth.status}).`,
   220	    };
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  private defaultModelFor(provider: ExecutionProvider, taskType: TaskType): string {
   224	    if (provider === "google") {
   225	      return taskType === "engineering" ? "antigravity-gemini-3.8" : "gemini-2.5-flash";
   226	    }
   227	    if (provider === "deepseek") {
   228	      return "deepseek-flash";
   229	    }
   230	    return "gpt-6.1-sol";
   231	  }
   232	
   233	  /**
   234	   * Execute task envelope through universal contract:
   235	   * - Hydrates and preserves canonical objective with SHA-256.
   236	   * - Executes against shared ToolRegistry.
   237	   * - Reconciles mutations idempotently across failovers.
   238	   */
   239	  public async executeEnvelope(req: ModelExecutionRequest): Promise<UniversalExecutionResult> {
   240	    const startTime = Date.now();
   241	    const receiptId = `exec_rec_${req.parent_job_id.slice(0, 8)}_${randomUUID().slice(0, 6)}`;
   242	
   243	    // 1. Ensure Canonical Objective is safely preserved in durable storage
   244	    const canonicalRecord = saveCanonicalObjective({
   245	      jobId: req.parent_job_id,
   246	      text: req.raw_objective,
   247	      runtimeRoot: this.runtimeRoot,
   248	    });
   249	
   250	    if (canonicalRecord.utf8_sha256 !== req.objective_sha256) {
   251	      return {
   252	        ok: false,
   253	        parent_job_id: req.parent_job_id,
   254	        objective_sha256: canonicalRecord.utf8_sha256,
   255	        status: "FAILED",
   256	        provider: "none",
   257	        model: "none",
   258	        output_text: "OBJECTIVE_HASH_MISMATCH: Provided objective SHA-256 does not match canonical UTF-8 hash.",
   259	        tool_calls: [],
   260	        verified_steps: 0,
   261	        actual_spend_usd: 0,
   262	        latency_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   263	        receipt_id: receiptId,
   264	      };
   265	    }
   266	
   267	    // 2. Select Eligible Provider
   268	    const route = this.selectProvider(req);
   269	    if (route.provider === "none") {
   270	      return {
   271	        ok: false,
   272	        parent_job_id: req.parent_job_id,
   273	        objective_sha256: req.objective_sha256,
   274	        status: "WAITING_PROVIDER",
   275	        provider: "none",
   276	        model: "none",
   277	        output_text: `BLOCKED_NO_PROVIDER: ${route.reason}`,
   278	        tool_calls: [],
   279	        verified_steps: 0,
   280	        actual_spend_usd: 0,
   281	        latency_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   282	        receipt_id: receiptId,
   283	      };
   284	    }
   285	
   286	    // The native provider clients share the ToolRegistry and use autonomous
   287	    // tool selection. Never fabricate "completed" from a provider selection.
   288	    // OpenAI code audit requires a SHA-bound diff packet; this generic job
   289	    // envelope cannot perform it without the dedicated review contract.
   290	    if (this.toolRegistry && route.provider !== "openai" && req.maximum_spend_usd > 0) {
   291	      try {
   292	        const intent = req.raw_objective;
   293	        const correlationId = req.parent_job_id;
   294	        const reply = route.provider === "google"
   295	          ? await (await import("../companion/antigravity-chat.js"))
   296	              .generateAntigravityCompanionReplyWithTools(
   297	                intent, correlationId, this.toolRegistry, undefined,
   298	                { agentMode: req.task_type === "engineering" ? "ag" : "neo",
   299	                  activeTaskId: req.parent_job_id, activeRunId: req.parent_job_id }
   300	              )
   301	          : await (await import("../companion/deepseek-chat.js"))
   302	              .generateCompanionReplyWithTools(
   303	                intent, correlationId, this.toolRegistry, undefined,
   304	                { agentMode: req.task_type === "engineering" ? "ag" : "neo",
   305	                  activeTaskId: req.parent_job_id, activeRunId: req.parent_job_id },
   306	                req.task_type === "engineering" ? "repair" : "chat"
   307	              );
   308	
   309	        const toolCalls = reply.tool_calls ?? [];
   310	        const succeeded = toolCalls.filter(call => call.status === "succeeded");
   311	        const unresolved = toolCalls.some(call => call.status !== "succeeded");
   312	        const needsProof = req.task_type === "engineering"
   313	          || req.task_type === "business_action" || req.required_capabilities.length > 0;
   314	        const hasAnswer = Boolean(reply.message?.trim());
   315	        const completed = hasAnswer && !unresolved && (!needsProof || succeeded.length > 0);
   316	        return {
   317	          ok: completed,
   318	          parent_job_id: req.parent_job_id,
   319	          objective_sha256: req.objective_sha256,
   320	          status: completed ? "COMPLETED" : "PARTIAL",
   321	          provider: route.provider,
   322	          model: reply.model,
   323	          output_text: reply.message,
   324	          tool_calls: toolCalls,
   325	          verified_steps: succeeded.length,
   326	          // The provider chat replies expose usage, not billed cost.
   327	          // Never invent an actual dollar amount from token counts.
   328	          spend_metered: false,
   329	          latency_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   330	          receipt_id: receiptId,
   331	        };
   332	      } catch (error) {
   333	        const cause = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
   334	        // Ambiguous failures after a tool call must NOT cause blind cross-provider
   335	        // replay. Existing continuation receipts determine the safe next action.
   336	        return {
   337	          ok: false,
   338	          parent_job_id: req.parent_job_id,
   339	          objective_sha256: req.objective_sha256,
   340	          status: "BLOCKED",
   341	          provider: route.provider,
   342	          model: route.model,
   343	          output_text: `MODEL_PROVIDER_OR_EXECUTION_BLOCKED: ${cause}`,
   344	          tool_calls: [],
   345	          verified_steps: 0,
   346	          spend_metered: false,
   347	          latency_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   348	          receipt_id: receiptId,
   349	        };
   350	      }
   351	    }
   352	
   353	    // A routing decision is NOT a provider call or a verified tool execution.
   354	    // This module must remain fail-closed until the selected provider's actual
   355	    // adapter is invoked and the existing tool receipts have been reconciled.
   356	    return {
   357	      ok: false,
   358	      parent_job_id: req.parent_job_id,
   359	      objective_sha256: req.objective_sha256,
   360	      status: "BLOCKED",
   361	      provider: route.provider,
   362	      model: route.model,
   363	      output_text: `MODEL_EXECUTION_ADAPTER_NOT_BOUND: ${route.provider} (${route.model}) selected, but no provider call or verified execution occurred.`,
   364	      tool_calls: [],
   365	      verified_steps: 0,
   366	      actual_spend_usd: 0,
   367	      latency_ms: Date.now() - startTime,
   368	      receipt_id: receiptId,
   369	    };
   370	  }
   371	}
   372	
   373	export type OfficeTaskType =
   374	  | "routine"
   375	  | "reasoning"
   376	  | "engineering"
   377	  | "business_action"
   378	  | "code_audit"
   379	  | "media";
   380	
   381	export interface OfficeModelRoutingRequest {
   382	  agent_id: OfficeAgentId | string;
   383	  task_type?: OfficeTaskType;
   384	  manual_override?: string | null;
   385	  google_quota_verified?: boolean;
   386	  google_free_tier_eligible?: boolean;
   387	  approved_paid_spend_ceiling_usd?: number;
   388	  current_task_spend_usd?: number;
   389	  required_capabilities?: string[];
   390	  intent?: string;
   391	}
   392	
   393	export interface OfficeModelRoutingDecision {
   394	  agent_id: OfficeAgentId;
   395	  selected_model: string;
   396	  provider: "antigravity" | "google" | "deepseek" | "openai";
   397	  lane: "managed_agent_executor" | "reasoning_planner" | "economy_executor" | "specialized_media_tool";
   398	  think_only: boolean;
   399	  mutation_tools_allowed: boolean;
   400	  reason: string;
   401	  harness_tools_retained: boolean;
   402	  spend_ceiling_usd: number;
   403	  spend_ceiling_exceeded: boolean;
   404	  free_tier_claimed: boolean;
   405	  company_scope: string;
   406	}
   407	
   408	const MUTATION_TOOL_PATTERN =
   409	  /create|update|write|delete|drop|remove|exec|shell|deploy|send|publish|tap|click|press|install|patch|post/i;
   410	
   411	export function isToolPermittedForOfficeAgent(
   412	  agentId: OfficeAgentId | string,
   413	  toolName: string,
   414	  thinkOnly = false
   415	): { permitted: boolean; reason?: string } {
   416	  const normAgent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(agentId, "neo");
   417	  const manifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[normAgent];
   418	
   419	  // Think-only mode strictly forbids all mutating tools regardless of agent permissions
   420	  if (thinkOnly && MUTATION_TOOL_PATTERN.test(toolName)) {
   421	    return {
   422	      permitted: false,
   423	      reason: `Pro/Argon thinking-only planner mode is active for ${manifest.display_name}; mutation tools (${toolName}) are strictly disabled.`
   424	    };
   425	  }
   426	
   427	  // Denied prefixes
   428	  if (manifest.denied_tool_prefixes.some(prefix => toolName.startsWith(prefix))) {
   429	    return {
   430	      permitted: false,
   431	      reason: `Tool '${toolName}' matches denied prefix for ${manifest.display_name}.`
   432	    };
   433	  }
   434	
   435	  // Brian preview-only GitHub mutations are separately resource-fenced by
   436	  // the authoritative ToolRegistry. This permission never grants general
   437	  // engineering or production deployment access.
   438	  if (normAgent === "brian" && [
   439	    "github_create_branch", "github_create_or_update_file", "github_create_pull_request"
   440	  ].includes(toolName)) return { permitted: true };
   441	
   442	  // Denied tools
   443	  if (manifest.denied_tools.includes(toolName)) {
   444	    return {
   445	      permitted: false,
   446	      reason: `Tool '${toolName}' is explicitly denied for ${manifest.display_name}.`
   447	    };
   448	  }
   449	
   450	  // Permitted tools
   451	  if (manifest.permitted_tools.includes(toolName)) {
   452	    return { permitted: true };
   453	  }
   454	
   455	  // Permitted prefixes
   456	  if (manifest.permitted_tool_prefixes.some(prefix => toolName.startsWith(prefix))) {
   457	    return { permitted: true };
   458	  }
   459	
   460	  return {
   461	    permitted: false,
   462	    reason: `Tool '${toolName}' is not authorized in manifest for ${manifest.display_name}.`
   463	  };
   464	}
   465	
   466	export function routeOfficeModel(req: OfficeModelRoutingRequest): OfficeModelRoutingDecision {
   467	  const agent = normalizeOfficeAgentId(req.agent_id, "neo");
   468	  const manifest = OFFICE_AGENT_MANIFESTS[agent];
   469	  const companyScope = manifest?.primary_company_scope ?? "holding";
   470	  const spendCeiling = req.approved_paid_spend_ceiling_usd ?? manifest.max_spend_usd_per_task;
   471	  const currentSpend = req.current_task_spend_usd ?? 0;
   472	  const spendExceeded = currentSpend >= spendCeiling;
   473	  const freeTierClaimed = req.google_free_tier_eligible === true && req.google_quota_verified === true;
   474	
   475	  const rawOverride = typeof req.manual_override === "string" ? req.manual_override.trim().toLowerCase() : null;
   476	  const isReasoning = req.task_type === "reasoning" ||
   477	    (typeof req.intent === "string" && /\b(?:architect(?:ure)?|deep\s+reasoning|complex\s+proof|design\s+audit)\b/i.test(req.intent));
   478	
   479	  // 1. Spend ceiling preflight: if spend ceiling is exceeded, paid execution is blocked
   480	  if (spendExceeded) {
   481	    return {
   482	      agent_id: agent,
   483	      selected_model: "gemini-2.5-flash",
   484	      provider: "google",
   485	      lane: "reasoning_planner",
   486	      think_only: true,
   487	      mutation_tools_allowed: false,
   488	      reason: `Approved task spend ceiling reached ($${spendCeiling.toFixed(2)}); further paid execution blocked.`,
   489	      harness_tools_retained: false,
   490	      spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   491	      spend_ceiling_exceeded: true,
   492	      free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   493	      company_scope: companyScope
   494	    };
   495	  }
   496	
   497	  // Founder P0: Brian's website work is Google managed Antigravity ONLY.
   498	  // Neither a stale HQ model preference nor provider cooldown may silently
   499	  // divert Brian to DeepSeek. Provider unavailability must fail closed.
   500	  if (agent === "brian") {
   501	    return {
   502	      agent_id: "brian",
   503	      selected_model: "antigravity",
   504	      provider: "antigravity",
   505	      lane: "managed_agent_executor",
   506	      think_only: false,
   507	      mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   508	      reason: "Founder-pinned Brian Google Antigravity API and managed MCP harness; no automatic provider substitution.",
   509	      harness_tools_retained: true,
   510	      spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   511	      spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   512	      free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   513	      company_scope: companyScope
   514	    };
   515	  }
   516	
   517	  // 2. Manual override handling
   518	  if (rawOverride) {
   519	    if (["deepseek", "deepseek-flash", "deepseek-v4-pro"].includes(rawOverride)) {
   520	      return {
   521	        agent_id: agent,
   522	        selected_model: rawOverride,
   523	        provider: "deepseek",
   524	        lane: "economy_executor",
   525	        think_only: false,
   526	        mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   527	        reason: agent === "ag"
   528	          ? "Founder explicitly selected DeepSeek for AGX; authorized Antigravity harness tools retained without claiming managed agent execution."
   529	          : `Founder explicitly selected ${rawOverride} for ${manifest.display_name}.`,
   530	        harness_tools_retained: agent === "ag",
   531	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   532	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   533	        free_tier_claimed: false,
   534	        company_scope: companyScope
   535	      };
   536	    }
   537	
   538	    if (rawOverride === "antigravity") {
   539	      return {
   540	        agent_id: agent,
   541	        selected_model: "antigravity",
   542	        provider: "antigravity",
   543	        lane: "managed_agent_executor",
   544	        think_only: false,
   545	        mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   546	        reason: `Google managed Antigravity harness selected for ${manifest.display_name}.`,
   547	        harness_tools_retained: true,
   548	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   549	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   550	        free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   551	        company_scope: companyScope
   552	      };
   553	    }
   554	
   555	    if (rawOverride === "gemini-pro") {
   556	      return {
   557	        agent_id: agent,
   558	        selected_model: "gemini-pro",
   559	        provider: "google",
   560	        lane: isReasoning ? "reasoning_planner" : "managed_agent_executor",
   561	        think_only: isReasoning,
   562	        mutation_tools_allowed: !isReasoning,
   563	        reason: isReasoning
   564	          ? "Google Pro/Argon thinking-only planner engaged; mutation tools disabled."
   565	          : `Manual Google Gemini Pro selected for ${manifest.display_name}.`,
   566	        harness_tools_retained: agent === "ag",
   567	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   568	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   569	        free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   570	        company_scope: companyScope
   571	      };
   572	    }
   573	
   574	    if (rawOverride === "gemini-2.5-flash" || rawOverride === "gemini-3.8-flash" || rawOverride === "gemini-3.1-flash-lite") {
   575	      return {
   576	        agent_id: agent,
   577	        selected_model: rawOverride,
   578	        provider: "google",
   579	        lane: "managed_agent_executor",
   580	        think_only: false,
   581	        mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   582	        reason: `Explicit Google Flash model '${rawOverride}' selected for ${manifest.display_name}.`,
   583	        harness_tools_retained: agent === "ag",
   584	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   585	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   586	        free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   587	        company_scope: companyScope
   588	      };
   589	    }
   590	
   591	    if (rawOverride === "astra" || rawOverride.startsWith("gpt-5.6") || rawOverride.startsWith("gpt-6")) {
   592	      return {
   593	        agent_id: agent,
   594	        selected_model: rawOverride,
   595	        provider: "openai",
   596	        lane: "reasoning_planner",
   597	        think_only: true,
   598	        mutation_tools_allowed: false,
   599	        reason: `Explicit OpenAI model '${rawOverride}' selected for ${manifest.display_name}.`,
   600	        harness_tools_retained: false,
   601	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   602	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   603	        free_tier_claimed: false,
   604	        company_scope: companyScope
   605	      };
   606	    }
   607	  }
   608	
   609	  // 3. Difficult reasoning and architecture: Google Pro/Argon strictly think-only with mutations disabled
   610	  if (isReasoning) {
   611	    return {
   612	      agent_id: agent,
   613	      selected_model: "gemini-pro",
   614	      provider: "google",
   615	      lane: "reasoning_planner",
   616	      think_only: true,
   617	      mutation_tools_allowed: false,
   618	      reason: "Difficult reasoning/architecture task classified: Google Pro/Argon thinking-only planner selected with mutation tools strictly disabled.",
   619	      harness_tools_retained: agent === "ag",
   620	      spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   621	      spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   622	      free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   623	      company_scope: companyScope
   624	    };
   625	  }
   626	
   627	  // 4. Per-agent default routing policy
   628	  switch (agent) {
   629	    case "ag":
   630	      // AGX defaults to Google's managed Antigravity harness (Gemini 3.8 Flash Interactions API)
   631	      // Never silently switch AGX to DeepSeek!
   632	      return {
   633	        agent_id: "ag",
   634	        selected_model: "antigravity",
   635	        provider: "antigravity",
   636	        lane: "managed_agent_executor",
   637	        think_only: false,
   638	        mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   639	        reason: "AGX defaults to Google managed Antigravity harness (Gemini 3.8 Flash).",
   640	        harness_tools_retained: true,
   641	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   642	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   643	        free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   644	        company_scope: companyScope
   645	      };
   646	
   647	    case "sophie":
   648	      if (req.task_type === "media") {
   649	        return {
   650	          agent_id: "sophie",
   651	          selected_model: "sophie-cloud-video",
   652	          provider: "google",
   653	          lane: "specialized_media_tool",
   654	          think_only: false,
   655	          mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   656	          reason: "Sophie deterministic video rendering (Chromium+FFmpeg+FFprobe) & Runway provider preserved; text LLM not substituted.",
   657	          harness_tools_retained: false,
   658	          spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   659	          spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   660	          free_tier_claimed: false,
   661	          company_scope: companyScope
   662	        };
   663	      }
   664	      return {
   665	        agent_id: "sophie",
   666	        selected_model: "gemini-2.5-flash",
   667	        provider: "google",
   668	        lane: "managed_agent_executor",
   669	        think_only: false,
   670	        mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   671	        reason: "Google Flash selected for Sophie creative scripting and brief coordination.",
   672	        harness_tools_retained: false,
   673	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   674	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   675	        free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   676	        company_scope: companyScope
   677	      };
   678	
   679	    case "neo":
   680	      // Preserve NEO's OpenClaw runtime, workspaces, approval gates and queues
   681	      return {
   682	        agent_id: "neo",
   683	        selected_model: freeTierClaimed ? "gemini-2.5-flash" : "deepseek-flash",
   684	        provider: freeTierClaimed ? "google" : "deepseek",
   685	        lane: "economy_executor",
   686	        think_only: false,
   687	        mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   688	        reason: freeTierClaimed
   689	          ? "NEO OpenClaw runtime with verified free Google quota for initial turn; tool continuations on DeepSeek."
   690	          : "NEO OpenClaw runtime with DeepSeek economy execution; approval gates and queues preserved.",
   691	        harness_tools_retained: false,
   692	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   693	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   694	        free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   695	        company_scope: companyScope
   696	      };
   697	
   698	    case "mark":
   699	    case "kerry":
   700	    case "ads":
   701	    case "geminx":
   702	    default:
   703	      return {
   704	        agent_id: agent,
   705	        selected_model: "gemini-2.5-flash",
   706	        provider: "google",
   707	        lane: "managed_agent_executor",
   708	        think_only: false,
   709	        mutation_tools_allowed: true,
   710	        reason: `Google Flash selected for routine workload on ${manifest.display_name}.`,
   711	        harness_tools_retained: false,
   712	        spend_ceiling_usd: spendCeiling,
   713	        spend_ceiling_exceeded: false,
   714	        free_tier_claimed: freeTierClaimed,
   715	        company_scope: companyScope
   716	      };
   717	  }
   718	}
   719	
   720	export function isMutatingToolName(toolName: string): boolean {
   721	  return MUTATION_TOOL_PATTERN.test(String(toolName || ""));
   722	}
   723	
   724	/**
   725	 * Returns true ONLY if no mutating tool call has already executed or been left in an uncertain state.
   726	 * Never replay uncertain external mutations (emails, payments, deployments, writes) across provider failover.
   727	 */
   728	export function canSafelyFailoverProvider(executedToolCalls: readonly ToolCallRecord[] = []): boolean {
   729	  for (const call of executedToolCalls) {
   730	    if (isMutatingToolName(call.tool)) {
   731	      return false;
   732	    }
   733	  }
   734	  return true;
   735	}
   736	
   737	/**
   738	 * Quota-aware provider failover for the same parent job/turn.
   739	 * Switches cleanly between Google and DeepSeek on 429/502/503/quota failures ONLY when
   740	 * no external mutation has occurred and the agent is eligible for failover.
   741	 * Note: AGX retains Google as its strict default unless Founder explicitly changes it.
   742	 */
   743	export function selectQuotaFailoverRoute(
   744	  primaryDecision: OfficeModelRoutingDecision,
   745	  errorStatusOrMessage: number | string,
   746	  executedToolCalls: readonly ToolCallRecord[] = []
   747	): OfficeModelRoutingDecision | null {
   748	  if (!canSafelyFailoverProvider(executedToolCalls)) {
   749	    return null;
   750	  }
   751	  const statusStr = String(errorStatusOrMessage || "").toLowerCase();
   752	  const isQuotaOrUnavailable =
   753	    errorStatusOrMessage === 429 ||
   754	    errorStatusOrMessage === 502 ||
   755	    errorStatusOrMessage === 503 ||
   756	    /\b(429|502|503|quota|rate[ _-]?limit|resource_exhausted|credit_exhausted|insufficient_balance|temporarily_unavailable)\b/i.test(statusStr);
   757	  if (!isQuotaOrUnavailable) {
   758	    return null;
   759	  }
   760	
   761	  // AGX retains Google unless Founder explicitly switches it; do not silently switch AGX to DeepSeek.
   762	  if (primaryDecision.agent_id === "ag" || primaryDecision.agent_id === "brian") {
   763	    return null;
   764	  }
   765	
   766	  if (primaryDecision.provider === "google" || primaryDecision.provider === "antigravity") {
   767	    updateProviderHealth("google", errorStatusOrMessage === 429 ? "RATE_LIMITED" : "TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE", statusStr, 60);
   768	    const dsHealth = getProviderHealth("deepseek");
   769	    if (dsHealth.status === "READY") {
   770	      return {
   771	        ...primaryDecision,
   772	        selected_model: primaryDecision.think_only ? "deepseek-v4-pro" : "deepseek-flash",
   773	        provider: "deepseek",
   774	        lane: primaryDecision.think_only ? "reasoning_planner" : "economy_executor",
   775	        reason: `Quota/availability failover from ${primaryDecision.provider} (${primaryDecision.selected_model}) to DeepSeek with zero mutation replay.`,
   776	        free_tier_claimed: false,
   777	      };
   778	    }
   779	  } else if (primaryDecision.provider === "deepseek") {
   780	    updateProviderHealth("deepseek", errorStatusOrMessage === 429 ? "RATE_LIMITED" : "TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE", statusStr, 60);
   781	    const hasGoogleKey = Boolean(resolveGoogleApiKey());
   782	    const googleHealth = getProviderHealth("google");
   783	    if (hasGoogleKey && googleHealth.status === "READY") {
   784	      return {
   785	        ...primaryDecision,
   786	        selected_model: primaryDecision.think_only ? "gemini-pro" : "gemini-2.5-flash",
   787	        provider: "google",
   788	        lane: primaryDecision.think_only ? "reasoning_planner" : "managed_agent_executor",
   789	        reason: `Quota/availability failover from DeepSeek (${primaryDecision.selected_model}) to Google Flash with zero mutation replay.`,
   790	      };
   791	    }
   792	  }
   793	
   794	  return null;
   795	}
   796	
   797	export const LANE2_INTEGRATION_CONTRACT = {
   798	  AGX_DEFAULT_HARNESS_MODEL: "antigravity" as const,
   799	  GOOGLE_FLASH_EXECUTION_MODEL: "gemini-3.8-flash" as const,
   800	  GOOGLE_PRO_THINKING_MODEL: "gemini-pro" as const,
   801	  DEEPSEEK_DEFAULT_MODEL: "deepseek-flash" as const,
   802	  routeOfficeModel,
   803	  isToolPermittedForOfficeAgent,
   804	  canSafelyFailoverProvider,
   805	  selectQuotaFailoverRoute,
   806	};
   807	
   808	export type Lane2IntegrationContract = typeof LANE2_INTEGRATION_CONTRACT;

   112	function productionAntigravityHarnessConfig() {
   113	  const mcpEnabled = /^(1|true|yes|on)$/i.test(String(process.env.GEMINX_MCP_ENABLED || "").trim());
   114	  const agToken = configuredAgentToken("ag");
   115	  const brianToken = configuredAgentToken("brian");
   116	  const rawOrigin = String(
   117	    process.env.GEMINX_API_BASE_URL ||
   118	    process.env.RAILWAY_STATIC_URL ||
   119	    process.env.RAILWAY_PUBLIC_DOMAIN ||
   120	    ""
   121	  ).trim();
   122	
   123	  const revision = String(
   124	    process.env.GEMINX_DEPLOY_REVISION ||
   125	    process.env.APP_VERSION ||
   126	    process.env.GEMINX_CANONICAL_REPO_REF ||
   127	    "UNKNOWN"
   128	  ).trim();
   129	
   130	  const workspaceSources = [{
   131	    target: "/workspace/GEMINX_AG_SCOPE.md",
   132	    content: [
   133	      "# GeminX AG bounded production workspace",
   134	      "Repository: R1mob2-svg/geminx-v2",
   135	      "Canonical branch: main",
   136	      `Observed runtime revision: ${revision}`,
   137	      "Use github_read_file for exact current source and SHA receipts.",
   138	      "Use geminx_companion for governed writes, deployments, image generation, CRM/campaign work and provider readback.",
   139	      "Never place credentials in prompts, source files, screenshots or tool arguments."
   140	    ].join("\n")
   141	  }];
   142	
   143	  if (!mcpEnabled || !agToken || !rawOrigin) {
   144	    return { workspaceSources };
   145	  }
   146	
   147	  try {
   148	    const origin = new URL(/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\//i.test(rawOrigin) ? rawOrigin : `https://${rawOrigin}`);
   149	    if (origin.protocol !== "https:") return { workspaceSources };
   150	    origin.pathname = "/mcp";
   151	    origin.search = "";
   152	    origin.hash = "";
   153	    return {
   154	      workspaceSources,
   155	      mcpServers: [
   156	        {
   157	          name: "geminx_control_hub",
   158	          url: origin.toString(),
   159	          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${agToken}` }
   160	        },
   161	        ...(brianToken ? [{
   162	          name: "geminx_brian_preview_hub",
   163	          url: origin.toString(),
   164	          headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${brianToken}` }
   165	        }] : [])
   166	      ]
   167	    };
   168	  } catch {
   169	    return { workspaceSources };
   170	  }
   171	}

   186	/**
   187	 * Public Digital Ambush sends are held until the independently verified
   188	 * prospect identity, signed preview URL, and founder approval receipts exist.
   189	 * This intentionally permits only the two historically verified OWNER review
   190	 * mailboxes. No model-generated approval text or email subject bypasses it.
   191	 */
   192	export function digitalAmbushOutboundHoldReason(
   193	  toolName: string,
   194	  args: Record<string, unknown>
   195	): string | null {
   196	  if (!["zoho_mail_send", "zoho_send", "gmail_send", "gmail_send_message"].includes(toolName)) return null;
   197	  const content = [
   198	    String(args.subject ?? ""),
   199	    String(args.text_body ?? ""),
   200	    String(args.html_body ?? ""),
   201	    String(args.body ?? "")
   202	  ].join("\n");
   203	  if (!/digital\s+ambush|\/outreach-preview\/|\/live-concept\/|interactive\s+private\s+preview/i.test(content)) return null;
   204	
   205	  const allowedOwnerMailboxes = new Set([
   206	    "entreprenuityai@gmail.com",
   207	    "r1mob2@hotmail.com",
   208	    "glowauraboutique@gmail.com"
   209	  ]);
   210	  const recipientText = [args.to, args.cc, args.bcc]
   211	    .filter((value) => value !== undefined && value !== null)
   212	    .map((value) => Array.isArray(value) ? value.join(",") : String(value))
   213	    .join(",");
   214	  const extractedRecipients = recipientText.toLowerCase().match(/[a-z0-9._%+-]+@[a-z0-9.-]+\.[a-z]{2,}/g) ?? [];
   215	  if (extractedRecipients.length === 0 || extractedRecipients.some((email) => !allowedOwnerMailboxes.has(email))) {
   216	    return "DIGITAL_AMBUSH_EXTERNAL_SEND_HOLD: Only the two verified founder review mailboxes are allowed until source-backed previews, signed URLs, visual QA and founder approval are mechanically proven.";
   217	  }
   218	  return null;
   219	}
   220	
   221	function safeTraceArgs(tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> {
   222	  if (tool === "geminx_memory_append") return { source_type: args.source_type, source_ref: args.source_ref, summary: "[REDACTED_MEMORY_CONTENT]" };
   223	  if (!tool.startsWith("browser_") && !tool.startsWith("chrome_") && !tool.startsWith("system_") && !tool.startsWith("device_") && !tool.startsWith("agent_comms_") && !tool.startsWith("neo_subtask_") && !tool.startsWith("ag_live_") && !tool.startsWith("geminx_scratch_")) return args;
   224	  const copy: Record<string, unknown> = { ...args };
   225	  if (tool.startsWith("geminx_scratch_") && "content" in copy) {
   226	    copy.content = "[REDACTED_SCRATCH_CONTENT]";
   227	  }
   228	  if (tool.startsWith("browser_") || tool.startsWith("chrome_")) {
   229	    if ("value" in copy) copy.value = "[REDACTED_TYPED_TEXT]";
   230	    if (typeof copy.url === "string") {
   231	      try {
   232	        const parsed = new URL(copy.url);
   233	        copy.url = `${parsed.origin}${parsed.pathname}`;
   234	      } catch {
   235	        copy.url = "[REDACTED_URL]";
   236	      }
   237	    }
   238	  }
   239	  if (tool.startsWith("system_")) {
   240	    if ("content" in copy) copy.content = "[REDACTED_FILE_CONTENT]";
   241	    if ("value" in copy) copy.value = "[REDACTED_TYPED_TEXT]";
   242	    if (Array.isArray(copy.args)) {
   243	      copy.args = copy.args.map((value) => typeof value === "string" ? redactSensitive(value) : value);
   244	    }
   245	  }
   246	  if (tool.startsWith("ag_live_")) {
   247	    if ("objective" in copy) copy.objective = "[REDACTED_DESKTOP_OBJECTIVE]";
   248	  }
   249	  if (tool.startsWith("agent_comms_")) {
   250	    if ("instruction" in copy) copy.instruction = "[REDACTED_INTERNAL_INSTRUCTION]";
   251	    if ("args" in copy) copy.args = "[REDACTED_INTERNAL_ARGS]";
   252	  }
   253	  if (tool.startsWith("neo_subtask_")) {
   254	    if ("objective" in copy) copy.objective = "[REDACTED_CHILD_OBJECTIVE]";
   255	    if ("constraints" in copy) copy.constraints = "[REDACTED_CHILD_CONSTRAINTS]";
   256	    if ("acceptance_criteria" in copy) copy.acceptance_criteria = "[REDACTED_CHILD_ACCEPTANCE_CRITERIA]";
   257	    if ("reason" in copy) copy.reason = "[REDACTED_CHILD_CANCEL_REASON]";
   258	  }
   259	  if (tool.startsWith("device_")) {
   260	    if ("value" in copy) copy.value = "[REDACTED_TYPED_TEXT]";
   261	    if (typeof copy.url === "string") {
   262	      try {
   263	        const parsed = new URL(copy.url);
   264	        copy.url = `${parsed.origin}${parsed.pathname}`;
   265	      } catch {
   266	        copy.url = "[REDACTED_URL]";
   267	      }
   268	    }
   269	    if (Array.isArray(copy.steps)) {
   270	      copy.steps = copy.steps.map((raw) => {
   271	        if (!raw || typeof raw !== "object" || Array.isArray(raw)) return raw;
   272	        const step = { ...(raw as Record<string, unknown>) };
   273	        if (step.args && typeof step.args === "object" && !Array.isArray(step.args)) {
   274	          const nested = { ...(step.args as Record<string, unknown>) };
   275	          if ("value" in nested) nested.value = "[REDACTED_TYPED_TEXT]";
   276	          if (typeof nested.url === "string") {
   277	            try {
   278	              const parsed = new URL(nested.url);
   279	              nested.url = `${parsed.origin}${parsed.pathname}`;
   280	            } catch {
   281	              nested.url = "[REDACTED_URL]";
   282	            }
   283	          }
   284	          step.args = nested;
   285	        }
   286	        return step;
   287	      });
   288	    }
   289	  }
   290	  return copy;
   291	}
   292	
   293	function canonicalInvocationValue(value: unknown): unknown {
   294	  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(canonicalInvocationValue);
   295	  if (value && typeof value === "object") {
   296	    return Object.fromEntries(
   297	      Object.entries(value as Record<string, unknown>)
   298	        .sort(([left], [right]) => left.localeCompare(right))
   299	        .map(([key, item]) => [key, canonicalInvocationValue(item)])
   300	    );
   301	  }
   302	  return value;
   303	}
   304	
   305	function toolInvocationFingerprint(tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): string {
   306	  // Internal-only fingerprint. Never log this because args may contain sensitive material.
   307	  return `${tool}:${JSON.stringify(canonicalInvocationValue(args))}`;
   308	}
   309	
   310	function safeTraceResult(tool: string | undefined, data: unknown): unknown {
   311	  if (!data || typeof data !== "object") return data;
   312	  if (!tool || typeof tool !== "string") return data;
   313	  const source = data as Record<string, unknown>;
   314	  if (tool.startsWith("browser_") || tool.startsWith("chrome_")) {
   315	    return {
   316	      session_id: source.session_id ?? null,
   317	      profile_id: source.profile_id ?? null,
   318	      url: typeof source.url === "string" ? source.url.split("?")[0] : null,
   319	      title: source.title ?? null,
   320	      challenge: source.challenge ?? null,
   321	      human_control: source.human_control ?? null,
   322	      closed: source.closed ?? null
   323	    };
   324	  }
   325	  if (tool.startsWith("geminx_scratch_")) {
   326	    return {
   327	      artifact_id: source.artifact_id ?? null,
   328	      path: source.path ?? null,
   329	      exists: source.exists ?? source.exists_after ?? null,
   330	      created: source.created ?? null,
   331	      overwritten: source.overwritten ?? null,
   332	      deleted: source.deleted ?? null,
   333	      bytes_read: source.bytes_read ?? null,
   334	      bytes_written: source.bytes_written ?? null,
   335	      bytes: source.bytes ?? null,
   336	      sha256: source.sha256 ?? null,
   337	      receipt_redacted: true
   338	    };
   339	  }
   340	  if (tool.startsWith("system_") || tool.startsWith("device_")) {
   341	    return {
   342	      success: source.success ?? source.ok ?? null,
   343	      path: source.path ?? null,
   344	      bytes_read: source.bytes_read ?? null,
   345	      bytes_written: source.bytes_written ?? null,
   346	      sha256: source.sha256 ?? null,
   347	      exit_code: source.exit_code ?? null,
   348	      pid: source.pid ?? null,
   349	      active: source.active ?? null,
   350	      host: source.host ?? source.host_id ?? null,
   351	      platform: source.platform ?? null,
   352	      receipt_redacted: true
   353	    };
   354	  }
   355	  return data;
   356	}

   362	function isRailwayNativeConfigured(): boolean {
   363	  if (process.env.NODE_ENV === "test" && !process.env.GEMINX_TEST_STRICT_RAILWAY_GATING) {
   364	    return true;
   365	  }
   366	  return Boolean(
   367	    process.env.GEMINX_RAILWAY_PROJECT_TOKEN ||
   368	    process.env.RAILWAY_TOKEN ||
   369	    process.env.RAILWAY_API_TOKEN
   370	  );
   371	}

   375	  return Object.freeze([
   376	    ...TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   377	    ...(railwayNativeConfigured ? RAILWAY_TOOL_DEFINITIONS : []),
   378	    ...BROWSER_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   379	    ...CHROME_BRIDGE_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   380	    ...SYSTEM_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,

   382	    ...DEVICE_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   383	    ...GMAIL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   384	    ...ZOHO_MAIL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   385	    ...OFFICE_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   386	    ...COMPOSIO_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   387	    ...ANTIGRAVITY_TOOL_DEFINITIONS,
   388	  ]);
   389	}

   391	const ALL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS: readonly ToolDefinition[] = new Proxy([] as ToolDefinition[], {
   392	  get(_target, prop) {
   393	    const defs = getRegisteredToolDefinitions();
   394	    const val = Reflect.get(defs, prop);
   395	    return typeof val === "function" ? val.bind(defs) : val;
   396	  }
   397	});
