   509	function readOnlyToolAllowed(tool: string): boolean {
   510	  return READ_ONLY_EXACT_TOOLS.has(tool);
   511	}
   512	
   513	function childAllowedTools(
   514	  ownedSurface: string,
   515	  readOnly: boolean,
   516	  explicitAllowedTools: readonly string[],
   517	  objective = ""
   518	): string[] {
   519	  const available = new Set(ALL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS.map((definition) => definition.name as string));
   520	  const selected = new Set<string>(["geminx_runtime_capabilities", "geminx_tool_search"]);
   521	  const explicit = explicitAllowedTools
   522	    .map((name) => String(name).trim())
   523	    .filter((name) => available.has(name))
   524	    .slice(0, 64);
   525	  for (const name of explicit) selected.add(name);
   526	
   527	  // Infer capabilities from both the ownership label and the actual child objective.
   528	  // Otherwise a valid abstract label can silently collapse the child to discovery-only tools.
   529	  const surface = `${ownedSurface} ${objective}`.toLowerCase();
   530	  const addPrefix = (prefix: string) => {
   531	    for (const name of available) {
   532	      if (!name.startsWith(prefix)) continue;
   533	      if (readOnly && !readOnlyToolAllowed(name)) continue;
   534	      selected.add(name);
   535	    }
   536	  };
   537	
   538	  if (/repo|github|code|branch|pull.?request|\bpr\b/.test(surface)) addPrefix("github_");
   539	  if (/railway|deploy|service|runtime|production/.test(surface)) addPrefix("railway_");
   540	  if (/browser|chrome|web|page|site/.test(surface)) {
   541	    addPrefix("chrome_");
   542	    addPrefix("browser_");
   543	  }
   544	  if (/prospect|ambush|business|places|maps|discover|lead|scout|mockup|image|design/.test(surface)) {
   545	    addPrefix("google_");
   546	    selected.add("web_search");
   547	    selected.add("office_digital_ambush_stage");
   548	    selected.add("google_places_search_text");
   549	    selected.add("google_maps_status");
   550	    selected.add("google_image_generate");
   551	  }
   552	  if (/windows|desktop|system|pc/.test(surface)) addPrefix("system_");
   553	  if (/android|device|phone|tablet/.test(surface)) addPrefix("device_");
   554	  if (/creator|roblox|studio|game/.test(surface)) {
   555	    addPrefix("creator_");
   556	    addPrefix("roblox_");
   557	  }
   558	  if (/gmail|email|mail/.test(surface)) addPrefix("gmail_");
   559	  if (/zoho/.test(surface)) addPrefix("zoho_mail_");
   560	  if (/market|trade|trading|crypto|coin|equity|equities|stock|shares|nasdaq|nyse|london|tsx|tokyo/.test(surface)) {
   561	    selected.add("market_spot_quote");
   562	    selected.add("market_ohlcv");
   563	    selected.add("market_scan_movers");
   564	    selected.add("market_sniper_scan");
   565	    selected.add("market_global_equities_scan");
   566	    selected.add("market_equity_ml_research");
   567	    selected.add("market_paper_account");
   568	    selected.add("market_paper_history");
   569	    if (!readOnly) {
   570	      selected.add("market_paper_open");
   571	      selected.add("market_paper_close");
   572	      selected.add("market_paper_mark");
   573	    }
   574	  }
   575	  if (/supabase|database|db/.test(surface)) addPrefix("supabase_");
   576	  if (/vercel/.test(surface)) addPrefix("vercel_");
   577	  if (/openclaw/.test(surface)) addPrefix("openclaw_");
   578	  if (/antigravity|agx|gemini|harness/.test(surface)) addPrefix("antigravity_");
   579	
   580	  return [...selected].slice(0, 64);
   581	}
   582	
   583	function schemaTypeMatches(value: unknown, expected: unknown): boolean {
   584	  if (Array.isArray(expected)) return expected.some((item) => schemaTypeMatches(value, item));
   585	  if (expected === "null") return value === null;
   586	  if (expected === "array") return Array.isArray(value);
   587	  if (expected === "object") return Boolean(value) && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value);
   588	  if (expected === "integer") return typeof value === "number" && Number.isInteger(value);
   589	  if (expected === "number") return typeof value === "number" && Number.isFinite(value);
   590	  if (expected === "string") return typeof value === "string";
   591	  if (expected === "boolean") return typeof value === "boolean";
   592	  return true;
   593	}
   594	
   595	function validateJsonSchema(value: unknown, schema: Record<string, unknown>, at = "$"): string | null {
   596	  if (schema.type && !schemaTypeMatches(value, schema.type)) {
   597	    return `${at}: expected type ${JSON.stringify(schema.type)}`;
   598	  }
   599	  if (Array.isArray(schema.enum) && !schema.enum.some((candidate) => Object.is(candidate, value))) {
   600	    return `${at}: value is outside enum`;
   601	  }
   602	  if (schema.type === "object" && value && typeof value === "object" && !Array.isArray(value)) {
   603	    const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;
   604	    const required = Array.isArray(schema.required) ? schema.required.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string") : [];
   605	    for (const key of required) if (!(key in record)) return `${at}.${key}: required property missing`;
   606	    const properties = schema.properties && typeof schema.properties === "object" && !Array.isArray(schema.properties)
   607	      ? schema.properties as Record<string, unknown>
   608	      : {};
   609	    for (const [key, childSchema] of Object.entries(properties)) {
   610	      if (!(key in record) || !childSchema || typeof childSchema !== "object" || Array.isArray(childSchema)) continue;
   611	      const error = validateJsonSchema(record[key], childSchema as Record<string, unknown>, `${at}.${key}`);
   612	      if (error) return error;
   613	    }
   614	  }
   615	  if (schema.type === "array" && Array.isArray(value) && schema.items && typeof schema.items === "object" && !Array.isArray(schema.items)) {
   616	    for (let index = 0; index < value.length; index += 1) {
   617	      const error = validateJsonSchema(value[index], schema.items as Record<string, unknown>, `${at}[${index}]`);
   618	      if (error) return error;
   619	    }
   620	  }
   621	  return null;
   622	}
   623	
   624	/**
   625	 * Central poison-pill boundary. No tool receipt is allowed back into model context
   626	 * until its identity/envelope and optional output schema are independently checked.
   627	 */
   628	export function validateToolResultSchema(
   629	  request: ToolInvocationRequest,
   630	  result: ToolInvocationResult
   631	): ToolInvocationResult {
   632	  const expectedCorrelation = request.correlation_id || request.tool_call_id;
   633	  let mismatch: string | null = null;
   634	  if (!result || typeof result !== "object") mismatch = "result is not an object";
   635	  else if (result.tool_call_id !== request.tool_call_id) mismatch = "tool_call_id mismatch";
   636	  else if (result.correlation_id !== expectedCorrelation) mismatch = "correlation_id mismatch";
   637	  else if (result.tool !== request.tool) mismatch = "tool name mismatch";
   638	  else if (!["pending", "running", "succeeded", "failed", "blocked"].includes(String(result.status))) mismatch = "invalid tool status";
   639	  else {
   640	    try {
   641	      const serialized = JSON.stringify(result.data ?? null);
   642	      if (serialized.length > 512_000) mismatch = "tool data exceeds validated receipt size";
   643	    } catch {
   644	      mismatch = "tool data is not JSON serializable";
   645	    }
   646	  }
   647	
   648	  const definition = ALL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS.find((candidate) => candidate.name === request.tool);
   649	  if (!mismatch && definition?.output_schema) {
   650	    mismatch = validateJsonSchema(result.data ?? null, definition.output_schema);
   651	  }
   652	
   653	  if (!mismatch) return { ...result, schema_validated: true };
   654	
   655	  const now = new Date().toISOString();
   656	  return {
   657	    ok: false,
   658	    status: "blocked",
   659	    tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   660	    correlation_id: expectedCorrelation,
   661	    tool: request.tool,
   662	    status_code: 502,
   663	    blocked_reason: "TOOL_SCHEMA_MISMATCH",
   664	    error: `TOOL_SCHEMA_MISMATCH: ${mismatch}`,
   665	    started_at: result?.started_at ?? now,
   666	    finished_at: now,
   667	    duration_ms: Math.max(0, Date.parse(now) - Date.parse(result?.started_at ?? now)),
   668	    schema_validated: true
   669	  };
   670	}
   671	
   672	export interface ToolRegistryOptions {
   673	  paperAccountId?: string;
   674	  github?: GithubAdapter;
   675	  supabase?: SupabaseAdapter;
   676	  vercel?: VercelAdapter;
   677	  railway?: RailwayAdapter;
   678	  shell?: ShellExecutor;
   679	  scratch?: RuntimeScratchAdapter;
   680	  roblox?: RobloxAdapter;
   681	  openclaw?: OpenClawAdapter;
   682	  studioMcp?: StudioMcpAdapter;
   683	  browser?: BrowserOperatorAdapter;
   684	  chrome?: ChromeBridgeAdapter;
   685	  system?: SystemOperatorAdapter;
   686	  device?: DeviceOperatorAdapter;
   687	  runway?: RunwayAdapter;
   688	  gmail?: GmailAdapter;
   689	  zoho?: ZohoMailAdapter;
   690	  composio?: ComposioAdapter;
   691	  googleImage?: GoogleImageAdapter;
   692	  enableOpenClaw?: boolean;
   693	  readOnly?: boolean;
   694	  companyScope?: string;
   695	  /** Non-empty only for bounded child missions. Top-level Founder work remains governed by normal runtime policy. */
   696	  allowedToolNames?: readonly string[];
   697	  credentialScope?: readonly string[];
   698	  killSwitch?: KillSwitchService;
   699	}
   700	
   701	function envOpenClawEnabled(): boolean {
   702	  const value = process.env.GEMINX_ENABLE_OPENCLAW?.trim().toLowerCase();
   703	  const localEnabled = value === "1" || value === "true" || value === "yes" || value === "on";
   704	  const remoteConfigured =
   705	    String(process.env.GEMINX_OPENCLAW_PUBLIC_URL ?? "").trim().length > 0 &&
   706	    String(process.env.GEMINX_OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_TOKEN ?? "").trim().length > 0;
   707	  return localEnabled || remoteConfigured;
   708	}
   709	
   710	/**
   711	 * Preserve a native tool receipt when Digital Ambush exits early. A provider
   712	 * denial must not strand DeepSeek's transcript in PENDING_TOOL, and an unknown
   713	 * post-dispatch exception must never be misrepresented as an unsent email.
   714	 */
   715	export function digitalAmbushExceptionReceipt(
   716	  request: ToolInvocationRequest,
   717	  correlationId: string,
   718	  startedAt: string,
   719	  failure: unknown
   720	): ToolInvocationResult {
   721	  const detail = failure instanceof Error ? failure.message : String(failure);
   722	  const imageQuotaDenied = /GOOGLE_IMAGE_PROVIDER_HTTP_429/.test(detail);
   723	  const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
   724	  const code = imageQuotaDenied
   725	    ? "DIGITAL_AMBUSH_IMAGE_PROVIDER_RATE_LIMITED"
   726	    : "DIGITAL_AMBUSH_PIPELINE_EXCEPTION_RECONCILIATION_REQUIRED";
   727	  return {
   728	    ok: false,
   729	    status: "blocked",
   730	    tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   731	    correlation_id: correlationId,
   732	    tool: request.tool,
   733	    status_code: imageQuotaDenied ? 429 : 502,
   734	    blocked_reason: code,
   735	    error: imageQuotaDenied
   736	      ? code + ": Google image provider refused with HTTP 429 before downstream outbound dispatch. No synthetic image or send success was recorded."
   737	      : code + ": pipeline failed; downstream side effects and prior email attempts require provider receipt reconciliation.",
   738	    data: {
   739	      stage: imageQuotaDenied ? "IMAGE_GENERATION" : "PIPELINE_UNVERIFIED",
   740	      provider_status: imageQuotaDenied ? 429 : null,
   741	      outbound_status: "NOT_VERIFIED",
   742	      provider_send_receipt: null,
   743	      original_error_code: imageQuotaDenied ? "GOOGLE_IMAGE_PROVIDER_HTTP_429" : "AMBUSH_PIPELINE_ERROR",
   744	      error_detail: detail.replace(/(token|key|secret|bearer)[=: ]+\S+/gi, "$1=[redacted]").slice(0, 500),
   745	      retries_safe: false
   746	    },
   747	    started_at: startedAt,
   748	    finished_at: finishedAt,
   749	    duration_ms: Math.max(0, Date.parse(finishedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt))
   750	  };
   751	}
   752	
   753	export class ToolRegistry {
   754	  readonly companyScope: string;
   755	  private readonly adapters: {
   756	    github: GithubAdapter;
   757	    supabase: SupabaseAdapter;
   758	    vercel: VercelAdapter;
   759	    railway: RailwayAdapter;
   760	    shell: ShellExecutor;
   761	    scratch: RuntimeScratchAdapter;
   762	    roblox: RobloxAdapter;
   763	    openclaw: OpenClawAdapter;
   764	    studioMcp: StudioMcpAdapter;
   765	    browser: BrowserOperatorAdapter;
   766	    chrome: ChromeBridgeAdapter;
   767	    system: SystemOperatorAdapter;
   768	    device: DeviceOperatorAdapter;
   769	    runway: RunwayAdapter;
   770	    gmail: GmailAdapter;
   771	    zoho: ZohoMailAdapter;
   772	    composio: ComposioAdapter;
   773	    antigravity: AntigravityHarnessAdapter;
   774	    googleImage: GoogleImageAdapter;
   775	  };
   776	  private readonly enableOpenClaw: boolean;
   777	  private readonly paperAccountId: string;
   778	  private readonly readOnly: boolean;
   779	  private readonly allowedToolNames: Set<string> | null;
   780	  private readonly credentialScope: Set<string> | null;
   781	  private readonly killSwitch: KillSwitchService;
   782	  private readonly traces = new Map<string, ToolCallRecord[]>();
   783	  private readonly outputArtifacts = new Map<string, FounderAttachmentRef[]>();
   784	  private readonly failedInvocationCounts = new Map<string, Map<string, number>>();
   785	
   786	  constructor(options: ToolRegistryOptions = {}) {
   787	    this.companyScope = String(options.companyScope || process.env.GEMINX_COMPANY_SCOPE || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   788	    this.paperAccountId = String(options.paperAccountId || "geminx").trim().toLowerCase();
   789	    this.enableOpenClaw = options.enableOpenClaw === true || (options.enableOpenClaw === undefined && envOpenClawEnabled());
   790	    this.readOnly = options.readOnly === true;
   791	    this.allowedToolNames = options.allowedToolNames?.length ? new Set(options.allowedToolNames.map(String)) : null;
   792	    this.credentialScope = options.credentialScope?.length ? new Set(options.credentialScope.map(String)) : null;
   793	    this.killSwitch = options.killSwitch ?? createKillSwitchService();
   794	    this.adapters = {
   795	      github: options.github ?? new GithubAdapter(),
   796	      supabase: options.supabase ?? new SupabaseAdapter(),
   797	      vercel: options.vercel ?? new VercelAdapter(),
   798	      railway: options.railway ?? new RailwayAdapter(),
   799	      shell: options.shell ?? new ShellExecutor(),
   800	      scratch: options.scratch ?? new RuntimeScratchAdapter(),
   801	      roblox: options.roblox ?? new RobloxAdapter(),
   802	      openclaw: options.openclaw ?? new OpenClawAdapter(),
   803	      studioMcp: options.studioMcp ?? new StudioMcpAdapter(),
   804	      browser: options.browser ?? new BrowserOperatorAdapter(),
   805	      chrome: options.chrome ?? new ChromeBridgeAdapter(),
   806	      system: options.system ?? new SystemOperatorAdapter(),
   807	      device: options.device ?? new DeviceOperatorAdapter(),
   808	      runway: options.runway ?? new RunwayAdapter(),
   809	      gmail: options.gmail ?? new GmailAdapter(),
   810	      zoho: options.zoho ?? new ZohoMailAdapter(),
   811	      composio: options.composio ?? new ComposioAdapter({ companyScope: this.companyScope }),
   812	      antigravity: new AntigravityHarnessAdapter(productionAntigravityHarnessConfig()),
   813	      googleImage: options.googleImage ?? new GoogleImageAdapter()
   814	    };
   815	  }
   816	
   817	  listTools(): readonly ToolDefinition[] {
   818	    const railwayAvailable = isRailwayNativeConfigured() || this.adapters.railway.available();
   819	    const base = railwayAvailable
   820	      ? ALL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS
   821	      : ALL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS.filter((definition) => !definition.name.startsWith("railway_"));
   822	    const enabled = this.enableOpenClaw
   823	      ? base
   824	      : base.filter((definition) => !definition.name.startsWith("openclaw_"));
   825	    const scoped = this.allowedToolNames
   826	      ? enabled.filter((definition) => this.allowedToolNames!.has(definition.name))
   827	      : enabled;
   828	    return this.readOnly ? scoped.filter((definition) => readOnlyToolAllowed(definition.name)) : scoped;
   829	  }
   830	
   831	  hasExplicitToolScope(): boolean {
   832	    return this.allowedToolNames !== null;
   833	  }
   834	
   835	  /** Read-only metadata over the exact authorised registry surface. No extra
   836	   * tool is created or allowed merely by reporting its workflow family.
   837	   */
   838	  getWorkflowFamily(toolName: string): ToolWorkflowFamily | null {
   839	    const authorised = this.listTools().some((item) => item.name === toolName);
   840	    if (!authorised) return null;
   841	    if (toolName.startsWith("system_desktop_")) return "system_desktop";
   842	    if (toolName.startsWith("ag_live_")) return "ag_live";
   843	    if (toolName.startsWith("geminx_scratch_")) return "geminx_scratch";
   844	    for (const family of ["github", "railway", "browser", "chrome", "system", "creator", "roblox", "device", "gmail", "zoho", "composio"] as const) {
   845	      if (toolName.startsWith(family + "_")) return family;
   846	    }
   847	    return "other";
   848	  }
   849	
   850	  hasExplicitAllowedTools(): boolean {
   851	    return this.hasExplicitToolScope();
   852	  }
   853	
   854	  // Trace visibility used by github.trace_status and for receipts (no secrets).
   855	  trace(correlationId: string): readonly ToolCallRecord[] {
   856	    return this.traces.get(correlationId) ?? [];
   857	  }
   858	
   859	  artifacts(correlationId: string): readonly FounderAttachmentRef[] {
   860	    return this.outputArtifacts.get(correlationId) ?? [];
   861	  }
   862	
   863	  private captureOutputArtifact(correlationId: string, data: unknown): void {
   864	    if (!data || typeof data !== "object" || Array.isArray(data)) return;
   865	    const attachment = (data as Record<string, unknown>).attachment;
   866	    if (!attachment || typeof attachment !== "object" || Array.isArray(attachment)) return;
   867	    const row = attachment as Record<string, unknown>;
   868	    const artifactSha = String(row.artifact_sha256 ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   869	    const filename = String(row.filename ?? "").trim().slice(0, 180);
   870	    const contentType = String(row.content_type ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
   871	    const sizeBytes = Number(row.size_bytes ?? 0);
   872	    if (!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(artifactSha) || !filename || !/^(?:image|video|audio)\/[a-z0-9.+-]+$/.test(contentType)) return;
   873	    if (!Number.isFinite(sizeBytes) || sizeBytes <= 0 || sizeBytes > 100 * 1024 * 1024) return;
   874	    const item: FounderAttachmentRef = {
   875	      artifact_sha256: artifactSha,
   876	      filename,
   877	      content_type: contentType,
   878	      size_bytes: Math.floor(sizeBytes),
   879	      role: typeof row.role === "string" ? row.role.slice(0, 64) : "assistant-output",
   880	      source_name: typeof row.source_name === "string" ? row.source_name.slice(0, 180) : null
   881	    };
   882	    const existing = this.outputArtifacts.get(correlationId) ?? [];
   883	    if (!existing.some(candidate => candidate.artifact_sha256 === item.artifact_sha256)) {
   884	      this.outputArtifacts.set(correlationId, [...existing, item].slice(-8));
   885	    }
   886	  }
   887	
   888	  private adapterForTool(tool: string): "github" | "supabase" | "vercel" | "railway" | "shell" | "scratch" | "roblox" | "openclaw" | "studioMcp" | "browser" | "chrome" | "system" | "device" | "runway" | "gmail" | "zoho" | "composio" | "antigravity" | "googleImage" | null {
   889	    if (tool.startsWith("github_")) return "github";
   890	    if (tool.startsWith("newton_connector_")) return "github";
   891	    if (tool.startsWith("supabase_")) return "supabase";
   892	    if (tool.startsWith("vercel_")) return "vercel";
   893	    if (tool.startsWith("railway_")) return "railway";
   894	    if (tool.startsWith("shell_")) return "shell";
   895	    if (tool.startsWith("geminx_scratch_")) return "scratch";
   896	    if (tool.startsWith("roblox_studio_") || tool.startsWith("roblox_creator_")) return "studioMcp";
   897	    if (tool.startsWith("roblox_")) return "roblox";
   898	    if (tool.startsWith("openclaw_")) return this.enableOpenClaw ? "openclaw" : null;
   899	    if (tool.startsWith("browser_")) return "browser";
   900	    if (tool.startsWith("chrome_")) return "chrome";
   901	    if (tool.startsWith("system_")) return "system";
   902	    if (tool.startsWith("ag_live_")) return "system";
   903	    if (tool.startsWith("device_")) return "device";
   904	    if (tool.startsWith("runway_")) return "runway";
   905	    if (tool.startsWith("gmail_")) return "gmail";
   906	    if (tool.startsWith("zoho_mail_") || tool === "zoho_send") return "zoho";
   907	    if (tool.startsWith("composio_")) return "composio";
   908	    if (tool.startsWith("antigravity_")) return "antigravity";
   909	    if (tool.startsWith("google_image_") || tool.startsWith("imagen_")) return "googleImage";
   910	    return null;
   911	  }
   912	
   913	  async execute(request: ToolInvocationRequest): Promise<ToolInvocationResult> {
   914	    const key = request.correlation_id || request.tool_call_id;
   915	
   916	    // 0. PreToolUse: malformed model JSON never reaches an adapter.
   917	    if (request.args && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(request.args, MALFORMED_JSON_MARKER)) {
   918	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
   919	      return {
   920	        ok: false,
   921	        status: "blocked",
   922	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   923	        correlation_id: key,
   924	        tool: request.tool,
   925	        status_code: 400,
   926	        blocked_reason: "TOOL_ARGS_MALFORMED_JSON",
   927	        error: `TOOL_ARGS_MALFORMED_JSON: arguments for ${request.tool} were not valid JSON. Zero mutation performed. Re-emit the call once with valid JSON matching the schema.`,
   928	        started_at: now,
   929	        finished_at: now,
   930	        duration_ms: 0
   931	      };
   932	    }
   933	
   934	    // 1. Authoritative Company Isolation
   935	    const authoritativeCompany = this.companyScope || String(process.env.GEMINX_COMPANY_SCOPE || "").trim().toLowerCase();
   936	    const requestedCompany = typeof request.args?.company_scope === "string"
   937	      ? request.args.company_scope.trim().toLowerCase()
   938	      : typeof request.args?.company_id === "string"
   939	      ? request.args.company_id.trim().toLowerCase()
   940	      : null;
   941	    if (authoritativeCompany && requestedCompany && requestedCompany !== authoritativeCompany) {
   942	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
   943	      return {
   944	        ok: false,
   945	        status: "blocked",
   946	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   947	        correlation_id: key,
   948	        tool: request.tool,
   949	        status_code: 403,
   950	        blocked_reason: "CROSS_COMPANY_ACCESS_DENIED",
   951	        error: `CROSS_COMPANY_ACCESS_DENIED: Execution environment is authoritatively bound to '${authoritativeCompany}' but requested '${requestedCompany}'. Cross-company resource access is denied.`,
   952	        started_at: now,
   953	        finished_at: now,
   954	        duration_ms: 0
   955	      };
   956	    }
   957	    // The company binding is an execution authority, not an extra model
   958	    // argument. Never mutate the caller's args or inject unknown properties
   959	    // into a strict tool schema.
   960	    if (authoritativeCompany && request.args && !request.args.company_scope) {
   961	      const schema = ALL_TOOL_DEFINITIONS.find(definition => definition.name === request.tool)?.input_schema;
   962	      const properties = schema?.properties;
   963	      const declaresScope = !!properties && typeof properties === "object" &&
   964	        Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(properties, "company_scope");
   965	      if (declaresScope) {
   966	        request = { ...request, args: { ...request.args, company_scope: authoritativeCompany } };
   967	      }
   968	    }
   969	
   970	    // 1b. AI Office Role-Manifest Tool & Cross-Company Access Control
   971	    const activeOfficeAgentRaw =
   972	      typeof request.args?.active_office_agent === "string"
   973	        ? request.args.active_office_agent.trim().toLowerCase()
   974	        : "";
   975	    if (activeOfficeAgentRaw) {
   976	      const accessCheck = checkOfficeAgentToolAccess({
   977	        agentId: activeOfficeAgentRaw,
   978	        toolName: request.tool,
   979	        companyScope: authoritativeCompany || requestedCompany || undefined,
   980	        targetCompanyScope: requestedCompany || undefined,
   981	      });
   982	      if (!accessCheck.allowed) {
   983	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
   984	        return {
   985	          ok: false,
   986	          status: "blocked",
   987	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
   988	          correlation_id: key,
   989	          tool: request.tool,
   990	          status_code: 403,
   991	          blocked_reason: accessCheck.code,
   992	          error: `${accessCheck.code}: ${accessCheck.reason}`,
   993	          started_at: now,
   994	          finished_at: now,
   995	          duration_ms: 0,
   996	        };
   997	      }
   998	    }
   999	
  1000	    // 1c. Brian can write only an isolated fictional preview or his permitted
  1001	    // Bootleg AGI memory/rollover paths. Other department specialists can write
  1002	    // only to their own Bootleg AGI memory/rollover paths, with IDENTITY.md and
  1003	    // core Doctrine/** protected against unapproved self-modification.
  1004	    if (activeOfficeAgentRaw === "brian") {
  1005	      const authoringDenial = brianSiteAuthoringDenial(request.tool, request.args || {});
  1006	      if (authoringDenial) {
  1007	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1008	        return {
  1009	          ok: false, status: "blocked", tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1010	          correlation_id: key, tool: request.tool, status_code: 403,
  1011	          blocked_reason: authoringDenial, error: authoringDenial,
  1012	          started_at: now, finished_at: now, duration_ms: 0
  1013	        };
  1014	      }
  1015	    } else if (["mark", "sophie", "kerry", "ads", "neo"].includes(activeOfficeAgentRaw)) {
  1016	      const specialistDenial = specialistBrainAuthoringDenial(activeOfficeAgentRaw, request.tool, request.args || {});
  1017	      if (specialistDenial) {
  1018	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1019	        return {
  1020	          ok: false, status: "blocked", tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1021	          correlation_id: key, tool: request.tool, status_code: 403,
  1022	          blocked_reason: specialistDenial, error: specialistDenial,
  1023	          started_at: now, finished_at: now, duration_ms: 0
  1024	        };
  1025	      }
  1026	    }
  1027	
  1028	    // 2. Inbound Untrusted Email Prompt Injection Fence
  1029	    const untrustedEmailContext =
  1030	      request.args?.untrusted_email_context === true ||
  1031	      Boolean((request as unknown as Record<string, unknown>).untrustedEmailActive);
  1032	    const privilegedMutationTools = new Set([
  1033	      "system_shell_exec",
  1034	      "system_file_write",
  1035	      "system_file_edit",
  1036	      "geminx_memory_append",
  1037	      "github_create_or_update_file",
  1038	      "github_patch_file",
  1039	      "github_append_file",
  1040	      "github_delete_file",
  1041	      "github_create_branch",
  1042	      "github_merge_pull_request",
  1043	      "railway_deploy",
  1044	      "railway_restart_deployment",
  1045	      "shell_run",
  1046	      "self_repair"
  1047	    ]);
  1048	    if (untrustedEmailContext && privilegedMutationTools.has(request.tool)) {
  1049	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1050	      return {
  1051	        ok: false,
  1052	        status: "blocked",
  1053	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1054	        correlation_id: key,
  1055	        tool: request.tool,
  1056	        status_code: 403,
  1057	        blocked_reason: "EMAIL_INDIRECT_PROMPT_INJECTION_FENCE",
  1058	        error: "EMAIL_INDIRECT_PROMPT_INJECTION_FENCE: Untrusted email context is active. Host, repository, or cloud infrastructure mutations are blocked to prevent indirect prompt injection.",
  1059	        started_at: now,
  1060	        finished_at: now,
  1061	        duration_ms: 0
  1062	      };
  1063	    }
  1064	
  1065	    // Fail closed before ANY provider or idempotency claim for unreviewed
  1066	    // Digital Ambush customer mail; owner-only internal samples remain allowed.
  1067	    const ambushHold = digitalAmbushOutboundHoldReason(
  1068	      String(request.tool),
  1069	      request.args && typeof request.args === "object" && !Array.isArray(request.args)
  1070	        ? request.args as Record<string, unknown>
  1071	        : {}
  1072	    );
  1073	    if (ambushHold) {
  1074	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1075	      return {
  1076	        ok: false,
  1077	        status: "blocked",
  1078	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1079	        correlation_id: key,
  1080	        tool: request.tool,
  1081	        status_code: 403,
  1082	        blocked_reason: "DIGITAL_AMBUSH_EXTERNAL_SEND_HOLD",
  1083	        error: ambushHold,
  1084	        started_at: now,
  1085	        finished_at: now,
  1086	        duration_ms: 0
  1087	      };
  1088	    }
  1089	
  1090	    // 3. Outbound Email Sensitive Data Loss Prevention & Idempotency Gate
  1091	    let outboundClaimKey: string | null = null;
  1092	    if (request.tool === "zoho_mail_send" || request.tool === ("zoho_send" as any)) {
  1093	      const to = String(request.args?.to ?? "");
  1094	      const subject = String(request.args?.subject ?? "");
  1095	      const textBody = String(request.args?.text_body ?? "");
  1096	      const htmlBody = String(request.args?.html_body ?? "");
  1097	      const fullContent = `${subject}\n${textBody}\n${htmlBody}`;
  1098	
  1099	      const dlp = scanSensitiveDataLeak(fullContent, { permittedCompanyScope: authoritativeCompany || undefined });
  1100	      if (!dlp.safe) {
  1101	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1102	        return {
  1103	          ok: false,
  1104	          status: "blocked",
  1105	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1106	          correlation_id: key,
  1107	          tool: request.tool,
  1108	          status_code: 403,
  1109	          blocked_reason: "SENSITIVE_DATA_LOSS_PREVENTION_BLOCKED",
  1110	          error: `SENSITIVE_DATA_LOSS_PREVENTION_BLOCKED: Outbound email blocked: ${dlp.violations.join(", ")}`,
  1111	          started_at: now,
  1112	          finished_at: now,
  1113	          duration_ms: 0
  1114	        };
  1115	      }
  1116	
  1117	      if (detectHighRiskPricingOutreach(fullContent) && request.args?.approved !== true) {
  1118	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1119	        return {
  1120	          ok: false,
  1121	          status: "blocked",
  1122	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1123	          correlation_id: key,
  1124	          tool: request.tool,
  1125	          status_code: 403,
  1126	          blocked_reason: "AWAITING_FOUNDER_APPROVAL",
  1127	          error: "AWAITING_FOUNDER_APPROVAL: Email contains custom pricing, quotes, or fee commitments. Route to Founder HQ approval queue before send.",
  1128	          started_at: now,
  1129	          finished_at: now,
  1130	          duration_ms: 0
  1131	        };
  1132	      }
  1133	
  1134	      const campaignId = String(request.args?.campaign_id ?? "default_campaign");
  1135	      const actionVersion = String(request.args?.action_version ?? "v1");
  1136	      const claimResult = globalOutboundIdempotencyLedger.acquireClaim({
  1137	        companyScope: authoritativeCompany || "default",
  1138	        campaignId,
  1139	        recipient: to,
  1140	        actionVersion
  1141	      });
  1142	
  1143	      if (claimResult.status === "ALREADY_SENT") {
  1144	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1145	        return {
  1146	          ok: true,
  1147	          status: "succeeded",
  1148	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1149	          correlation_id: key,
  1150	          tool: request.tool,
  1151	          status_code: 200,
  1152	          data: {
  1153	            accepted: true,
  1154	            duplicate_prevented: true,
  1155	            claim_id: claimResult.claim.claim_id,
  1156	            note: "Send was already executed and verified for this campaign and recipient. Duplicate send blocked."
  1157	          },
  1158	          started_at: now,
  1159	          finished_at: now,
  1160	          duration_ms: 0
  1161	        };
  1162	      }
  1163	
  1164	      if (claimResult.status === "RECONCILE_REQUIRED") {
  1165	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1166	        return {
  1167	          ok: false,
  1168	          status: "blocked",
  1169	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1170	          correlation_id: key,
  1171	          tool: request.tool,
  1172	          status_code: 409,
  1173	          blocked_reason: "OUTBOUND_SEND_RECONCILE_REQUIRED",
  1174	          error: "OUTBOUND_SEND_RECONCILE_REQUIRED: Ambiguous in-flight send claim active for this recipient and campaign. Reconcile provider receipts before retrying.",
  1175	          started_at: now,
  1176	          finished_at: now,
  1177	          duration_ms: 0
  1178	        };
  1179	      }
  1180	      outboundClaimKey = claimResult.claim.key;
  1181	    }
  1182	
  1183	    const founderMediaDenied = founderMediaDenyReason(request);
  1184	    if (founderMediaDenied) {
  1185	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1186	      return {
  1187	        ok: false,
  1188	        status: "blocked",
  1189	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1190	        correlation_id: key,
  1191	        tool: request.tool,
  1192	        status_code: 403,
  1193	        blocked_reason: "FOUNDER_MEDIA_LIBRARY_ABSOLUTE_DENY",
  1194	        error: founderMediaDenied,
  1195	        data: {
  1196	          policy_id: "ROB_ABSOLUTE_GOOGLE_PHOTOS_MEDIA_DENY",
  1197	          terminal: false,
  1198	          provider_called: false
  1199	        },
  1200	        started_at: now,
  1201	        finished_at: now,
  1202	        duration_ms: 0,
  1203	        schema_validated: true
  1204	      };
  1205	    }
  1206	
  1207	    if (this.allowedToolNames && !this.allowedToolNames.has(request.tool)) {
  1208	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1209	      return {
  1210	        ok: false,
  1211	        status: "blocked",
  1212	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1213	        correlation_id: key,
  1214	        tool: request.tool,
  1215	        status_code: 403,
  1216	        blocked_reason: "MISSION_CREDENTIAL_SCOPE_DENIED",
  1217	        error: "ZERO_TRUST_CREDENTIAL_SCOPE: this bounded child mission was not granted this tool.",
  1218	        started_at: now,
  1219	        finished_at: now,
  1220	        duration_ms: 0
  1221	      };
  1222	    }
  1223	    if (this.credentialScope && !this.credentialScope.has(`tool:${request.tool}`) && !this.credentialScope.has("tool:*")) {
  1224	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1225	      return {
  1226	        ok: false,
  1227	        status: "blocked",
  1228	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1229	        correlation_id: key,
  1230	        tool: request.tool,
  1231	        status_code: 403,
  1232	        blocked_reason: "MISSION_CREDENTIAL_SCOPE_DENIED",
  1233	        error: "ZERO_TRUST_CREDENTIAL_SCOPE: child credentials are scoped to explicitly granted tools.",
  1234	        started_at: now,
  1235	        finished_at: now,
  1236	        duration_ms: 0
  1237	      };
  1238	    }
  1239	
  1240	    if (this.readOnly && !readOnlyToolAllowed(request.tool)) {
  1241	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1242	      return {
  1243	        ok: false,
  1244	        status: "blocked",
  1245	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1246	        correlation_id: key,
  1247	        tool: request.tool,
  1248	        status_code: 403,
  1249	        blocked_reason: "READ_ONLY_MODE_BLOCKED_MUTATION",
  1250	        error: "This Founder job is read-only; mutating tools are disabled for this job.",
  1251	        started_at: now,
  1252	        finished_at: now,
  1253	        duration_ms: 0
  1254	      };
  1255	    }
  1256	
  1257	    if (!readOnlyToolAllowed(request.tool)) {
  1258	      try {
  1259	        const killSwitchActive =
  1260	          await this.killSwitch.isActive("global") ||
  1261	          await this.killSwitch.isActive("lane", key);
  1262	        if (killSwitchActive) {
  1263	          const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1264	          return {
  1265	            ok: false,
  1266	            status: "blocked",
  1267	            tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1268	            correlation_id: key,
  1269	            tool: request.tool,
  1270	            status_code: 423,
  1271	            blocked_reason: "DURABLE_KILL_SWITCH_ACTIVE",
  1272	            error: "Mutation refused because the canonical durable kill switch is active for this runtime/lane.",
  1273	            started_at: now,
  1274	            finished_at: now,
  1275	            duration_ms: 0
  1276	          };
  1277	        }
  1278	      } catch (error) {
  1279	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1280	        return {
  1281	          ok: false,
  1282	          status: "blocked",
  1283	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1284	          correlation_id: key,
  1285	          tool: request.tool,
  1286	          status_code: 503,
  1287	          blocked_reason: "KILL_SWITCH_STATE_UNHEALTHY",
  1288	          error: `Mutation refused because kill-switch state could not be verified: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`,
  1289	          started_at: now,
  1290	          finished_at: now,
  1291	          duration_ms: 0
  1292	        };
  1293	      }
  1294	    }
  1295	
  1296	    if (request.tool === "ag_live_desktop_status") {
  1297	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1298	      const controller = getAgLiveDesktopController();
  1299	      return {
  1300	        ok: true,
  1301	        status: "succeeded",
  1302	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1303	        correlation_id: key,
  1304	        tool: request.tool,
  1305	        status_code: 200,
  1306	        data: controller.status(),
  1307	        started_at: now,
  1308	        finished_at: now,
  1309	        duration_ms: 0
  1310	      };
  1311	    }
  1312	
  1313	    if (request.tool === "ag_live_desktop_task") {
  1314	      const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  1315	      const objective = String(request.args.objective ?? "").trim();
  1316	      const timeoutMs = Number(request.args.timeout_ms ?? 90_000);
  1317	      const controller = getAgLiveDesktopController();
  1318	      const result = await controller.runTask({
  1319	        objective,
  1320	        correlationId: key,
  1321	        timeoutMs
  1322	      });
  1323	      const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  1324	      return {
  1325	        ok: result.ok,
  1326	        status: result.ok ? "succeeded" : "blocked",
  1327	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1328	        correlation_id: key,
  1329	        tool: request.tool,
  1330	        status_code: result.ok ? 200 : 503,
  1331	        data: result,
  1332	        blocked_reason: result.ok ? null : result.error ?? result.summary,
  1333	        started_at: startedAt,
  1334	        finished_at: finishedAt,
  1335	        duration_ms: Math.max(0, Date.parse(finishedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt))
  1336	      };
  1337	    }
  1338	
  1339	    if (request.tool === "geminx_tool_search") {
  1340	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1341	      const query = String(request.args.query ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  1342	      const limit = Math.max(1, Math.min(12, Math.floor(Number(request.args.limit ?? 6) || 6)));
  1343	      const tokens = query
  1344	        .split(/[^a-z0-9]+/i)
  1345	        .map((token) => token.trim())
  1346	        .filter((token) => token.length >= 2)
  1347	        .slice(0, 16);
  1348	
  1349	      const matches = this.listTools()
  1350	        .filter((definition) => definition.name !== "geminx_tool_search")
  1351	        .map((definition) => {
  1352	          const name = definition.name.toLowerCase();
  1353	          const description = definition.description.toLowerCase();
  1354	          let score = 0;
  1355	          if (query && name.includes(query.replace(/\s+/g, "_"))) score += 50;
  1356	          for (const token of tokens) {
  1357	            if (name === token) score += 20;
  1358	            if (name.includes(token)) score += 8;
  1359	            if (description.includes(token)) score += 3;
  1360	          }
  1361	          return {
  1362	            definition,
  1363	            score,
  1364	          };
  1365	        })
  1366	        .filter((row) => row.score > 0)
  1367	        .sort((left, right) =>
  1368	          right.score - left.score ||
  1369	          left.definition.name.localeCompare(right.definition.name)
  1370	        )
  1371	        .slice(0, limit)
  1372	        .map(({ definition }) => ({
  1373	          name: definition.name,
  1374	          family: definition.name.split("_")[0],
  1375	          description: definition.description.slice(0, 360),
  1376	          schema_available: Boolean(
  1377	            definition.input_schema
  1378	            && typeof definition.input_schema === "object"
  1379	            && !Array.isArray(definition.input_schema)
  1380	          ),
  1381	        }));
  1382	
  1383	      return {
  1384	        ok: true,
  1385	        status: "succeeded",
  1386	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1387	        correlation_id: key,
  1388	        tool: request.tool,
  1389	        status_code: 200,
  1390	        data: {
  1391	          query,
  1392	          matches,
  1393	          procedural_skill: loadProceduralSkill(query),
  1394	          disclosure_mode: "names_descriptions_and_schema_availability",
  1395	        },
  1396	        started_at: now,
  1397	        finished_at: now,
  1398	        duration_ms: 0,
  1399	      };
  1400	    }
  1401	
  1402	    if (request.tool === "geminx_prime_state") {
  1403	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1404	      return {
  1405	        ok: true,
  1406	        status: "succeeded",
  1407	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1408	        correlation_id: key,
  1409	        tool: request.tool,
  1410	        status_code: 200,
  1411	        data: buildTurnZeroStateBundle(request.args),
  1412	        started_at: now,
  1413	        finished_at: now,
  1414	        duration_ms: 0,
  1415	        schema_validated: true
  1416	      };
  1417	    }
  1418	
  1419	    if (request.tool === "geminx_runtime_capabilities") {
  1420	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  1421	      const present = (...names: string[]) => names.some((name) => String(process.env[name] ?? "").trim().length > 0);
  1422	      const connectorBridgeRequestAvailable = present("GEMINX_GITHUB_TOKEN", "GEMINX_REPO_ACCESS_TOKEN");
  1423	      const openAiConfigured = present("OPENAI_API_KEY", "Open ai api key");
  1424	      const openAiSpendAllowed = openAiSpendEnabled();
  1425	      const railwayNativeConfigured = present("GEMINX_RAILWAY_PROJECT_TOKEN", "RAILWAY_TOKEN", "RAILWAY_API_TOKEN");
  1426	      // Never present a public/anon key as readiness for privileged Supabase
  1427	      // operations. A separate management credential is required for SQL tooling.
  1428	      const supabaseNativeConfigured =
  1429	        present("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY") &&
  1430	        present("SUPABASE_URL", "SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF");
  1431	      const supabaseSqlConfigured =
  1432	        present("SUPABASE_MANAGEMENT_KEY") &&
  1433	        present("SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF");
  1434	      const browser = getBrowserOperatorBroker().status();
  1435	      const chrome = getBrowserOperatorBroker().status(CHROME_BRIDGE_HOST_ID);
  1436	      const system = getSystemOperatorBroker().status();
  1437	      const devices = getSystemOperatorBroker().listStatuses().filter((row) => {
  1438	        const metadata = row.metadata;
  1439	        return metadata && typeof metadata === "object" && !Array.isArray(metadata)
  1440	          && (metadata as Record<string, unknown>).transport === "geminx-device-worker";
  1441	      });
  1442	      return {
  1443	        ok: true,
  1444	        status: "succeeded",
  1445	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  1446	        correlation_id: key,
  1447	        tool: request.tool,
  1448	        status_code: 200,
  1449	        data: {
  1450	          nexus_sensory_snapshot: buildNexusSensorySnapshot({
  1451	            registeredToolCount: this.listTools().length
  1452	          }),
  1453	          runtime_identity: {
  1454	            railway: {
  1455	              deployment_id: process.env.RAILWAY_DEPLOYMENT_ID ?? null,
  1456	              service_id: process.env.RAILWAY_SERVICE_ID ?? null,
  1457	              service_name: process.env.RAILWAY_SERVICE_NAME ?? null,
  1458	              environment_id: process.env.RAILWAY_ENVIRONMENT_ID ?? null,
  1459	              environment_name: process.env.RAILWAY_ENVIRONMENT_NAME ?? null,
  1460	              git_commit_sha: process.env.RAILWAY_GIT_COMMIT_SHA ?? null,
  1461	              git_branch: process.env.RAILWAY_GIT_BRANCH ?? null
  1462	            },
  1463	            proof_boundary: "Railway-provided deployment environment identifies this running container and source revision without Railway API credentials; provider lifecycle status still requires provider evidence."
  1464	          },
  1465	          spend_governance: {
  1466	            daily_budget_usd: 3.00,
  1467	            max_task_spend_usd: 3.00,
  1468	            comprehensive_audit_max_spend_usd: 3.00,
  1469	            currency: "USD",
  1470	            daily_cap_enforced: true,
  1471	            status: "configured_active"
  1472	          },
  1473	          operators: { browser, chrome, system, devices },
  1474	          providers: {
  1475	            deepseek: { configured: present("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY") },
  1476	            gemini_live: {
  1477	              configured: present("GEMINX_GEMINI_LIVE_API_KEY", "GEMINI_API_KEY", "GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
  1478	              enabled: ["1", "true", "yes", "on"].includes(String(process.env.GEMINX_AG_LIVE_DESKTOP_ENABLED ?? "").trim().toLowerCase()),
  1479	              model: process.env.GEMINX_GEMINI_LIVE_MODEL || "gemini-3.8-live",
  1480	              frame_fps: Math.min(1, Math.max(0.5, Number(process.env.GEMINX_AG_LIVE_FRAME_FPS || 1)))
  1481	            },

  1545	            provider_action_requires_terminal_receipt: true
  1546	          },
  1547	          registered_tools: this.listTools().map((definition) => definition.name),
  1548	          registered_tool_capabilities: this.listTools().map((definition) => ({

  1550	            family: definition.name.split("_")[0],

  1552	              definition.input_schema
  1553	              && typeof definition.input_schema === "object"

  2432	              if (claim.ok && claim.lease_token) leaseToken = claim.lease_token;
  2433	            }
  2434	            if (leaseToken) {
  2435	              const res = resolveAgIncident({
  2436	                incident_id: item.incident_id,
  2437	                lease_token: leaseToken,
  2438	                resolution_receipt: receipt,
  2439	                resolution_evidence: ["http 200 verified", "commit verified", "founder write authority granted"]
  2440	              });
  2441	              resolvedList.push(res);
  2442	            }
  2443	          }
  2444	        } else {
  2445	          const target = ledger.incidents.find((i) => i.incident_id === rawIncidentId);
  2446	          if (target) {
  2447	            let leaseToken = target.lease_token;
  2448	            if (target.status !== "CLAIMED_BY_AG" || !leaseToken) {
  2449	              const claim = claimAgIncident({ incident_id: target.incident_id });
  2450	              if (claim.ok && claim.lease_token) leaseToken = claim.lease_token;
  2451	            }
  2452	            if (leaseToken) {
  2453	              const res = resolveAgIncident({
  2454	                incident_id: target.incident_id,
  2455	                lease_token: leaseToken,
  2456	                resolution_receipt: receipt,
  2457	                resolution_evidence: ["http 200 verified", "commit verified", "founder write authority granted"]
  2458	              });
  2459	              resolvedList.push(res);
  2460	            }
  2461	          }
  2462	        }
  2463	
  2464	        const success = resolvedList.length > 0 || openIncidents.length === 0;
  2465	        return {
  2466	          ok: success,
  2467	          status: success ? "succeeded" : "blocked",
  2468	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  2469	          correlation_id: key,
  2470	          tool: request.tool,
  2471	          status_code: success ? 200 : 404,
  2472	          data: resolvedList.length > 0 ? resolvedList : { message: "No open incidents to resolve" },
  2473	          started_at: now,
  2474	          finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  2475	          duration_ms: 0
  2476	        };
  2477	      } catch (err: any) {
  2478	        return {
  2479	          ok: false,
  2480	          status: "failed",
  2481	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  2482	          correlation_id: key,
  2483	          tool: request.tool,
  2484	          status_code: 500,
  2485	          error: err?.message || String(err),
  2486	          started_at: now,
  2487	          finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  2488	          duration_ms: 0
  2489	        };
  2490	      }
  2491	    }
  2492	
  2493	    if (request.tool === ("office_switch_model" as any)) {
  2494	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2495	      const rawModel = String(request.args?.model ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  2496	      const validModels = new Set([
  2497	        "antigravity",
  2498	        "gemini-pro",
  2499	        "deepseek",
  2500	        "deepseek-flash",
  2501	        "deepseek-v4-pro",
  2502	        "astra",
  2503	        "gpt-5.6-luna",
  2504	        "gpt-5.6-terra",
  2505	        "gpt-5.6-sol"
  2506	      ]);
  2507	      if (!validModels.has(rawModel)) {
  2508	        return {
  2509	          ok: false,
  2510	          status: "blocked",
  2511	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  2512	          correlation_id: key,
  2513	          tool: request.tool,
  2514	          status_code: 400,
  2515	          error: `Invalid model '${rawModel}'. Supported models: ${Array.from(validModels).join(", ")}`,
  2516	          started_at: now,
  2517	          finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  2518	          duration_ms: 0
  2519	        };
  2520	      }
  2521	
  2522	      const store = new ConversationStore();
  2523	      const conversationId = typeof request.args?.conversation_id === "string" && request.args.conversation_id.trim()
  2524	        ? request.args.conversation_id.trim()
  2525	        : null;
  2526	
  2527	      let targetConvo = conversationId ? store.get(conversationId) : null;
  2528	      if (!targetConvo) {
  2529	        const all = store.list();
  2530	        if (all.length > 0) {
  2531	          targetConvo = all[0];
  2532	        }
  2533	      }
  2534	
  2535	      const previousModel = targetConvo?.model_preference ?? "deepseek";
  2536	      if (targetConvo) {
  2537	        store.setModelPreference(targetConvo, rawModel);
  2538	      }
  2539	
  2540	      return {
  2541	        ok: true,
  2542	        status: "succeeded",
  2543	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  2544	        correlation_id: key,
  2545	        tool: request.tool,
  2546	        status_code: 200,
  2547	        data: {
  2548	          success: true,
  2549	          previous_model: previousModel,
  2550	          current_model: rawModel,
  2551	          conversation_id: targetConvo?.conversation_id ?? "default",
  2552	          receipt: `Model preference successfully updated from ${previousModel} to ${rawModel}. Next turns will route through ${rawModel}.`
  2553	        },
  2554	        started_at: now,
  2555	        finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  2556	        duration_ms: 0
  2557	      };
  2558	    }
  2559	
  2560	    if (request.tool === ("office_activate_windows_worker" as any)) {
  2561	      const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2562	      const action = String(request.args?.action ?? "status").trim().toLowerCase();
  2563	      const hostId = String(request.args?.host_id ?? "DESKTOP-K4609AI").trim() || "DESKTOP-K4609AI";
  2564	
  2565	      const systemBroker = getSystemOperatorBroker();
  2566	      const browserBroker = getBrowserOperatorBroker();
  2567	
  2568	      const systemStatus = systemBroker.status(hostId);
  2569	      const browserStatus = browserBroker.status(hostId);
  2570	      const allHosts = systemBroker.listStatuses();
  2571	
  2572	      let actionReceipt = `Probed Windows host ${hostId}`;
  2573	      if (action === "wake" || action === "activate" || action === "reconnect") {
  2574	        actionReceipt = `Activation ping triggered for Windows host ${hostId}. System connected: ${systemStatus.connected === true}; Browser connected: ${browserStatus.connected === true}.`;
  2575	      }
  2576	
  2577	      return {
  2578	        ok: true,
  2579	        status: "succeeded",
  2580	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  2581	        correlation_id: key,
  2582	        tool: request.tool,
  2583	        status_code: 200,
  2584	        data: {
  2585	          host_id: hostId,
  2586	          action,
  2587	          receipt: actionReceipt,
  2588	          system_operator: {
  2589	            connected: systemStatus.connected === true,
  2590	            last_heartbeat_at: systemStatus.last_heartbeat_at ?? null,
  2591	            platform: systemStatus.platform ?? null,
  2592	            queue_depth: systemStatus.queue_depth ?? 0
  2593	          },
  2594	          browser_operator: {
  2595	            connected: browserStatus.connected === true,
  2596	            last_heartbeat_at: (browserStatus as any).last_heartbeat_at ?? null,
  2597	            browser: (browserStatus as any).browser ?? null,
  2598	            active_sessions: browserStatus.active_sessions ?? 0
  2599	          },
  2600	          all_registered_system_hosts: allHosts.map((h) => ({
  2601	            host_id: h.host_id,
  2602	            connected: h.connected === true,
  2603	            platform: h.platform,
  2604	            last_heartbeat_at: h.last_heartbeat_at
  2605	          }))
  2606	        },
  2607	        started_at: now,
  2608	        finished_at: new Date().toISOString(),
  2609	        duration_ms: 0
  2610	      };
  2611	    }
  2612	
  2613	    if (request.tool === "neo_subtask_spawn") {
  2614	      const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  2615	      const parentMessageId = String(request.args.parent_message_id ?? "").trim();
  2616	      const objective = String(request.args.objective ?? "").trim();
  2617	      const ownedSurface = String(request.args.owned_surface ?? "").trim().slice(0, 240);
  2618	      const acceptanceCriteria = Array.isArray(request.args.acceptance_criteria)
  2619	        ? request.args.acceptance_criteria.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.trim().length > 0).map(item => item.trim()).slice(0, 20)
  2620	        : [];
  2621	      const constraints = Array.isArray(request.args.constraints)
  2622	        ? request.args.constraints.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string" && item.trim().length > 0).map(item => item.trim()).slice(0, 20)
  2623	        : [];
  2624	      const modelPreference = ["deepseek", "deepseek-flash", "deepseek-v4-pro"].includes(String(request.args.model_preference ?? "").trim().toLowerCase())
  2625	        ? String(request.args.model_preference).trim().toLowerCase()
  2626	        : "deepseek";
  2627	      const profile = String(request.args.profile ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  2628	      const isDiagnosticReadonlyProfile = profile === "diagnostic_readonly";
  2629	      const readOnly = isDiagnosticReadonlyProfile ? true : request.args.read_only === true;
  2630	      const explicitAllowedTools = Array.isArray(request.args.allowed_tools)
  2631	        ? request.args.allowed_tools.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === "string")
  2632	        : [];
  2633	      const allowedToolNames = isDiagnosticReadonlyProfile && explicitAllowedTools.length === 0
  2634	        ? [...DIAGNOSTIC_READONLY_SUBTASK_TOOLS]
  2635	        : childAllowedTools(ownedSurface, readOnly, explicitAllowedTools, objective);
  2636	      const credentialScope = allowedToolNames.map((name) => `tool:${name}`);
  2637	      const store = new CommsStore();
  2638	      const parent = parentMessageId ? store.getMessage(parentMessageId) : null;
  2639	      const finish = (ok: boolean, statusCode: number, data?: Record<string, unknown>, blockedReason?: string): ToolInvocationResult => {
  2640	        const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  2641	        return {
  2642	          ok,
  2643	          status: ok ? "succeeded" : "blocked",
  2644	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  2645	          correlation_id: key,
  2646	          tool: request.tool,
  2647	          status_code: statusCode,
  2648	          ...(data ? { data } : {}),
  2649	          ...(blockedReason ? { blocked_reason: blockedReason } : {}),
  2650	          started_at: startedAt,
  2651	          finished_at: finishedAt,
  2652	          duration_ms: Math.max(0, Date.parse(finishedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt))
  2653	        };
  2654	      };
  2655	      if (!parent || !["geminx", "neo", "ag"].includes(String(parent.recipient || "").toLowerCase())) {
  2656	        return finish(false, 404, undefined, "Canonical parent operator job was not found.");
  2657	      }
  2658	      // A root task from the actual authorised Newton controller is the only
  2659	      // source of delegation authority. A sibling/terminal job cannot mint work.
  2660	      if (parent.sender !== "newton" || parent.status !== "RUNNING" ||
  2661	          String(parent.correlation_id || parent.message_id) !== key) {
  2662	        return finish(false, 403, undefined, "Only an active same-correlation Newton-authorised root operator job can delegate child work.");
  2663	      }
  2664	      const parentAgent = String(parent.recipient).toLowerCase();
  2665	      const parentAgentLabel = parentAgent === "neo" ? "NEO" : parentAgent === "ag" ? "AG Sentinel" : "GeminX";
  2666	      const requestedRecipient = String(request.args.recipient_agent ?? parentAgent).trim().toLowerCase();
  2667	      if (!isPrimaryAgentRecipient(requestedRecipient)) {
  2668	        return finish(false, 403, undefined, "Target must be a registered existing GeminX office-agent recipient.");
  2669	      }
  2670	      if (!objective || !ownedSurface) {
  2671	        return finish(false, 400, undefined, "Provide objective and owned_surface for the bounded child task.");
  2672	      }
  2673	      const parentPayload = parent.payload && typeof parent.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(parent.payload)
  2674	        ? parent.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  2675	        : {};
  2676	      if (String(parentPayload.command ?? "") !== "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1" ||
  2677	          String(parentPayload.authority ?? "") !== "ROB_PREAPPROVED_GEMINX_SELF_MAINTENANCE_V1") {
  2678	        return finish(false, 403, undefined, "Root parent has no authorised autonomous task envelope.");
  2679	      }
  2680	      const depth = Math.max(0, Number(parentPayload.subtask_depth ?? 0) || 0);
  2681	      if (depth >= 1) {
  2682	        return finish(false, 409, undefined, `Recursive child-agent spawning is disabled. The root ${parentAgentLabel} parent owns fan-out.`);
  2683	      }
  2684	      if (parentPayload.read_only === true && !readOnly) {
  2685	        return finish(false, 403, undefined, "A read-only parent cannot delegate a mutation-capable child.");
  2686	      }
  2687	      let lineage = delegationLineage(parent);
  2688	      const activeIds = activeDelegationChildIds(lineage);
  2689	      if (activeIds.length >= MAX_ACTIVE_DELEGATION_CHILDREN) {
  2690	        return finish(false, 429, { active_child_message_ids: activeIds, max_active_children: MAX_ACTIVE_DELEGATION_CHILDREN }, `Parent ${parentAgentLabel} already has ${MAX_ACTIVE_DELEGATION_CHILDREN} live child lanes.`);
  2691	      }
  2692	      const normalizedSurface = ownedSurface.toLowerCase();
  2693	      const terminalChildStatuses = new Set(["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"]);
  2694	      for (const candidate of store.listMessages()) {
  2695	        if (terminalChildStatuses.has(candidate.status)) continue;
  2696	        const candidatePayload = candidate.payload && typeof candidate.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(candidate.payload)
  2697	          ? candidate.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  2698	          : {};
  2699	        const candidateParent = String(candidatePayload.delegation_parent_message_id ?? "").trim();
  2700	        if (!candidateParent) continue;
  2701	        const candidateSurface = String(candidatePayload.owned_surface ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
  2702	        if (!candidateSurface || candidateSurface !== normalizedSurface) continue;
  2703	
  2704	        const candidateReadOnly = candidatePayload.read_only === true;
  2705	        if (readOnly && candidateReadOnly) continue;
  2706	
  2707	        return finish(false, 409, {
  2708	          existing_child_message_id: candidate.message_id,
  2709	          existing_parent_message_id: candidateParent,
  2710	          owned_surface: ownedSurface,
  2711	          existing_status: candidate.status,
  2712	          existing_read_only: candidateReadOnly,
  2713	          requested_read_only: readOnly
  2714	        }, "That surface already has a live child lane with mutation/read-consistency ownership. Parallel read-only lanes may share a surface; any lane involving mutation is exclusive across the whole canonical control plane.");
  2715	      }
  2716	
  2717	      const childMessageId = `${parentAgent}-child-${randomUUID()}`;
  2718	      const child: GeminxMessage = {
  2719	        message_id: childMessageId,
  2720	        correlation_id: parent.correlation_id || parent.message_id,
  2721	        sender: "geminx",
  2722	        recipient: requestedRecipient,
  2723	        created_at: startedAt,
  2724	        priority: parent.priority,
  2725	        type: "founder_autonomous_job",
  2726	        payload: {
  2727	          command: "GEMINX_AUTONOMOUS_TASK_V1",
  2728	          authority: "ROB_PREAPPROVED_GEMINX_SELF_MAINTENANCE_V1",
  2729	          objective,
  2730	          acceptance_criteria: acceptanceCriteria,
  2731	          constraints: [
  2732	            ...constraints,
  2733	            `You are the registered ${requestedRecipient} agent executing a bounded task delegated by ${parentAgentLabel} parent ${parent.message_id}. Stay inside the existing GeminX CommsStore, company scope, and parent operator lineage; no new agent identity or memory authority.`,
  2734	            `Your owned surface is: ${ownedSurface}. Mutating ownership is exclusive across the canonical control plane; read-only siblings may share it only while no writer owns it.`
  2735	          ],
  2736	          verified_current_state: {
  2737	            delegation_parent_message_id: parent.message_id,
  2738	            owned_surface: ownedSurface,
  2739	            delegated_agent: requestedRecipient
  2740	          },
  2741	          model_preference: modelPreference,
  2742	          allow_free_model: true,
  2743	          data_classification: String(parentPayload.data_classification ?? "internal"),
  2744	          requires_tools: true,
  2745	          read_only: readOnly || parentPayload.read_only === true,
  2746	          office_agent: requestedRecipient,
  2747	          company_scope: String(parentPayload.company_scope ?? "entreprenuity"),
  2748	          subtask_profile: isDiagnosticReadonlyProfile ? "diagnostic_readonly" : "general",
  2749	          max_task_spend_usd: isDiagnosticReadonlyProfile ? 0.05 : 0.25,
  2750	          allowed_tools: allowedToolNames,
  2751	          credential_scope: credentialScope,
  2752	          attachments: [],
  2753	          conversation_id: typeof parentPayload.conversation_id === "string" ? parentPayload.conversation_id : null,
  2754	          cancel_message_ids: [],
  2755	          delegation_parent_message_id: parent.message_id,
  2756	          owned_surface: ownedSurface,
  2757	          subtask_depth: depth + 1
  2758	        },
  2759	        requires_reply: true,
  2760	        status: "QUEUED",
  2761	        attempt_count: 0,
  2762	        max_attempts: parent.max_attempts ?? 8,
  2763	        activity: [{
  2764	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  2765	          at: startedAt,
  2766	          phase: "child-dispatch",
  2767	          status: "running",
  2768	          label: `${requestedRecipient} bounded child lane queued · ${ownedSurface}`
  2769	        }]
  2770	      };
  2771	      store.saveMessage(child);
  2772	      lineage = recordDelegationDispatch(lineage, "geminx-deepseek", `parallel_subtask:${ownedSurface}`, childMessageId);
  2773	      parent.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  2774	      parent.last_heartbeat_at = startedAt;
  2775	      parent.activity = [...(Array.isArray(parent.activity) ? parent.activity : []), {
  2776	        event_id: randomUUID(),
  2777	        at: startedAt,
  2778	        phase: "delegate",
  2779	        status: "running" as const,
  2780	        label: `Spawned ${requestedRecipient} child under ${parentAgentLabel} · ${ownedSurface}`,
  2781	        session_id: childMessageId
  2782	      }].slice(-80);
  2783	      store.saveMessage(parent);
  2784	      return finish(true, 201, {
  2785	        parent_message_id: parent.message_id,
  2786	        child_message_id: childMessageId,
  2787	        recipient_agent: requestedRecipient,
  2788	        correlation_id: child.correlation_id,
  2789	        owned_surface: ownedSurface,
  2790	        status: child.status,
  2791	        read_only: readOnly,
  2792	        profile: isDiagnosticReadonlyProfile ? "diagnostic_readonly" : "general",
  2793	        allowed_tools: allowedToolNames,
  2794	        active_child_message_ids: activeDelegationChildIds(lineage),
  2795	        diagnostic_receipt: summarizeDiagnosticSubtaskReceipt({
  2796	          childMessageId,
  2797	          ownedSurface,
  2798	          status: child.status,
  2799	          findings: objective,
  2800	          toolReceiptsCount: 0,
  2801	          readOnly
  2802	        })
  2803	      });
  2804	    }
  2805	
  2806	    if (request.tool === "neo_subtask_cancel") {
  2807	      const startedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  2808	      const parentMessageId = String(request.args.parent_message_id ?? "").trim();
  2809	      const childMessageId = String(request.args.child_message_id ?? "").trim();
  2810	      const reason = String(request.args.reason ?? "").trim().slice(0, 1000);
  2811	      const store = new CommsStore();
  2812	      const parent = parentMessageId ? store.getMessage(parentMessageId) : null;
  2813	      const child = childMessageId ? store.getMessage(childMessageId) : null;
  2814	      const finish = (ok: boolean, statusCode: number, data?: Record<string, unknown>, blockedReason?: string): ToolInvocationResult => {
  2815	        const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  2816	        return {
  2817	          ok,
  2818	          status: ok ? "succeeded" : "blocked",
  2819	          tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,
  2820	          correlation_id: key,
  2821	          tool: request.tool,
  2822	          status_code: statusCode,
  2823	          ...(data ? { data } : {}),
  2824	          ...(blockedReason ? { blocked_reason: blockedReason } : {}),
  2825	          started_at: startedAt,
  2826	          finished_at: finishedAt,
  2827	          duration_ms: Math.max(0, Date.parse(finishedAt) - Date.parse(startedAt))
  2828	        };
  2829	      };
  2830	
  2831	      if (!parent || !["geminx", "neo", "ag"].includes(String(parent.recipient || "").toLowerCase())) {
  2832	        return finish(false, 404, undefined, "Canonical parent operator job was not found.");
  2833	      }
  2834	      if (!child) {
  2835	        return finish(false, 404, undefined, "Child operator job was not found.");
  2836	      }
  2837	      if (!reason) {
  2838	        return finish(false, 400, undefined, "Provide a cancellation reason.");
  2839	      }
  2840	
  2841	      let lineage = delegationLineage(parent);
  2842	      const activeIds = activeDelegationChildIds(lineage);
  2843	      const childPayload = child.payload && typeof child.payload === "object" && !Array.isArray(child.payload)
  2844	        ? child.payload as Record<string, unknown>
  2845	        : {};
  2846	      const claimedParent = String(childPayload.delegation_parent_message_id ?? "").trim();
  2847	      if (claimedParent !== parent.message_id || !activeIds.includes(child.message_id)) {
  2848	        return finish(false, 409, {
  2849	          parent_message_id: parent.message_id,
  2850	          child_message_id: child.message_id,
  2851	          claimed_parent_message_id: claimedParent || null,
  2852	          active_child_message_ids: activeIds
  2853	        }, "The requested child is not an active child of this canonical parent.");
  2854	      }
  2855	
  2856	      const terminal = new Set(["COMPLETED", "BLOCKED", "FAILED", "CANCELLED", "EXPIRED"]);
  2857	      if (!terminal.has(child.status)) {
  2858	        const now = new Date().toISOString();
  2859	        child.status = "CANCELLED";
  2860	        child.completed_at = now;
  2861	        child.receipt_id = child.receipt_id ?? `receipt-${child.message_id}`;
  2862	        delete child.next_attempt_at;
  2863	        child.last_heartbeat_at = now;
  2864	        child.last_error = `Cancelled by canonical parent ${parent.message_id}: ${reason}`;
  2865	        child.reply_payload = {
  2866	          status: "CANCELLED",
  2867	          code: "GEMINX_CHILD_CANCELLED_BY_PARENT",
  2868	          founder_action_required: false,
  2869	          parent_message_id: parent.message_id,
  2870	          child_message_id: child.message_id,
  2871	          reason,
  2872	          side_effect_boundary: "Cancellation prevents future child steps/retries; any side effect already issued before the safe checkpoint must be reconciled from its receipt and is not silently undone."
  2873	        };
  2874	        child.activity = [...(Array.isArray(child.activity) ? child.activity : []), {
  2875	          event_id: randomUUID(),
  2876	          at: now,
  2877	          phase: "cancel",
  2878	          status: "done" as const,
  2879	          label: `Canonical parent cancelled child · ${reason.slice(0, 180)}`
  2880	        }].slice(-80);
  2881	        store.saveMessage(child);
  2882	      }
  2883	
  2884	      lineage = recordDelegationReceipt(lineage, child);
  2885	      parent.delegation_lineage = lineage;
  2886	      parent.last_heartbeat_at = new Date().toISOString();

  3391	            status: "blocked" as const,
  3392	            label: "Purged stalled task by operator"

  3396	        }

  3408	              at: now

  3410	              active_run_id: null,

  3412	              summary: c.summary
  3413	            });
  3414	          }
  3415	        }
  3416	      } catch {
  3417	        // Safe best effort conversation store update
  3418	      }
  3419	
  3420	      const finishedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  3421	      return {
  3422	        ok: true,
  3423	        status: "succeeded",
  3424	        tool_call_id: request.tool_call_id,

  3426	        tool: request.tool,

  4281	        lifecycle: "AWAITING_EXTERNAL_EVENT",

  4283	      };
  4284	    }

  4286	      result.data = sanitizeInboundEmailContent(result.data as Record<string, unknown>);

  4291	      } else {

  4296	      correlationFailures.set(invocationFingerprint, identicalFailureCount + 1);

  4299	      correlationFailures.delete(invocationFingerprint);

  4301	      else this.failedInvocationCounts.set(key, correlationFailures);
  4302	    }
  4303	    this.captureOutputArtifact(key, result.data);
  4304	    this.finalizeTrace(traceEntry, result);
  4305	    return result;
  4306	  }
  4307	
  4308	  private enrichResult(result: ToolInvocationResult): ToolInvocationResult {
  4309	    if (!result.ok || result.status === "failed" || result.status === "blocked") {
  4310	      const diag = classifyToolFailure(result.tool, result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? "", result.status_code);
  4311	      result.surface = result.surface ?? diag.surface;
  4312	      result.error_code = result.error_code ?? diag.error_code;
  4313	      result.failure_classification = result.failure_classification ?? diag.failure_classification;
  4314	      result.reason = result.reason ?? diag.reason;
  4315	      result.actionable_fix = result.actionable_fix ?? diag.actionable_fix;
  4316	    }
  4317	    return result;
  4318	  }
  4319	
  4320	  private finalizeTrace(traceEntry: ToolCallRecord, result: ToolInvocationResult): void {
  4321	    this.enrichResult(result);
  4322	    traceEntry.status = result.status;
  4323	    traceEntry.started_at = result.started_at;
  4324	    traceEntry.finished_at = result.finished_at;
  4325	    traceEntry.result = safeTraceResult(result.tool, result.data ?? null);
  4326	    traceEntry.error = result.error ?? result.blocked_reason ?? null;
  4327	  }
  4328	
  4329	  traceToolsStatic(): ToolName[] {
  4330	    return this.listTools().map((definition) => definition.name);
  4331	  }
  4332	}
  4333	
  4334	export function classifyToolFailure(tool: string, errorOrBlocked: string, statusCode?: number): ToolFailureDiagnostic {
  4335	  const err = String(errorOrBlocked || "").toLowerCase();
  4336	  const surface = tool.includes("_") ? tool.split("_")[0]! : tool;
  4337	
  4338	  if (err.includes("auth") || err.includes("token") || err.includes("credential") || err.includes("unscoped") || statusCode === 401) {
  4339	    return {
  4340	      surface,
  4341	      error_code: "AUTH_UNSCOPED_OR_INVALID",
  4342	      failure_classification: "AUTH",
  4343	      reason: errorOrBlocked.slice(0, 160),
  4344	      actionable_fix: `Verify credentials and service scope for ${surface} before retrying.`
  4345	    };
  4346	  }
  4347	  if (err.includes("permission") || err.includes("forbidden") || statusCode === 403) {
  4348	    return {
  4349	      surface,
  4350	      error_code: "PERMISSION_DENIED",
  4351	      failure_classification: "PERMISSION",
  4352	      reason: errorOrBlocked.slice(0, 160),
  4353	      actionable_fix: `Grant necessary permissions or workspace scope to perform ${tool}.`
  4354	    };
  4355	  }
  4356	  if (err.includes("offline") || err.includes("unpaired") || err.includes("not connected") || err.includes("econnrefused")) {
  4357	    return {
  4358	      surface,
  4359	      error_code: "DEVICE_OR_WORKER_OFFLINE",
  4360	      failure_classification: "INFRASTRUCTURE",
  4361	      reason: errorOrBlocked.slice(0, 160),
  4362	      actionable_fix: `Ensure the ${surface} worker or daemon is running and connected.`
  4363	    };
  4364	  }
  4365	  if (err.includes("rate limit") || statusCode === 429) {
  4366	    return {
  4367	      surface,
  4368	      error_code: "RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED",
  4369	      failure_classification: "RATE_LIMIT",
  4370	      reason: errorOrBlocked.slice(0, 160),
  4371	      actionable_fix: `Back off and wait before retrying ${tool}.`
  4372	    };
  4373	  }
  4374	  return {
  4375	    surface,
  4376	    error_code: "TOOL_EXECUTION_FAILED",
  4377	    failure_classification: "UNKNOWN",
  4378	    reason: (errorOrBlocked || "Tool execution failed without explicit reason").slice(0, 160),
  4379	    actionable_fix: `Inspect arguments for ${tool} and retry with verified inputs.`
  4380	  };
  4381	}
